### [24] [ECP-Bench: Benchmarking and Learning Entertainment Content Promotion with Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22150)
*Hyomin Kim,Bowen Chen,Jin Huang,Zhao Wang,Qiaozhu Mei,Shingo Takamatsu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ECP-Bench，一个覆盖电影、游戏、音乐三大领域、33项任务、五类内容推广技能的基准（190万条目、42万余问题），系统评估大模型在完整内容推广流程中的能力及跨任务、跨领域泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内容推广涉及从内容理解到市场反响预测的一系列技能，但LLM在此类决策中的能力尚缺乏系统研究。现有工作多局限于单一任务（如流行度预测）或单一领域（如电影），难以揭示LLM在完整推广流程中的表现及跨任务、跨领域泛化能力，因此需要构建更全面的评测基准。

Method: 构建ECP-Bench基准，包含190万个电影、游戏和音乐条目，以及覆盖五类内容推广技能族的33项任务、共423,451个问题。基于此对前沿模型与开源权重模型进行评估，并对比微调前后表现、知识截止点前后稳定性以及跨领域泛化能力。

Result: 前沿模型总体准确率仅51.9%，且在知识截止点之后的内容上优势大幅削弱，最多下降19.1个百分点；而在ECP-Bench上微调的开源权重模型准确率最高达60.3%，在知识截止点前后表现更稳定，并能泛化到未见过的内容与任务，展现出有意义的跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: ECP-Bench揭示了前沿LLM在完整内容推广流程中的能力局限，尤其是对时效性内容表现不稳定；而针对性微调的开源模型在准确率、稳定性与泛化性上均具优势，表明基准化微调是提升内容推广决策支持能力的有效路径。

Abstract: Content promotion spans a broad set of skills, from understanding content to forecasting its market reception. However, LLMs' ability to support such promotion decisions remains underexplored. Existing studies are often limited to a single task (e.g., popularity prediction) or a small set of tasks within a single domain (e.g., movies). As a result, there is a lack of understanding of LLMs' abilities in the full promotion process and how these abilities generalize across different tasks and domains. In this work, we introduce ECP-Bench, a benchmark containing 1.9M movie, game, and music items and 423,451 questions across 33 tasks in five content-promotion skill families. Our evaluation shows that frontier models achieve only 51.9\% overall accuracy and lose much of their advantage on post-cutoff content, with drops of up to 19.1 percentage points. In contrast, open-weight models fine-tuned on ECP-Bench achieve up to 60.3\%, remain substantially more stable across the knowledge cutoff, generalize to unseen content and tasks, and exhibit meaningful cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [25] [Guiding the coarse levels of semantic IDs makes the fine levels learnable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22227)
*Bin Wang,Zhengyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 Guided SID，通过确定性监督索引分配强制让 RQ-VAE 的粗粒度层级编码预定义、文本grounded且任务相关的类别属性，使语义ID天然对LLM与下游任务有意义，无需额外对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式检索用短语义ID表示物品，但分词器独立训练仅做重建，其编码与下游LLM及任务不对齐。现有方案需额外对齐语料、推理/RL、逐token编码器或分词器监督，且恢复的语义来自内容本身，未必是任务所需。

Method: Guided SID 让最重要的层级按构造即可解释：用属性标签覆盖最近邻选择，以确定性监督索引分配强制粗粒度RQ-VAE层级编码一个预定义的文本grounded、任务相关类别属性，同时保持码本可学习（仍接收重建梯度）。用trie-merge构造将高基数或集合值属性映射到固定码预算，并保持合并桶语义连贯。

Result: 引导本身几乎无额外代价：尽管固定了粗粒度层级，碰撞与重建指标达到或优于vanilla基线。在仅SID编码不同的端到端A/B中，引导检索器在测量范围内所有列表长度上提升recall@k（k=1为1.36倍，k=10为1.39倍），MRR从0.0260升至0.0355，预定义属性预测频率提升4.2倍。

Conclusion: 通过按构造赋予关键层级任务相关语义，Guided SID 在不增加固有成本的前提下显著提升生成式检索效果与可解释性，为语义ID设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Generative retrieval represents each item by a short Semantic ID and casts recommendation as autoregressive generation of that sequence. Because the tokenizer is trained independently to reconstruct an item embedding, its codes are aligned with neither the downstream LLM nor the end task. Nearly every SID system therefore spends extra effort to bridge this gap--alignment corpora, reasoning/RL, or per-token encoders to make codes legible, or learned tokenizer supervision to make them task-aware--yet the recovered meaning is content-derived and may not be the meaning the task needs. We introduce Guided SID, which instead makes the levels that matter most meaningful by construction: we force the coarse RQ-VAE levels to encode a predefined categorical attribute--chosen to be text-grounded (hence legible to the LLM) and task-relevant--by deterministic supervised index assignment (overriding nearest-neighbor selection with the attribute label) while keeping the codebooks learnable (they still receive reconstruction gradients). A trie-merge construction maps any high-cardinality or set-valued attribute onto the fixed code budget while keeping merged buckets semantically coherent. Guiding costs nothing intrinsically: collision and reconstruction match or beat the vanilla baseline despite pinning the coarse level. In a matched end-to-end A/B differing only in the SID encoding, the guided retriever improves recall@k at every list length we measure (1.36x at k=1, 1.39x at k=10), raises mean reciprocal rank from 0.0260 to 0.0355, and predicts the pre-defined attribute 4.2x more often.

</details>


### [26] [Scoring With the Engine: Retrieval Exposure, Cross-Engine Divergence, and the Limits of Engine-Agnostic GEO Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22655)
*Benjamin Tannenbaum*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 通过对ChatGPT、Copilot、Google与Perplexity的观测审计发现，同一提示在不同引擎间引用的URL重叠极低（平均Jaccard仅0.0079），说明端到端可见性依赖引擎特有的曝光与选择过程，无法用无引擎的确定性页面评分完全替代，但后者仍可估计页面质量或查询-页面匹配度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究试图用确定性的、无需实时引擎的页面评分来近似生成式引擎的可见性。作者认为这类评分混淆了两个阶段：对实时引擎的曝光（exposure）与在曝光条件下的引用选择（citation selection），因此需要厘清无引擎评分的真正估计目标（estimand）。

Method: 在2026年6月6日对ChatGPT、Microsoft Copilot、Google和Perplexity进行观测审计，使用15个固定商业提示，得到589条引用观测，对应528个唯一URL和356个域名。计算同提示跨引擎的URL Jaccard相似度，并以保持各提示四引擎URL全集与各引擎列表长度的超几何基线作对照。另在6月5日至6日进行同引擎的纵向比较。

Result: 跨引擎URL重叠极小：平均成对Jaccard为0.0079，中位数为0，84.9%的引擎对无共享URL；四引擎均观测的10个提示上平均Jaccard为0.0072，仅为超几何基线（0.1272）的5.7%，零重叠出现率86.7%（基线期望12.3%）。所有60对比较的Top-5 URL重叠均为零。单引擎仅覆盖四引擎URL并集的11.4%-42.6%，96.4%的URL仅出现在一个引擎中。同引擎纵向比较显示平均URL集合周转率为67.0%。

Conclusion: 结果并不否定无引擎页面评分，而是明确了其估计目标。无实时引擎计算的评分可估计页面质量或查询-页面匹配度，而端到端可见性还额外依赖引擎特有的曝光与选择。作者主张将页面匹配度、观测曝光、条件选择与最终可见性作为相互独立的量分别报告。

Abstract: Recent work asks whether generative-engine visibility can be approximated with deterministic, engine-free page scores. We separate two stages such scores can conflate: exposure to a live engine and citation selection conditional on exposure. In an observational audit of ChatGPT, Microsoft Copilot, Google, and Perplexity, 15 fixed commercial prompts produced 589 citation observations on 6 June 2026, corresponding to 528 unique URLs and 356 domains. Same-prompt cross-engine URL overlap was extremely small: mean pairwise Jaccard similarity was 0.0079, the median was zero, and 84.9% of engine pairs shared no cited URL. On the ten prompts observed on all four engines, mean exact-URL Jaccard was 0.0072. A matched-size hypergeometric baseline preserving each prompt's four-engine URL universe and each engine's list length predicts 0.1272, so observed overlap was only 5.7% of that baseline; zero URL overlap occurred in 86.7% of comparisons versus 12.3% expected. Top-five exact-URL overlap was zero in all 60 pairwise comparisons. A single engine captured only 11.4%-42.6% of the four-engine URL union, and 96.4% of observed URLs appeared in only one engine. A separate 5-to-6 June same-engine comparison found 67.0% mean URL-set turnover. These results do not invalidate engine-free page scoring; they identify its estimand. A score computed without a live engine can estimate page quality or query-page fit, while end-to-end visibility additionally depends on engine-specific exposure and selection. We therefore argue for reporting page fit, observed exposure, conditional selection, and final visibility as distinct quantities.

</details>


### [27] [Beyond Raw Engagement: A Counterfactual Observability Framework for Recommender Systems at Netflix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22747)
*Chaoran Guo,Ding Tong,Ting-Po Lee,Scarlet Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于Netflix多个推荐系统的通用评估框架，将推荐系统可观测性视为反事实测量问题，通过偏差削减、相对性与增量性测量方法，服务内容创作者和模型开发者两类利益相关者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模推荐系统的性能理解仍是未被充分探索的挑战，尤其对内容创作者和模型开发者而言。他们可获得的原始互动信号（如浏览量和点击）混杂了内容质量、模型行为、展示偏差和受众触达等因素，难以将结果归因于正确的来源。

Method: 提出一个跨Netflix多个推荐系统的通用评估框架，将推荐系统可观测性建模为反事实测量问题：估计在没有特定内容项或模型决策时，推荐系统会如何表现以及随之而来的互动情况。提出三项以利益相关者为中心的可观测性原则，并提出涵盖偏差削减、相对性和增量性的测量方法，适用于单阶段和级联推荐系统。

Result: 通过在Netflix的多次生产部署验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架可从单一测量基础同时服务内容创作者和模型开发者两类受众，增强了Netflix多个推荐系统的可观测性。

Abstract: Understanding the performance of large-scale recommender systems remains an underexplored challenge, especially for content creators and model developers. The raw engagement signals available to them, such as views and clicks, conflate content quality, model behavior, presentation bias, and audience reach, making it hard to attribute outcomes to the right cause.
  In this work, we present a general evaluation framework that enhances observability across multiple recommender systems at Netflix and demonstrate its effectiveness through several production deployments. The framework treats recommender-system observability as a counterfactual measurement problem: estimating what the recommender would have done, and what engagement would have followed, in the absence of a specific content item or model decision. We articulate three stakeholder-centered observability principles for content creators and model developers, and propose measurement methodologies covering bias reduction, relativity, and incrementality, applicable to both single-stage and cascading recommender systems and serving both audiences from a single measurement foundation.

</details>


### [28] [Parameterized Dense-Sparse Fusion for Hybrid Retrieval: Tuning a Rank-Score Mix on BEIR SciFact with Qdrant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22770)
*Satyanarayan Pati,Srikanth Patil*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出参数化混合排序器融合稠密与稀疏检索，在SciFact调参冻结后在测试集上超过纯稠密与等权RRF，并在BEIR多单元复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在稠密嵌入列表与稀疏词法列表融合时，少量显式可控参数能否提升检索排序效果，并对比纯稠密与等权RRF基线。

Method: 参数向量包括稠密先验α、分数-排名混合λ、RRF平滑κ、可选的逐查询列表几何系数及可关闭稀疏检索的路由边距τ。在SciFact训练集(809查询)上网格搜索并冻结参数，在测试集(300)评估，并在9个zip的BEIR宏平均及全部20个索引单元上重复训练-冻结流程。

Result: 调优后(α=0.8, λ=0.75, κ=20)达0.753 nDCG@10与0.889 recall@10，优于稠密BGE(0.742/0.871)和等权RRF(0.707 nDCG@10)；列表条件α仅增+0.0006 nDCG，稀疏关闭路由被拒。BEIR宏平均等权RRF(0.479)不敌稠密(0.519)；20单元上该方法胜RRF 20/20、胜稠密16/20。

Conclusion: 参数量小且可解释，调优后略优于纯稠密并明显优于等权RRF，但系数依赖数据集；其他语料应复用参数范围而非直接复制SciFact点。

Abstract: We study a parameterized hybrid ranker that fuses a dense embedding list and a sparse lexical list. The method has a small, explicit parameter vector: a dense prior $α\in [0,1]$, a score-versus-rank mix $λ\in [0,1]$, an RRF smoothing parameter $κ> 0$, optional list-geometry coefficients that move $α$ per query, and a router margin $τ$ that can turn sparse search off. We grid-search those ranges on SciFact train (809 queries) and freeze the chosen values on SciFact test (300). The tuned rank-score mix ($α= 0.8$, $λ= 0.75$, $κ= 20$) reaches 0.753 nDCG@10 and 0.889 recall@10, outperforming dense BGE (0.742 / 0.871) and equal-weight RRF (0.707 nDCG@10) on that test split. A list-conditioned $α$ adds +0.0006 nDCG; a sparse-off router is rejected by the same train split (any $τ$ that skipped approximately 50% of queries lost nDCG). These coefficients are dataset-specific. Equal RRF with the same models does not beat dense on a nine-zip BEIR macro-average (0.479 vs. 0.519 nDCG@10). Repeating the same train-then-freeze sweep independently on all 20 indexed units beats equal RRF on 20/20 and dense on 16/20 (unit-mean nDCG@10 0.467 vs. 0.462 dense vs. 0.420 RRF). Other corpora should reuse the ranges, not a copy of the SciFact point.

</details>


### [29] [Per-Query Gating of LLM Rerankers for Multi-Hop Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22880)
*Andre Bacellar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文研究能否用一个仅依赖LLM调用前特征的学习型逐查询门控（gate）来跳过无益的LLM reranker，并在多跳检索基准上量化其节省率与代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM reranker在图增强稠密检索管线（如HippoRAG2）上带来明显成本：约\$0.2-0.3/千次查询及约一秒尾部延迟，而其在九个（数据集，K）单元中的七个可提升最终跳top-K覆盖率（最高+34.8 pp）。因此希望在不损害覆盖率的前提下跳过那些reranker无帮助的查询。

Method: 构建一个可执行的逐查询门控，仅使用LLM调用前的特征：两个检索列表的27个分数与词汇统计量，以及对小查询嵌入的PCA；所有选择（包括回退策略与阈值）均在训练折内完成并一次性应用于留出查询。另设第二种规则：基于Platt校准的有害跳过概率，按预先指定的期望有害跳过率预算设定每个单元阈值。有害跳过（rerank本可找到目标而回退未找到）与总体覆盖率一并报告。

Result: 在2WikiMultiHopQA、MuSiQue和HotpotQA的九个单元上，门控跳过了51%的调用，平均留出LastHop@K代价为1.2 pp；四个单元满足预注册的1 pp规则；有害跳过出现在八个单元（190次有害 vs 136次有益）；同等跳过率下的随机门控在高增益单元上损失2-11 pp。预算规则下，1 pp预算时跳过42%、-0.8 pp、66次有害跳过、六个单元在1 pp内，但实际危害在六个单元超出承诺（均值1.45 vs 0.83 pp），体现选择乐观；0.5 pp预算实际约实现1 pp。有害概率校准良好但判别力很弱（AUC 0.16-0.70，校准后ECE 0.025，前0.094）。

Conclusion: 学习型预LLM门控可以在可接受的覆盖率损失下跳过相当比例的reranker调用，但有害跳过不可避免且在部分单元超出预算承诺；校准概率虽可靠但判别力弱，故有害率控制存在选择乐观。早期版本报告的73%"无损"节省基于oracle回退与错误的MuSiQue目标，论文对此予以纠正。

Abstract: LLM rerankers add of the order of \$0.2-0.3 per 1,000 queries and about a second of tail latency on top of a graph-augmented dense pipeline such as HippoRAG2, and on three multi-hop benchmarks they improve final-hop top-K coverage on seven of nine (dataset, K) cells, by up to +34.8 pp. We ask whether a learned per-query gate can skip the reranker where it will not help, using only features available before the LLM call (27 score and lexical statistics of the two retrieval lists plus a PCA of a small query embedding) with an executable fallback. Every choice, including the fallback and the threshold, is made inside the training fold and applied once to held-out queries, and harmful skips (the rerank would have found the target, the fallback did not) are reported next to the aggregate coverage. Across nine cells on 2WikiMultiHopQA, MuSiQue and HotpotQA the gate skips 51% of calls at an average held-out LastHop@K cost of 1.2 pp; four cells meet a pre-registered 1 pp rule, harmful skips occur in eight (190 harmful against 136 beneficial), and a random gate at the same skip rate loses 2 to 11 pp on the high-lift cells. A second rule sets each cell's threshold from a pre-specified budget on the expected harmful-skip rate over Platt-calibrated harm probabilities (ECE 0.025 after calibration, 0.094 before): at a 1 pp budget the gate skips 42% at -0.8 pp with 66 harmful skips and six cells within 1 pp, but realised harm exceeds the promise in six cells (mean 1.45 vs 0.83 pp), a selection optimism we quantify; a 0.5 pp budget realises about 1 pp. The harm probabilities are calibrated but barely discriminative (AUC 0.16 to 0.70). An earlier version reported 73% "lossless" savings; that figure rested on an oracle fallback and a wrong MuSiQue target, and we document both.

</details>


### [30] [Inherit4Rec: Parameter Inheritance for Efficient Scaling of Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23111)
*Ruihao Zhang,Bo Chen,Xiao Wang,Jinlong Jiao,Tijian Hu,Qinglin Jia,Xiuqiang He,Xiangyu Zhao,Chaoyi Ma,Ruiming Tang,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出Inherit4Rec参数继承框架，支持稠密到稠密（D2D）扩展与稠密到稀疏（D2S）转换，在动态推荐数据下保持模型能力并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统中扩增模型容量是突破性能瓶颈的有效手段，但从零训练更大的稠密模型需要大量数据与时间，且增长的计算量与严格的线上服务预算冲突。参数继承可同时支持稠密模型增长与稀疏转换，但现有方法主要面向静态语料，在动态演化的推荐数据下会出现明显性能下降。

Method: 提出Inherit4Rec参数继承框架：Inherit4Rec-D2D结合混合增长与非对称训练，在扩展时保持前向函数不变并维持更新连续性；Inherit4Rec-D2S通过共激活感知划分与负载均衡损失构建SMoE网络，在保留稠密模型能力的同时促进专家激活均衡。

Result: 在KuaiRand-1K与工业短视频推荐数据集上的实验表明，两种转换在所有预测目标上均持续优于所评估的继承基线方法。

Conclusion: 结果证明了Inherit4Rec在工业推荐系统中用于持续容量扩展与计算高效稀疏转换的有效性。

Abstract: Scaling model capacity has emerged as an effective approach to overcoming performance bottlenecks in industrial recommender systems. However, repeatedly training larger dense models from scratch demands substantial data and time, while their growing computation conflicts with the strict serving budgets of industrial systems. Parameter inheritance provides a promising route for both dense model growth and sparse conversion, yet existing methods are primarily designed for static corpora and can suffer sharp performance drops under dynamically evolving recommendation data. To address these challenges, we propose Inherit4Rec, a parameter-inheritance framework that supports both Dense-to-Dense (D2D) growth and Dense-to-Sparse (D2S) conversion. Inherit4Rec-D2D combines hybrid growth with asymmetric training to preserve the forward function at expansion and maintain update continuity. Inherit4Rec-D2S constructs SMoE networks through co-activation-aware partitioning and a load-balancing loss, preserving dense-model capabilities while promoting balanced expert activation. Experiments on KuaiRand-1K and an industrial short-video recommendation dataset show that both transformations consistently outperform the evaluated inheritance baselines across all prediction objectives. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of Inherit4Rec for continual capacity expansion and computation-efficient sparse conversion in industrial recommender systems.

</details>


### [31] [Improving disruptive research in the EU: why strengthening European Research Council grants alone is not enough](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23115)
*Alonso Rodríguez-Navarro*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 欧盟颠覆性创新竞争力不足，根源在于颠覆性研究水平偏低；以石墨烯为例，德法意西乃至ERC资助的研究都难以与新加坡竞争，欧美中也存在差距，必须对研究政策进行根本性改革。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟颠覆性创新竞争力不足，威胁到公民习以为常的社会福利。作者认为除结构性与经济性缺陷外，还需提升颠覆性研究以增强颠覆性创新能力，而当前颠覆性研究水平过低。

Method: 以石墨烯研究为案例（欧盟设有专门计划），对比德国、法国、意大利、西班牙与新加坡的研究表现，并进一步比较欧盟与新加坡、美国、中国的研究竞争力；同时结合若干其他技术领域的例证与文献证据进行论证。

Result: 德、法、意、西均无法与新加坡竞争；更令人担忧的是，欧洲研究理事会（ERC）资助的石墨烯研究也竞争不过新加坡的研究；欧盟与美国、中国的差距同样巨大，其他技术领域情况类似。

Conclusion: 欧盟必须对研究政策进行剧烈变革；但此类变革面临政策制定者乃至科学家群体中长期存在的"虚荣文化"——数十年来一直宣称并不存在的研究卓越。若不进行剧烈变革，欧盟成为与美国、中国同等水平技术领导者的前景并不现实。

Abstract: Disruptive innovation in the EU is not sufficiently competitive; this weakness puts at risk the social benefits that its citizens take for granted. This report argues that, in addition to addressing structural and economic deficiencies, the EU must improve disruptive research to strengthen its disruptive innovation capacity. Currently, the level of disruptive research is too low. Using graphene research as an example, for which the EU has a specific programme, this report shows that Germany, France, Italy, and Spain cannot compete with Singapore. Even more concerning, the research funded by the European Research Council on graphene fails to compete with research conducted in Singapore. Similarly, the EU is far from competing with the USA or China. A few examples in this report and cited references evidence that the situation is similar in other technologies. To overcome this situation, the EU must adopt drastic changes in research policy. However, such changes face a vanity culture among policymakers and, perhaps, scientists who have been proclaiming an inexistent research excellence for decades. Without drastic changes, the prospect of the EU becoming a technological leader at the level of the USA and China cannot be considered realistic.

</details>


### [32] [From Prompt to Recommendation: A Fitted Stage Model of Brand Visibility in AI Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23162)
*Benjamin Tannenbaum*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究分析34,960条无品牌提示观察数据，发现自有域名曝光与品牌化fan-out是LLM提及品牌的关键驱动因素，基于历史与实时信号构建的预测模型AUC达0.963（GPT）和0.942（Gemini）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在生成式引擎（GPT、Gemini）的检索与生成路径中，哪些可观测因素决定了目标品牌是否被提及，并量化自有域名引用、品牌化fan-out与历史可见性对提及率的独立贡献，从而为AI搜索可见性提供可预测的诊断工具。

Method: 分析75个匿名项目的34,960条无品牌提示引擎观察数据，覆盖2,854个监控提示及2026年6-9月的重复运行。通过同项目、同提示、同引擎的重复运行进行组内对比，分离自有域名曝光与品牌化fan-out的效应；并拟合带历史先验与实时检索指标的时序logit诊断模型，在最新30%留存集上评估AUC，另进行199条提示的页面语料验证。

Result: 无品牌且无自有域名曝光时提及率仅2.8%（GPT）/3.8%（Gemini）；仅有自有域名引用时升至49.0%/58.4%；两者兼具时达91.4%/100%。在控制品牌化fan-out不存在的提示单元内，自有域名曝光平均带来40.2（GPT）与49.0（Gemini）个百分点的提及率提升；此前未提及且当前无曝光下次提及率仅1.6%/1.9%，而此前提及且有曝光则达80.5%/83.7%。全模型AUC为0.963/0.942，优于仅历史（0.937/0.917）与仅实时信号（0.880/0.840）；页面语料验证中提示-页面匹配对Gemini曝光预测（AUC 0.641）强于GPT（0.545）。

Conclusion: 该方程为预测性与观察性模型，而非对专有引擎内部机制的因果描述。结果表明显著性与实时检索曝光（尤其自有域名引用与品牌化fan-out）是LLM品牌提及的核心可观测驱动因素，且相关性位于引擎中介曝光效应的上游，可用于AI搜索可见性的诊断与预测。

Abstract: We analyze 34,960 unbranded prompt-engine observations from 75 anonymized Aiso projects, covering 2,854 distinct monitored prompts and repeated GPT and Gemini runs from June-September 2026. When neither the target brand nor its own domain appears in the observable live retrieval path, target mention rates are 2.8% for GPT and 3.8% for Gemini. With an own-domain citation but no branded fan-out, they rise to 49.0% and 58.4%. When both own-domain exposure and a branded fan-out occur, mention rates reach 91.4% and 100%.
  The relationship persists within the same project, prompt, and engine across repeated runs: among prompt cells that vary in own-domain exposure while holding branded fan-out absent, exposure is associated with a mean mention-rate increase of 40.2 percentage points on GPT and 49.0 points on Gemini. Prior visibility is independently persistent. A previous non-mention plus no current own-domain exposure yields next-run mention rates of 1.6% and 1.9%; previous mention plus current exposure yields 80.5% and 83.7%.
  We fit a chronological diagnostic model using prior-run history and contemporaneous retrieval indicators: $ \operatorname{logit}P(M_t=1)=α_e+β_e\operatorname{logit}(\widetilde P_{t-1})+γ_e E_t+δ_e F_t+θ_e^\top X. $ On the latest 30% holdout, the full model achieves AUC 0.963 on GPT and 0.942 on Gemini, compared with 0.937/0.917 for prior history alone and 0.880/0.840 for live signals alone. A manually curated prompt sensitivity gives nearly identical AUCs (0.960 and 0.943). A separate 199-prompt page-corpus validation finds that prompt-page match predicts Gemini exposure (AUC 0.641) more clearly than GPT exposure (0.545), placing relevance upstream of a larger engine-mediated exposure effect. The equation is predictive and observational, not a causal description of proprietary engine internals.

</details>


### [33] [Semantic Candidate-Job Matching: A Comparative Evaluation of Dense Embedding Models in Hybrid Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23307)
*Sai Yashwant,Siddhartha Jain,Anurag Dubey,Samaroha Chatterjee,Gantala Thulsiram*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 比较稠密嵌入模型在职位-候选人语义匹配中的表现，评估 EmbeddingGemma(base)、Cached-MNRL 微调版与 MPNet 在 RRF 混合检索管线下的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用嵌入基准与企业在高并发招聘场景下的职位-候选人匹配约束之间存在差距，需要为嵌入策略的选择提供结构化的、可复现的比较依据。

Method: 用 LLM 解析职位描述生成结构化英文检索文本与语言相关关键词，候选人简历索引为语义增强表示；在融合向量相似度与全文相关性的 RRF 混合检索管线中，对比 EmbeddingGemma(base)、Cached-MNRL 微调的 EmbeddingGemma 与 MPNet。文中还以数学形式记录了所考虑的多种对比微调目标（含 AnglE/CoSENT 风格），并说明仅保留 Cached-MNRL 适配的经验依据；评估采用微调收敛诊断、基于已部署 AI-Match 分数的批量比较评估及独立 LLM-as-a-Judge 相关性评分。

Result: 作者给出了 Cached-MNRL-only 作为优选配置的经验理由，并在批量比较评估数据集与已部署评分管线上对三种模型进行了基准对比；同时明确说明本次报告仅使用了收敛诊断与批量评分指标，而非完整的 Recall@K、MRR、nDCG 评测框架。

Conclusion: 该工作将通用嵌入基准与企业职位-候选人匹配约束相衔接，提出了在真实检索条件下比较嵌入策略的结构化基础，并定义了包含标准 IR 指标的更完整评估框架供后续可复现的模型选择使用。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative evaluation of dense embedding models for semantic candidate-job matching in high-volume staffing workflows. Incoming job descriptions are converted into structured English search text and language-specific keywords through LLM-based parsing, and candidate profiles are indexed as semantically enriched resume representations. We evaluate EmbeddingGemma (base) against EmbeddingGemma fine-tuned with Cached Multiple Negatives Ranking Loss (MNRL) within a unified hybrid retrieval pipeline that fuses vector similarity and full-text relevance via reciprocal rank fusion (RRF), and benchmark both against the MPNet model on a batch comparative evaluation dataset scored through the deployed job-candidate matching scoring pipeline. We further document, with mathematical detail, the broader set of contrastive fine-tuning objectives considered during model development (including AnglE/CoSENT-style refinement) and the empirical rationale for retaining Cached-MNRL-only adaptation as the preferred configuration. To support reproducible model selection, we define a broader evaluation framework comprising standard information retrieval metrics (Recall@K, mean reciprocal rank, nDCG) under the exact hybrid-retrieval protocol; the metrics used for the evaluation reported in this paper are fine-tuning convergence diagnostics and a batch comparative evaluation using the deployed AI-Match score and an independent LLM-as-a-Judge relevance score, and we state this scope explicitly rather than implying the full framework was measured. The paper addresses the gap between general-purpose embedding benchmarks and enterprise job-candidate matching constraints, providing a structured basis for comparing embedding strategies under realistic job-candidate retrieval conditions.

</details>


### [34] [From Ranked Documents to Reliable Contexts: An Answer-Oriented Context Construct Framework for AI Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23354)
*Yunfei Zhong,Yinqiong Cai,Lixin Su,Haosheng Qian,Lixin Zou,Yixing Fan,Sheng Xu,Jiafeng Guo,Daiting Shi,Jingzhou He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文针对AI搜索中检索目标从"排序文档"向"构建可靠生成上下文"的转变，提出三阶段框架（答案支撑、内容可信度、上下文组织），并配套工业级优化流程与评估协议，实验在检索层与答案层均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Web搜索面向人类用户，用户自行阅读排序文档并综合信息。而在AI搜索中，检索到的文档成为生成模型的输入，检索目标随之从按"搜索满意度"排序文档，转变为构建可靠上下文以生成正确答案。这一范式转变需要新的检索框架。

Method: 将上述转变形式化为"面向答案的上下文构建"，分三阶段：（1）答案支撑：识别对答案生成有信息贡献的候选文档；（2）内容可信度：从来源、时间、事实三个维度评估信息是否为正确答案提供可靠依据；（3）上下文组织：在有限上下文预算下选择、整合并结构化保留信息，以保证生成的一致性与鲁棒性。此外构建了涵盖先验与后验优化的工业级工作流，并建立覆盖检索侧上下文与最终答案的系统评估协议。

Result: 在检索（Retrieval）和答案（Answer）两个层面均取得一致提升，验证了该框架及其工业实现的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为AI搜索提供了从文档排序到上下文构建的框架化解决方案，并通过工业落地与系统评估证明了其在检索与答案层面的有效性。

Abstract: Traditional Web search follows a human-facing paradigm in which users inspect ranked documents and synthesize information themselves. In AI Search, retrieved documents instead serve as inputs to a generation model, shifting the retrieval objective from ranking documents by Search Satisfaction to constructing reliable context for correct answer generation. We formulate this shift as answer-oriented context construction through a three-stage framework: (1) Answer Support identifies candidate documents that contribute information to answer generation; (2) Content Trustworthiness assesses whether this information provides a reliable basis for correct answers from source, temporal, and factual perspectives; and (3) Context Organization selects, consolidates, and structures retained information under a finite context budget for consistent and robust generation. We further develop an industrial workflow spanning prior and posterior optimization and establish a systematic evaluation protocol covering both retrieval-side context and final answers. Experiments show consistent improvements at both Retrieval and Answer levels, demonstrating the effectiveness of the framework and its industrial implementation.

</details>


### [35] [PSD: Pseudo Self-Distillation of Memory Representation Capabilities for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23449)
*Pirzada Suhail,Menglin Xia,Xuchao Zhang,Mayukh Das,Chetan Bansal,Saravan Rajmohan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出伪自蒸馏（PSD）框架，使小语言模型通过多阶段训练从强黑盒oracle中蒸馏行为，构建分层记忆表示，从而以低成本实现记忆增强型LLM agent。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆系统正成为LLM agent的核心组件，但构建和维护记忆依赖反复调用大型专有模型，成本高昂，成为大规模部署记忆增强agent的主要障碍。而标准蒸馏方法需要教师logits或隐藏状态，闭源模型并不提供。

Method: PSD使用同一个小模型扮演两个角色：教师模型看到包含oracle答案的特权提示作为参考上下文，学生模型只看到任务提示。学生模型学习复现教师的输出分布，将oracle引导的行为吸收进自身权重，无需访问oracle内部。通过多阶段训练流程实现单模型蒸馏设置，并将外部oracle知识经由提示注入。

Result: 在LoCoMo上，PSD训练的Qwen3-0.6B、1.7B和4B在下游检索任务上达到或超过GPT-4.1-mini，部署成本仅为极小一部分，其中off-policy PSD在多数条件下效果最佳。该记忆构建能力还能分布外迁移到LongMemEval，尽管学生仅在LoCoMo上训练、未接触LongMemEval数据。

Conclusion: PSD为小模型提供了一种低成本、无需访问oracle内部的黑盒蒸馏路径来构建分层记忆表示，性能可媲美大型专有模型，且具备良好的分布外泛化能力。

Abstract: Memory systems are becoming a core component of LLM agents, but constructing and maintaining memory remains expensive because it relies on repeated calls to large proprietary language models. This cost creates a major barrier to deploying memory-enhanced agents at scale. In this paper, we present Pseudo Self-Distillation (PSD), a framework that enables small language models (SLMs) to construct hierarchical memory representations by distilling behavior from a strong black-box oracle through a multi-stage training pipeline. Standard distillation methods require access to teacher logits or hidden states, which closed models do not expose. Unlike conventional self-distillation settings, where supervision is derived from a model's own predictions, sampled rollouts, or aggregated outputs, PSD enables a single-model distillation setup while channeling external oracle knowledge through the prompt. PSD uses a single small model in two roles: a teacher that sees a privileged prompt containing the oracle's answer as reference context, and a student that sees only the task prompt. The student learns to reproduce the teacher's output distribution, absorbing oracle-guided behavior into its own weights without accessing the oracle's internals. On LoCoMo, PSD-trained Qwen3-0.6B, 1.7B, and 4B match or exceed GPT-4.1-mini on downstream retrieval at a fraction of the deployment cost, with off-policy PSD achieving the strongest results across most conditions. We further show that this memory-construction capability transfers out-of-distribution to LongMemEval, despite the students being trained exclusively on LoCoMo with no exposure to LongMemEval data.

</details>


### [36] [Beyond Relevance: Structured Semantic Supervision for Product Search with LLM-Augmented Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23646)
*Girish A. Koushik,Swapnil Bhosale,Samarth Agrawal,Hadeel Sadany,Constantin Orasan,Xiatian Zhu,Diptesh Kanojia*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: LLM在电商搜索中最有用的角色是暴露和近似结构化的语义监督信号，而不是直接替代人类判断；结构化解释类信号比标量中心性特征更有价值。

Abstract: E-commerce search requires distinguishing products that are merely related to a query from those that directly satisfy the user's shopping intent. We augment query-product pairs with structured LLM-generated query and product attributes and human-validated relevance, explanations, and centrality judgments, and evaluate these signals using a simple dual-encoder retriever and MLP re-ranker. On an augmented subset of ESCI, a human-feature oracle reaches $0.9382$ nDCG@10, while a human-free trained $Q+P$ configuration reaches $0.9258$. Synthetic approximations of the human signals reach $0.9150$ overall but provide substantial gains for difficult, low-performing queries. Ablations show that most of the oracle improvement comes from post-edited explanations and annotator comments rather than the scalar centrality feature, suggesting that LLMs are most useful for exposing and approximating structured semantic supervision rather than replacing human judgment directly.

</details>


### [37] [MuSeR: Scalable Long-sequence Recommendation with Multi-interest Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23677)
*Yongkang Fu,Beining Bao,Yu Jiang,Xiangyu Zhao,Hongyang Wei,Guangxing Chen,Zuodong Yang,Shantao Li,Zonggang Wu,Yuqi Lu,Shouke Qin,Hanmeng Liu,Maolin Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MuSeR是一个面向工业检索的多兴趣序列表示框架，通过分层时序压缩、解耦多查询兴趣提取和跨模态语义对齐，使10^4-10^5长度的用户行为序列在固定服务预算下可用，并在离线基准与百度APP在线A/B测试中取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统受限于延迟和内存预算，通常只截断保留数百条行为，导致超长序列中的长期兴趣未被充分利用；同时用户存在跨新闻、问答、短视频等多模态的异构多意图，稀疏ID嵌入难以刻画。因此需要一套能在真实实时生产管线中联合落地长序列、多兴趣、多模态建模的系统方案。

Method: 基于已部署的MGS系统构建MuSeR检索框架，包含三部分：(i) 分层时序压缩——近期行为保留全分辨率，较早片段逐步池化，使每用户10^4-10^5条交互可在固定服务预算内表示；(ii) 带正交正则化的解耦多查询兴趣提取；(iii) 跨模态语义对齐——用从大语言模型蒸馏的文本摘要增强稀疏item ID。部署层面采用异步用户表示刷新与自适应缓存，以及跨异构硬件的分层beam-search检索。

Result: 在三个公开基准和大规模工业数据集上，MuSeR的Recall@K一致优于强长序列与多兴趣基线。在百度APP首页信息流、发现页和短视频场景的在线A/B测试中，日活提升+0.26%、总会话时长提升+0.89%（均统计显著，p<0.05），同时降低了服务延迟与成本。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献并非新的建模原语，而是系统级集成：使长期、多兴趣与多模态建模能够在实时生产管线中联合部署，并给出维持其运行的工程实践，验证了系统集成在工业推荐中的实际价值。

Abstract: Ultra-long user behavior sequences carry rich signals of stable and diverse preferences, yet industrial recommender systems typically truncate histories to a few hundred actions under strict latency and memory budgets, leaving long-term interests under-utilized. Users also pursue multiple heterogeneous intents across modalities such as news, Q&A, and short video, which sparse ID embeddings alone struggle to represent. We present Multi-interest Sequence Representation (MuSeR), a retrieval framework built on the deployed MGS system, which integrates three components: (i) hierarchical temporal compression, which retains recent actions at full resolution while progressively pooling older segments, so that per-user histories of $10^{4}$-$10^{5}$ interactions fit within a fixed serving budget; (ii) disentangled multi-query interest extraction with orthogonality regularization; and (iii) multimodal semantic alignment, which augments sparse item IDs with textual summaries distilled from a large language model. For industrial deployment, MuSeR further adopts asynchronous user-representation refresh with adaptive caching and hierarchical beam-search retrieval across heterogeneous hardware. On three public benchmarks and a large-scale industrial dataset, MuSeR consistently improves Recall@$K$ over strong long-sequence and multi-interest baselines. In online A/B tests on Baidu APP's homepage feed, discovery feed, and short-video scenarios, MuSeR yields +0.26% daily active users and +0.89% total session duration (both statistically significant, p<0.05), alongside reduced serving latency and cost. Rather than proposing a new modeling primitive, our contribution is a system-level integration that makes long-term, multi-interest, and multimodal modeling jointly deployable in a real-time production pipeline, together with the engineering practices required to sustain it.

</details>


### [38] [UNIQUE: A Unified Retrieval and Ranking System for Large-Scale Feed Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23718)
*Zhuang Liu,Yongkang Fu,Zuodong Yang,Guangxing Chen,Zonggang Wu,Yuqi Lu,Shouke Qin,Shantao Li,Maolin Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UNIQUE 是一个统一的检索与排序推荐框架，采用单层扁平量化，通过早期融合架构将生成式编码检索与目标感知排序结合，实现端到端训练，并在工业级大规模场景中验证了效果与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业移动信息流系统依赖检索-排序流水线，在严格延迟约束下服务大规模、异构且快速变化的内容。现有流水线存在两个关键问题：候选检索中的分层量化不稳定，以及检索与排序阶段分离造成的信息损失。这些问题损害了长尾和冷启动推荐，并使高效服务变得复杂。

Method: 提出 UNIQUE 框架，采用单层扁平量化：将生成式基于编码的检索与目标感知排序整合进一个早期融合架构，使其能够在共享表示下进行端到端训练，同时保持高效的候选生成。此外引入平衡量化机制，以缓解码本不平衡并改善长尾表示。

Result: 离线实验从检索和排序两个角度评估 UNIQUE，码本分析显示其资源分配比分层量化更均衡。在 Mobile Baidu 首页信息流、发现页和短视频推荐场景中部署，服务大规模真实流量。在线 A/B 测试显示总观看时长提升 0.96%，总分发量提升 1.08%，新用户和高活跃用户改善明显。服务测量显示 P99 延迟 89 ms，在线推理 MFU 为 44.23%。

Conclusion: UNIQUE 为工业推荐中的统一检索与排序提供了一个稳定、高效且可投入生产的框架。

Abstract: Industrial mobile feed systems rely on a retrieval-ranking pipeline to serve large-scale, heterogeneous, and fast-changing content under strict latency constraints. However, existing pipelines still suffer from two critical issues: hierarchical quantization instability in candidate retrieval and information loss between separated retrieval and ranking stages. These issues hurt long-tail and cold-start recommendation and complicate efficient serving. To address them, we present UNIQUE, a unified retrieval and ranking recommendation framework with single-layer flat quantization. UNIQUE integrates generative code-based retrieval and target-aware ranking into one early-fusion architecture, enabling end-to-end training under a shared representation while preserving efficient candidate generation. A balanced quantization mechanism is further introduced to mitigate codebook imbalance and improve long-tail representation. Offline experiments evaluate UNIQUE from both retrieval and ranking perspectives, while codebook analysis shows more balanced resource allocation than hierarchical quantization. We deploy UNIQUE in the homepage feed, discovery-page, and short-video recommendation scenarios of Mobile Baidu, serving large-scale real-world traffic. Online A/B tests achieve a 0.96% gain in total watch duration and a 1.08% gain in total distribution volume, with notable improvements for new users and highly active users. Serving measurements show 89 ms P99 latency and 44.23% online inference MFU. These results show that UNIQUE provides a stable, efficient, and production-ready framework for unified retrieval and ranking in industrial recommendation.

</details>


### [39] [A Redundancy Reduction Approach for Controllable Sequential Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23849)
*Veronika Ivanova,Marina Munkhoeva,Ivan Razvorotnev,Evgeny Frolov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出解相关正则化训练框架BT-SR（基于Barlow Twins目标），通过共享同一预测目标的历史序列构造正样本对，重塑点积序列推荐器的表示几何，抑制流行度驱动的集中效应，实现精度与长尾曝光之间的可控权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列推荐通常面临长尾物品分布与流行度驱动的集中问题，实践者往往被迫在短列表精度与长尾曝光之间做取舍。作者希望研究特征解相关是否可作为塑造表示几何的机制，并分析其对流行度集中效应的影响。

Method: 提出解相关正则化训练框架，在下一项预测目标上加入辅助的冗余削减项，并以BT-SR实现（采用Barlow Twins目标）。为避免合成扰动，将共享同一预测目标的历史序列配对作为标签一致的正样本对。同时给出几何分析，说明解相关如何抑制用户表示空间中可能赋予热门物品全局打分优势的共享低秩方向，并提出基于分桶的对齐集中度指标来量化该效应。

Result: 在五个公开基准上，BT-SR一致提升了下一项排序质量；解相关强度可作为简单控制旋钮，在头部与尾部物品间重新分配精度，实现精度—曝光权衡。分析还显示解相关对头尾曝光的影响因数据集而异，反映其与数据时间结构的交互作用。

Conclusion: 特征解相关是一种有效塑造序列推荐表示几何并调控流行度集中度的机制，可在不牺牲整体排序质量的前提下提供精度与长尾曝光的可控权衡。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation must operate under long-tailed item distributions and popularity-driven concentration, often forcing practitioners to trade short-list accuracy against long-tail exposure. In this work, we study feature decorrelation as a mechanism for shaping representation geometry in dot-product sequential recommenders, and analyze how this, in turn, affects popularity-driven concentration. We propose a decorrelation-regularized training framework that augments next-item prediction with an auxiliary redundancy-reduction term, and instantiate it with BT-SR, which uses the Barlow Twins objective. To form label-consistent positive pairs without synthetic corruptions, we pair user histories that share the same next-item target. Beyond accuracy, we provide a geometric analysis showing how decorrelation suppresses shared low-rank directions in the user representation space that can give popular items a global scoring advantage, and we introduce a bucket-based alignment concentration metric to quantify this effect. Experiments on five public benchmarks show that BT-SR consistently improves next-item ranking quality, while the decorrelation strength acts as a simple control knob that reallocates accuracy across head and tail items, enabling accuracy-exposure trade-offs. Our analysis also reveals that the impact on head-vs-tail exposure differs across datasets, reflecting interactions between decorrelation and data temporal structure.

</details>


### [40] [Graded-Relevance Composed Multimodal Retrieval for E-commerce Visual Search at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24152)
*Anubhav Gupta,Hrushikesh Mohapatra,Prijith Chandra,Asish Mohapatra,Anuj Garg,Arvind Maan,Sudip Datta,Venkat Bulusu,Sitesh Kumar Jalan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 GradCIR 方法，用 VLM 自动构建 4 级分级相关性数据，结合迭代难负样本挖掘与层级感知角损失训练 CIR 检索器，在 Walmart 生产环境部署并取得显著指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模电商目录的视觉搜索需同时支持相似性查询与"图像+文本"的修改型查询（CIR）。但现有 CIR 方法将相关性视为二值，仅在单一正样本的三元组上训练，与真实目录中大量候选部分满足查询、需按部分匹配程度排序的场景不符，因此需要按分级相关性来训练检索器。

Method: 方法包含三部分：(i) 用 VLM 自动构建训练数据，通过目标检测+修饰合成生成查询，并产出 4 级相关性标签，无需人工标注；(ii) 迭代相关性反馈循环，从训练中的检索器挖掘难负样本以扩充训练集；(iii) 层级感知的角损失目标，直接在分级标签上训练而非压缩为二值。整体称为 GradCIR，实例化为在 350 万分级样本对上训练的 PaliGemma2 双塔编码器。

Result: 受控的分级与二值消融实验隔离了监督粒度的影响，NDCG@10 提升 4.9%–5.9%；同一方案用于其他多模态编码器可将早期融合骨干提升最多 8.5% NDCG@10。在公开 FashionIQ 基准上，微调后的 GradCIR 平均召回率达 0.6703，略优于最强同行评审的有监督基线，并达到或超过所有已发表的 CLIP-L 级别零样本 CIR 方法。

Conclusion: GradCIR 证明分级相关性监督对 CIR 检索更有效且具可扩展性，已在 Walmart 生产环境上线，服务真实视觉搜索用户流量。

Abstract: Visual search on large e-commerce catalogs must serve both "similarity" queries that ask for items resembling an uploaded image and "modifier" queries that comprise an image and text describing a desired modification (e.g. a color change or style swap). The latter is the setting known as composed image retrieval (CIR). Existing CIR methods, however, treat relevance as binary and train on triplets with a single positive target - a poor fit for real catalogs where many candidates partially satisfy a user query and ranking across that partial-match spectrum drives the customer experience. We propose a methodology for training CIR retrievers on graded relevance, consisting of: (i) a VLM to curate training data, generating both queries (object detection + modifier synthesis) and 4-level relevance labels without manual annotation, (ii) an iterative relevance-feedback loop that expands the training set by mining hard negatives from the in-training retriever, and (iii) a hierarchy-aware angular objective to train the retriever directly on the graded labels rather than collapsing them to a binary split. We call this methodology GradCIR and instantiate it on a PaliGemma2 bi-encoder trained on 3.5M graded pairs curated from raw Walmart catalog data. A controlled graded-vs-binary ablation isolates the supervision granularity and shows lift of 4.9%-5.9% in NDCG@10. The same recipe applied to other multimodal encoders lifts early-fusion backbones by up to 8.5% NDCG@10. On the public FashionIQ benchmark, GradCIR (applied to PaliGemma2) reaches 0.6703 average recall when fine-tuned, slightly ahead of the strongest peer-reviewed supervised baseline we compare against, and matching or exceeding all published CLIP-L-class zero-shot CIR methods. The system is deployed in production at Walmart, where it's serving live visual-search user traffic.

</details>


### [41] [Auditing Source Exposure in Baidu and Google AI Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24407)
*Yibo Li,Enci Guan,Yuedan Cai,Geng Liu,Francesco Pierri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对百度和谷歌的中英文AI搜索概览进行跨语言审计，发现不同平台-语言设置的概览可用性、来源曝光与重叠度差异显著，答案语义相似度与来源曝光反映AI搜索的不同维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成概览正成为搜索界面的重要层级，但其在中文搜索中的行为研究不足，因此需要跨语言审计其触发机制、来源曝光与答案相似度。

Method: 从MS MARCO采样英文查询并翻译成中文，在百度和谷歌开展跨语言审计；分析概览触发情况、可见来源域名曝光及其集中度、来源重叠度，并比较匹配查询意图下生成答案的嵌入语义相似度。

Result: 各平台-语言设置在概览可用性和来源曝光上差异显著；聚合层面可见宿主域名清单重叠度低，匹配查询答案的中位余弦相似度为0.701至0.813。

Conclusion: 答案级语义相似度与聚合来源曝光刻画了AI中介搜索的不同维度；AI搜索评估应同时考虑生成答案内容与来源可见性在平台、语言和信息环境间的分布。

Abstract: AI-generated overviews are becoming an increasingly prominent layer of search interfaces, yet their behavior in Chinese-language search remains underexplored. We conduct a cross-lingual audit of AI overview behavior on Baidu and Google using English queries sampled from MS MARCO and their translated Chinese counterparts. Our analysis examines when overviews are triggered across platform-language settings, which host domains receive visible exposure in Chinese-language overviews, how concentrated that exposure is, and how source overlap varies across settings. We also compare the embedding-based semantic similarity of generated answers for matched query intents. The results reveal substantial differences across platform-language settings in overview availability and visible source exposure. At the aggregate level, the settings exhibit low overlap in visible host-domain inventories, while matched-query answers yield median cosine similarities ranging from 0.701 to 0.813. These findings indicate that answer-level semantic similarity and aggregate source exposure capture distinct dimensions of AI-mediated search. Evaluations of AI search should therefore consider not only the content of generated answers but also how source visibility is distributed across platforms, languages, and information environments.

</details>


### [42] [What Makes a Good Semantic ID for Generative Recommendation? A Reproducibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24430)
*Yufei Chen,Junchen Fu,Jujia Zhao,Yukun Zhao,Zhaochun Ren*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模可复现研究，在统一实验框架下系统考察语义ID（SID）设计对生成式推荐的影响，发现SID设计的效果大多非单调，没有普适最优方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐中物品常以语义ID表示，但SID的设计（构建策略、码本组织、码长）差异很大，其对推荐性能的真实影响尚不清晰，缺乏统一控制的研究。

Method: 围绕"什么构成好的语义ID"这一核心问题，在统一实验框架下开展大规模可复现研究，从四个方面分析：不同SID设计的相对有效性、码本利用率与推荐质量的关系、语义码长的影响、SID设计对局部语义保持的影响，并进行跨数据集对照分析。

Result: SID设计效果大多非单调：无单一设计普遍最优，常用的RQ-VAE和OPQ设计在不同数据集上表现不一致；一级码本最均衡的方法并非始终最佳推荐器，说明利用率有诊断价值但不充分；扩大生成骨干或加长SID并非总有收益；语义邻域分析显示没有单一设计在所有局部语义保持指标上占优，不同设计具互补优势且跨数据集稳定。

Conclusion: 研究提供了对语义ID设计的可控、可复现理解，并为未来生成式推荐系统设计提供了实践性启示。

Abstract: Generative recommendation has emerged as an active research direction, where items are commonly represented by semantic IDs (SIDs): discrete codes generated token by token. Despite strong empirical results, SID designs vary widely in construction strategy, codebook organization, and code length, making their true impact on recommendation performance unclear.
  We conduct a large-scale reproducibility study to systematically investigate the impact of semantic ID design on generative recommendation under a unified experimental framework. We focus on a fundamental question: What makes a good semantic ID for generative recommendation? To answer this question, we examine four aspects: the relative effectiveness of different semantic ID designs, the connection between codebook utilization and recommendation quality, the effect of semantic code length, and the influence of semantic ID design on local item semantic preservation. Through a unified evaluation and additional cross-dataset controlled analyses, we find that the effects of SID design are largely non-monotonic: no single SID design is universally best, and commonly used RQ-VAE- and OPQ-based designs can behave inconsistently across datasets. The method with the most balanced first-level codebook is not consistently the best recommender, showing that utilization is diagnostic but insufficient. Scaling either the generative backbone or the SID length is also not always beneficial. Finally, semantic-neighborhood analysis reveals that no single SID design dominates all notions of local semantic preservation; instead, different designs exhibit complementary strengths that remain stable across datasets and neighborhood sizes. Our study provides a controlled and reproducible understanding of semantic ID design and offers practical insights for future generative recommender systems.

</details>


### [43] [UK-PRBENCH: A Paragraph-Level Precedent Retrieval Benchmark for United Kingdom Case Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24613)
*Damith Premasiri,Tharindu Ranasinghe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出UK-PRBench，一个面向英国判例法的段落级先例检索基准，并评估现有检索模型，发现该任务仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先例检索基准与方法多在文档级进行，将整篇判决书视为相关性单位。然而判决书涉及多个法律问题，仅少数段落与查询相关，文档级设定对法律实务者不够理想，因此需要段粒度的先例检索基准。

Method: 构建UK-PRBench基准，数据来自英国国家档案馆的判决书，覆盖英国广泛的法院与裁判所，并据此评估当前最先进的检索模型，建立基线结果。

Result: 实验表明，段落级先例检索对现有检索方法仍具挑战性，存在较大改进空间。

Conclusion: UK-PRBench为细粒度先例检索提供了标准化评估基准，有助于推动英国法律领域检索系统的发展。

Abstract: Prior case retrieval (PCR) aims to identify precedent cases relevant to a given query case. Existing PCR benchmarks and methods predominantly operate at the document level, treating entire judgments as the unit of relevance. This formulation is suboptimal for legal practitioners, as judgments address multiple legal issues and only a small subset of paragraphs is relevant to a particular query. Addressing this gap, we introduce UK-PRBench, a benchmark for paragraph-level precedent retrieval in UK case law, constructed from judgments obtained from the UK National Archives and covering a broad range of UK courts and tribunals. Furthermore, we evaluate state-of-the-art retrieval models and establish baseline results. Our experiments show that paragraph-level precedent retrieval remains challenging for current retrieval approaches, highlighting substantial room for improvement. UK-PRBench provides a standardised benchmark for evaluating fine-grained precedent retrieval and advancing retrieval systems for the UK legal domain.

</details>

### [44] [When Does Learning Beat Heuristics? A Case Study in Kubernetes Scheduler Score Plugins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22142)
*Wang Xuying,Zhibek Sarypbekova*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 学习型打分函数在生产调度中未能超越简单启发式，根因是训练目标错配（回归而非排序）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的Kubernetes调度器打分插件本质上是手工调优的启发式规则。作者希望验证：用生产集群trace中真实放置决策训练的机器学习打分函数，能否匹配或超越这些启发式；若不能，原因是什么。

Method: 在常用调度器模拟器上实现外部HTTP打分插件，评估两个学习模型：基于工程特征的随机森林，以及基于任务依赖图的图神经网络编码器；两者均用大规模生产集群trace训练。进行四轮特征工程迭代、离线trace占用率重建消融、两类模型的特征丰富度与数据量控制对比，以及推理延迟和容器内存敏感性分析。

Result: 按标准回归拟合（R²），两模型随四轮特征工程迭代小幅但单调提升，R²约0.042。但在真正重要的Top-1排序准确率上，两者（65-66%）均被单特征平凡启发式（按空闲CPU排序，74-84%）击败。朴素特征下R²接近0。

Conclusion: 该差距最佳解释是目标错配：模型用点回归（MSE）训练，而非排序专用目标，与学习排序领域的长期区分一致，也呼应了训练目标与部署任务对齐时RL调度器可超越启发式的证据。因此主要障碍是目标错配而非架构。代码、数据管线与实验脚本已开源。

Abstract: Kubernetes scheduler plugins that score candidate nodes are, in production, hand-tuned heuristics. We ask whether a learned scoring function - trained on real placement decisions from a production cluster trace - can match or exceed these heuristics, and if not, why. We implement an external, HTTP-backed scoring plugin for a widely used scheduler simulator and evaluate two learned models (a Random Forest over engineered features, and a graph neural network encoder over per-job task-dependency graphs) trained on a large-scale production cluster trace. Under standard regression fit (R^2), both models improve modestly but monotonically across four feature-engineering iterations, reaching R^2 of about 0.042. However, on the metric that actually matters for scheduling - Top-1 ranking accuracy, whether the model scores the machine the production scheduler actually chose highest - both learned models are outperformed by a trivial single-feature heuristic (rank by free CPU: 74-84% vs. 65-66% for either model). We show this gap is best explained by objective mismatch: both models were trained with pointwise regression (MSE) rather than a ranking-specific objective, echoing a long-standing distinction in the learning-to-rank literature. This parallels prior evidence that RL-trained schedulers can outperform heuristics when the training objective is aligned with the deployment task, suggesting objective misalignment, not architecture, is the primary obstacle here. We further report an ablation of the occupancy reconstruction required to make offline trace data usable (naive features yield R^2 near 0), a controlled comparison isolating feature richness and data volume between the two model families, and a sensitivity analysis of inference latency and serving-container memory constraints. Code, data pipelines, and experiment scripts are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [45] [The Tethys Dataset: Seven Years of Hourly Smart Water Metering and a Pipeline for Making It Usable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22358)
*Dimitrios Amaxilatis,Themistoklis Sarantakos,Georgios Mylonas,Ioannis Chatzigiannakis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文发布了Tethys数据集：来自某市政水网24栋建筑、91个月的小时级用水数据，并附有量化的质量说明（而非仅提供清洗后数据）。原始可用率59.4%，存在4次全网性中断共595天；聚合过程引入了205,200个不可能的累积值下降，修复仅需一行代码。由于水表为累积式，缺口两侧读数可确定缺口期间流量，75.9%的小时基于测量，24.1%标记为未知。数据集、逐小时溯源信息及生成流程均已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用水需求预测与漏损检测方法依赖公开数据集，但水务领域此类数据稀缺、时间跨度短，且往往仅提供经过未记录清洗流程的数据，掩盖了原始部署中的缺陷。作者希望提供一个附带质量量化说明的数据集，而非以清洗替代透明。

Method: 发布Tethys数据集：某市政水网24栋建筑、跨度91个月的小时级用水量数据；提供逐小时数据的溯源（provenance）信息，并公开生成数据的完整流水线；对聚合过程造成的累积指数异常进行识别与修正（一行代码）。

Result: 原始可用率为59.4%；数据缺失非随机：4次全网性中断共计595天，同时影响整个建筑群。聚合过程在累积指数中静默引入205,200个不可能的下降值，修正仅需一行代码。基于累积水表特性，缺口两侧读数可确定缺口期间流量，因此75.9%的小时数据基于实际测量，24.1%报告为未知。

Conclusion: Tethys以"量化质量说明"取代"掩盖缺陷"的方式发布数据，公开数据集、逐小时溯源及生成流水线，为用水需求预测和漏损检测研究提供透明、可复现的数据基础。

Abstract: Methods for water demand forecasting and leak detection are based on public datasets, and for water those are scarce, short, or released only after an undocumented cleaning process, hiding defects of the deployment they came from. We present Tethys: 91 months of hourly water consumption data from 24 buildings of a municipal water network, published with a quantitative account of its quality, rather than in place of one. Raw availability is 59.4%, while loss is not random: 4 fleet-wide outages totalling 595 days interrupt the entire estate at once. We show that the aggregation producing the released files silently introduced 205,200 impossible decreases in a cumulative index, and that correcting it is a one-line change. Because the meters are cumulative, the readings bracketing a short gap fix the volume that passed through it, so 75.9% of hours rest on a measurement, while 24.1% are reported as unknown. We release the dataset, its per-hour provenance, and the pipeline that produces it.

</details>


### [46] [Trust-Aware Output Management for Physical Neural Network in Cloud-Continuum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22443)
*Maliheh Hariri,Stefan Fischer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向异构物理神经网络(PNN)的信任感知输出管理框架,通过边缘层信任评分与雾层可靠性/不确定性融合,显著降低不安全接受率并提升输出精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PNN为云边协同计算带来新机遇,但其输出可能受噪声、漂移、延迟和不完整可靠性信息影响。现有基座管理方法主要关注发现、调用和监控,而返回输出的可靠性问题常被忽视,因此需要在调用后阶段对输出可靠性进行管理。

Method: 每个输出用质量与上下文信息表示;边缘层用轻量级信任分数决定接受、拒绝或转发至雾层;雾层对兼容输出进行不一致性检测,并采用可靠性-不确定性感知融合;同时跟踪历史信任以检测持续退化并支持重校准请求。评估采用受控与随机化的PNN输出模型。

Result: 在20个随机种子下,完整信任策略将不安全接受率从原始处理的约61.5%降至约5.0%,接受输出MAE从0.504降至0.242;风险覆盖分析表明改进并非仅由降低本地接受率导致;雾层融合方法的平均误差最低,较强不确定性感知基线有小而稳定的优势;原型每证据记录增加约2μs边缘处理开销。

Conclusion: 结果表明,调用后的可靠性管理能够提升PNN输出在边-雾-云系统中使用的安全性,信任感知的输出管理是一种可行且低开销的方案。

Abstract: Physical Neural Networks (PNNs) introduce new opportunities for cloud continuum computing, but their outputs may be affected by noise, drift, delay, and incomplete reliability information. Existing substrate-management approaches mainly focus on discovery, invocation, and monitoring, while the reliability of the returned output is often left unaddressed. This paper proposes a trust-aware output management framework for heterogeneous PNNs. Each output is represented with quality and context information, and a lightweight edge-level trust score decides whether it should be accepted, rejected, or forwarded to the fog. At the fog layer, compatible outputs are checked for disagreement and combined using reliability- and uncertainty-aware fusion. Historical trust is also tracked to detect sustained degradation and support recalibration requests. The framework is evaluated using controlled and randomized PNN output models. Across 20 random seeds, the proposed full-trust policy reduces unsafe acceptance from approximately 61.5\% for raw output handling to about 5.0\%, while accepted-output MAE decreases from 0.504 to 0.242. Risk coverage analysis shows that this improvement is not explained only by lower local acceptance. The proposed fog fusion method also achieves the lowest aggregate mean error among the evaluated methods, with a small but consistent advantage over strong uncertainty-aware baselines. The prototype adds about $2~μ\text{s}$ of edge processing per evidence record. These results show that post-invocation reliability management can improve the safe use of PNN outputs across edge--fog--cloud systems.

</details>


### [47] [Mask-Aware Execution for Efficient JEPA Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22674)
*Md Musfiqur Rahman Sanim,Zhihao Shu,Bahram Afsharmanesh,Amirali Mirian,Wei Niu,Gagan Agrawal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出 M-JEPA，一种掩码感知的执行架构，在不改变 JEPA 学习目标的前提下重构训练流程，实现最高 1.7 倍端到端训练加速和 4.75 倍 patch-embedding 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 正成为视觉、视频、音频等领域的核心表示学习范式，但现有训练流程效率低下：每个输入需经过多个掩码特定分支，存在冗余的目标端计算和内存受限的 token 路由，这些开销随掩码数量增长，限制了 GPU 效率。

Method: M-JEPA 将掩码无关计算与掩码相关路由分离，具体包括：共享上下文编码器执行、支持反向传播的融合 token 路由与切片、在目标 token 并集上的稀疏目标编码器执行，以及针对稀疏输入的掩码 patch embedding。该方法保持训练语义不变，并在五种 JEPA 变体上实现。

Result: 在 NVIDIA A100 GPU 上，相比最先进基线，M-JEPA 在 2-10 个掩码情况下实现最高 1.7 倍端到端训练加速；使用掩码 patch embedding 时，在高稀疏度下实现 4.75 倍 patch-embedding 加速。

Conclusion: 结果表明，执行重构而非修改 JEPA 目标函数，是提升 JEPA 训练效率的关键手段。

Abstract: Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) are becoming a core representation-learning primitive and a building block for latent world models across vision, video, audio, brain dynamics, and time series. Despite (potential of) wide deployment, current JEPA training pipelines are inefficient: each input is executed through multiple mask-specific branches, with redundant target-side work, and memory-bound token routing. These costs grow with the number of masks and limit GPU efficiency. We present M-JEPA, a mask-aware execution architecture that restructures JEPA training without changing the learning objective. M-JEPA separates mask-independent computation from mask-dependent routing, enabling shared context encoder execution, fused token routing and slicing with backward support, sparse target encoder execution over the union of target tokens, and masked patch embedding for sparse inputs. The resulting pipeline preserves training semantics while reducing computation, memory traffic, and synchronization overhead. We implement M-JEPA for five JEPA variants and evaluate it on NVIDIA A100 GPUs. Compared against the state-of-the-art baselines, M-JEPA achieves up to 1.7x end-to-end training speedup for 2-10 masks. Separately, with masked patch embedding, 4.75x patch-embedding speedup at high sparsity. These results show that execution restructuring, rather than changes to the JEPA objective, is a key lever for efficient JEPA training.

</details>


### [48] [Replacing Large Language Models with Jev Decision Models for Low-Latency Edge Service Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22753)
*Delong Li,Xu Wang,Haochen Gong,Rui Lang,Guangsheng Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 将Jev的决策导向API集成到边缘服务编排中，可降低决策延迟与API费用，同时保持服务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言服务请求在执行前需语言模型决策，该过程占用请求延迟预算，因此需要降低这一开销同时保证服务完成。

Method: 集成Jev决策导向API到边缘服务编排，提取四个有界意图字段并应用共享验证器、准入策略与调度器，全程考虑决策等待；对比短结构化输出DeepSeek部署（实时API测量加建模执行），并进行真实两节点OCR服务的自托管Qwen与规则基线测试。

Result: 三个连续测量块中，Jev中位客户端决策延迟降低15.9-26.5%；八个配对OCR条件下，Jev在七个中匹配DeepSeek正确按时完成数，在一个中超过；无缓存时端到端中位延迟低11.1-25.3%，每次正确完成API费用低69.0-70.6%；重复请求缓存基本消除延迟差异。

Conclusion: 决策模型替换可在测试服务路径中同时降低响应延迟与API费用，并指出新解释（fresh interpretation）是延迟节省的主要机会。

Abstract: Natural-language service requests can require a language-model decision before execution starts, consuming part of the request's latency budget. We integrate Jev's decision-oriented application programming interface (API) into edge service orchestration to reduce this overhead while retaining service completion. The integration extracts four bounded intent fields and applies a shared validator, admission policy, and scheduler, accounting for decision waiting throughout the request timeline. We compare Jev with a short, structured-output DeepSeek deployment using live API measurements followed by modeled execution, and then a real two-node optical character recognition (OCR) service with self-hosted Qwen and rule-based references. Across three consecutive measurement blocks, Jev reduces median client decision latency by 15.9-26.5%. In eight paired OCR conditions, Jev matches DeepSeek's correct, on-time completion count in seven and exceeds it in one. Without caching, median end-to-end latency on requests completed correctly by both systems is 11.1-25.3% lower; API fees per correct completion are 69.0-70.6% lower. Repeated-request caching largely removes the latency difference. The results demonstrate that decision-model substitution can lower both response latency and API fees in the tested service path, and identify fresh interpretation as the main opportunity for latency savings.

</details>


### [49] [NSP: Accelerating Variable-Length LLM Training via Nested Sequence Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22755)
*Yi'ou Wang,Xiaoyang Li,Yijie Zheng,Shouda Liu,Yuxuan Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出NSP，一种通过在共享GPU上嵌套不同大小的序列并行（SP）组来解决长尾语料训练中通信-均衡权衡的序列并行训练系统，端到端吞吐优于Static SP达1.48倍、FlexSP达1.16倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM在长尾语料上训练时面临通信与负载均衡的核心权衡：序列长度异质使得单一SP度难以适配——度小则长序列负载严重不均衡，度大则占多数的短序列承担过多通信。现有动态SP系统虽在批内混合SP度，但需将GPU划分成不相交的组并行运行多个组，反而重新引入了组间不均衡，并被迫采用代价高昂的微批变通方案。

Method: NSP在单次训练迭代中于共享GPU上嵌套不同大小的SP组，使长序列使用较大的SP组、短序列使用较小的SP组，从而仅在需要处产生通信，并在每GPU而非每组层面实现负载均衡。具体通过树结构路由规划器在内存约束下分配序列，并由执行器通过层间阶段流式传输和树级重计算来利用该层次结构。NSP支持常见SP后端且无需修改模型。

Result: 在内部生产GPU集群上，针对Qwen3-MoE工作负载、最长384K token上下文及多个长尾数据集进行评测，NSP持续提升端到端训练吞吐，相较Static SP最高提升1.48倍，相较FlexSP最高提升1.16倍。

Conclusion: NSP通过嵌套SP组的方式有效解决了长尾长上下文训练中的通信-均衡权衡问题，在无需改动模型、兼容现有SP后端的前提下显著提升训练吞吐，验证了在共享GPU上层次化序列并行调度的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Long-context LLM training on long-tailed corpora faces a central communication--balance tradeoff. Such sequence-length heterogeneity makes any single sequence-parallelism (SP) degree a poor fit for the workload: a small degree leaves the few long sequences badly imbalanced, while a large degree forces the many short sequences that dominate the workload to pay excessive communication. Existing dynamic-SP systems mix SP degrees within a batch, but to run several groups at once they partition the GPUs into disjoint groups, which reintroduces imbalance across groups and forces costly micro-batch workarounds.
  We present NSP, a sequence-parallel training system that resolves this tradeoff by nesting differently sized SP groups on shared GPUs within a single training iteration. This lets long sequences use larger SP groups while keeping short sequences on smaller ones, so communication is incurred only where needed and load is balanced per GPU rather than per group. NSP realizes this idea with a tree-structured routing planner that assigns sequences under memory constraints and an executor that exploits the resulting hierarchy through inter-level phase streaming and tree-level recomputation. NSP supports common SP backends and requires no model changes. We evaluate NSP on Qwen3-MoE workloads with up to 384K-token contexts across multiple long-tail datasets on an internal production GPU cluster. Across these settings, NSP consistently improves end-to-end training throughput, outperforming Static SP by up to 1.48x and FlexSP by up to 1.16x.

</details>


### [50] [From Idle to Urgent: A Resource-Harvested HPC Workflow for High-Fidelity Seismic Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22814)
*Tsuyoshi Ichimura,Kohei Fujita,Hideaki Ito,Wataru Sakurai,Muneo Hori,Lalith Maddegedara*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出一种紧急交互式HPC工作流，将高保真3D非线性分析与代理神经网络动态结合，兼顾平时资源利用与震后快速决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模地震发生后需在极短时间内做出可靠决策，但传统高保真3D非线性分析计算代价高昂，难以满足应急时效要求。

Method: 设计两个专用HPC内核以提升效率；平时利用闲置算力构建训练数据集；应急时耦合基于神经网络的逆分析与物理仿真，并进行动态细化。

Result: 正常运行时能耗降低76%、吞吐量提升3.7倍；应急时相比传统计算成本降低超过97.9%，可在震后30分钟内生成高可靠的空间时程地震动分布。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了"资源收割"与危机快速响应的统一，为大规模地震应急决策提供了高效可行的HPC-神经网络协同方案。

Abstract: We propose an Urgent Interactive HPC workflow that dynamically integrates high-fidelity 3D nonlinear analysis with surrogate neural networks (NNs) to enable rapid decision-making during large-scale earthquakes. This approach achieves both "Resource Harvesting", which utilizes idle computing capacity during non-emergency periods, and immediate response during crises. By developing two specialized HPC kernels, the proposed method reduces energy-to-solution by 76% and improves throughput by 3.7-fold during normal operations to efficiently construct training datasets, while during emergencies, it couples NN-based inverse analysis with physics-based simulations and dynamic refinement to reduce conventional computational costs by over 97.9%, enabling the generation of highly reliable spatial time-history ground motion distributions within 30 minutes post-earthquake.

</details>

*Jialiang Huang,Hongxuan Tang,Jingchang Chen,Yuxuan Liu,Yixiao Chen,Yuan Cheng,Yi Tao,Jingli Zhou,Yupeng Chen,Haoyu Chen,Jiarui Wang,Shengkai Lin,Chuqi Zhang,Bryan Lee Teng,Lian Guo,Zhe Fu,Wenjun Gao,Yisong Wang,Liang Zhao,Zehao Wang,Ziwei Xie,Yongqiang Guo,Peixin Cong,Ziyi Gao,Shuiping Yu,Hanwei Xu,Zuofan Wu,Zhizhou Ren,Yuyang Zhou,Bowei Zhang,Zhihuan Huang,Qihao Zhu,Lei Wang,Tianle Lin,Han Yu,Jiewen Hu,Dejian Yang,Shuo Yang,Shanghao Lu,Shaoyuan Chen,Junjie Qiu,Zhangli Sha,Yinmin Zhong,Yongtong Wu,Shiyu Wang,Wei Liu,Bingzheng Xu,Longhao Chen,Qiushi Du,Yuzhen Huang,Shirong Ma,Yaohui Wang,Mingshu Chen,Tongrui Xiong,Y. Z. Wang,Zonglin Li,Mingqi Wei,Haiyang Shen,Chengyuan Zhang,Chao Jin,Zili Zhang,R. H. Yang,Xinbo Xu,Jian Zhou,Ruidong Zhu,Yuzhe Guo,Zelun Pan,Shaoheng Nie,Erhang Li,Shuhan Lin,Zheng Liu,Anshuo Chen,Zilong Lyu,Sinuo Cao,Rui Yu,Chuhao Wang,Junyi Guo,Junxiao Song,Kaifeng Chen,Menghao Ye,Junxian Li,Di Wu,Haiyang Ma,Yilun Wang,Haoran Yang,Yizai Cai,Shichun Liu,Yiping Wang,Junbo Sun,Shicheng Xu,Xiao Bi,Ying He,Yichao Zhang,Mingxing Zhang,Liyue Zhang,Panpan Huang,Wenfeng Liang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本报告提出 DeepSeek Elastic Compute (DSec)，一个面向大规模 LLM 智能体训练与评测的生产级沙箱平台，通过统一 SDK 提供多种沙箱后端，并与 RL 框架协同设计，支撑高并发、高密度的沙箱执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模 agentic 训练与评测需要在隔离、有状态的执行环境中运行，会产生突发式沙箱创建、异构功能与隔离需求、长交互状态保留以及大规模镜像低复用等挑战，因此需要弹性的执行平台而非单一沙箱运行时。

Method: DSec 通过统一 SDK 暴露 FnCall、容器、microVM 和 full-VM 沙箱后端；在集群层面协调放置与生命周期管理，用独立版本化层组合环境，结合内存共享、回收与 CPU 调度实现高密度执行，并借助 3FS 分布式文件系统按需加载镜像数据；同时与 RL 框架协同设计，将带状态的 rollout 执行与可抢占的 GPU 训练解耦，协调沙箱生命周期以保留 rollout 状态并回收空闲资源，并缓解 reward hacking 等智能体不当行为。

Result: 单个生产规模单元约 160 节点，每天服务约 300 万个沙箱；生产环境支持超过 38 万个并发沙箱，持续每秒创建超过 5000 个沙箱。

Conclusion: 评测与部署经验表明，这些机制降低了环境搭建与镜像分发开销，提升了内存效率，并在高密度超卖下保持了延迟敏感型性能。

Abstract: Large-scale agentic training and evaluation with large language models (LLMs) rely on isolated, stateful execution environments in which models inspect repositories, invoke tools, execute commands, and interact with task-specific services. These workloads create sandboxes in large bursts, span heterogeneous functionality and isolation requirements, retain state across long interactions, and draw from large image corpora with limited reuse. Supporting them therefore requires an elastic execution platform rather than a single sandbox runtime.
  This report presents DeepSeek Elastic Compute (DSec), a production sandbox platform that exposes FnCall, container, microVM, and full-VM sandbox backends through a unified SDK. DSec coordinates placement and lifecycle management across the cluster, composes environments from independently versioned layers, combines memory sharing, reclamation, and CPU scheduling for high-density execution, and loads image data on demand from Fire-Flyer File System (3FS), a cluster-wide distributed filesystem. DSec is co-designed with the reinforcement learning (RL) framework, decouples stateful rollout execution from preemptible GPU training, coordinates sandbox lifecycle with training to preserve rollout state while reclaiming idle resources, and mitigates agent misbehavior such as reward hacking.
  A single production-scale unit of DSec spans around 160 nodes, serving about 3 million sandboxes per day; in production, it supports over 380,000 concurrent sandboxes and sustains over 5,000 sandbox creations per second. Our evaluation and deployment experience show that these mechanisms reduce environment setup and image-distribution overhead, improve memory efficiency, and preserve latency-sensitive performance under high-density overcommit.

</details>


### [52] [Silent Failures at the $2^{32}$ Boundary: A Technical Report on Large-Tensor Matrix Multiplication in PyTorch's Apple MPS Backend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22991)
*Junichiro Niimi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PyTorch 的 MPS 后端在张量规模超过 $2^{32}$ 元素时，对批量矩阵乘法（torch.bmm，进而影响 torch.matmul 与 attention）会静默返回错误结果，且无异常或警告；作者通过系统性扫描归纳出三条错误规则，并发布了扫描工具与防护代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apple Silicon 统一内存机型（192GB 以上）使桌面 GPU 上放置超 $2^{32}$ 元素的张量成为常态，而这一规模此前未被充分检验；作者希望揭示 MPS 后端在该规模下的静默正确性问题，因为静默错误比显式报错更危险，会污染下游任务结果。

Method: 采用黑盒方法，在单机上对 torch.bmm 进行扫描：覆盖 2 种 dtype、4 种内存布局、6 种形状、42 个 batch size（4096 到 65538），PyTorch 2.14.0 下共 1584 次运行，另在 10 个早期版本上做缩减扫描；以 CPU 上的 float64 计算作为参考基准判定每次结果；并在 CUDA 上做对照实验，还用公开情感分类器验证影响。

Result: 在 macOS 27.0 上，PyTorch 2.4.1 至 2.14.0 的所有受测版本中，torch.bmm 均返回相对误差大于 1 的结果且不报错。三条规则可解释 2.14.0 上的全部结果：输出超过 $2^{32}$ 元素且某操作数为转置视图时，整个输出错误，等同于忽略该操作数 strides 的计算；连续输入超过 $2^{32}$ 元素时，仅超出部分的 batch 错误，等同于索引在 $2^{32}$ 处回绕的计算；作为视图且元素数至少 $2^{31}$ 的操作数则抛异常。CUDA 上 bmm 正确，但 torch.arange 在超过 $2^{32}$ 元素时同样静默出错。公开情感分类器中一个超限 batch 损坏了三分之一输出并使其坍缩到单一类别。

Conclusion: MPS 后端在超过 $2^{32}$ 元素时存在静默错误，而且规模变大会把显式错误变成静默失败，风险随内存增长而上升。作者发布扫描框架、原始结果以及一个阻止任何 MPS 操作触及 $2^{32}$ 及以上元素的防护，代码见 https://github.com/jniimi/mps-silent-failures。

Abstract: Apple Silicon machines with 192GB or more of unified memory make it routine to place tensors with more than $2^{32}$ elements on a desktop GPU. We show that PyTorch's Metal Performance Shaders (MPS) backend silently returns wrong results for batched matrix multiplication at this scale. On macOS 27.0, torch.bmm, and therefore torch.matmul and eager attention, returns relative errors above 1 without an exception or a warning, in every PyTorch release from 2.4.1 to 2.14.0 that we tested. On one machine, we sweep bmm over two dtypes, four memory layouts, six shapes and 42 batch sizes between 4096 and 65538 (1584 runs on PyTorch 2.14.0, and a reduced sweep on ten earlier releases), and judge every result against a float64 computation on the CPU. Three rules account for every outcome on 2.14.0. When the output exceeds $2^{32}$ elements and an operand is a transposed view, the entire output is wrong and equals a computation that ignores the strides of that operand. When a contiguous input exceeds $2^{32}$ elements, only the batches beyond that point are wrong, and they equal a computation whose index wraps around at $2^{32}$. Operands that are views with at least $2^{31}$ elements raise an exception instead, so a larger problem can turn an explicit error into a silent failure. A control on CUDA is correct for bmm, although torch.arange is silently wrong above $2^{32}$ elements there as well. In a public sentiment classifier, one oversized batch corrupts a third of the outputs and collapses them onto a single class. The study is black-box: we report what the backend returns, compared with reference results. We release the sweep harness, the raw results and a guard that stops any MPS operation touching $2^{32}$ or more elements at https://github.com/jniimi/mps-silent-failures.

</details>


### [53] [Accurate Simulation of Distributed Training Jobs with Network Contention Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23278)
*Yeonho Yoo,Hyunho Lee,Hyunmok Choi,Chuck Yoo,Gyeongsik Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MoSim是一个考虑动态网络竞争的GPU集群模拟器，用于评估分布式训练作业调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU集群模拟器忽略网络竞争或用固定惩罚近似，忽略了调度决策对作业共享网络接口和链路的影响，导致平均JCT的MAPE高达73.64%。

Method: MoSim结合无GPU表征与网络竞争模型：无需GPU即可获得作业的计算时间、网络时间和网络流量，再根据当前worker分配估计共享网络接口对每次迭代时间的影响。

Result: 相比现有模拟器，MoSim将平均JCT误差降低最多3.28倍，尾部（99百分位）JCT误差降低7.79倍，makespan误差降低8.48倍；NIC竞争因素建模平均误差仅8.63%；输入构建开销降低44.6倍。

Conclusion: MoSim通过建模动态网络竞争显著提升DT作业模拟精度，并大幅降低输入构建开销。

Abstract: Trace-driven simulation is widely used to evaluate distributed training (DT) jobs in GPU clusters, but existing simulators either ignore network contention or approximate it with a fixed penalty. This misses how scheduling decisions determine which jobs share server network interfaces and inter-server links, thereby changing networking time during training. As a result, our motivating experiments demonstrate that they incur large errors, reaching up to 73.64% mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) in average job completion time (JCT). This paper introduces MoSim, a GPU-cluster simulator that models DT job execution under dynamic network contention. MoSim combines GPU-free characterization with network contention model: it obtains each job's compute time, networking time, and networking volume without GPUs, then uses the current worker assignment to estimate how shared network interfaces affect each job's iteration time. Our evaluation shows that, compared with existing simulators, MoSim reduces simulation error for average JCT by up to 3.28$\times$, tail (99th-percentile) JCT by up to 7.79$\times$, and makespan by up to 8.48$\times$, while modeling NIC contention factors with only 8.63% error on average. By avoiding real-GPU profiling, MoSim also reduces input construction overhead by 44.6$\times$.

</details>


### [54] [Accurate Distributed Tracing for Large-Scale AI Infrastructure: Time Synchronization as a Foundation for Reliable Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23301)
*Hesham Elbakoury,Ankur Sharma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出TempoTrace系统，通过将IEEE 1588v2 PTP时间同步与分布式追踪协同设计，解决大规模AI基础设施中因时钟精度不足导致的因果乱序、故障归因失真与性能诊断不可靠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模AI基础设施中的分布式追踪在时钟精度不足时会静默失效：因果事件被错误排序、故障归因被破坏、性能诊断不可靠。现有追踪系统未充分考虑异构、多租户GPU集群中时钟精度对因果序的影响，因此需要将时间同步与追踪进行协同设计。

Method: TempoTrace协同设计PTP时间同步与分布式追踪。方法包括：（1）形式化证明NTP级时钟在WAN/云条件下产生25-30%的因果倒置，LAN NTP下为11.3%；（2）引入Laplace重尾噪声模型，得到加权期望乱序率27.76%（高斯下29.37%）；（3）通过GPUDirect RDMA硬件时间戳将GPU到主机的时钟不确定度从2.1 us降至0.056 us目标残差标准差，配合亚100 ns的PTP同步；（4）使用有界Sketch Vector Clock追踪因果关系，四参与者下假阳性率低于10^-5；（5）混合规则与XGBoost的诊断引擎；（6）形式化证明的多租户模型提供隔离的逻辑时钟域；（7）针对RoCEv2/ECMP网络，用P4带内网络遥测校正路径延迟不对称；（8）应用置信策略允许负载指定精度需求与降级回退。

Result: 诊断引擎在受控验证中达到macro-F1 0.974（1,800个事件语料，McNemar p=1.25e-23）；Sketch Vector Clock假阳性率低于10^-5；P4带内遥测使归因准确率达99.1%；物理五节点测量显示NTP倒置率44.978%，且拥塞下Laplace拟合优于高斯（AIC）。

Conclusion: TempoTrace通过在异构、多租户GPU集群中协同设计PTP时间同步与分布式追踪，有效保留了因果序并提升了故障归因与性能诊断的可靠性。评估结合物理五节点测量与从512到16,384块H100 GPU、跨InfiniBand与RoCEv2网络的受控合成验证。

Abstract: Distributed tracing in large-scale AI infrastructure fails silently when clock accuracy is insufficient: causal events are misordered, fault attribution is corrupted, and performance diagnoses are unreliable. We present TempoTrace, a system that co-designs IEEE 1588v2 PTP time synchronization with distributed tracing to preserve causal ordering across heterogeneous, multi-tenant GPU clusters. We formally prove that NTP-grade clocks produce causal inversions at 25-30% of directed operation pairs under WAN/cloud conditions (11.3% under LAN NTP). A Laplace heavy-tail noise model extends the analysis beyond Gaussian assumptions, giving a weighted expected misorder rate of 27.76% vs. 29.37% Gaussian, confirming robustness to tail shape. TempoTrace reduces GPU-to-host timestamp uncertainty from 2.1 us to a design target of 0.056 us residual standard deviation via GPUDirect RDMA hardware timestamping, with sub-100 ns PTP synchronization. A bounded Sketch Vector Clock tracks causal relationships with false-positive rate below 10^-5 for up to four participants. A hybrid rule-based and XGBoost diagnosis engine achieves macro-F1 0.974 in controlled validation (1,800-incident corpus; McNemar p=1.25e-23). A formally proven multi-tenant model provides isolated logical clock domains. For RoCEv2/ECMP fabrics, P4-based in-band network telemetry corrects path-delay asymmetry, yielding 99.1% attribution accuracy. Application Confidence Policies let workloads specify precision requirements and degraded-mode fallback. Evaluation combines physical five-node measurements (NTP inversion rate 44.978%, Laplace fit preferred over Gaussian by AIC under congestion) and controlled synthetic validation with illustrative configurations from 512 to 16,384 H100 GPUs across InfiniBand and RoCEv2 fabrics.

</details>


### [55] [Co-occurrence Patterns of LoRA Adapters in Production Diffusion Model Inference Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23321)
*Tao Zhang,Bin Liao,Tao Zhou,Yanping Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文基于阿里巴巴生产级扩散模型推理数据集GenTD26，用图论方法构建LoRA适配器共现网络，从静态结构与动态演化两方面进行刻画，并提出基于top-k共现统计的预加载策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA已成为云端个性化大模型与扩散模型服务的关键技术，但生产推理负载下适配器的共现模式、资源竞争关系与演化规律缺乏系统定量的研究。

Method: 基于GenTD26生产数据集，采用图论框架构建适配器共现网络，从静态结构与动态演化两个角度进行表征；并据此提出基于top-k共现统计的预加载策略，辅以频率阈值与时间窗口的敏感性分析。

Result: (1)共现网络极度稀疏，使用频率呈显著重尾分布；(2)首个适配器带来66.1%执行延迟开销，之后边际成本递减；(3)共现关系由基础模型驱动：90.6%多适配器请求共享同一主导基础模型，66.2%显著共现边为同模型适配器对，85.8%多适配器请求中所有适配器对形成显著共现边；(4)生态呈核心-边缘两极结构，模型级周Jaccard相似度0.696，12小时窗口内top-10热门模型流失率54.5%；预加载策略k=3时覆盖81.0%测试集共现对。

Conclusion: 研究为LoRA推理服务中的缓存预加载、自适应调度与GPU显存管理提供了数据驱动的依据。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has become a key technology for serving large-scale personalized large language models and diffusion models in the cloud. However, the co-occurrence patterns, resource contention relationships, and evolutionary regularities of adapters under production inference workloads have not been systematically or quantitatively studied. Based on GenTD26, Alibaba's production diffusion model inference dataset, this paper adopts a graph-theoretic framework to construct an adapter co-occurrence network and conducts a characterization from both static structure and dynamic evolution. Our main findings are as follows. (1) The co-occurrence network is extremely sparse, and adapter usage frequency follows a significant heavy-tailed distribution. (2) Introducing the first adapter incurs a 66.1% execution-latency overhead, with diminishing marginal costs afterwards. (3) Co-occurrence relationships are driven by base models: in 90.6% of multi-adapter requests, all adapters share the same dominant base model; 66.2% of significant co-occurrence edges connect same-model adapter pairs; and in 85.8% of multi-adapter requests, all adapter pairs form significant co-occurrence edges. (4) The adapter ecosystem exhibits a core-periphery bipolar structure, with a weekly Jaccard similarity of 0.696 at the model level and a churn rate of 54.5% for the top-10 hottest models within a 12-hour window. Based on these findings, we propose a preloading strategy built on top-k co-occurrence statistics; offline experiments show that it covers 81.0% of test-set co-occurrence pairs at k=3, and sensitivity analyses across frequency thresholds and time windows verify the robustness of the conclusions. These results provide a data-driven basis for cache preloading, adaptive scheduling, and GPU memory management in LoRA inference services.

</details>


### [56] [Economical and efficient big data sharing with i-Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23438)
*Thepparit Banditwattanawong,Masawee Masdisornchote,Putchong Uthayopas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出i-Cloud云缓存机制，应对大数据在混合云中数据流出成本非均匀的问题，节省成本并提升缓存性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大数据在云中大规模共享导致云网络拥塞、服务延迟及公有云数据流出费用上升。客户端云缓存可缓解这些问题，但混合云环境中不同公有云供应商的数据流出成本非均匀，缓存机制需感知该成本差异。

Method: 将i-Cloud方法作为云缓存的核心机制进行部署，通过感知和学习数据流出成本模式来实现缓存决策。

Result: 在代表性场景下，i-Cloud每节约数据流出成本达14.78%，即每年节省4,425美元；字节命中率提升17.24%，延迟节省17.96%，缓存命中率提升29.33%，优于LRU、GDSF和LFU-DA方法。

Conclusion: i-Cloud能够学习统一成本模式，在非均匀成本环境中仍表现良好，是一种有效的云缓存核心机制。

Abstract: Big data can be hosted on cloud and being shared distributedly through cloud services in an unprecedented volume, variety and velocity. This causes not only cloud network congestions and delayed cloud services but also increases in public cloud data-out charges. Client-side cloud cache alleviates these problems. Furthermore, cloud cache must be aware of nonuniform data-out costs when big data is stored in hybrid clouds built with different public cloud providers. Deploying i-Cloud approach as the core mechanism of cloud cache could save data-out cost up to 14.78% or 4,425 USD saved per annum based on our representative scenario, and delivered 17.24% byte-hit, 17.96% delay-saving and 29.33% cache hit outperforming LRU, GDSF and LFU-DA approaches. A main finding is that i-Cloud, learning uniform cost patterns, could perform well against nonuniform cost environment.

</details>


### [57] [Conflicting Pattern Formation by Teams of Anonymous, Fully Disoriented Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23454)
*Animesh Maiti,Prakhar Shukla,Subhash Bhagat*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究两组无方向感知的匿名移动机器人在同一平面上同时完成聚集与圆形成这两个冲突任务，并提出半同步非刚性运动下的分布式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作依赖方向轴一致性和共同手性，本研究希望放宽至完全无方向感知的机器人，探讨该冲突任务在此条件下的可解性。

Method: 为半同步、非刚性运动且完全无方向感知的机器人设计分布式算法；聚集组使用全局弱多重检测，圆形成组使用局部弱多重检测。

Result: 在更弱的假设（无轴一致性和手性）下，给出了同时解决聚集与圆形成冲突问题的可行分布式算法。

Conclusion: 表明即使机器人完全无方向感知，只需对两组分别配置不同强度的多重检测能力，即可在半同步非刚性模型下同时完成冲突的聚集与圆形成任务。

Abstract: Two groups of autonomous, anonymous, and oblivious mobile robots are deployed in the two-dimensional Euclidean plane, each assigned a distinct task. We study a setting where the two groups must simultaneously solve two conflicting pattern formation problems: the \textit{gathering problem}, where robots gather at a point not known to them a priori, and the \textit{circle formation problem}, where robots occupy distinct positions on the boundary of a circle. Although each robot knows its own task, it cannot identify other members of its group.
