A prior solution~\cite{Conflict-1} addressed this problem for asynchronous robots having {\it direction-only axis agreement} and {\it global weak multiplicity detection} capability available to all robots in both groups. In contrast, in this work, we consider fully {\it disoriented robots} without any axis agreement or common \textit{chirality}. We study the feasibility of a solution to this problem for {\it disoriented robots}. We propose a distributed algorithm that solves the problem for semi-synchronous disoriented robots with non-rigid movements. Our proposed algorithm assumes global weak multiplicity detection only for the gathering group, while for the circle formation group, it requires local weak multiplicity detection.

</details>


### [58] [Explicit State and Resource Contracts for Low-Precision Pipeline Parallel Training under Captured Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23536)
*Genlang Chen,Junyi Zhu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: QEffect 提出面向低精度流水线训练的显式状态与资源契约运行时，用四条不变量统一治理 eager 与 CUDA Graph 捕获执行，保证位级一致并显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CUDA Graphs 通过静态虚拟地址重放张量操作来消除启动开销，但 FP8 流水线训练中 scaling 状态、microbatch、延迟反向任务持续变化，且 split-backward 调度（如 1F1B、Zero-Bubble）打破 LIFO 生命周期，导致传统数据流图无法向运行时传达隐藏的数值更新、非 LIFO 工作归属与缓存有效性，从而引发静默的跨流数据损坏。

Method: QEffect 形式化四条基础不变量：(1) 隐藏 scaling 更新的时间序列化；(2) 保留反向资源的代际所有权；(3) 跨优化器边界的缓存权重有效性版本化；(4) 双向调用者-图流完成同步。这些不变量统一治理 eager 与捕获执行，并支持跨进程重启重建临时图资源。基于工作所有权语义，还提出仿射直接梯度放置机制以消除冗余内存拷贝。集成于 TorchTitan 与 NVIDIA Transformer Engine。

Result: 在延迟 scaling 翻转下与原生基线保持严格位级一致；可确定性地捕获跨流顺序违规；支持无缺陷冷启动恢复。在 NVIDIA H800 GPU 上，捕获的 Transformer 层相比 eager 执行获得 1.82–2.79x 加速，直接梯度放置额外带来 1.132x 增益，每 rank-step 消除 96 次矩阵拷贝。

Conclusion: QEffect 通过显式状态与资源契约解决了低精度流水线训练中 CUDA Graphs 的状态漂移与非 LIFO 生命周期问题，实现了正确性、可重启性与显著性能提升。

Abstract: CUDA Graphs eliminate launch overheads by replaying tensor operations over static virtual addresses. However, FP8 pipeline training continuously alters the scaling states, microbatches, and deferred backward tasks that those fixed addresses represent. Split-backward schedules (e.g., 1F1B, Zero-Bubble) decouple input-gradient ($dI$) and weight-gradient ($dW$) computations to minimize bubbles, breaking traditional LIFO lifecycles. Standard dataflow graphs cannot inform the runtime of hidden numerical updates, non-LIFO work ownership, or cache validity, leading to silent cross-stream data corruption. We present QEffect, an explicit state and resource contract runtime for low-precision pipeline training. QEffect formalizes four foundational invariants: (1) temporal serialization of hidden scaling updates, (2) generational ownership of retained backward resources, (3) validity versioning for cached weights across optimizer boundaries, and (4) bidirectional caller-graph stream completion synchronization. These invariants uniformly govern eager and captured execution, allowing ephemeral graph resources to be cleanly rebuilt across process restarts. Leveraging work-ownership semantics, we also introduce an affine direct-gradient placement mechanism that eliminates redundant memory copies. Integrated with TorchTitan and NVIDIA Transformer Engine, QEffect maintains strict bitwise parity with native baselines across delayed-scaling rollovers, deterministically traps cross-stream ordering violations, and enables flawless cold-start resumption. On NVIDIA H800 GPUs, captured Transformer layers achieve 1.82--2.79x speedup over eager execution, while direct gradient placement delivers an additional 1.132x gain by eliminating 96 matrix copies per rank-step.

</details>


### [59] [SAGE: Optimal-Stopping Peer Selection for Decentralised Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23773)
*Ke Xiao,Qiyuan Wang,Christos Anagnostopoulos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE（顺序锚点门控交换），一种在单模型承载交换预算下的最优停止式对等节点选择器，用于去中心化联邦学习中的协作者选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习用点对点模型交换替代服务器聚合，使协作者选择成为不确定性下的局部决策。固定探测预算在容易的选择上浪费努力，而在难以区分的对等节点上又不够用。

Method: 接收方基于自身持有的锚点证据对候选邻居打分，一旦优势被认证即选定；仅当进一步证据能收回成本时才继续探测，否则回退到随机gossip。方法包含最优停止规则、锚点调度以及对"证书"必要性的理论证明。

Result: 证明了停止问题在有限阶段存在最优规则，锚点调度在对等风险差距和置信水平上达到阶最优；选择器以高概率不会返回差于随机gossip的节点，而无证书即承诺的选择器无此保证，并存在可分性阈值。实验中SAGE-OS比固定预算少用75.5%证据即匹配gossip，通信开销为两个已发表选择器的一半。

Conclusion: 关键决策不在于将哪个对等节点排在第一，而在于证据是否足以支持任何排序。始终依据证据行动的选择器在所有测试配置中都不如gossip。

Abstract: Decentralised federated learning replaces server aggregation with peer-to-peer model exchange, making collaborator selection a local decision under uncertainty. Fixed probe budgets waste effort on easy choices yet fall short when peers are hard to distinguish. We propose SAGE (Sequential Anchor-Gated Exchange), an optimal-stopping peer selector under a one-model-bearing-exchange budget. A receiver scores candidate neighbours on receiver-owned anchor evidence and selects once an advantage is certified. It continues probing only while further evidence repays its cost, and otherwise falls back to random gossip. We show that the stopping problem admits an optimal rule attained at a finite stage, and that the anchor schedule is order-optimal in the peer-risk gap and the confidence level. We further show that the selector never returns a peer worse than random gossip with high probability, and prove that no such guarantee holds for selectors that commit without a certificate. A separability threshold follows, below which no probing budget improves on gossip. Experiments span two image benchmarks, two graph families and three heterogeneity levels. Selectors that always act on their evidence lose to gossip in every configuration tested. SAGE-OS matches gossip on 75.5% less evidence than a fixed budget, at half the communication overhead of two published selectors. The operative decision is not which peer to rank first, but whether the evidence justifies ranking at all.

</details>


### [60] [Byzantine Causal Reliable Broadcast (BCRB) with Constant-Size Message Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24018)
*Purv Patel,Ajay D. Kshemkalyani*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了两种拜占庭因果可靠广播（BCRB）协议，将因果排序与BRB层解耦，达到常数级O(1)消息元数据开销和O(n²)通信字复杂度，并给出了概率安全性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步拜占庭可靠广播（BRB）能保证一致性但缺乏消息排序保证；而现有拜占庭因果可靠广播（BCRB）协议存在较高的元数据与通信开销（O(n³)通信字复杂度），因此需要更高效的BCRB方案。

Method: 提出新颖BCRB协议，将因果排序与BRB层解耦。给出两种变体：（1）基于门限加密和序列门控的密码学版本——发送者立即广播密文，解密份额搭载在带外ACK上，防止提前解密与抢跑；（2）非密码学版本。二者均实现概率性因果安全。采用独立指数链路延迟分布下的随机变量路径分析评估安全违例概率。

Result: 协议实现常数级O(1)消息元数据开销和O(n²)通信字复杂度（优于现有O(n³)）。两种变体均满足活性；弱安全违例概率上界为O(f⁻³·ln³f)，其中f为拜占庭进程数上界，f<n/3且f=O(n)；密码学版本的强安全违例概率上界为O(f⁻¹·ln²f)。另给出改进方案可100%保证弱安全，保持O(1)消息空间开销，但消息数与通信字复杂度为O(n³)。

Conclusion: 通过解耦因果排序与BRB层，该工作显著降低了BCRB的通信与元数据开销，并提供严格的安全性概率分析；同时给出了以更高消息复杂度换取确定性弱安全的权衡方案。

Abstract: Asynchronous Byzantine Reliable Broadcast (BRB) is a fundamental primitive that guarantees agreement and validity in distributed systems subject to Byzantine faults, but it lacks ordering guarantees. In this paper, we address Byzantine Causal Reliable Broadcast (BCRB), which builds on BRB to enforce causal message ordering. We present a novel BCRB protocol that decouples causal ordering from the BRB layer, achieving constant-size $\mathcal{O}(1)$ message metadata overhead and $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$ communication word complexity as against $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ communication word complexity of existing protocols; here $n$ is the number of processes.
  We present two variants of our protocol: a cryptographic version using a threshold encryption scheme and sequence gating, and its non-cryptographic version. In the cryptographic version, senders broadcast ciphertexts immediately, and decryption shares are piggybacked on out-of-band ACKs, preventing early decryption and front-running. In both versions, causal safety is achieved probabilistically. We evaluate the probability of causal safety violations using a random variable path analysis under independent exponential link delay distributions. We show that both variants satisfy liveness and the probability of weak safety violation is bounded by $\mathcal{O}(f^{-3}\cdot\ln^3 f)$, where $f$ is the upper bound on the number of Byzantine processes, and $f < n/3$ and $f=\mathcal{O}(n)$. Further, for the crypto version, we show that the probability of strong safety violation is bounded by $\mathcal{O}(f^{-1} \cdot \ln^2 f)$. We also show how to modify our two protocols to guarantee 100\% weak safety keeping $\mathcal{O}(1)$ message space overhead but with $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ messages and $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ communication word complexity.

</details>


### [61] [MCP-GRANITE Benchmark: GRANularity Interface TEsting for MCP-Based LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24161)
*Demetris Paschalides,Moysis Symeonides,George Pallis,Marios D. Dikaiakos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出MCP-GRANITE基准框架，将工具接口粒度作为受控变量，在边缘/IoT场景下评估9个本地部署模型（268M-20.9B）、81个多步场景、4个粒度级别共8,748次试验。结果显示4工具接口在任务完成率、工具选择F1、参数准确度与延迟之间权衡最优，粒度是MCP智能体的关键设计参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体通过MCP等标准协议调用外部工具，工具接口设计成为关键却研究不足的因素。功能如何拆分为工具会影响智能体能否选对工具并构造有效参数，这在资源受限、无法扩展模型规模的边缘设备上尤为关键。

Method: 提出开源可扩展基准框架MCP-GRANITE，把工具接口粒度视为受控变量，面向边缘与IoT场景下的MCP智能体进行评估。框架包含9个领域的81个多步场景，并在从细粒度原语工具到单一整体工具的4个粒度级别上实例化。评估9个本地部署模型（268M–20.9B参数），共8,748次试验，采用任务完成率、工具选择F1、参数准确度、延迟与资源占用等指标。

Result: 4工具接口提供最佳权衡：相较细粒度原语，任务完成率提升16.4%，相较单一整体工具提升33.6%，同时参数准确度接近翻倍。模型规模与任务完成率仅弱相关，与延迟强相关，与参数准确度的关联则不够稳健。在最优粒度下，3.2B模型的表现优于粒度不匹配时的20.9B模型。

Conclusion: 工具接口粒度是MCP智能体的关键设计参数，合理的粒度设计对边缘场景下的任务成功与效率具有决定性作用，其影响甚至超过单纯增大模型规模。

Abstract: As LLM agents increasingly interact with external tools through standardized protocols such as MCP, tool-interface design becomes a critical yet underexplored factor. How funψtionality is decomposed into tools affects whether an agent can select the right tool and construct valid arguments. This choice is especially consequential at the edge, where resource constraints limit which models can run locally and scaling up is often not an option. We present MCP-GRANITE, an open-source extensible benchmark framework that treats tool-interface granularity as a controlled variable for MCP-based agents, evaluated under edge and IoT scenarios. It comprises 81 multi-step scenarios across 9 domains, instantiated at 4 granularity levels from fine-grained primitive tools to a single tool. We evaluate 9 locally deployed models (268M-20.9B parameters) across 8,748 trials using task completion, tool selection F1, argument accuracy, latency, and resource-usage metrics. Results show that a 4-tool interface offers the best trade-off, improving task completion by 16.4% over fine-grained primitives and 33.6% over a single monolithic tool, while nearly doubling argument accuracy. Model size is only weakly correlated with task completion and strongly with latency, while its association with argument accuracy is less robust, and a 3.2B model at the optimal granularity outperforms a 20.9B model at a mismatched one. These findings identify tool-interface granularity as a key design parameter for MCP-based agents.

</details>


### [62] [A principled approach for energy-efficient training via phase-aware GPU frequency tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24205)
*Miguel Braga,Júlio Pinto,Rahma Nouaji,Olivier Michaud,Bettina Kemme,Oana Balmau,Cláudia Brito,Ricardo Macedo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PAFT是一种相位感知、动态自适应的GPU频率调节系统，利用训练流水线中的瓶颈作为能耗优化机会，在几乎不影响性能的前提下降低训练能耗，最高节能46%，平均开销4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI模型训练带来前所未有的计算需求，成为数据中心能耗的重要来源。但训练过程中相当一部分能耗并未转化为有效计算，原因是训练流水线各环节存在瓶颈。传统上瓶颈被视为性能问题，本文则将其视为能耗优化机会。

Method: PAFT持续监控流水线行为并施加细粒度频率调整，适应工作负载与系统变化。其核心思路是：当GPU处于停顿受限状态时，机会性地降低其时钟频率以匹配瓶颈设备的速度，从而在不影响执行时间的情况下节省能耗；采用相位感知、动态可调的调频策略。

Result: 在十二个广泛使用的模型上进行实验，PAFT一致优于所有基线方法，实现最高46%的能耗节省，平均性能开销仅4%。

Conclusion: 将训练瓶颈视为能耗优化机会而非单纯的性能问题，可以有效降低AI训练能耗。PAFT证明了相位感知的自适应GPU频率调节能够以极小开销获得显著节能效果。

Abstract: Modern AI model training imposes unprecedented computational demands, making it a key contributor to datacenter energy consumption. Yet a significant fraction of the energy consumed during training does not translate to useful computation due to bottlenecks throughout the training pipeline. We present PAFT, a phase-aware, dynamically adaptable GPU frequency tuning system that reduces energy consumption of training workloads with minimal performance overhead. The key insight behind PAFT is that bottlenecks represent an energy optimization opportunity, rather than purely a performance problem: when GPUs are bound to stall, PAFT opportunistically reduces their clock frequencies to match the pace of bottlenecked devices, saving energy without impacting execution time. PAFT achieves this by continuously monitoring pipeline behavior and applying fine-grained frequency adjustments, adapting to workload and system changes. Experiments conducted on twelve widely used models show that PAFT consistently outperforms all baselines, achieving energy savings of up to 46% with an average overhead of 4%.

</details>


### [63] [Toward GPU-Resident Climate Models: A Feasibility Study on Lossy Compression for the Spherical Harmonic Transform's Communication Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24294)
*Lorenzo Breschi,Flavio Vella*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 在球谐变换(SHT)主导的GPU化业务大气模式中，用GPU常驻有损压缩（ZFP）压缩全局pencil转置通信数据，可在相同存储预算下比float16截断大幅降低误差，并进一步获得约1.93倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业务伪谱大气模式（如ECMWF IFS）几乎仅运行在CPU上，GPU移植尚在开发。此类模式依赖SHT，每个时间步的前向与逆向SHT都依赖跨计算节点的全局pencil转置，在大规模节点数下这些全局集合通信主导墙钟时间。因此需要降低这些通信开销，同时保持精度。

Method: 研究GPU常驻的有损压缩方案，以DYAMOND高分辨率业务数据集中的代表性场作为输入；将实测的GPU压缩吞吐量与SimGrid网络模拟相结合，评估压缩对SHT全局通信时间的影响。重点对比ZFP在不同位率下的表现与float16截断。

Result: 在约等于float16的存储预算下，ZFP以每值16位(rate-16)压缩可达到与float16截断相同的通信时间缩减，同时平均相对误差约低1600倍；ZFP以每值8位(rate-8)可获得约为float16的1.93倍加速，同时平均相对误差仍低约4倍。

Conclusion: GPU常驻有损压缩（ZFP）是缓解伪谱大气模式中SHT全局通信瓶颈的有效手段：在相同存储预算下显著优于float16，在更低位率下兼具更高加速比与更好精度，为GPU化业务预报模式的通信优化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Operational pseudospectral atmospheric models such as the ECMWF Integrated Forecasting System (IFS) run today almost exclusively on CPUs; GPU ports are under active development but not yet used in production. These models rely on the Spherical Harmonic Transform (SHT). Each time-step requires forward and inverse SHTs, and both passes depend on global pencil transposition that redistribute multi-dimensional arrays across compute nodes. At large node counts these global collectives dominate wall-clock time. We investigate GPU-resident lossy compression, using representative fields from the DYAMOND high-resolution operational dataset as input, and combining measured GPU compression throughput with SimGrid network simulation, we show that ZFP at 16 bits per value (rate-16), about the same storage budget as float16, matches the communication-time reduction of float16 truncation while delivering approximately $\mathbf{1600\times}$ lower mean relative error. ZFP at 8 bits per value (rate-8) achieves approximately $\mathbf{1.93\times}$ the speedup of float16 while retaining $\mathbf{4\times}$ lower mean relative error.

</details>


### [64] [Conduit: An Experience Data Plane for Distributed Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24456)
*Sitong Zhang,Tuo Shi,Mario Di Francesco,Zeke Wang,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Conduit 将分布式强化学习中的经验管理抽象为显式的系统优化问题，通过 Experience Data Plane（EDP）与容量约束、带宽感知的放置策略及延迟感知调度，在 RLlib 上实现最高 97% 经验路径延迟降低与 38% 端到端迭代延迟降低，并扩展至 1024 GPU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着强化学习负载扩大，Experience Buffer 不再只是回放队列，而成为每次迭代都要经过的大容量、延迟敏感经验路径的存储基底，负责移动、变换、采样与批处理经验。现有 RL 系统将该路径嵌入框架控制流或暴露为请求驱动的缓冲服务，导致经验放置固定、经验路径工作难以作为运行时优化目标独立调度。

Method: 提出框架无关运行时 Conduit，核心是 Experience Data Plane（EDP），将经验摄入、放置与交付暴露为显式控制点，从而分离 RL 经验处理语义与框架执行逻辑。基于 EDP 引入两类机制：一是容量约束、带宽感知的放置，在异构互连和设备内存约束下将经验状态分布到 CPU/GPU 内存层级与节点；二是延迟感知调度，控制经验路径处理的运行时机，在保持 RL 语义的同时降低暴露的经验路径延迟。

Result: 在 RLlib 上集成且不改变其框架执行逻辑的情况下，Conduit 将暴露的经验路径延迟最多降低 97%，端到端迭代延迟最多降低 38%，可扩展至 1,024 个 GPU，并保持收敛性。

Conclusion: Conduit 表明 RL 经验管理可作为独立的运行时系统优化问题，通过显式的数据平面抽象与放置、调度机制，在不改动框架执行逻辑的前提下显著降低经验路径与端到端延迟，并具备大规模可扩展性与收敛保持能力。

Abstract: Distributed reinforcement learning (RL) scales training by parallelizing actors and learners around an Experience Buffer. As RL workloads grow, however, the buffer becomes more than a replay queue: it is the storage substrate of a large-capacity, latency-critical experience path that every iteration traverses to move, transform, sample, and batch experiences before learner updates can begin. Existing RL systems embed this path inside framework control flow or expose it as a request-driven buffer service, leaving experience placement fixed and experience-path work difficult to schedule independently as a runtime-level optimization target. We present Conduit, a framework-agnostic runtime that exposes RL experience management as an explicit systems optimization problem. At its core is the Experience Data Plane (EDP), a runtime abstraction that separates RL experience-handling semantics from framework-specific execution logic by exposing experience ingestion, experience placement, and experience delivery as explicit control points. Built on EDP, Conduit introduces capacity-constrained, bandwidth-aware placement, which distributes experience state across CPU/GPU memory tiers and nodes under heterogeneous interconnect and device-memory constraints, and latency-aware scheduling, which controls when experience-path handling runs to reduce exposed experience-path latency while preserving RL semantics. Integrated with RLlib without changing its framework execution logic, Conduit reduces exposed experience-path latency by up to 97% and end-to-end iteration latency by up to 38%, scales to 1,024 GPUs, and preserves convergence.

</details>


### [65] [Bridging the Vendor Gap: Enabling AMD GPU Support for Awkward Array via ROCm/HIP for the HL-LHC Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24628)
*Ianna Osborne,Maxym Naumchyk,Tai Sakuma,Andres Rios-Tascon,Peter Elmer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出$rawkward$，一个Rust实现的Awkward Array内核引擎，新增ROCm/HIP后端，通过一组可复用的优化模式在AMD GPU上恢复CUDA级性能，并保持公共API不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HL-LHC对分析吞吐量要求提升一个数量级，而算力增长越来越多来自非单一厂商的GPU（如基于AMD加速器的El Capitan、Frontier、LUMI）；但Scikit-HEP分析栈（尤其Awkward Array）长期以CUDA为先，缺乏面向AMD加速器的性能可移植方案。

Method: 在Rust支撑的$rawkward$内核引擎中为Awkward Array的嵌套、锯齿、变长数据结构添加ROCm/HIP后端；采用Rust宏与match分派层，在单一后端无关调用点生成厂商特定内核策略，并利用类型系统在编译期保证缓冲区大小与生命周期正确性。针对AMD的64-lane wavefront、更高寄存器压力和更昂贵的发散，提出循环展平、128位向量化加载、拆分融合内核、基于profile引导的启动配置等优化模式。

Result: 朴素地把CUDA源码移植到HIP在不规则内核上损失5–10倍性能；采用上述优化模式后可恢复CUDA级性能且不改变公共API。在双路AMD Instinct MI210节点上，相对128核EPYC 7763 CPU，GPU加速比为1.03倍（带宽受限的sum）到12.5倍（count）；Rust CPU内核在十二个内核上总体匹配或优于现有C++内核（几何平均运行时比0.37倍）。

Conclusion: 这些优化模式构成了一套实用的、性能可移植且厂商无关的HEP分析内核方案。

Abstract: The High-Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC) will demand order-of-magnitude gains in analysis throughput, and increasingly those gains must come from GPUs that are not made by a single vendor. Leadership-class systems such as El Capitan, Frontier and LUMI are built on AMD accelerators, yet the Scikit-HEP analysis stack---and Awkward Array in particular---has grown up CUDA-first. We report on $rawkward$, a Rust-backed kernel engine that adds a ROCm/HIP backend for Awkward Array's nested, jagged, variable-length data structures. Our central finding is that a naive source-level port of CUDA kernels to HIP loses $5$--$10\times$ in performance on irregular kernels, because AMD's $64$-lane wavefronts, higher register pressure and more expensive divergence behave fundamentally differently from NVIDIA's $32$-thread warps. We show that a small, reusable set of optimization patterns---loop flattening, $128$-bit vectorized loads, splitting fused kernels, and profile-guided launch configuration---recovers CUDA-class performance without changing the public API. A Rust macro-and-match dispatch layer keeps a single, backend-agnostic call site while emitting vendor-specific kernel strategies, and the type system enforces buffer-size and lifetime correctness at compile time. On a two-socket AMD Instinct MI210 node we measure GPU speedups from $1.03\times$ (bandwidth-bound $sum$) up to $12.5\times$ ($count$) over $128$ EPYC~7763 CPU cores, and the Rust CPU kernels match or beat on aggregate the incumbent C++ kernels (geometric-mean runtime ratio $0.37\times$ across twelve kernels). We argue that these patterns constitute a practical recipe for performance-portable, vendor-agnostic HEP analysis kernels.

</details>


### [66] [Analytical Power-Aware Provisioning for Prefill-Decode Disaggregated AI Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24639)
*Mingyuan Yan,Haiyu Wang,Linxuan Biao,H. Jonathan Chao,Sai Qian Zhang,Wenqi Cui*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向PD分离式AI推理的分析框架，刻画服务容量与平均功耗，并给出容量-功耗帕累托前沿以指导供电配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI推理集群受限于可用电力，需要在服务容量与功耗之间联合决策；PD分离架构下prefill与decode实例数量难以确定，因为服务容量同时受工作负载特征、硬件约束、排队与KV缓存预留影响。现有方法多依赖性能剖析与仿真，缺少解析性洞察。

Method: 构建解析框架：基于输入-输出长度的联合分布与硬件计算、内存上限推导服务容量模型，显式刻画KV缓存预留导致的prefill与decode耦合以及请求排队影响；在此基础上建立功耗模型，将单实例功耗表示为归一化服务吞吐的函数；两模型结合确定候选部署间的服务容量-功耗帕累托前沿。

Result: 框架可解析地给出给定工作负载与硬件下配置部署的服务容量与平均功耗，并生成服务容量-功耗帕累托前沿，供服务商在不同工作负载或可用功率下选择部署方案。

Conclusion: 该解析方法为电力受限的PD分离式AI推理提供可解释的配置依据，支持随工作负载或功率变化动态选择prefill/decode实例配置。

Abstract: Power availability increasingly constrains the operation of AI inference fleets, creating a need for provisioning methods that jointly consider serving capacity and power consumption. Prefill--decode (PD) disaggregation has emerged as a prevalent architecture for large-scale inference serving. However, determining the appropriate numbers of prefill and decode instances is challenging because serving capacity depends jointly on workload characteristics, hardware constraints, queueing, and KV-cache reservations. Existing approaches largely rely on profiling and simulation, providing limited analytical insight into how provisioning decisions shape the tradeoff between serving capacity and power consumption.
  This paper develops an analytical framework for power-aware provisioning of PD-disaggregated AI inference. Given an inference workload and hardware, the framework models the serving capacity and average power consumption of a provisioned deployment. The serving-capacity model is derived from the joint distribution of input--output lengths and hardware compute and memory limits. In particular, it explicitly captures the coupling between prefill and decode induced by KV-cache reservations, as well as the impact of request queueing. On this basis, the power model determines per-instance power consumption as a function of normalized serving throughput. Together, the models determine the serving capacity--power Pareto front among candidate provisioned deployments, enabling the service provider to choose a provisioned deployment as the workload or available power changes.

</details>


### [67] [Mitigating Front-Running Attacks through Fair and Resilient Transaction Dissemination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24713)
*Wassim Yahyaoui,Joachim Bruneau-Queyreix,Jérémie Decouchant,Marcus Völp*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出HERMES，一种面向mempool的新型传播协议，利用鲁棒最小结构优化数据传播、均衡负载，并在存在故障与拜占庭行为者时保证公平性，以应对抢跑攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代区块链中高效、公平且容错的信息传播对性能与安全至关重要。交易生命周期多个阶段（从交易创建与传播到共识层区块传播）都受影响。现有mempool协议（如L{Ø}）已解决部分安全威胁，但若公平性未从根本上嵌入传播层，用于共享交易、区块及协调mempool，其他威胁仍然存在，尤其是抢跑攻击。

Method: HERMES通过随机化覆盖结构的选择，并强制节点证明其遵守mempool传播策略及所做出的随机选择，从而应对抢跑攻击；同时利用鲁棒的最小结构来优化数据传播与负载均衡。

Result: 实验结果表明，与传统的广播协议相比，HERMES在许可链和无许可链上均取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: HERMES通过在传播层嵌入公平性并随机化覆盖结构，在保证容错与抵御拜占庭行为者的同时提升了传播性能，适用于许可链与无许可链。

Abstract: In modern blockchains, efficient, fair, and faulttolerant information dissemination is critical for performance and security. Several stages of the transaction lifecycle are affected, from the creation and dissemination of transactions to the dissemination of blocks in the consensus layer. Mempool protocols, such as L{Ø}, already address some of modern blockchains' security threats. However, others remain unless fairness is embedded most fundamentally in the dissemination layer used by these protocols to share transactions and blocks and to reconcile mempools. This paper introduces HERMES, a novel dissemination protocol for mempools that leverages robust minimal structures to optimize data propagation, balance load, and ensure fairness despite faults and Byzantine actors. Specifically, we address front-running attacks by randomizing the choice of an overlay structure while forcing nodes to prove adherence to the mempools' dissemination policies and the random choice made. Experimental results show significant performance improvements compared to traditional broadcast protocols for both permissioned and permissionless blockchains.

</details>


### [68] [Tiga: Compiling Graph Message Passing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24802)
*Mingyuan Chi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Tiga 是一个即时编译器，将消息传递程序的定义与其交互的遍历、计算和存储方式分离，支持遍历特化、融合与自动微分，并通过磁盘流式分区在内存受限设备上处理十亿级边的图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图消息传递是学习算法、物理模拟和数值求解器的通用表达方式，但其执行效率依赖交互结构与数据移动，而这些在以张量操作序列表达的程序中容易被掩盖。在笔记本等内存受限系统上，物化连接关系和中间消息还可能耗尽设备内存。

Method: Tiga 将消息传递程序的定义与其交互的遍历、计算和存储分离，在多级中间表示中保留图关系与归约器代数，实现遍历特化、关系生成与聚合的融合，以及反向模式自动微分。后端针对 CPU 和 GPU 做特定 lowering。运行时通过从磁盘经主机内存流式加载图分区，并使用页大小的设备暂存缓冲，将执行扩展到超出设备内存容量；分区所有权与 halo 交换将同一编程模型扩展到分布式执行。Python 接口可与普通 PyTorch 张量和 autograd 互操作。

Result: 数值检验验证了可微工作负载的输出与梯度；一个可微几何工作负载刻画了融合前向与反向执行的内存—时间权衡。与匹配的 Torch 和 PyTorch Geometric 基线相比，在生成关系工作负载上减少了运行时和设备内存使用；卸载式前向执行可在单个内存受限 GPU 上处理十亿级边的图。异构设备上的测量进一步刻画了分布式执行的通信与负载均衡开销。

Conclusion: Tiga 通过将消息传递程序的定义与遍历、计算和存储解耦，在保持与 PyTorch 生态互操作的同时，降低了运行时与设备内存使用，并使内存受限的单 GPU 能够处理十亿级边的图，还可扩展至分布式执行。

Abstract: Graph message passing offers a common way to express learning algorithms, physical simulations, and numerical solvers. Efficient execution depends on interaction structure and data movement, which can be obscured when a program is expressed as a sequence of tensor operations. On memory-constrained systems such as laptops, materializing connectivity and intermediate messages can also exhaust device memory. We present Tiga, a just-in-time compiler that separates the definition of a message-passing program from how its interactions are traversed, computed, and stored. Tiga preserves graph relations and reducer algebra in a multi-level intermediate representation, enabling traversal specialization, fusion of relation generation with aggregation, and reverse-mode automatic differentiation. Backend-specific lowering targets CPUs and GPUs. Its runtime extends execution beyond device-memory capacity by streaming graph partitions from disk through host memory with page-sized device staging buffers; partition ownership and halo exchange extend the same programming model to distributed execution. A Python interface interoperates with ordinary PyTorch tensors and autograd for forward and backward computation. Numerical checks validate outputs and gradients for differentiable workloads. A differentiated geometric workload characterizes the memory--time tradeoff of fused forward and backward execution. Evaluation against matched Torch and PyTorch Geometric baselines demonstrates reduced runtime and device-memory use for generated-relation workloads, while offloaded forward execution processes billion-edge graphs on a single memory-limited GPU. Measurements on heterogeneous devices further characterize the communication and load-balance costs of distributed execution.

</details>


### [69] [Who Pays for the KV Cache? Attributing Shared AI Inference Spend Across Kubernetes and LLM Provider Bills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24991)
*Timothy Urista*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出开源工具 unalloc，将 OpenCost、LiteLLM、OpenAI 与 Anthropic 的异构成本数据合并为统一账本以识别无主支出，并通过五组案例研究揭示 AI 成本归属在多系统接缝处的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织为 AI 付费的账目彼此割裂：自托管推理的 Kubernetes 分配、网关日志、以及 API 提供商的按 token 账单分散在不同系统中。这种割裂导致大量支出无法追溯到具体归属方，而现有归因方法在系统接缝处会失效，需要工具化的整合与实证研究来定位问题。

Method: 开发开源工具 unalloc，将 OpenCost、LiteLLM、OpenAI 和 Anthropic 成本数据联接为单一精确账本，报告无主支出占比。运行五组案例：带 paged KV 内存与前缀缓存的 vLLM 式服务模拟器；在真实 KV 缓存下运行多租户 trace 的 PyTorch transformer；torch.distributed 上的张量并行与流水线并行推理；针对 mock 提供商 API 的未修改 CLI；以及四个下游用例。

Result: 在多 pod 构造场景（一个月合成 OpenCost 分配数据）中，仅在 LeaderWorkerSet leader pod 上设置 owner 标签会使 66% 的 GPU 账单无主；自然回退键将 61% 归给某 Helm chart 名称，同时表面未分配份额降至 4%；启用所有来源会对全部网关支出重复计数；只读取一页计费 API 仅报告四分之一支出。在共享推理服务器内，计量规则决定付费方：NVIDIA H100 上运行 vLLM 时，token 计量在每种负载下都比等时份额计量为检索密集型租户多分配 12–14 个百分点；GPU 利用率在配置负载 2–16 请求/秒下读数为 97–99%，功耗随负载变化。

Conclusion: 两种计量方式都不是真实基准，作者将结果与近期基于 Shapley 的能耗归因方法进行对照。代码、原始数据、证据、图表与论文均可从仓库复现。

Abstract: Organizations pay for AI through disconnected ledgers: Kubernetes allocations for self-hosted inference, gateway logs, and per-token bills from API providers. We present unalloc, an open-source tool that joins OpenCost, LiteLLM, OpenAI and Anthropic cost data into one exact ledger and reports the share of spend with no owner, and use it to study where attribution breaks at the seams between these systems. Five case studies run inference for real or simulate it: a vLLM-style serving simulator with paged KV memory and prefix caching; a PyTorch transformer serving a multi-tenant trace with a real KV cache; tensor- and pipeline-parallel inference on torch.distributed; the unmodified CLI against mock provider APIs; and four downstream use cases. At the seams, in a constructed multi-pod deployment scenario -- one month of synthetic OpenCost allocations, not observed billing data -- owner labels set only on LeaderWorkerSet leader pods leave 66% of that deployment's GPU bill unowned, and the natural fallback key assigns 61% of it to a Helm chart name while the headline unallocated share falls to 4%; enabling every source double counts all gateway spend; and reading one page of a billing API reports a quarter of spend. Inside a shared inference server the metering rule decides who pays: on an NVIDIA H100 running vLLM, a token meter assigns a retrieval-heavy tenant 12-14 percentage points more of the bill than an equal time-share meter at every load tested, while GPU utilization reads 97-99% across configured loads of 2 to 16 requests per second (3.7 to 26.9 completed requests per second; the configured rate counts session-initial arrivals only) and power draw tracks load. Neither meter is a ground truth; we position these results against recent Shapley-based energy attribution. Code, raw data, captured evidence, figures and the paper regenerate from the repository.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [70] [PRQuant: Permutation Residual Quantization for Low-Overhead Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22106)
*Peiran Wang,Anqi Wang,Jiaying Zhao,Huiwen Yang,Zhenyu Ming,Rongqian Wang,Yiwu Yao,Kun Tian,Xin Yao,Gong Zhang,Fan Yang,Zhongyi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRQuant：免训练、低开销的权重量化框架，通过通道重排与静态残差补偿提升低比特量化精度并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性层低比特量化精度受少量离群值主导；现有平滑、旋转或残差方法虽可缓解，却常给权重引入新的精度瓶颈，且多为在线实现，执行开销大。

Method: 提出PRQuant（Permutation Residual Quantization）：在AWQ式缩放后，识别对权重量化误差贡献最大的输入通道，将其重排为连续尾部块，并离线构建其残差权重子张量；推理时连续结构使激活端可直接使用尾部块，免去昂贵的在线gather操作，将分散残差补偿转为规整的尾部增强GEMM。

Result: 有效降低down-projection重建误差；消融显示平滑与残差补偿是数值提升主因，重排带来稳定小幅数值收益并实现硬件友好的连续布局、消除动态gather开销；在五个下游基准上平均精度优于默认MXFP4及所评估的PTQ基线，在Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507和Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507上分别较MXFP4提升1.24和0.55。

Conclusion: PRQuant以免训练、低开销方式兼顾精度与效率，是低比特线性层量化的有效方案。

Abstract: Accuracy of Low-bit quantization of linear layers is often dominated by a small number of outliers. Although existing methods, such as smoothing, rotation, or residual-based approaches, may mitigate this problem, they often introduce new accuracy bottlenecks to weights. Besides, most of these techniques are implemented as online approaches, which can result in heavy execution overheads. To address the afore-mentioned issues, We propose PRQuant (Permutation Residual Quantization), a training-free and low-overhead framework that combines channel reorganization with static weight-side residual compensation. After AWQ-style scaling, PRQuant identifies the input channels that contribute most to weight quantization error, permutes them into contiguous tail blocks, and constructs their residual weight sub-tensors offline. During inference, this contiguous structure enables the activation side to use tail blocks seamlessly without the expensive online gathering operation, and turns scattered residual compensation into a regular tail-augmented GEMM, substantially reducing latency. Experiments demonstrate that PRQuant effectively reduces down-projection reconstruction error. Ablation studies confirm that smoothing and residual compensation are the primary drivers of numerical improvement, while permutation provides a consistent marginal numerical benefit and, more importantly, enables a hardware-friendly contiguous layout that eliminates dynamic gathering overhead. Overall, PRQuant outperforms default MXFP4 and the evaluated PTQ baselines in average accuracy across five downstream benchmarks, improving over MXFP4 by 1.24 and 0.55 on Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 and Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507, respectively.

</details>


### [71] [Generalized Multimodal Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22107)
*Huizi Cui,Zongbo Han,Chenggong Ding,Naichuan Xiao,Jialong Yang,Jingdong Chen,Guangyu Wang,Qinghua Hu,Changqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于大规模合成多模态数据集训练的通用多模态基础模型，可适应任意模态组合与预测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态融合模型部署后仅能处理预定义模态和单一任务，难以快速适配新的下游应用，因此需要一种通用的多模态融合模型。

Method: 基于生成大规模合成多模态数据集的学习范式，数据集包含多样的因果结构，形式化刻画真实世界多模态数据的生成过程；据此构建通用多模态基础模型，训练时编码可迁移的多模态关联模式，推理时通过上下文示例激活合适的关联。

Result: 在涵盖12种模态、11类预测任务的18个真实数据集上，模型在无需任务特定适配的情况下达到与专用模型相当的性能。

Conclusion: 通用多模态基础模型不依赖特定模态，通过合成数据学习可迁移的多模态关联，可泛化到任意模态组合与预测任务。

Abstract: Making prediction with multimodal data is widely used in diverse scenarios. Existing multimodal fusion models, once deployed, can only handle predefined modalities (e.g., vision, text and audio) and single tasks, making it difficult to quickly adapt to new downstream applications. Therefore, a natural yet rather aggressive question arises, whether there exists a general multimodal fusion model that can be applied to arbitrary modality combinations and arbitrary prediction tasks. We argue that a unified multimodal fusion model should not depend on specific modalities and instead encode transferable patterns of multimodal correlation. To this end, we propose a simple and effective learning paradigm based on training over the generation of large-scale synthetic multimodal datasets with diverse causal structures that formally characterize the generative processes of multimodal data in real world. Building on this framework, we propose the generalized multimodal foundation model, a unified foundation model for generalized multimodal learning. By constructing large-scale synthetic multimodal datasets with diverse correlation patterns, our model encodes transferable multimodal correlations during training and activates appropriate associations through in-context examples during inference. Extensive experiments on 18 real-world datasets spanning 12 modalities and 11 prediction tasks demonstrate that our model achieves competitive performance with specialized models without task-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [72] [Correcting Learning-based Perception for Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22108)
*Yan Miao,Hussein Darir,Sayan Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两步式的ML感知修正策略：离线用感知契约原像刻画不确定性，运行时用风险启发式选择状态驱动控制，在45个存在安全违规的ACC场景中使73%恢复安全，且平均仅增加2.8%完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的感知在自主系统中很重要，但与传统传感器不同，ML感知何时失效的边界难以刻画；错误感知会导致下游控制做出不安全或过度保守的决策，因此需要对ML状态估计进行修正。

Method: 两步策略：第一步离线计算，利用感知契约的原像来刻画ML模块状态估计所产生的不确定性；第二步在运行时使用风险启发式，从不确定估计中挑选特定状态来驱动控制决策。并在多种天气与道路场景下的多个基于视觉的自适应巡航控制器（ACC）上进行仿真评估。

Result: 在原本使用Yolo和LaneNet的感知控制导致安全违规的45个ACC场景中，73%的场景通过运行时感知修正保持了安全性；27%无法恢复，原因是感知契约原像的构造不完全符合规范。该方法并不过度保守，被修正场景平均仅增加2.8%的完成时间，干预程度轻微。

Conclusion: 运行时感知修正策略能有效提升ML感知控制系统的安全性，同时保持较低的时间代价与较小的干预，但其效果受限于感知契约原像构造的合规性。

Abstract: Learning-enabled perception is important in many autonomous systems. Unlike traditional sensors, the boundary where ML perception does or does not work is poorly characterized. Incorrect perception can lead to unsafe or overtly conservative downstream control actions. In this paper, we propose a two-step strategy for correcting ML-based state estimation. First, an offline computation is used to characterize the uncertainties resulting from the ML module's state estimation, using preimages of perception contracts. Second, at runtime, a risk heuristic is used to choose particular states from the uncertain estimates to drive the control decisions. We perform extensive simulation-based evaluation of this runtime perception correction strategy on different vision-based adaptive cruise controllers (ACC modules), in different weather conditions, and road scenarios. Out of 45 ACC scenarios where the original perception-based control system using Yolo and LaneNet led to safety violations, in 73% of the scenarios, our runtime perception correction preserved safety; our method wouldn't be able to recover 27% of the scenarios where the construction of the preimages of perception contracts is not fully conformant. Further, our runtime perception correction strategy is not overly conservative---on the average only a 2.8% increase in completion time is experienced in the corrected scenarios, with mild interventions.

</details>


### [73] [A Shared Learning Rate Is Not a Neutral Control in Selective On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22109)
*Chencheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 选择器比较中共享学习率并非中性控制：选择性与密集监督在学习率网格上表现出不同敏感度（选择器-学习率纠缠），导致比较结论随学习率大幅变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中比较不同选择器时通常固定一个共享学习率，视为中性控制。作者旨在检验该控制是否真的中性，揭示共享学习率会系统性影响密集与选择性训练的对比结论。

Method: 在 GSM8K 上用 LoRA（Qwen2.5-1.5B 学生、7B 教师）进行 8 倍学习率网格实验，比较密集监督与多种选择性监督（随机 5%、全变分选择器、可教性选择器）；分析 AdamW 更新幅度与梯度范数；并做预注册的冻结评分消融（12 种子/格）。另在完整微调的 1e-6 至 1e-5 区间及 MATH-500 上验证。

Result: 密集监督统计上平坦（波动 1.8pp，p=0.26），而各选择性分支随学习率变化：随机 5% 为 5.4pp、全变分为 6.7pp、可教性选择器达 17.7pp；密集对选择性结论从 lr=1e-4 时的 10.1pp 变为 5e-5 时的 5.1pp（2.0 倍差异），六对选择器显著性判断中有两对在相邻学习率间翻转。AdamW 更新幅度与学习率偏差在 2.2% 内，尽管梯度范数差 15.5 倍。冻结评分消融显示实时评分增加 3.79±1.69pp 敏感度（p=0.035），冻结分支仍显著纠缠（p=0.015）。完整微调下模式加剧：密集波动 19.8pp、选择性 49.5pp，结论从 +3.6pp（不显著）到 +34pp（p=0.005）。MATH-500 上 LoRA 未复现学习率依赖，但选择性训练约 10pp 成本仍存在。

Conclusion: 选择器比较必须先报告臂×学习率矩阵而非单一共享学习率列；选择器-学习率纠缠源于选择本身而非步长，反馈环加剧而非导致该现象。

Abstract: Selective on-policy distillation trains a student only at the token positions a selector scores highest, and the literature compares selectors under a single shared learning rate--a control chosen to be neutral. We show it is not. Under LoRA on GSM8K (Qwen2.5-1.5B student, 7B teacher), across an 8x learning-rate grid, dense supervision is statistically flat (swing 1.8 pp, p=0.26) while every selective arm moves with the rate: 5.4 pp for a random 5% subset, 6.7 pp for a total-variation selector, up to 17.7 pp for a teachability selector. Consequently the dense-versus-selective verdict reads 10.1 pp at lr=1e-4 but 5.1 pp at 5e-5--a 2.0x difference decided by a parameter the protocol treats as scenery--and two of six pairwise significance calls between selectors flip between adjacent rates without any rank inversion. We call this selector-rate entanglement and trace it to selection itself rather than step size: AdamW update magnitudes track the rate to within 2.2% despite 15.5x gradient-norm differences across arms. A preregistered frozen-scoring ablation (selection scored by the initial student; criterion, budget, and on-policy rollouts unchanged; 12 seeds per cell) shows live scoring adds 3.79+/-1.69 pp of rate sensitivity (p=0.035) while the frozen arm remains significantly entangled (p=0.015): the feedback loop aggravates the phenomenon rather than causing it. Under full fine-tuning at the rates this literature actually uses (1e-6 to 1e-5) the pattern grows: dense itself swings 19.8 pp, the selective arm 49.5 pp, and the verdict ranges from a non-significant +3.6 pp at the published operating point to +34 pp (p=0.005) one notch hotter. On MATH-500 the rate dependence does not reproduce under LoRA, scoping that result, while the ~10 pp cost of selective training does. We prescribe reporting the arm x rate matrix, not a shared-rate column, as a precondition for selector comparisons.

</details>


### [74] [Toward Fairness in Machine Learning Models for Predicting Treatment Retention and Premature Discontinuation in Medication for Opioid Use Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22113)
*Tongnian Wang,Carolina Vivas-Valencia,Cici Bauer,Yanmin Gong,Kim-Kwang Raymond Choo,Yuanxiong Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了预测阿片类使用障碍药物治疗（MOUD）保留与提前中断的机器学习模型的算法公平性，并考察了偏差缓解技术的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MOUD的保留率和完成率长期偏低，促使研究者使用机器学习预测保留情况并识别提前中断风险患者。然而，这些模型在不同患者群体间的公平性尚未得到充分探讨，引发其在治疗决策支持中应用的担忧。

Method: 使用横断面治疗事件数据集TEDS-D（含2015—2019年美国出院个体的治疗事件），训练四个机器学习模型预测接受门诊MOUD个体的提前中断及保留超过180天情况。按种族、族裔、年龄和性别划分患者子群，评估整体性能与子群错误率，并辅以模型解释分析；同时评估偏差缓解技术对公平性和预测性能的影响。

Result: 即使整体预测性能看似可接受，MOUD结果预测的机器学习模型仍可能表现出子群层面的性能差距；偏差缓解可缩小但不能完全消除这些差距，且存在权衡。

Conclusion: 研究强调了公平性感知评估和透明报告子群性能的重要性，为MOUD治疗中风险分层与护理优先级排序中负责任、情境敏感的机器学习应用提供了实践启示。

Abstract: Persistent low retention and completion rates in medications for opioid use disorder (MOUD) have driven the use of machine learning (ML) models to predict retention and identify patients at risk of premature discontinuation. However, the fairness of these models across patient populations remains largely unexplored, raising concerns about their application in treatment decision support. This study systematically assesses algorithmic fairness in ML models for predicting MOUD retention and premature discontinuation and investigates the effectiveness of bias mitigation techniques. Using the cross-sectional Treatment Episode Data Set-Discharges (TEDS-D), which includes treatment episodes for individuals in the U.S. discharged between 2015 and 2019, we trained four ML models to predict premature treatment discontinuation and retention beyond 180 days among individuals receiving outpatient MOUD. We evaluated overall performance and subgroup-level error rates across patient subgroups defined by race, ethnicity, age, and sex, complemented by model explanation analyses. We further assessed bias mitigation techniques and their effects on both fairness and predictive performance. Our findings demonstrate that ML models for MOUD outcome prediction can exhibit subgroup-level performance gaps even when overall predictive performance appears acceptable and that bias mitigation can reduce, but not fully eliminate, these gaps without trade-offs. By demonstrating the importance of fairness-aware evaluation and transparent reporting of subgroup performance, this study provides practical insights for the responsible and context-sensitive use of ML models for risk stratification and care prioritization in MOUD treatment settings.

</details>


### [75] [ZoAQ: Adaptive Zeroth-Order Querying via Query-Reuse Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22115)
*Yangyang Feng,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZoAQ是一种基于查询复用的自适应零阶优化方法，通过复用过去的函数评估来同时指导更新与查询预算决策，无需额外验证查询，从而显著减少扰动查询次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化（ZOO）中扰动查询是主要成本。固定预算每步花费相同查询数；而自适应控制器为测试估计器可靠性往往需要额外的oracle调用，可能抵消其节省。因此需要一个不依赖额外验证查询的自适应查询分配机制。

Method: 提出ZoAQ，围绕查询复用构建：不丢弃每步后的历史评估，而是让其同时服务于下一次更新以及是否继续查询的决策，从而实现无额外验证查询的自适应查询分配。并给出理论分析，刻画这种一致性何时能识别支持下降的更新，并引导控制器达到足够的查询预算。

Result: 在合成目标上，相比固定基线（120万次查询）减少43-48%查询；黑盒攻击中，MNIST和CIFAR-10分别以平均320和625次查询达到100%成功率；在四种OPT微调设置中，相比固定K=4节省43-46%前向评估，各任务准确率变化在-0.018到+0.010之间。

Conclusion: ZoAQ通过查询复用实现高效自适应零阶优化，在不增加验证查询的前提下大幅降低查询/评估成本，并在合成任务、黑盒攻击与语言模型微调中验证了有效性与稳定性。

Abstract: Zeroth-order optimization (ZOO) estimates updates from function evaluations, making perturbation queries a primary cost. Fixed budgets spend the same number of queries at every step, while adaptive controllers may offset their savings by using additional oracle calls to test estimator reliability. We introduce ZoAQ, an adaptive ZOO method built around query reuse. Rather than discarding past evaluations after each step, ZoAQ makes them useful for both the next update and the decision to query further. This enables adaptive query allocation without extra validation queries. Our analysis characterizes when this agreement identifies an update that supports descent and guides the controller to a sufficient query budget. On synthetic objectives, ZoAQ reduces queries by 43-48% relative to fixed baselines using 1.2M queries. In black-box attacks, it reaches 100% success with 320 and 625 average queries on MNIST and CIFAR-10, respectively. Across four OPT fine-tuning settings, ZoAQ saves 43-46% forward evaluations relative to fixed K=4, with accuracy changes within tasks ranging from -0.018 to +0.010.

</details>


### [76] [LE4Mob: Towards Inductive, Distance-Aware and General-Purpose Location Embedding for Human Mobility Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22117)
*Xinglei Wang,Stephen Law,Zichao Zeng,Junyuan Liu,Guangsheng Dong,Tao Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LE4Mob，一种归纳式、距离感知、源自地理信息的位置嵌入框架，用于人类移动性建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有位置嵌入依赖移动观测数据，无法表示未见过的位置，且对地理距离的保持约束较弱，限制了跨数据集与跨任务的重用。

Method: LE4Mob在对比式语言-位置预训练基础上，引入距离感知正则化目标，促使嵌入空间保留空间关系；从地理上下文预训练，可归纳生成未见位置的嵌入，且不依赖下游任务监督。

Result: 在个体级下一位置预测与群体级通勤流生成任务上，跨多数据集与研究区域实验显示LE4Mob优于强基线，在归纳场景及下游模型直接依赖位置嵌入交互时优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 距离感知、源自地理的位置表示可作为人类移动性建模的可重用基础。

Abstract: Location representations provide mobility models with fundamental information about the spatial position, functional characteristics, and relationships of places. However, existing embeddings are often dependent on mobility observations, unable to represent unseen locations, and weakly constrained to retain geographic distance. This limits their reuse across datasets and mobility tasks. To address these limitations, we propose LE4Mob, an inductive, distance-aware, and geography-derived location embedding framework for mobility modelling. LE4Mob extends contrastive language-location pre-training while introducing a distance-aware regularisation objective that encourages the embedding space to preserve spatial relationships. Pre-trained from geographic context, LE4Mob can encode rich spatial-semantic information and generate embeddings for unseen locations inductively. Its independence from downstream mobility task supervision also makes it transferable across different mobility tasks. We evaluate LE4Mob on individual-level next location prediction and population-level commuter flow generation. Experiments across multiple datasets and study areas show that LE4Mob outperforms strong baselines, with particular advantages in inductive settings and when downstream models rely directly on interactions between location embeddings. These findings demonstrate the potential of distance-aware, geography-derived location representations as reusable foundations for human mobility modelling.

</details>


### [77] [Success Leaves Detours: Learning Executable Walkthroughs for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22120)
*Kaijie Chen,Chenyu Fang,Liang Yan,Bo Li,Bo Zhang,Peng Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Trace，一种基于信用引导与依赖落地的框架，从稀疏奖励轨迹中归纳可执行的 Walkthrough 记忆，在多个基准上显著优于现有测试时学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时自进化智能体依赖复用过往经验，但稀疏奖励轨迹包含大量失败、循环和绕路；而现有摘要往往丢失执行所需的状态条件与动作依赖关系，导致经验难以真正落地执行。

Method: Trace 将噪声轨迹编译为可执行的 Walkthrough Memory：从奖励与持久状态变化中检测进展锚点，传播信用以识别有价值的状态转移，并利用跨回合的成功与失败证据估计动作前置条件；随后通过反向依赖切片将所需事实追溯到其生产者，抽取依赖一致的动作链，同时剔除无关的循环与绕路，最终得到编码入口条件、有序的状态—动作—效果步骤以及完成与失败谓词的 Walkthrough。

Result: 在 J-TTL、WebShop 和 ScienceWorld 三个环境、三种开源 LLM 上，Trace 一致优于八个测试时学习与记忆基线。相比最强基线，平均 AUC 与 Final-3 分别提升 30.0% 和 40.5%，且使用的推理 token 更少。

Conclusion: 长程交互任务中，状态条件的可执行过程比完整轨迹或抽象摘要更具复用价值，Trace 验证了以依赖为落地依据的可执行 Walkthrough 归纳的有效性。

Abstract: Test-time self-evolving agents improve by reusing past experience, yet sparse-reward trajectories contain failures, loops, and detours, while summaries often omit the state conditions and action dependencies needed for execution. We study executable Walkthrough induction from sparse-reward trajectories: extracting compact, state-conditioned, and verifiable procedures. Our key observation is that delayed credit identifies actions associated with progress but cannot determine whether they produce facts required by later actions. We propose Trace, a credit-guided, dependency-grounded framework that compiles noisy trajectories into executable Walkthrough Memory. It detects progress anchors from rewards and persistent state changes, propagates credit to identify valuable transitions, and estimates action prerequisites from cross-episode success and failure evidence. Backward dependency slicing then traces required facts to their producers, extracting dependency-consistent action chains while removing irrelevant loops and detours. The resulting Walkthroughs encode entry conditions, ordered state--action--effect steps, and completion and failure predicates, supporting reuse, intermediate-state resumption, and programmatic verification. Experiments on J-TTL, WebShop, and ScienceWorld with three open-source LLMs show that Trace consistently outperforms eight test-time learning and memory baselines. Compared with the strongest baseline, it improves average AUC and Final-$3$ by $30.0%$ and $40.5%$, respectively, while using fewer inference tokens. These results show that long-horizon interaction benefits more from state-conditioned executable procedures than from complete trajectories or abstract summaries.

</details>


### [78] [Modelling daily activity patterns from mobile phone location data via deep representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22121)
*Xinglei Wang,Junyuan Liu,Guangsheng Dong,Zichao Zeng,Stephen Law,James Haworth,Tao Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自监督模型ACE（Activity Chain Encoder），利用预训练城市嵌入结合访问时间与时长，通过Transformer建模停留序列，实现无标签手机定位数据的活动模式表征与挖掘，在伦敦数据上识别出六类工作日活动模式群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动采集的手机定位数据虽能大规模、长时序地反映人类移动性，但无法直接揭示活动目的；而访问地点的功能特征虽提供情境信息，其与活动目的的关系在混合用途城市环境中仍不确定，因此需要一种无需确定性活动标签的表征方法来挖掘行为上有意义的人群活动模式。

Method: 将活动模式挖掘概念化为表征、聚类与解释的一体化流程，并针对表征阶段提出ACE（Activity Chain Encoder）。ACE为自监督模型，融合预训练城市嵌入与访问时间、时长，使用Transformer建模停留的时序组织；训练采用掩码活动建模与身份引导对比学习，无需确定性活动目的标签。随后将学习到的日表征聚合为用户级画像，进行聚类，并通过时间-功能模式及来自人口普查的人口统计背景加以解释。

Result: 在伦敦手机应用定位数据上应用ACE，并与三种代表性方法对比，ACE支持识别出六个差异化的工作日活动模式群体，这些群体具有不同的日节律、城市功能情境与人口统计关联。这些互补证据进一步支持构建有实证基础的活动模式画像（personas）。

Conclusion: 研究为从无标签手机定位数据中推导具有行为意义的人群模式提供了整体性路径；全部分析流程的源代码已公开于 https://github.com/xlwang233/ACE。

Abstract: Passively collected mobile phone location data provide large-scale, longitudinal observations of human mobility but do not directly reveal activity purposes. The functional characteristics of visited locations offer useful contextual information, yet their relationship with activity purpose remains uncertain, particularly in mixed-use urban environments. We conceptualise activity pattern mining as an integrated process of representation, clustering, and interpretation, and propose the Activity Chain Encoder (ACE) for the representation stage. ACE is a self-supervised model that combines pre-trained urban embeddings with visit timing and duration and uses a Transformer to model the sequential organisation of stays. It is trained using masked activity modelling and identity-guided contrastive learning without requiring deterministic activity purpose labels. Learned daily representations are aggregated into user-level profiles, clustered, and interpreted through temporal-functional patterns and Census-derived demographic context. Applied to mobile phone app location data from London and compared with three representative methods, ACE supports the identification of six differentiated weekday activity-pattern groups characterised by distinct daily rhythms, urban functional contexts, and demographic associations. These complementary forms of evidence further support the development of empirically grounded activity-pattern personas, establishing a holistic route for deriving behaviourally meaningful population patterns from unlabelled mobile phone location data. The source code for the entire analytical pipeline developed in this study is publicly available at https://github.com/xlwang233/ACE.

</details>


### [79] [Concurrency-Aware Process Model Forecasting with Causal Nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22614)
*Yongbo Yu,Jari Peeperkorn,Johannes De Smedt,Jochen De Weerdt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了过程模型预测（PMF），从预测直接跟随图转向预测因果网，通过预测关系与绑定计数的时间序列来重建具有AND/XOR语义的未来过程模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PMF方法直接预测直接跟随图，无法显式表达并发关系，限制了未来过程模型的表现力。

Method: 预测关系与绑定计数的时间序列，并据此重建具有AND/XOR语义的未来过程模型（因果网）；同时提出一种考虑部分迹并构建一致性检查所需工作流网的评估协议。

Result: 在四个事件日志上的实验表明，预测模型的一致性水平接近从对应未来窗口观测中重新挖掘的模型，并优于静态发现基线；过滤低频绑定可改善多数一致性指标，但会削弱模型捕获的并发行为。

Conclusion: 将PMF扩展到因果网能够更好地刻画并发语义，且在一致性上接近未来窗口重挖模型，优于静态基线；过滤低频绑定在提升一致性的同时会牺牲部分并发表达能力。

Abstract: Process model forecasting (PMF) aims to predict the process model that will characterize a future period, thereby providing a process-level view of how behavior is expected to evolve. Existing PMF methods, however, forecast directly-follows graphs, which cannot explicitly represent concurrency. We extend PMF to causal nets by forecasting time series of relation and binding counts and using these forecasts to reconstruct future process models with AND/XOR semantics. To evaluate the resulting models, we introduce a protocol that accounts for partial traces and constructs the workflow nets required for conformance checking. Experiments on four event logs show that the forecasted models achieve conformance levels close to those of models re-mined from observations in the corresponding future windows. They also outperform static discovery baselines, which retain high precision on the structurally stable log but exhibit substantial precision losses on the other three logs. Filtering infrequent bindings improves most conformance metrics, although it also removes much of the concurrent behavior captured by the models.

</details>


### [80] [Rank Portability Does Not Imply Feasibility Portability: Target-Specific Evaluation of Joint Hardware Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22122)
*Wesley Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验了"跨设备架构排序可迁移即可用代理设备支持目标端模型选择"这一假设，发现排序可迁移性、可行性可迁移性与目标特定决策支持是三种不同的评估对象，跨设备评估应报告哪些目标环境真正支持所声称的工作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨设备硬件评估常默认：若架构排序能在设备间迁移，则代理设备即可支撑目标端的模型选择。作者认为这一假设缺乏严格检验，尤其是在联合延迟-能耗可行性这一更严格的约束下，排序相关性良好并不等于可行集与决策支持也能迁移。

Method: 在NAS-Bench-201上对两个公开架构族做压力测试，考察跨设备排名相关性与目标可比可行集重叠；引入忠实的AdaProxy诊断以排除"排序失败源于适配不足"的解释；并用精确有限样本分裂共形分析刻画单侧90%阈值所需的最小校准样本量。随后在HW-GPT-Bench的13台设备、10,000个GPT架构上复现该现象，以RTX3080为代理，统计目标延迟SRCC及匹配联合约束下的代理复用违例风险。

Result: NAS-Bench-201上跨设备排名相关性中等，但目标可比可行集重叠不完全；AdaProxy诊断显著改善延迟排名，说明边界失败并非仅由适配弱导致；有限样本分析显示单侧90%阈值至少需要9个校准观测，存在证据瓶颈。在HW-GPT-Bench上，相对RTX3080代理，目标延迟SRCC为0.951–0.996，但在匹配联合约束下代理复用违例风险高达33.3%–100%。

Conclusion: 排序可迁移性、可行性可迁移性与目标特定决策支持是三个彼此独立的评估对象：高排名相关性并不能保证可行集重叠或决策可用。跨设备评估应明确报告哪些目标环境真正支持所声称的工作点，而不能仅凭排序相关性推断代理设备的适用性。

Abstract: Cross-device hardware evaluation often assumes that if architecture rankings transfer across devices, a proxy device can support target-side model selection. We stress-test this assumption for joint latency-energy feasibility across two public architecture families. On NAS-Bench-201, cross-device rank correlations are moderate, while target-comparable feasible-set overlap remains incomplete. A faithful AdaProxy diagnostic substantially improves latency ranking, showing that the observed boundary failures are not simply due to weak adaptation. Exact finite-sample split-conformal analysis also exposes an evidence bottleneck: a finite one-sided 90% threshold requires at least nine calibration observations. We then replicate the phenomenon on 10,000 GPT architectures across 13 HW-GPT-Bench devices. Relative to an RTX3080 proxy, target latency SRCC ranges from 0.951 to 0.996, yet proxy-reuse violation risk ranges from 33.3% to 100% under matched joint constraints. These results show that rank portability, feasibility portability, and target-specific decision support are distinct evaluation objects. Cross-device evaluations should therefore report which target environments actually support the operating point being claimed.

</details>


### [81] [StationPDE: Station-Oriented Surface PDE Learning for Multi-Station Multivariate Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22123)
*Xiao Wang,Changjian Chen,Rongwen Li,Hongwu Liu,Kun Fang,Zhuo Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出StationPDE，将离散站点观测构建为地形感知连续场，分解物理演化为地表风输送与高空推断，并结合数据驱动扩散分支与自适应路由，在多站点多元天气预报上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多站点预报模型仅学习离散站点间统计依赖，缺乏显式物理演化；而基于PDE的气象模型虽具可解释物理动力学，却依赖连续场与高空变量，地表站点数据无法满足。需弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: StationPDE从离散站点观测构建地形感知的连续地表场，将物理演化分解为地表风输送（演化可观测变量）与高空推断（用可学习水平扩散近似缺失的高空变量影响）；并行引入数据驱动扩散分支捕捉互补运动模式，并通过自适应路由器融合两路预报进行站点级多元预测。

Result: 在Weather2K与MeteoNet数据集上，StationPDE持续优于最强基线，MSE平均降低约9.6%。

Conclusion: StationPDE通过将站点数据映射为连续场并融合物理演化与数据驱动方法，为站点级天气预报提供了可解释且高性能的新范式，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-station multivariate weather forecasting aims to forecast future weather variables at multiple weather stations from historical surface observations. Existing station forecasting models learn statistical dependencies among discrete stations, but lack explicit physical evolution. Meanwhile, PDE-based weather models provide interpretable physical dynamics, yet require continuous fields and upper-air variables unavailable in surface station data. To bridge this gap, we propose StationPDE, a station-oriented surface PDE learning model. StationPDE constructs a terrain-aware continuous surface field from discrete station observations and decomposes its physical evolution into surface wind transport and upper-air inference. Surface wind transport explicitly evolves observable weather variables, while upper-air inference uses learnable horizontal diffusion to approximate the missing influence of unavailable upper-air variables. A parallel data-driven diffusion branch captures complementary motion patterns, and an adaptive router integrates the two forecasts for station-level multivariate forecasting. Experiments on Weather2K and MeteoNet show that StationPDE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, reducing MSE by about $9.6\%$ on average compared with the strongest baseline. Code and implementation details are available at https://github.com/hnu-vis/StationPDE.

</details>


### [82] [SolarFlowRefiner: Refinement-Aware Flow Matching for Surface Solar Radiation Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22126)
*Udbhav Srivastava,Antonita Racheal,Yiheng Chen,Runlong Yu,Xinyue Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SolarFlowRefiner，一种感知细化的流匹配框架，用于从粗分辨率 ERA5 变量和卫星通道重建高分辨率地表太阳辐射场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率地表太阳辐射对光伏预测和电网运行很重要，但物理一致的重分析产品分辨率太粗，无法刻画局地云致变化。单个 ERA5 网格内可能同时存在日照区和云影区，导致缺失的高分辨率修正既空间锐利又本质模糊。一步式预测器常过度平滑这些结构，而事后细化存在阶段不匹配：生成器被独立优化，但其输出却决定细化器的初始状态。

Method: SolarFlowRefiner 采用条件 FlowMatch 生成器，先对上采样 ERA5 基线预测归一化修正；细化器则在当前 FlowMatch 输出与目标残差之间的"预测条件状态"上训练，从而接触生成器产生的结构化误差；细化目标还通过 FlowMatch 采样器反向传播，使生成与修正联合优化以服务最终重建。

Result: 在按天分块的 ERA5–SolarCube 基准上，相比独立生成和事后细化均取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: SolarFlowRefiner 为生成式预测器与迭代校正器的耦合提供了通用策略，可推广到更广泛的任务。

Abstract: High-resolution surface solar radiation (SSR) is important for solar forecasting and grid operation. However, physically consistent reanalysis products are too coarse to resolve localized cloud-driven variability. In this paper, we study a multisource downscaling task that reconstructs high-resolution SolarCube SSR fields from coarse ERA5 radiative variables and co-registered satellite channels. The task is challenging because a single ERA5 grid cell may contain both sunlit and cloud-shadowed regions. As a result, the missing high-resolution correction can be spatially sharp and inherently ambiguous. One-stage predictors often oversmooth these structures. Post-hoc refinement also introduces a stage-wise mismatch: the generator is optimized independently, even though its output determines the refiner's initial state. We introduce SolarFlowRefiner, a refinement-aware flow-matching framework for SSR downscaling. A conditional FlowMatch generator first predicts a normalized correction to an upsampled ERA5 baseline. The refiner is then trained on prediction-conditioned states between the current FlowMatch output and the target residual. This exposes the refiner to the structured errors produced by the generator. The refinement objective is also backpropagated through the FlowMatch sampler, allowing generation and correction to be jointly optimized for the final reconstruction. Experiments on a day-blocked ERA5--SolarCube benchmark show consistent improvements over standalone generation and post-hoc refinement. More broadly, SolarFlowRefiner provides a general strategy for coupling generative predictors with iterative correctors.

</details>


### [83] [Helix-FNO: Spectral-Domain Operator Learning Coupled with a High-Fidelity Mechanistic Model for Fast Surrogate Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22129)
*Jiabao Zhao,Chuwei Wang,Jinxi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Helix-FNO 教师-学生架构：以三十二状态机理模型为教师，Fourier 神经算子为学生学习解算子，实现毫秒级推理，比机理模型快三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全尺度处理过程的机理仿真模型虽可外推且可信，但运行过慢，难以支撑现代决策引擎在 5 分钟决策节奏下所需的数千次前向评估；传统代理建模常只针对单一配置学习单一解，对新进水条件、控制设置或厂区布局泛化差。

Method: Helix-FNO 采用教师-学生架构：教师（32 状态机理模型）提供高保真输入场-解配对数据集，并用拉丁超立方与基于不确定性的主动学习筛选，覆盖边界与过载工况；学生（Fourier 神经算子）在谱域学习解算子本身而非单个解，从而由"学一个实例"转向"学一整族方程"。文中给出算子形式、谱卷积定义、加权蒸馏损失与主动学习准则，并分析截断 Fourier 展开相对于参数化解流形光滑性的逼近误差。

Result: 示例性研究在精度、数据集效率与推理延迟上，将 Helix-FNO 与物理信息网络、数据驱动循环代理进行对比，并将各方法置于速度-精度 Pareto 前沿上。所得算子在毫秒级推理下比机理教师快三个数量级。

Conclusion: Helix-FNO 具备大规模候选筛选与在线决策支持所需的能力，可作为机理模型的高效可外推算子替代方案。

Abstract: Mechanistic simulation models of full-scale treatment processes remain the only trustworthy, extrapolative description of the underlying physico-chemical dynamics, yet their runtime is far too slow to support the thousands of forward evaluations that a modern decision engine requires at a 5-minute decision cadence. The standard remedy-surrogate modelling-often produces a network that learns a single solution for a single configuration, so it generalises poorly to new influent profiles, control settings or plant layouts. This paper presents Helix-FNO, a teacher-student architecture that couples a thirty-two-state mechanistic teacher with a Fourier neural operator (FNO) student. The teacher supplies a high-fidelity dataset of input-field-to-solution pairs, curated by Latin-hypercube and uncertainty-based active learning to cover the boundary and overload regimes that matter in practice; the student learns, in the spectral domain, the solution operator itself rather than any single solution, thereby moving from learning one instance to learning an entire family of equations. We give the operator formulation, the spectral convolution definition, the weighted distillation loss and the active-learning criterion, and we analyse the approximation error of a truncated Fourier expansion with respect to the smoothness of the parametric solution manifold. An illustrative study compares Helix-FNO against a physics-informed network and a data-driven recurrent surrogate on accuracy, dataset efficiency and inference latency, and places the methods on a speed-accuracy Pareto front. The resulting operator is three orders of magnitude faster than the mechanistic teacher at millisecond inference, which is precisely the capability required for massive candidate screening and online decision support.

</details>


### [84] [Hierarchical Bayesian optimization of an aircraft-based multi-agent system-of-systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22130)
*Paul Saves,Thierry Lefebvre,Nathalie Bartoli,Jasper Bussemaker,Nikolaos Kalliatakis,Nabih Naeem,Prajwal Prakasha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层贝叶斯优化框架，利用高斯过程元建模处理系统之系统（SoS）架构设计中的离散选择、条件依赖与异构变量，并在野火扑救多智能体航空系统中验证，提升了搜索效率与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统之系统（SoS）架构设计依赖高保真多物理、多学科仿真，但仿真计算代价高且易出现评估失败，导致直接优化困难；传统代理模型方法难以处理离散架构选择、条件依赖及异构设计变量。

Method: 构建分层贝叶斯优化框架，采用高斯过程元模型处理离散架构决策、条件依赖结构及异构设计变量，以在有限仿真预算下探索大规模、结构多样的设计空间。

Result: 与常规代理模型方法相比，分层建模显著提升了搜索效率与稳健性，可在有限仿真预算下探索大型且结构多样的设计空间；并在欧盟COLOSSUS项目的野火扑救多智能体航空系统案例中成功应用。

Conclusion: 该框架为SoS架构建模与模型探索提供了可扩展方法，可为航空、可持续出行及韧性导向的系统设计提供可迁移经验，是分层贝叶斯优化应用于真实SoS问题的首批实践之一。

Abstract: Developing innovative system architectures increasingly relies on advanced modeling and optimization techniques to frame the architecting process and define the corresponding computational problems. For complex System-of-Systems (SoS), high-fidelity multiphysics and multidisciplinary simulations are essential for capturing detailed behaviors. However, their computational expense and the risk of evaluation failures make direct optimization challenging. To overcome these limitations, surrogate-based approaches, like Bayesian optimization, have emerged as effective tools for managing expensive, black-box simulation tasks. This work introduces a hierarchical Bayesian optimization framework that leverages Gaussian process meta-modeling to handle discrete architectural choices, conditional dependencies, and heterogeneous design variables inherent to SoS problems. Results show that the hierarchical formulation improves search efficiency and robustness compared to conventional surrogate-based methods, enabling the exploration of large and structurally diverse design spaces with limited simulation budgets.
  We apply the approach to an aircraft-based multi-agent system for wildfire suppression, a use case developed within the EU-funded COLOSSUS project that illustrates how SoS principles can coordinate heterogeneous aerial platforms with complementary roles, supporting both sustainable mobility and emergency response missions. Our framework provides a scalable methodology for SoS architecting and model exploration, offering transferable insights for applications in aviation, sustainable mobility, and resilience-oriented system design. By combining hierarchical representations with surrogate-based optimization, this work is among the first practical demonstrations of hierarchical Bayesian optimization applied to real-world SoS problems, advancing both methodology and practice.

</details>


### [85] [Weak Ties, Strong Signals: Efficient Training Data Detection in Diffusion LLMs via Independent Token Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22145)
*Hongyao Yu,Tianqu Zhuang,Ziyuan Xu,Hao Fang,Jiaxin Hong,Bin Chen,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对扩散大语言模型（dLLM）中训练数据泄露检测问题，指出随机掩码方法的token级近似存在非负估计误差，并提出独立token采样框架ITS来提升检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLM在去噪过程中可能暴露敏感训练数据，但检测困难：dLLM缺乏因果架构中高效的单次概率分解能力。现有方法依靠随机掩码在有限查询预算下获取可解的token级检测信号，却无法控制被掩码token之间的依赖关系，导致检测信号被掩盖。

Method: 作者证明token级近似会引入非负的结构性估计误差，并用被掩码token间的累积条件互信息（CMI）进行理论刻画，说明可靠检测需要内部依赖较弱的掩码token集合。为避免直接估计CMI的高昂成本，提出独立token采样（ITS）框架：利用注意力导出的成对依赖代理近似CMI感知的选择准则，并引入多样性促进策略提升多轮采样中的token覆盖度。

Result: 在多个数据集上，ITS在不同模型和数据集下持续优于现有最优基线。在ArXiv数据集上AUC提升0.18，并在有限查询预算下保持较强性能。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过控制掩码token间的依赖关系、降低近似误差，可显著提升dLLM训练数据使用检测的可靠性与查询效率。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a compelling alternative to autoregressive models, yet they may expose sensitive training data during denoising. Detecting such usage is challenging because dLLMs lack the efficient one-pass probability decomposition of causal architectures. Existing methods rely on random masking to obtain tractable token-wise detection signals under limited query budgets, but fail to control dependencies among masked tokens. We demonstrate that this token-wise approximation introduces a non-negative structural estimation error, which is theoretically characterized by the cumulative conditional mutual information (CMI) among masked tokens and can obscure subtle memorization signals. This insight suggests that reliable detection requires masked token sets with weak internal dependency. To avoid the prohibitive cost of directly estimating CMI over token combinations, we propose \textit{Independent Token Sampling} (ITS), a query-efficient framework that uses an attention-derived pairwise dependency proxy to approximate the CMI-aware selection criterion. ITS further incorporates a diversity-promoting strategy to improve token coverage across sampling rounds, yielding aggregated token-wise signals that are less affected by dependency-induced approximation error. Experiments on multiple datasets show that ITS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across different models and datasets, achieving an AUC improvement of 0.18 on the ArXiv dataset while maintaining strong performance under limited query budgets. The code is available at https://github.com/Chrisqcwx/DLLM-MIA .

</details>


### [86] [GRRR: The Geometry of Reshaping, Rotation, and Routing in Decoder LLM post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22146)
*Jianing Qi,Hao Tang,Zhigang Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将后训练（SFT与RL）引起的权重变化投影到预训练权重矩阵的SVD框架中，分解为对角、非对角和零空间三个分量，发现移除对角分量通常能保留大部分后训练收益，表明收益主要来自对预训练通路的重构与扩展，而非改变奇异值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究后训练如何相对于预训练权重改变大语言模型（LLM）的权重，理解后训练增益的来源机制，即这些增益究竟是通过改变预训练模型的奇异值实现的，还是通过对已有通路的重组与扩展实现的。

Method: 在12条包含监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）的后训练链上，将每个权重更新表示在预训练矩阵的SVD框架中，从而分解出三个几何上不同的分量：对角值（重塑奇异值）、非对角值（旋转预训练输入与输出方向之间的耦合）以及零空间值（在矩阵原始非零SVD核心之外进行路由）。

Result: 在数学评测套件上，移除对角分量通常能保留大部分后训练带来的性能增益。

Conclusion: 后训练带来的性能提升主要由对预训练通路的重新配置与扩展所承载，而非显著改变预训练模型的奇异值。

Abstract: We study how post-training changes the weights of Large Language Models (LLMs) relative to their pretrained weights. Across 12 post-training chains with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), we express each weight update in the pretrained matrix's singular value decomposition (SVD) frame. This decomposition separates the changes of three geometrically distinct components: diagonal values, which reshapes singular values; off-diagonal values, which rotates the coupling between pretrained input and output directions; and null-space values, which routes outside the matrix's original nonzero SVD core. On a math evaluation suite, we find that removing the diagonal component usually preserves most of the gains from post-training. These results suggest that post-training gains are carried primarily by reconfiguring and extending pretrained pathways rather than by substantially changing singular values of pre-trained models.

</details>


### [87] [SafeTune: A Unified Faithful Library for Auditing and Repairing Safety Drift in Fine-Tuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22153)
*Pratinav Seth,Saisab Sadhu,Anshul Kaushal,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SafeTune 是一个开源库，统一了四类缓解大模型微调后“安全漂移”的干预范式，并提供共享的可解释性、评估与部署工具，通过统一配置驱动的工作流和模块化注册表支持方法扩展与公平比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对微调大语言模型出现的安全漂移问题，现有方法分散在互不兼容的实现、生命周期阶段和评估协议中，导致难以采用和比较。因此需要一个统一框架来整合不同干预范式并支持一致的评测。

Method: SafeTune 统一四类干预范式：事后权重恢复、安全约束微调、基于梯度的遗忘学习、推理时引导；同时提供共享的可解释性、评估和部署工具。采用配置驱动的一致工作流，同时保留各范式所需的独特输入与干预点；通过模块化注册表支持新方法、基准、评判器、模型和微调领域，而无需重构整体流水线。

Result: 通过受控对比实验，以及在金融和医疗场景的部署案例研究，SafeTune 能够刻画安全漂移、在常见拒答行为与能力评估上评测可行干预，并支持校准或分层缓解策略。

Conclusion: SafeTune 提供了统一、可扩展的安全漂移缓解与评估平台，便于方法比较与落地应用，并展示了在敏感领域的部署可行性。

Abstract: Methods for addressing safety drift in fine-tuned Large Language Models (LLMs) are scattered across incompatible implementations, lifecycle stages, and evaluation protocols, making them difficult to adopt and compare. We introduce SafeTune, a source-available library that unifies four intervention paradigms: post-hoc weight recovery, safety-constrained fine-tuning, gradient-based unlearning, and inference-time steering, alongside shared interpretability, evaluation, and deployment utilities. SafeTune provides a consistent configuration-driven workflow while preserving the distinct inputs and intervention points each paradigm requires. Its modular registry supports new methods, benchmarks, judges, models, and fine-tuning domains without redesigning the surrounding pipeline. We demonstrate SafeTune through controlled comparisons and finance and medical deployment case studies, showing how it characterizes safety drift, evaluates feasible interventions on common refusal-behavior and capability evaluations, and supports calibrated or layered mitigation.

</details>


### [88] [A Comparative Framework for Evaluating Foundation Models on Tabular Data: A Case Study in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22154)
*Majid Lotfian Delouee,Sjors G. J. G. In 't Veld,Martijn C. Schut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向临床的表格基础模型（TFM）比较评估框架，从六个临床维度打分排序，并在两个医疗用例中验证，同时给出45个TFM的分类法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据是临床实践中最常见的数据形式（化验结果、用药记录、诊断编码、人口统计等）。随着表格基础模型数量与种类激增，临床医生或数据科学家难以判断"给定任务该选哪个模型、为什么"。现有综述只罗列模型能力，缺少针对真实应用需求的结构化比较方法。

Method: 提出comparative evaluation framework，从六个具临床意义的维度对TFM打分与排名：对新数据集的泛化能力、患者隐私保护、所需数据量、随数据规模与特征空间的扩展性、对临床医生的可解释性、跨患者亚组的公平性。每个维度拆分为可测量子组件，子组件可选组合为补充性复合分数（super-metrics）以提供跨维度诊断视角。框架应用于两个医疗用例（缺铁筛查、心衰预测），并给出按底层架构组织的45个TFM分类法。

Result: 在缺铁筛查与心衰预测两个用例中，同一套指标因各临床情境关注重点不同，导致模型排名不同，说明框架能反映任务特定需求。同时提供了45个TFM按架构组织的分类参考。

Conclusion: 该框架为临床与数据科学从业者在选择表格基础模型时提供了结构化、可比的评估途径，并借分类法帮助研究者与实践者导航这一快速扩展的领域。

Abstract: Tabular data is the most common format in clinical practice, encompassing laboratory results, medication records, diagnostic codes, and patient demographics. As foundation models for tabular data have grown in number and variety, a practical question has become harder to answer: which model should a clinician or data scientist actually choose for a given task, and why? Existing surveys catalogue what these models can do, but they stop short of providing a structured way to compare them against the specific demands of a real application. We introduce \system{}, a comparative evaluation framework that scores and ranks tabular foundation models (TFMs) across six clinically meaningful dimensions: how well a model generalizes to new datasets, how effectively it protects patient privacy, how much data it needs to perform well, how it scales with growing datasets and feature spaces, how interpretable its predictions are to clinicians, and how fairly it performs across patient subgroups. Each dimension is broken down into measurable sub-components, and groups of sub-components can optionally be combined into supplementary compound scores, called super-metrics, that provide a diagnostic view of how a model performs across several dimensions simultaneously. To show how the framework works in practice, we apply it to two healthcare use cases, screening for iron deficiency and predicting heart failure, demonstrating how the same set of metrics leads to different model rankings depending on what matters most in each clinical context. We also provide a taxonomy of 45 TFMs organized by their underlying architecture, which serves as a reference for researchers and practitioners looking to navigate this rapidly expanding field.

</details>


### [89] [From Latent Biomarkers to Clinical Rules: Embedding-Guided Rule Mining and Attribution-Based Translation for Interpretable Tabular Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22155)
*Majid Lotfian Delouee,Hamed Ayoobi,Sjors G. J. G. In 't Veld,Martijn C. Schut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个四步流程，在FT-Transformer的隐空间中挖掘决策规则，并将其翻译回可测量的临床特征，在六个公开临床数据集上验证，五个数据集上优于原始特征规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策支持工具需要准确预测与可理解解释并存。基于规则的模型具有透明性，但直接从原始临床测量中导出的规则可能遗漏多变量交互产生的模式，因此需要一种兼顾透明度与预测能力的规则发现方法。

Method: 提出四步流程：1）在FT-Transformer的隐空间中挖掘决策规则；2）将能稳定区分患者群体的嵌入维度视为潜在生物标志物；3）使用小型决策树挖掘规则；4）通过梯度-输入显著性和CLS注意力归因将规则翻译回可测量的临床特征。在六个公开临床与人群健康数据集、四个嵌入维度上评估。

Result: 翻译后的规则在六个数据集中的五个上优于原始特征规则，平均AUROC提升0.04至0.23。在心脏病数据集上，嵌入空间规则达到0.98 AUROC，但翻译后降至0.72，表明高性能潜在规则并不总能由简单的原始特征条件表示。

Conclusion: 隐空间规则发现能够揭示预测性模式，同时可将其翻译为临床可测量、可被临床医生评估的特征，兼具预测能力与可解释性。

Abstract: Clinical decision support tools are most useful when accurate predictions are accompanied by understandable explanations. Rule-based models provide transparency, but rules derived directly from raw clinical measurements may miss patterns arising from interactions between multiple variables. We present a four-step pipeline that mines decision rules in the latent space of an FT-Transformer and translates them back into measurable clinical features. Embedding dimensions that consistently separate patient groups are treated as latent biomarkers, rules are mined using small decision trees, and selected rules are translated using gradient-input saliency and CLS attention attribution. We evaluate the framework on six public clinical and population health datasets at four embedding dimensions. Translated rules outperformed raw-feature rules in five of six datasets, with mean AUROC gains ranging from 0.04 to 0.23. On the heart disease dataset, embedding-space rules reached 0.98 AUROC, but translation reduced this to 0.72, showing that high-performing latent rules cannot always be represented by simple raw-feature conditions. These results show that latent-space rule discovery can uncover predictive patterns while translating them into clinically measurable features that can be evaluated by clinicians.

</details>


### [90] [The Limits of Speculation: Bounding Speculative Decoding in Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22156)
*Aidar Amankulov,Denis Mamatin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将MoE模型中的推测解码预算选择形式化为离线随机最短路径问题，构建反事实仿真Oracle分析验证成本，发现被拒候选形成严格线性边界，揭示了边际成本与期望进展之间的必要平衡条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型中的推测解码面临输入相关的专家加载导致的验证成本不稳定问题，需要理解这一过程的机理，为设计自适应在线启发式方法提供数学参考。

Method: 将推测预算选择形式化为参考序列上的离线随机最短路径（SSP）问题，构建诊断性Oracle，通过反事实仿真刻画MoE验证成本，并在边际增量空间（Delta Space）中分析Qwen3-Coder与EAGLE-3配对的决策。

Result: Oracle决策分析显示，被拒绝的候选形成严格的线性边界，表明复杂的全局优化在局部由边际成本与期望进展之比的必要平衡条件所支配。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解MoE推测解码的验证成本机理提供了严格的数学参考点，可指导未来自适应在线启发式方法的设计。

Abstract: Speculative decoding in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models faces the problem of unstable verification cost caused by input-dependent expert loading. To study the physics of this process, we formulate speculation-budget selection as an offline Stochastic Shortest Path (SSP) problem over reference sequences and build a diagnostic Oracle that uses counterfactual simulation to account for MoE verification cost. A detailed analysis of the Oracle's decisions on the Qwen3-Coder and EAGLE-3 pairing, in the space of marginal deltas (Delta Space), shows that rejected candidates form a strict linear boundary. This result demonstrates that a complex global optimization is locally governed by a necessary condition balancing marginal cost against expected progress ($\frac{Δ\mathbb{E}[Cost]}{Δ\mathbb{E}[a]}$), providing a rigorous mathematical reference point for designing future adaptive online heuristics.

</details>


### [91] [PAGE: Partition-Aware Gated KV-Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22157)
*Pankaj Kumar,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将KV缓存逐出重新定义为"逐输入准入决策"，提出无需训练、无需标签的PAGE方法，通过prefill注意力中的"早-晚top-k头一致性下降"指标来判断输入是否属于"容量受限类"，从而在必要时保留完整缓存，将灾难性错误率降低约29倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV-cache逐出方法只决定"保留哪些token"，却不判断"是否该逐出"，导致基准均值掩盖了一类输入：在这些输入上压缩会把准确率从99%拉到0%。作者希望以逐输入的方式识别这类"容量受限"输入，避免灾难性精度崩溃。

Method: 将逐出重构为逐输入准入决策；提出单一无标签标量——prefill注意力中"成对top-k头一致性的早-晚下降幅度"（early-to-late drop in pairwise top-k head agreement），在解码前即预测输入所属类别。PAGE对该下降值设阈值：下降大时启用基础逐出器，否则保留完整缓存。无需训练、无需精度标签；新架构族可用约100条无标签输入做per-model pilot重新校准阈值。

Result: 该下降指标在四个架构族上一致地按逐出安全性排序输入；作为安全护栏，PAGE在容量受限场景将危害率从0.75降至0.026（29倍），跨四种逐出器、四个模型、两个基准，将99%→0%的崩溃变为稳定的89%，且无需重训逐出器。在逐出本已安全的场景中该门控保持惰性；受保护的容量受限类仅占少数可识别输入。但作为压缩器，实际压缩率为1.8–3.4倍（均值2.9倍）对名义16倍预算，在静态分配下batch 16时趋近1；在同等内存下训练式逐出器更优。

Conclusion: PAGE是一种逐输入安全护栏而非压缩器，其价值在于针对性安全增益而非平均性能提升。它可即插即用地保护任意基础逐出器免于灾难性失效，但并非压缩效率的解决方案，且在同内存条件下不及训练式逐出器。
