TL;DR: 本文研究个性化联邦强化学习，提出Per-FedAvg-PG算法，通过局部元策略梯度步实现协作学习，证明其收敛性并分析无Hessian变体的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化联邦强化学习中，各智能体在各自的马尔可夫决策过程中行动，需要协作学习一个共享的MAML式策略初始化，使其经单步本地策略梯度适应后对个体有效。现有监督学习理论中的有界梯度和异质性假设在强化学习场景下需要重新审视，且精确元梯度依赖内循环策略Hessian，计算成本高。

Method: 提出Per-FedAvg-PG算法，智能体在通信轮次间执行τ步局部随机元策略梯度步。基于强化学习场景的结构特征，在标准策略类正则性下证明各智能体目标具有一致有界的梯度和Hessian。进一步分析无Hessian变体，界定其偏差并展示元梯度非零的固定点。

Result: 证明Per-FedAvg-PG在K=O(ε^{-3/2})轮、τ=Θ(ε^{-1/2})局部步下达到ε近似一阶稳定点。实验识别内循环策略Hessian为实际瓶颈，无Hessian变体存在阶为α的稳定性地板。在表格和神经网络导航实验中验证了预测行为，迁移到未见智能体的样本成本比独立训练低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 结果识别出适应步长作为可调个性化旋钮，曲率估计作为决定精确元梯度是否可负担的关键量。

Abstract: We study personalized federated reinforcement learning, in which $n$ agents, each acting in its own Markov decision process, collaborate through a server to learn a shared MAML-style policy initialization that becomes effective for an individual agent once that agent adapts it with a single local policy-gradient step. We propose Per-FedAvg-PG, in which agents take $τ$ local stochastic meta-policy-gradient steps between communication rounds, and prove that it reaches an $\varepsilon$-approximate first-order stationary point of the personalized objective in $K=\mathcal O(\varepsilon^{-3/2})$ rounds with $τ=Θ(\varepsilon^{-1/2})$ local steps. The analysis rests on a structural feature of the reinforcement learning setting: under standard policy-class regularity, the per-agent objectives have uniformly bounded gradients and Hessians with explicit constants, so the bounded-gradient and bounded-heterogeneity conditions imposed by the supervised theory hold automatically and no separate heterogeneity assumption is needed. The exact meta-gradient requires the inner-loop policy Hessian, which our experiments identify as the practical bottleneck. We therefore analyze the Hessian-free variant, bound its bias, and exhibit fixed points at which the meta-gradient is nonzero and of order $α$, showing that the resulting stationarity floor is a property of the method rather than of the bound. Experiments on tabular and neural navigation confirm the predicted behavior and show transfer to unseen agents at an order of magnitude lower sample cost than independent training. Together these results identify the adaptation step size as a tunable personalization knob and the curvature estimate as the quantity that governs whether exact meta-gradients are affordable.

</details>


### [160] [A Hybrid Attention Model Learning Unified Time-aware Patch Representation for Irregular Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22836)
*Zhihao Lin,Li Lin,Qi Zhang,Kaiwen Xia,Shuai Wang,Jialin Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合注意力模型，通过时间感知的patch编码与时间偏置注意力，在不做插补的情况下统一建模不规则多变量时间序列，并构建了300亿观测的VersaTSA数据集，实现IMTS上的最优零样本预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策常依赖不规则多变量时间序列（IMTS），其观测间隔不一致、变量间采样异步且缺失具有信息性。现有时间序列基础模型要么通过插补引入虚假值，要么使用基于索引的位置编码而忽略连续时间，缺乏能遵循IMTS原始模式的基础模型。

Method: 提出混合注意力模型学习统一的时间感知patch表示：1）时间感知patch编码，将变数量的片内时间戳映射为固定大小嵌入，无需插补即可统一不规则patch格式；2）时间偏置注意力机制，以辅助注意力偏移校准片间时间错位与异步跨通道依赖；3）在decoder-only Transformer上采用混合因果掩码，历史上下文保持双向全局视图，预测区间严格自回归；并构建保留原始采样稀疏性的VersaTSA数据集（300亿观测）支持大规模预训练。

Result: 在三个IMTS基准和标准规则MTS基准上实验显示，模型在IMTS上达到最优零样本性能，迁移到规则预测任务时也保持有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了遵循IMTS原始模式的基础模型空白，无需插补即可统一处理不规则时间序列，并具备向规则预测迁移的泛化能力。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) have recently delivered impressive zero-shot performance across diverse forecasting tasks. However, real-world decision-making frequently relies on \emph{irregular multivariate time series} (IMTS), where inconsistent inter-observation intervals and asynchronous sampling across variables coexist with informative missingness. Existing TSFMs handle such inputs either through imputation that injects spurious values or through index-based positional encodings that ignore continuous time. There is still a gap in the foundation model that follows the original IMTS patterns. In this paper, we propose a hybrid attention model that learns a unified time-aware patch representation for IMTS forecasting. We first design a \emph{time-aware patch encoding} that maps a variable number of intra-patch timestamps into a fixed-size embedding, producing a uniform format for irregular patches without resorting to imputation. We then introduce a \emph{time bias attention} mechanism that calibrates inter-patch temporal misalignment and asynchronous cross-channel dependencies as auxiliary attention offset. Finally, on top of a decoder-only Transformer backbone, we adopt a \emph{hybrid causal mask} that preserves a bidirectional full view over the historical context while keeping the forecast horizon strictly autoregressive. To support large-scale pretraining under irregular settings, we also curate VersaTSA, an archive of $30$B observations that retains the native sampling sparsity of its sources. Experiments on three IMTS benchmarks and a standard regular-MTS benchmark show that our model achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on IMTS and remains competitive when transferred to regular forecasting.

</details>


### [161] [Testing the Construct Validity of a Functional Valence Axis in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22850)
*Weihan Li,Xinlei Chen,Yuhan Song,Xiaofeng Lin,Tianshi Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究对比激活方向所表征的构念是否可被充分识别，以迷宫任务中的“好—坏结果”方向为案例，通过控制性干预分离“已实现结果”与“获知该结果的信息历史”。结果表明该方向并非绑定于表面形式，但强烈依赖于先前的告知历史，支持一种功能性、价值相关的解释，而非一个历史不变的标量效价状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比激活方向常被根据其“解码出什么”或“多强地操控行为”来解读，但这不足以判定该方向所代表的构念，而非对比提取过程中某个相关特征。作者希望厘清：什么样的证据才足以识别这类方向所表征的构念。

Method: 在迷宫任务中研究一个“好—坏结果”方向，采用控制性干预将已实现结果与使其被获知的信息历史分离开来。跨多个LLM检查点，比较“一种显式结果编码上拟合的方向”向“另一种显式结果编码”的迁移；并比较同一已实现结果经由“已告知”与“未告知”历史达成时的迁移情况。此外进行匹配的迷宫强化学习（RL）实验，考察RL后方向对参考MDP剩余回报的预测力及策略对该方向的依赖。

Result: 跨多个LLM检查点，在一种显式结果编码上拟合的方向能很好地迁移到另一种显式结果编码，说明该读出并不绑定于表面形式。但当同一已实现结果分别经由已告知与未告知历史达成时，迁移显著退化：即使两种历史随后都接收到相同的显式结果，事件后的读出仍强烈受先前告知的制约。在匹配的迷宫RL实验中，RL后的方向对参考MDP剩余回报的预测力显著增强，策略在测试位点上对该方向更加依赖，而这种历史依赖性依然存在。

Conclusion: 结果支持对该方向作功能性、价值相关的解读，但不能将其等同于一个历史不变的标量效价状态。识别此类方向的构念需要能够分离结果与信息历史的干预性证据。

Abstract: Contrastive activation directions are often interpreted from what they decode or how strongly they steer behavior. But what evidence is sufficient to identify the construct represented by such a direction, rather than a correlated feature of the contrast used to extract it? We study this question for a good--bad outcome direction in a maze task, using controlled interventions that separate the realised outcome from the informational history through which it became known. Across multiple LLM checkpoints, directions fitted on one explicit outcome encoding transfer well to another, indicating that the readout is not tied to surface form. In contrast, when the same realised outcome is reached through announced and unannounced histories, transfer degrades substantially: even after both histories receive the same explicit outcome, the post-event readout remains strongly conditioned on the earlier announcement. In a matched maze-RL run, the post-RL direction becomes substantially more predictive of reference-MDP remaining return and the policy becomes more dependent on it at the tested sites, while this history dependence persists. These results support a functional, value-related interpretation of the direction, but not its identification with a history-invariant scalar valence state.

</details>


### [162] [CurvFlow-DTA: dual-graph discrete Ricci curvature flow for drug--target affinity prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22862)
*Jicheng Ma,Yunyan Yang,Juan Zhao,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CurvFlow-DTA，用加权 Forman 曲率流替代静态曲率，并在药物分子图与蛋白质残基接触图上建模，通过配对条件选择器与双分支 Flow-GINE 实现 DTA 预测，在 Davis 与 KIBA 上均优于基线，尤其在冷启动场景表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有曲率感知 DTA 方法主要使用药物图上的静态曲率，蛋白质多依赖序列特征，未充分利用图几何的配对自适应能力，可能限制在冷启动与未见实体筛选中的适应性。

Method: CurvFlow-DTA 在分子图与蛋白质残基接触图上采用加权 Forman 曲率流，为每个实体预计算与标签无关的流轨迹；由配对条件选择器决定双分支 Flow-GINE 读取的时间范围；使用冻结 ESM-2 提供残基级表示与接触分数以构建蛋白质图；推理仅需 SMILES 与蛋白序列，无需结合复合物结构。

Result: 在 Davis 与 KIBA 上，所有 warm 与 cold-start 设置均优于协议匹配的 Ricci-GraphDTA 基线。warm 划分 MSE 在 Davis 下降 19.9%，KIBA 下降 18.9%；六个冷启动对比中 MSE 下降 14.3%–27.4%，CI 持续更高。在文献基线集合中，CurvFlow-DTA 在两个 warm 基准及六个冷启动设置中的四个取得最低 MSE。

Conclusion: 曲率流与配对自适应选择机制能有效提升 DTA 预测，尤其在冷启动场景，且仅依赖 SMILES 与序列即可推理，具备实用筛选潜力。

Abstract: Graph neural networks are widely used for drug--target affinity (DTA) prediction, and discrete Ricci curvature has recently been used to characterize molecular graph geometry. Existing curvature-aware DTA approaches mainly use static curvature on the drug graph while representing proteins primarily with sequence-derived features. This leaves pair-adaptive use of graph geometry underexplored, which may limit adaptation to unseen entities in cold-start settings relevant to practical screening.
  We present CurvFlow-DTA, which replaces a single static curvature representation with weighted Forman curvature flow on both molecular and protein residue--residue contact graphs. A label-independent flow trajectory is precomputed for each entity, and a pair-conditioned selector determines the horizons read by a dual-branch Flow-GINE. A frozen ESM-2 supplies residue-level representations and contact scores used to construct the protein graph. Inference requires only SMILES strings and protein sequences, without a bound complex structure.
  On Davis and KIBA, CurvFlow-DTA improves on the protocol-matched Ricci-GraphDTA baseline in every warm and cold-start setting. Warm-split mean squared error (MSE) decreases by $19.9\%$ on Davis and $18.9\%$ on KIBA. Across the six cold-start comparisons, MSE decreases by $14.3$--$27.4\%$, with higher concordance index (CI) throughout. Within our compiled set of literature baselines, CurvFlow-DTA achieves the lowest MSE on both warm benchmarks and across four out of six cold-start evaluation settings.

</details>


### [163] [Causilo Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22866)
*Minyong Cho,Minho Jeong,Dooho Lee,Jinmo Lee,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Causilo 是一种表格基础模型，结合前沿预测性能与极快推理速度，在 TabArena 上达到 1785.4 Elo，中位推理时间仅 0.10 秒/1K 样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在表格任务中同时实现顶尖预测性能与高效推理具有挑战性，现有模型往往难以兼顾两者，因此需要一种能处于性能—效率帕累托前沿的表格基础模型。

Method: Causilo 沿用 TabICL 的“先列后行”架构，但在行压缩前引入额外的行细化模块：列编码后，在各行内单元格表示之间交换信息；细化后的单元格再经过额外的列阶段访问上下文集，最后压缩为行嵌入。为提升推理效率，两个行阶段均通过固定数量的摘要令牌进行交叉注意力，使注意力成本相对特征数保持线性。模型在约 3600 万合成表上预训练。

Result: 在 TabArena 上取得 1785.4 Elo，中位推理时间 0.10 秒/1K 测试样本；相比 TabPFN-3.5-Fast 推理时间减少 31.6%，位于性能—效率帕累托前沿。在 TabArena、BeyondArena 和 ScoringBench 上均取得前沿水平表现，且推理显著更快。

Conclusion: Causilo 通过行细化与线性复杂度注意力设计，在多个基准上实现前沿预测性能与更快推理，验证了性能与效率兼顾的可行性。

Abstract: We introduce Causilo, a tabular foundation model (TFM) that combines frontier predictive performance with exceptionally fast inference. On TabArena, Causilo achieves 1785.4 Elo, at a median inference time of 0.10 seconds per 1K test samples. It outperforms TabPFN-3.5-Fast with 31.6% less inference time, placing it on the performance--efficiency Pareto frontier. Causilo follows TabICL's column-then-row architecture but introduces another row-refinement module before row compression. This module exchanges information among cell representations within each row after column encoding. The refined cells then visit the context set again through an additional column stage before being compressed into row embeddings. For inference efficiency, both row stages use cross-attention through a fixed number of summary tokens, keeping their attention cost linear in the number of features. Pretrained on approximately 36M synthetic tables, Causilo delivers strong benchmark results across TabArena, BeyondArena, and ScoringBench, achieving frontier-level performance with substantially faster inference.

</details>


### [164] [Leveraging Inference-Time Compute for Diffusion Models via Global Scheduling of Denoising Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22867)
*Yuan Cao,Yifu Tang,Hangqi Li,Zeyu Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散模型采样中的计算预算分配问题，证明最优分配满足可分离凹整数规划与水填充结构，并提出实验可验证的分配算法，在同等质量下减少20%–50%的函数评估次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型采样时可通过在每一步生成多个候选噪声样本并用验证器打分来提升样本质量，但这需要额外的网络评估开销。核心问题是在固定函数评估预算下，如何在去噪轨迹的各步之间分配搜索努力。

Method: 将问题建模为计算预算分配问题：(1) 证明在步长主导阶下，某步评估 K 个候选的期望增益可分解为步特定的内源性敏感度参数与通用样本量因子（K 个标准正态抽样最优值的期望）之积；(2) 对固定敏感度剖面，最优分配等价于可分离凹整数规划，具有水填充结构；(3) 证明当敏感度跨实例变化时，任何自适应策略的最坏情况遗憾线性增长，因而采用离线锚定、在线仅恢复实例松弛的设计；并将分析从独立随机搜索扩展到更广的局部搜索算子族。

Result: 在固定总敏感度下，最优分配相对均匀分配的优势随敏感度在majorization序下的离散程度增加而增大。理论分析被实例化为可实现算法。在三个扩散采样器家族上的实验表明，所提分配方法以少20%–50%的函数评估达到均匀基准的质量。

Conclusion: 该工作为扩散模型推理时的验证器引导搜索提供了具有理论保证的预算分配框架，兼顾离线锚定与在线自适应的平衡，并显著提升采样效率。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate a sample by traversing a denoising trajectory, a sequence of stochastic noise-reduction steps that transforms pure noise into a draw from a target distribution. At deployment time, additional computation can improve sample quality without retraining: at each step, the sampler draws several candidate noise samples, scores the resulting predictions with a quality criterion called the verifier, and retains the best candidate at the cost of one network evaluation per candidate. This raises a resource allocation question: given a fixed budget of function evaluations, how should search effort be distributed across the steps of the denoising trajectory? We formulate this as a computational budget allocation problem. First, we show that, to leading order in the step size, the expected gain from evaluating $K$ candidates at a step factorizes into an endogenous, step-specific sensitivity parameter times a universal sample-size factor equal to the expected best of $K$ standard-normal draws. Second, for a fixed sensitivity profile, the optimal allocation solves a separable concave integer program with water-filling structure; at fixed total sensitivity, its advantage over uniform allocation increases with sensitivity dispersion in the majorization order. Third, we prove that when sensitivities vary across instances, no adaptive policy can avoid worst-case regret that grows linearly in the trajectory length, which motivates a design that anchors the allocation offline and adapts online only to recover instance-specific slack. We extend the analysis from independent random search to a broader family of local search operators, and instantiate it as an implementable algorithm. Experiments on three families of diffusion samplers show that the proposed allocation attains the quality of the uniform benchmark with 20 to 50 percent fewer function evaluations.

</details>


### [165] [Towards Full Pipeline FP8 Reinforcement Learning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22870)
*Fanchao Chen,Ziheng Jiang,Ziyun Wei,Zheng Zhong,Du Li,Chi Zhang,Haibin Lin,Shivaram Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现全流程FP8强化学习存在训练不稳定问题，其根源是复合FP8量化噪声扭曲重要性比率，导致负优势token梯度被错误置零。作者提出Calibrated Clipping方法动态对齐FP8裁剪边界，消除熵激增并恢复BF16水平性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FP8量化可加速LLM的RL训练，但全流程FP8 RL仍存在严重训练不稳定问题。以往工作多关注训练-推理不匹配（如TIS纠错），却忽视了FP8量化噪声的复合影响。作者揭示这种不稳定表现为训练中期熵激增和输出乱码，需要找到根本原因并解决。

Method: 作者将不稳定归因于复合FP8量化噪声扭曲了重要性比率，使负优势token被过度推出信任域、梯度被错误置零，导致病态输出未被惩罚而累积。为此提出Calibrated Clipping：通过匹配下界裁剪分位数并相应重新平衡上界，使FP8裁剪边界对齐高精度BF16分布，是一种动态方法。

Result: 在GRPO和DAPO算法、8B至32B多种模型规模、以及多种FP8缩放粒度上的大量实验表明，该方法成功消除了熵激增，并恢复了与BF16基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: Calibrated Clipping有效解决了全流程FP8 RL的训练不稳定问题，在多种算法、规模与量化粒度下均能恢复BF16基线水平的性能，为FP8加速RL训练提供了稳定可行的方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a key technique for improving the reasoning and agentic abilities of large language models (LLMs). Although FP8 quantization can accelerate RL training, maintaining stability throughout an FP8 RL pipeline remains challenging. While previous works have focused on resolving train-inference mismatches using correction techniques like TIS, we reveal that full-pipeline FP8 RL still suffers from severe training instability, manifesting as anomalous mid-training entropy surges and garbled outputs. We trace this instability to a previously overlooked cause: compounded FP8 quantization noise distorts the importance ratio, disproportionately pushing negative-advantage tokens outside the trust region and erroneously zeroing out their gradients. As a result, pathological outputs are not properly penalized and accumulate over the course of training. To address this, we propose Calibrated Clipping, a dynamic method that aligns the FP8 clipping bounds with high-precision BF16 distributions by matching the lower-bound clipping quantile and rebalancing the upper bound accordingly. Extensive experiments across GRPO and DAPO algorithms, model scales from 8B to 32B, and multiple FP8 scaling granularities demonstrate that our approach successfully eliminates entropy surges and restores performance comparable to the BF16 baseline.

</details>


### [166] [Prioritized Rollouts for Efficient World Model-based Vision-Language-Action Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22879)
*Yifei Sheng,Haoxiang Ren,Zhilong Zhang,Haonan Wang,Runjie Xu,Yihao Sun,Nan Tang,Zhichao Wu,Lei Yuan,Haoxin Lin,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 U-GROW，一种轻量级、即插即用的采样层，利用策略不确定性引导世界模型生成更有价值的 rollout，从而提升 VLA 模型基于模型的强化学习微调效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在具身智能中很有潜力，但用强化学习微调受限于真实机器人交互成本。基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）虽能通过世界模型生成 rollout 降低成本，但随着 VLA 策略和世界模型规模增大，计算开销仍然很高。现有方法通常平等对待所有状态，忽视了不同状态对策略改进的价值差异。

Method: 论文发现策略不确定性可以识别更有改进潜力的状态：策略仅在少部分状态（常为决策敏感阶段）表现出高不确定性。基于此提出 U-GROW，一个轻量级、即插即用的采样层，通过修改 branched-start 分布，将更多模型 rollout 导向这些信息量高的状态，而不改变策略优化目标，可直接集成到现有 MBRL 流程中。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界操作任务上的实验表明，U-GROW 兼具高效性与有效性，支持用策略不确定性引导经验生成。

Conclusion: 策略不确定性可作为引导经验生成的信号，U-GROW 以极低改动成本提升 MBRL 微调 VLA 的效率，为具身智能中 VLA 的高效训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for embodied intelligence, but fine-tuning them with reinforcement learning (RL) remains constrained by the cost of real-world robot interaction. Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) reduces this cost by using a learned world model to generate rollouts for policy optimization. However, it becomes computationally expensive as VLA policies and world models scale. Existing methods typically treat states equally, overlooking substantial differences in their utility for policy improvement. In this paper, we show that policy uncertainty helps identify states with greater potential for policy improvement. The policy exhibits high uncertainty at only a small subset of states, often during decision-sensitive stages where small action differences can alter task outcomes, suggesting that policy improvements at these states could be particularly valuable. Building on these findings, we introduce U-GROW, a lightweight, plug-and-play sampling layer that directs more model rollouts to these informative states. By modifying only the branched-start distribution, U-GROW can be integrated into existing MBRL pipelines without changing the policy optimization objective. Experiments in both simulated and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate the efficiency and effectiveness of U-GROW, supporting the use of policy uncertainty to guide experience generation.

</details>


### [167] [Merge++: Universal Merge Refinement Through Data-Free Checkpoint Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22886)
*Aditya Pola,Vineeth N. Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Merge++ 是一种数据无关的模型合并后处理细化方法，通过反转专家检查点合成任务代表性图像，再用这些图像蒸馏专家知识到合并模型，无需额外数据即可普遍提升合并算法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据无关的模型合并方法全部在权重空间操作，只能对参数做算术运算，从未观察到每个专家的行为表现，而行为信号只能通过前向评估获得。获取该信号需要评估输入，但数据无关的设定禁止使用数据。

Method: Merge++ 通过反转专家检查点来合成任务代表性图像，然后利用这些合成图像将专家知识蒸馏到合并模型中。该方法除了检查点本身之外不需要任何额外数据，且适用于各种合并算法，作为独立于底层权重空间方法的互补细化阶段。

Result: 该方法能持续改进从简单任务算术到最先进谱方法的各类合并算法，平均增益为 +2 到 +8 分，个别配置上最高提升 +25.9 分。

Conclusion: Merge++ 突破了数据无关合并仅限权重空间的限制，通过合成图像引入行为信号，作为一种通用、即插即用的细化阶段，可稳定提升多种合并算法的效果。

Abstract: Model merging consolidates fine-tuned experts into one multi-task model without retraining. All existing data-free methods approach this problem entirely in weight space. Restricted to arithmetic on parameters, these methods never observe how each expert behaves, a signal that only emerges through forward evaluation. Accessing this behavioral signal requires inputs to evaluate on, which the data-free setting prohibits. We propose Merge++, a post-hoc method that addresses this by inverting the expert checkpoints to synthesize task-representative images, then distilling expert knowledge into the merged model using those images. Merge++ requires no additional data beyond the checkpoints themselves. It applies universally across merging algorithms and operates as a complementary refinement stage independent of the underlying weight-space method. The method consistently improves merging algorithms ranging from simple task arithmetic to state-of-the-art spectral methods, with average gains of +2 to +8 points and up to +25.9 on individual configurations.

</details>


### [168] [Are Coreset Selection Methods Worth Their Cost?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22894)
*Yangze Liu,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出端到端基准，将选择与训练统一计入可审计的墙钟时间预算，覆盖4个数据集、11种选择器、5种比例、3个随机种子，共1500余次运行。结果显示在预算约束下，没有任何复杂选择器能胜出，获胜者均为类平衡随机采样、重复随机采样或全数据训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有coreset选择研究通常以固定子集大小下的下游准确率作为评价标准，忽略了选择子集所耗时间以及训练配方的影响，导致评价结果可能具有误导性。作者希望引入预算感知的端到端评测，检验在公平预算下复杂选择策略是否仍然有效。

Method: 构建端到端基准，标准化下游训练流程，并将选择与训练计入同一可审计的墙钟时间预算。实验涵盖CIFAR-10到ImageNet-1K的4个数据集、11种选择器、5种选择比例、3个随机种子，发布超过1500次运行。在CIFAR-10和Tiny ImageNet上设置8个墙钟预算锚点，并在ImageNet-1K上进行固定预算对决；同时开展逐数据集成本审计，并量化子集复用时的回报，还记录了9处代码修正。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和Tiny ImageNet的8个预算锚点中，没有任何锚点由复杂选择器获胜，获胜者均为类平衡随机采样、重复随机采样或全数据训练。在ImageNet-1K固定预算对决中，用全部数据训练更少轮次优于所有被测选择策略，且成本最低。成本审计显示选择成本在每个规模上都由固定的全数据集扫描主导，无法通过选择更小比例来摊销，其绝对大小也无法从一个数据集外推到另一个数据集。此外，修复了广泛使用代码库中的9处正确性问题，其中一处使标准Herding基线偏移近6个百分点。

Conclusion: 选择时间并非免费的预处理步骤，忽略它的评测会衡量错误的量。预算感知的端到端评测表明，在公平预算下复杂coreset选择策略难以战胜简单随机或全数据训练基线，因此未来研究应报告包含选择成本在内的完整预算。

Abstract: Coreset selection picks a representative subset of the labeled training set to make training cheaper. However, it is usually evaluated by downstream accuracy at a fixed subset size, ignoring both the time spent selecting the subset and the training recipe behind each reported number. We introduce an end-to-end benchmark that standardizes downstream training and charges selection and training to the same auditable wall-clock budget, spanning 4 datasets from CIFAR-10 to ImageNet-1K, 11 selectors, 5 fractions, and 3 seeds, with over 1,500 released runs. Repeated-sampling work has shown that budget-aware evaluation already favors random strategies. Our two budget studies test whether that verdict survives when every selector is granted its most favorable operating point. Across eight wall-clock budget anchors on each of CIFAR-10 and Tiny ImageNet, no anchor is won by a sophisticated selector: every winner is class-balanced random sampling, repeated random sampling, or full-data training. In fixed-budget duels on ImageNet-1K, training on all data for fewer epochs beats every selection strategy we probe while also costing the least. A per-dataset cost audit shows that selection cost is dominated at every scale by a fixed full-dataset scan, so it cannot be amortized away by selecting a smaller fraction, and its absolute size does not extrapolate from one dataset to another. We further quantify when selection does pay back through subset reuse, and document 9 correctness fixes to a widely used codebase, one of which shifts a standard Herding baseline by nearly 6 points. Selection time is not free preprocessing, and an evaluation that ignores it measures the wrong quantity.

</details>


### [169] [Computationally efficient safe exploration in reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22919)
*Shreeram Murali,Shankar A. Deka,Dominik Baumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Nadaraya-Watson估计器的轻量级安全强化学习算法CoLSafe-MDP，用于约束MDP中的安全探索与优化，计算复杂度为常数级，显著优于三次方复杂度的GP方法，并在网格环境与火星地形数据上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中的强化学习需要在探索过程中提供安全保证，但典型RL算法缺乏此类保证；而依赖高斯过程（GP）的改进方法计算开销巨大（随数据点数量立方增长）。因此需要一个计算轻量、仍能保证安全探索的算法。

Method: 基于Nadaraya-Watson估计器构建算法CoLSafe-MDP，该估计器具有常数级的计算扩展性并能给出估计值的边界，从而在约束马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中实现安全探索与优化。

Result: 在网格基环境中以及观测火星地形数据上评估了算法性能；估计器实现常数时间扩展，相比随数据点三次方扩展的GP方法有显著改进。

Conclusion: CoLSafe-MDP为约束MDP中的安全强化学习提供了一种计算高效、可扩展的替代方案，克服了GP-based方法在计算成本上的瓶颈。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in real-life applications requires safety guarantees during exploration. Typical reinforcement learning algorithms do not provide such guarantees, and many modifications that do rely on Gaussian processes (GPs), which have a large computational cost. We propose a computationally lightweight algorithm based on the Nadaraya-Watson estimator that safely explores and optimizes constrained Markov decision processes (MDPs). Our algorithm, \textsc{CoLSafe-MDP}, uses an estimator that scales in constant-time with bounds on the estimates, a significant improvement from its GP-based counterparts that scale cubically with the number of data points. We then evaluate its performance in a grid-based environment and on observational Martian terrain data.

</details>


### [170] [Joint Domain-Class Modeling for Federated Learning Under Feature Skew](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22932)
*Sina Najafi,Mostafa Tavassolipour,Seyed Pooya Shariatpanahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出JDFL，一种轻量、与优化器无关的联邦学习扩展，通过推断伪域并扩展分类头为域-类联合逻辑输出，结合相似性感知软标签和随机目标分配两种监督策略，在特征偏移场景下提升全局测试精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中客户端共享标签，但由于潜在的、客户端特有的外观因素，条件输入分布p_i(x|y)存在差异，导致特征偏移，模型性能下降。作者希望在不共享原始数据的前提下，让这种潜在域变化变得可用。

Method: JDFL从简短的本地更新信号中推断出称为伪域的域聚类；将分类头扩展为输出M×C的联合（域-类）逻辑值，在共享骨干网络下表示域条件外观；采用两种监督策略：相似性感知软标签（在邻近推断域间传递证据，同时允许域特定特化）和逐样本随机目标分配（扰动联合输出监督，作为低成本训练正则化）。该方法与FedAvg、SCAFFOLD等标准FL方法兼容，改动极小。

Result: 在标准域偏移图像基准上，两种监督模式均持续提升全局测试精度；消融和敏感性研究表明增益来自所提出的监督与参数化方式，而非单纯增加模型容量。

Conclusion: JDFL通过伪域推断与联合域-类分类头设计，有效利用潜在域变化，为特征偏移下的联邦学习提供了一种轻量、通用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without centralizing private data, but performance often degrades under feature skew: clients share labels while the conditional input distributions $p_i(x\!\mid\!y)$ vary due to latent, client-specific appearance factors. We propose Joint Domain-Class Federated Learning (JDFL), a lightweight, optimizer-agnostic extension that makes this latent domain variation usable without sharing raw data. JDFL first infers domain clusters called pseudo-domains from brief local update signals. It then expands the classifier head to output $M\times C$, joint (domain-class) logits. This allows the model to represent domain-conditioned appearance while keeping a shared backbone. To train the expanded head we introduce two complementary supervision strategies based on simple intuitions: a similarity-aware soft-labeling that transfers evidence between nearby inferred domains while allowing domain-specific specialization, and a per-sample randomized target assignment that perturbs supervision across the joint outputs and serves as a low-cost training-time regularizer. JDFL integrates with existing standard FL methods (e.g., FedAvg, SCAFFOLD) with minimal changes. Empirically, both supervision modes consistently improve global test accuracy on standard domain-shifted image benchmarks; ablations and sensitivity studies show the gains stem from the proposed supervision and parametrization rather than mere capacity increase.

</details>


### [171] [Token Utility Is Selection-Conditioned: Coupled Selection of Prompt Context and Response Supervision for Efficient Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22943)
*Can Wu,Xinrui Chen,Ou Wu,Yi Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BRIDGE 提出一种面向大模型指令微调的数据选择方法，通过预算约束下响应与提示上下文的交互式联合选择，缓解双方独立评估导致的“选择状态不匹配”，在数学推理、代码生成和指令遵循任务上整体优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效的大模型指令微调需要选取带有提示上下文的响应监督数据。现有方法通常分别评估提示和响应两侧，导致评估时与保留训练子集之间存在选择状态不匹配的问题。

Method: BRIDGE 通过共享的、以验证为导向的交互代理来评估每个侧面在另一侧保留状态下的条件效用。采用预算化的交替选择，将预计算的交互在当前对侧子集上聚合以更新条件分数；并用结构感知投影将条件响应分数转化为连贯的监督区间。

Result: 在三个模型家族上，BRIDGE 在数学推理、代码生成和指令遵循任务上整体优于比较的选择方法。在数学推理中，其相对于独立选择的优势随压缩率提高而增大。

Conclusion: BRIDGE 通过将响应与提示上下文的选择置于同一交互式条件效用框架下并结合结构感知投影，实现了更有效的预算化数据选择，且在高压缩场景下优势更明显。

Abstract: Efficient large language model (LLM) instruction tuning requires selecting response supervision with supporting prompt context. Existing methods typically value both sides separately, risking selection-state mismatch between valuation and retained training subsets. BRIDGE (Budgeted Response-Prompt Interaction via Directional Gradient-guided Efficient Token Selection) captures selection-conditioned token utility through a shared validation-directed interaction surrogate valuing each side under the other's retained state. Budgeted alternating selection coordinates retained subsets by aggregating precomputed interactions over the current opposite-side subset to update conditional scores. Structure-aware projection converts conditional response scores into coherent supervision spans. Across three model families, BRIDGE leads compared selection methods overall in mathematical reasoning, code generation, and instruction following. In mathematical reasoning, its advantage over independent selection grows with compression.

</details>


### [172] [Beyond Similarity: Coverage-Aware Prompt Selection for Time Series Forecasting with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22977)
*Daeun Ji,Minkyoung Kim,Dongkuk Kim,Yohan Lee,Beomsoo Kim,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CASP-LLM，用覆盖感知的语义提示框架缓解基于相似度的提示检索偏差，无需新增可学习参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于相似度的检索是LLM上下文学习、检索增强生成和基于提示的时间序列预测中的主流规则，但它集中于近重复候选，导致偏向主导模式而忽略稀有但信息丰富的事件。该问题在提示式时间序列预测中尚未被考察。

Method: 以基于提示的时间序列预测为测试平台，针对无冗余控制地按余弦相似度检索top-K导致的选择偏差，提出CASP-LLM：结合使用追踪与饱和门技术，构建覆盖正则化器，且不引入可学习参数。

Result: 在六个长期基准和M4短期基准上，CASP-LLM在大多数数据集-预测步长设置上匹配或超越基于相似度的LLM预测器，例外为Electricity、M4-Monthly和少样本长时程设置。对照研究将失败模式定位于跨批次使用层面，而非单次检索冗余：MMR等检索内多样化无效，而正则化训练中的锚点使用有效。

Conclusion: 提示选择偏差的关键在于跨批次使用而非单次检索冗余，覆盖感知正则化可有效缓解该偏差，并在多数基准上提升或持平相似度方法。

Abstract: Similarity-based retrieval is the dominant rule for conditioning large language models (LLMs) in in-context learning, retrieval-augmented generation, and prompt-based time series forecasting. The rule concentrates on near-duplicate candidates, an issue that has motivated diversity-aware retrieval but remains unexamined in other retrieval-conditioned pipelines. We study this issue using prompt-based time series forecasting as a test bed, where a learned prompt pool is retrieved by similarity. Dominant methods in this setting retrieve top-K entries by cosine similarity without redundancy control, producing a bias toward dominant temporal patterns while overlooking rare but informative events. We propose CASP-LLM, a coverage-aware semantic prompting framework that addresses this prompt selection bias by combining usage-tracking and saturating-gate techniques into a coverage regularizer that adds no learnable parameters. On six long-term benchmarks and the M4 short-term benchmark, CASP-LLM matches or improves on similarity-based LLM forecasters on most dataset-horizon settings, with the exceptions of Electricity, M4-Monthly, and the few-shot long-horizon setting. A controlled study locates the failure mode at the cross-batch usage level rather than per-retrieval redundancy: within-retrieval diversification such as MMR does not help, whereas regularizing anchor usage across training does.

</details>


### [173] [LPINNs: First-Layer Gated Localization for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22984)
*Lakshay Chawla,Hardik Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过在PINN第一隐藏层激活上乘以输入相关的定位函数，为第一层单元引入感受野，从而在长域和高阶算子问题上显著降低求解误差，且无需分域或界面损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINN在整个计算域上共享一个表示，在长域和高阶算子上优化困难。作者希望寻找一种最小化的替代方案，在不进行域划分、不增加界面损失的前提下改善优化性能。

Method: 将未改变的稠密PINN的第一隐藏层激活乘以输入相关的定位函数，为第一层单元赋予感受野。在非线性谐振子、长空间区间热方程和四维四阶制造问题上，以10组配对随机种子，系统筛选了13类定位函数（每类最多3种参数化）。

Result: 三种配置在相同预算下大幅降低误差：(i) 固定高斯定位在2π谐振子域上3k轮将RMSE从4.8369×10⁻¹降至8.83×10⁻³；(ii) 可学习中心与宽度的反二次族在8π热域上10k轮从2.896×10⁻¹降至3.06×10⁻²；(iii) 固定bump定位在4π四维域上10k轮从1.75947×10¹降至2.260×10⁻¹。三者中每个配对种子均有改善。但该机制并非稳赢：谐振子上仅2/13族优于基线，其余11族中有10族差9–23倍；四维上4族非有限、5族比基线差三个数量级以上。反二次族是唯一在三方程上均优于基线的族。

Conclusion: 第一层定位可为基线PINN在长域和高阶问题上带来可测量的改进，但效果高度依赖定位函数的选择，并非普适的免费提升。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) use one shared representation over the computational domain, which can become difficult to optimize on long domains and for high-order operators. We study a minimal alternative: multiply the first hidden activation of an otherwise unchanged dense PINN by input-dependent localization functions, giving first-layer units receptive fields without partitioning the domain or adding interface losses. We screen 13 families of localization functions, in up to three parameterizations each, on a nonlinear harmonic oscillator (HO), a heat equation on a long spatial interval, and a manufactured four-dimensional (4D) fourth-order problem, with ten paired seeds throughout. Three configurations give large reductions in solution error at matched budgets: (i) Fixed Gaussian localization functions on the $2π$ HO domain cut mean solution RMSE from $4.8369\times10^{-1}$ to $8.83\times10^{-3}$ at 3k epochs. (ii) The inverse-quadratic family with learnable centers and widths cuts it from $2.896\times10^{-1}$ to $3.06\times10^{-2}$ on the $8π$ heat domain at 10k epochs. (iii) Fixed bump localization functions cut it from $1.75947\times10^{1}$ to $2.260\times10^{-1}$ on the $4π$ 4D domain at 10k epochs. Every paired seed improves in these three comparisons. The screen also shows that the mechanism is not a free win: on HO only 2 of 13 families beat the baseline, and 10 of the remaining 11 are 9 to 23 times worse; on 4D four families are non-finite and five are more than three orders of magnitude worse than the baseline. The inverse-quadratic family is the only one that beats the baseline on all three equations. Overall, these results show that first-layer localization can provide measurable improvements to baseline PINNs on long-domain and high-order problems.

</details>


### [174] [On attention heads and bilinear forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22990)
*Andrew O'Desky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究训练后大语言模型注意力头中双线性形式的对称与反对称部分，引入正交不变剖面映射发现训练后双线性形式的剖面聚集于秩一双线性形式的剖面附近，并证明在Zariski稠密子集上对称部分可分解为双曲形式与零形式之和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索训练后大语言模型注意力头中双线性形式的代数结构，理解其对称与反对称部分的性质，并揭示训练过程中双线性形式趋向秩一结构的现象。

Method: 引入从实双线性形式到三维单纯形的正交不变剖面映射，分析训练后双线性形式的剖面分布；对注意力头中双线性形式的对称部分进行代数分解研究。

Result: 训练后双线性形式的剖面聚集在秩一双线性形式的剖面附近；证明对于query-key矩阵的Zariski稠密子集，注意力头中双线性形式的对称部分是双曲形式与零形式之和。

Conclusion: 训练后的注意力头双线性形式在剖面空间上表现出趋向秩一结构的特性，且其对称部分具有可分解为双曲形式与零形式的代数结构。

Abstract: We study the symmetric and antisymmetric parts of bilinear forms in the attention heads of trained large language models. We introduce an orthogonally invariant profile map from real bilinear forms to a three-dimensional simplex and observe that profiles of trained bilinear forms accumulate near profiles of rank-one bilinear forms. We prove that the symmetric part of a bilinear form in an attention head is the sum of a hyperbolic form and a zero form for a Zariski-dense subset of query-key matrices.

</details>


### [175] [Interpretable Multi-Hypersphere Deep Anomaly Detection for Open-set Supervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23008)
*Zhiji Yang,Fangyong Wang,Yue Li,Xianli Pan,Jianhua Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可解释多超球深度异常检测（IMHD-AD），为每个已知正常类构建独立超球，联合优化中心与半径，实现开集接受/拒绝与几何可解释决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多类开集异常检测需仅用已知正常类样本刻画由多个异质子分布构成的正常接受域，并识别未见异常。现有单超球方法无法显式表达类特定位置与接受范围；现有多超球或多类方法未充分整合类间边界约束、可学习接受范围与可解释决策。

Method: IMHD-AD在共享特征空间中为每个已知正常类构建独立超球，采用目标在内、非目标在外约束，将类特定超球中心与半径直接嵌入最终网络层，与共享表示联合优化；以跨超球的最小符号边界分数同时判定开集接受或拒绝，并提供几何解释。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST与CIFAR-10上，IMHD-AD在30组开集对比中的28组取得最高AUC；二维合成研究进一步表明模型架构需在已知正常类紧凑性与未知异常可分性之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: IMHD-AD通过可学习多超球与统一边界约束，兼顾类特定建模、开集判别与决策可解释性，在多个数据集上表现优越，并揭示了紧凑性与可分性的架构权衡。

Abstract: Multi-class open-set anomaly detection requires a model to characterize the normal acceptance domain formed by multiple heterogeneous subdistributions using only class-labeled samples from known normal classes, and to identify previously unseen anomalies at test time. Existing single-hypersphere methods cannot explicitly represent class-specific locations and acceptance ranges, while current multi-hypersphere or multi-class approaches do not fully integrate inter-class boundary constraints, learnable acceptance ranges, and interpretable decisions. To address these limitations, we propose Interpretable Multi-Hypersphere Deep Anomaly Detection (IMHD-AD). IMHD-AD constructs an independent hypersphere for each known normal class in a shared feature space. With target-inside and non-target-outside constraints, IMHD-AD embeds the class-specific hypersphere centers and radii directly into the final network layer and jointly optimizes them with the shared representation. The minimum signed boundary score across hyperspheres simultaneously determines open-set acceptance or rejection and provides a faithful geometric explanation of each decision. On MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10, IMHD-AD achieves the highest AUC in 28 of 30 open-set comparisons. A two-dimensional synthetic study further shows that model architecture must balance the compactness of known normal classes against the separability of unknown anomalies.

</details>


### [176] [WaveFront Decoding: Parallelized Self-Speculative Decoding for Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23033)
*Hyeongju Ha,Jae-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Wavefront Decoding (WFD)，一种针对循环语言模型的无训练自推测解码框架，通过将不同深度的token状态组织成对角波前，实现草稿与验证的同步批处理，在多个模型上取得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型通过权重共享块增加有效深度而不增加参数量，但每个生成token需要T次顺序循环块调用，导致解码延迟大幅增加。现有方法难以兼顾效率与质量，需要一种无需训练的加速方案。

Method: WFD利用循环模型的两个特性：中间循环输出可作为有效草稿预测，权重共享允许不同位置和循环深度的token状态在一次批量循环块调用中处理。WFD将混合深度状态组织成对角波前，在浅层持续起草新位置，同时将先前位置推进至全深度验证。与阶段分离的"先起草后验证"调度不同，WFD在同一循环调用中共同批处理起草与验证，被拒绝的草稿用全深度预测校正。此外引入跨循环KV共享以减少波前KV流量。

Result: 在Spec-Bench六个任务类别上，WFD相比自回归解码在Ouro-2.6B上实现2.42倍加速，在Huginn-3.5B上实现3.54倍加速，持续优于先起草后验证方法。跨循环KV共享进一步降低波前KV流量，将Huginn-3.5B上的加速提升至4.81倍。

Conclusion: WFD为循环语言模型提供了一种高效的无训练自推测解码方案，通过波前调度实现草稿与验证的协同批处理，显著降低解码延迟，并可通过KV共享进一步优化性能。

Abstract: Looped language models repeatedly apply a weight-shared block to increase effective depth without increasing parameter count, but the resulting T sequential recurrent-block calls per generated token substantially increase decoding latency. To address the issue, we introduce Wavefront Decoding (WFD), a training-free self-speculative decoding framework designed for looped language models. WFD exploits two properties of these architectures: intermediate recurrence outputs provide effective draft predictions, and weight sharing allows token states at different positions and recurrence depths to be processed in one batched recurrent-block call. WFD organizes these mixed-depth states into a diagonal wavefront, continuously drafting new positions at shallow depth while advancing earlier positions toward full-depth verification. Unlike the phase-separated draft-then-verify schedule, WFD therefore co-batches drafting and verification within the same recurrent calls, while rejected drafts are corrected using full-depth predictions. Across six Spec-Bench task categories, WFD achieves 2.42x speedup on Ouro-2.6B and 3.54x on Huginn-3.5B over autoregressive decoding, consistently outperforming draft-then-verify. Cross-recurrence KV sharing further reduces wavefront KV traffic and increases WFD's speedup to 4.81x on Huginn-3.5B.

</details>


### [177] [Optimizers for Diffusion Models: A Controlled Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23055)
*Arman Bolatov,Egor Shulgin,David Li,Abduragim Shtanchaev,Sebastian U. Stich,Maxim Panov,Eric Moulines,Peter Richtárik,Martin Takáč*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首个离散扩散模型优化器基准测试，覆盖4种扩散形式与7种优化器。结果显示AdamW虽是强默认选项但并非总是最优，且在不同扩散形式上最优优化器会变化，值得像训练配方其他部分一样认真对待。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型已能比肩自回归语言模型，但其训练方式尤其是优化器的选择长期被忽视——优化器从一篇论文沿用到下一篇，从未被系统比较。而新优化器几乎只在自回归预训练上验证，其目标函数与损失曲面都不同，难以直接迁移。因此需要一个受控的优化器基准测试。

Method: 对四种扩散形式进行受控优化器基准测试：掩码扩散（text8）、均匀扩散（QM9，及通过高斯对偶的LM1B）、图像高斯扩散（CelebA-64），每个任务均有已发表的参考值。测试七种优化器：AdamW、Lion、Muon、SOAP、MARS、MARS-M、Schedule-Free。所有优化器采用相同搜索协议，胜出者在全预算下用三个随机种子重新训练。

Result: AdamW是强默认基线但并非总正确：在四个任务中有两个被明确超越，且胜出者随扩散形式变化。在自回归语言模型预训练上验证的方法迁移性良好：Muon、MARS-M和SOAP各自在至少一种扩散形式上击败了调优后的AdamW。

Conclusion: 优化器选择对离散扩散模型训练至关重要，应与训练配方其他部分同等重视，因为最优优化器随扩散形式而异。基准、全部运行和图表均可在https://github.com/armanbolatov/diffusion-baselines 上端到端复现。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models now match autoregressive language models on several benchmarks, while the question of how best to train them has received far less attention: the optimizer is inherited from one paper to the next and never compared. New optimizers, meanwhile, are validated almost exclusively on autoregressive pretraining, a different objective on a different loss surface. We present a controlled optimizer benchmark across four diffusion formulations, to our knowledge the first for discrete diffusion: seven optimizers (AdamW, Lion, Muon, SOAP, MARS, MARS-M, Schedule-Free) on masked diffusion (text8), uniform diffusion (QM9, and LM1B through the Gaussian duality) and Gaussian diffusion on images (CelebA-64), each on a task with published reference values. Every optimizer receives the same search protocol, and every winner is retrained at the full budget with three seeds. AdamW is a strong default but not always the right choice: it is beaten by a resolved margin on two of the four tasks, and the winner changes with the formulation, so the optimizer deserves the same care as the rest of the training recipe. Notably, methods validated on autoregressive language model pretraining transfer well: Muon, MARS-M and SOAP each beat the tuned AdamW on at least one diffusion formulation. The benchmark, all runs and every figure are reproducible end to end from the released code at https://github.com/armanbolatov/diffusion-baselines.

</details>


### [178] [MolSC: Leveraging Substituent Contributions to Enhance Fine-grained Molecular Understanding in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23073)
*Hyuntae Park,Sooyeon Kim,Jiwon Park,SangKeun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出MolSC数据集与MolSC-Bench基准，聚焦"取代基对分子性质的贡献"这一细粒度任务，发现现有分子LLM（含GPT-5.2、Gemini-3-Flash）预测可靠性有限，而基于MolSC训练可显著提升该能力并改善下游分子任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子大语言模型虽在多种化学任务上表现良好，但难以捕捉细粒度的结构—性质关系，尤其是分子上局部微小修饰如何改变其行为；因此需要针对"取代基贡献"这一细粒度理解能力进行专门建模与评测。

Method: 构建MolSC数据集（取代基贡献数据，定义为将特定取代基连接到分子骨架后引起的性质变化），从人工标注的生物活性记录中整理，覆盖结构警示风险、靶点特异性生物活性与理化描述符，含181K取代基级训练样本；同时提出MolSC-Bench（1,541个留出评测样本），在骨架、取代基和分子三个层面与MolSC完全不相交。

Result: 实验表明，现有分子LLM及GPT-5.2、Gemini-3-Flash等强专有模型在取代基贡献预测上可靠性有限；而使用MolSC训练可大幅提升该能力，并在多种下游分子任务上取得强劲表现。

Conclusion: 取代基贡献学习是细粒度分子理解的关键组成部分，通过专门的数据构建与训练可有效弥补现有分子LLM在局部结构—性质关系建模上的不足。

Abstract: Recent advances in natural language processing have led to molecular Large Language Models (LLMs) with strong performance across diverse chemistry tasks. However, they still struggle to capture fine-grained structure-property relationships, particularly how small, localized modifications alter a molecule's behavior. To address this limitation, we introduce MolSC, a dataset of substituent contributions, defined as property changes induced by attaching specific substituents to molecular scaffolds. Curated from manually annotated bioactivity records, MolSC spans structural-alert liability, target-specific bioactivity, and physicochemical descriptors, and contains 181K substituent-level examples for training. We further propose MolSC-Bench, a held-out evaluation benchmark of 1,541 examples disjoint from MolSC at the scaffold, substituent, and molecule levels. Our experiments show that existing molecular LLMs and strong proprietary models such as GPT-5.2 and Gemini-3-Flash show limited reliability in substituent contribution prediction. In contrast, training on MolSC substantially improves this ability and achieves strong performance across diverse downstream molecular tasks. These results highlight substituent contribution learning as a key component of fine-grained molecular understanding.

</details>


### [179] [AirGC-CD: Gaussian-Circulant Precoding for Exactly Debiasable PAPR Reduction in Over-the-Air Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23084)
*Jonggyu Jang,Hyeonsu Lyu,Hyun Jong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AirGC-CD：在裁剪前用部分高斯循环矩阵预编码本地更新，使裁剪输出精确高斯，可用闭式Bussgang增益无偏反演，并用闭式近似最优裁剪比平衡裁剪方差与回退SNR损失，同时将传输维度由d压缩到m，仅需O(d log d)开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中联邦学习虽降低通信开销，但波形PAPR随模型维度增长。为保持放大器线性，只能裁剪峰值或回退功率：裁剪在接收端引入不可去除的偏置，回退损害平均SNR。此外传输未压缩，每个模型参数占用一次信道，难以支撑大模型训练。

Method: AirGC-CD在每个本地更新裁剪前用部分高斯循环矩阵预编码。预编码输出无论更新是否稀疏均为精确高斯，使裁剪针对已知分布设计，可通过单一标量Bussgang增益闭式平均反演，并证明聚合结果精确无偏，裁剪仅增加方差。裁剪比是唯一自由参数，权衡裁剪方差与回退SNR损失，推导其闭式近似最优值。预编码线性，兼作压缩器，将传输从模型维度d降至草图维度m，经两次FFT仅耗O(d log d)，而高斯草图耗O(md)。

Result: 在五个图像数据集上的实验表明，AirGC-CD在多数设置下优于基线空中FL方案，尤其在低SNR时表现突出，且每轮使用更少的信道次数。

Conclusion: AirGC-CD通过高斯化预编码同时解决了PAPR裁剪偏差、功率回退与传输未压缩三大问题，实现无偏聚合、闭式最优参数与低复杂度压缩，在低SNR和通信受限场景下具有优势。

Abstract: Over-the-air federated learning lets edge devices transmit their local updates simultaneously, reducing the communication overhead. The resulting waveform, however, has a peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR) that grows with the model dimension, and keeping the amplifier in its linear range leaves two remedies: clipping the peaks or backing off the transmit power. Neither remedy is without cost: i) the clipping distortion appears at the receiver as a bias that cannot be removed, and ii) back-off keeps the signal intact but degrades the average signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). Independent of this trade-off, the transmission remains uncompressed, spending one channel use per model parameter, which keeps large-model training out of reach. To address these challenges, we propose AirGC-CD, an over-the-air scheme that precodes each local update with a partial Gaussian circulant matrix before clipping. In AirGC-CD, the precoder's output is exactly Gaussian regardless of the update's sparsity, so the clipping function is designed for a known distribution instead of inheriting it from the data. This enables the clipping to be inverted on average by a single scalar Bussgang gain in closed form, and we prove that the resulting aggregate is exactly unbiased, with clipping adding only variance. The clipping ratio is then the only free parameter left, trading the variance of the clipping against the SNR loss from back-off, and we derive its near-optimum in closed form. Since the precoder is linear, it also acts as a compressor, reducing the transmission from the model dimension d to the sketch dimension m at a cost of only O(dlog d) via two fast Fourier transforms, whereas a Gaussian sketch costs O(md). Experiments on five image datasets show that AirGC-CD outperforms baseline over-the-air FL schemes in most settings, particularly at low SNR, while using fewer channel uses per round.

</details>


### [180] [Neural Spectral Capacity: Measuring and Designing Architectures from Network Specification Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23087)
*Chenyu Zhu,Ruoyu Zhao,Zhichao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Neural Spectral Capacity (NSC)，一个基于权重矩阵奇异值谱的闭式标量，可仅凭架构规格即可计算，并配套精确动态规划求解器 NSC-DP，在架构排序与压缩任务上超越 #Params、#FLOPs 及代表性免训练代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Transformer 设计与压缩中的标准标量 #Params 和 #FLOPs 只刻画规模与计算量，无法反映架构结构。参数量预算相同但深度-宽度、注意力头、FFN 分配不同的两个架构会得到相同评分，却表现出不同性能。因此需要一个能体现结构差异的容量度量。

Method: 提出 NSC，基于每个权重矩阵的奇异值谱构建闭式标量。在标准随机初始化下，借助 Marchenko-Pastur 定律，NSC 可仅从架构规格直接计算，无需实例化模型、数据或梯度。其逐层可加结构支持 NSC-DP，一种精确动态规划求解器，在资源约束下于 CPU 上数秒内返回全局最大化 NSC 的架构。

Result: 在七个 Transformer 与 CNN 家族中，NSC 在排序上优于 #Params、#FLOPs 和代表性免训练代理。在 FlexiBERT 上，对于参数量差异小于 10% 的配对，τ=0.505，而 #Params 降至 0.082；NSC-DP 在 2 秒内于 WikiText-103 上发现超越人工设计基线的 Transformer-XL 架构；将 LLaMA-7B 剪枝为在八个常识推理任务上表现最佳的 5.7B 模型，无需校准数据，比最强免训练代理基线快约 5900 倍。

Conclusion: NSC 提供了一个从架构规格即可计算的闭式容量标量，弥补了 #Params 与 #FLOPs 在结构刻画上的不足；NSC-DP 则给出资源约束下全局最优的架构搜索保证，且在实际架构发现与免校准剪枝中展现出高效与优越性能。

Abstract: Modern Transformer design and compression both reduce to allocating capacity under a budget. The standard scalars for these decisions, #Params and #FLOPs, capture size and compute but not architectural structure: two architectures with identical parameter budgets but different depth-width, head, or FFN allocations receive identical scores yet behave differently. We propose Neural Spectral Capacity (NSC), a closed-form scalar grounded in the singular-value spectrum of each weight matrix. Under standard random initialization, the Marchenko-Pastur law renders NSC computable from the architectural specification alone, with no model instantiation, data, or gradients. Its layer-wise additive structure admits NSC-DP, an exact dynamic-programming solver returning the architecture globally maximizing NSC under resource constraints in seconds on a CPU -- a guarantee that black-box search over existing training-free proxies cannot provide. Empirically, NSC outperforms #Params, #FLOPs, and representative training-free proxies in ranking across seven Transformer and CNN families (on FlexiBERT, $τ= 0.505$ on pairs differing in #Params by less than 10%, where #Params collapses to 0.082); NSC-DP discovers a Transformer-XL architecture on WikiText-103 that beats the human-designed baseline in 2 seconds; and prunes LLaMA-7B to the best 5.7B model across eight commonsense reasoning tasks without any calibration data, about 5900x faster than the strongest training-free proxy baseline.

</details>


### [181] [The Role of Coordinates in Pareto Regret for Adversarial Multi-Objective Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23092)
*Changkun Guan,Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗性多目标赌博机中Pareto遗憾的下界与上界，证明在不知道哪个坐标更“容易”时，较小累积损失无法改善最坏情况Pareto遗憾阶，并提出自适应算法达到匹配的最优率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有理论表明，在对抗性多目标赌博机中，某个坐标更“容易”（累积损失更小）可能降低Pareto遗憾；但实际中往往不知道哪个坐标更容易。因此需要研究这种信息缺失对Pareto遗憾最坏情况阶的影响。

Method: 将损失定义为1减奖励，用某坐标上各臂的最小累积损失衡量坐标的“容易度”。作者先证明下界：设L_d为坐标d在T轮中的最小累积损失，对K≥4、T≥6及至少2个坐标，极小极大期望Pareto遗憾为Ω(min{T-L_0, √(K(T-L_0))})，即使已知L_0=min_d L_d也成立。随后在已知L_0时，将Poly-INF应用于固定坐标得到匹配上界；在未知L_0时，提出奖励倍增版Poly-INF以自适应未知量。

Result: 负面上，缺乏“哪个坐标更容易”的信息会消除优势：更小的累积损失不能改善最坏情况Pareto遗憾阶，且下界随L_0单调递减。正面上，已知L_0时对固定坐标应用Poly-INF可达到与下界同阶的Pareto遗憾上界；未知L_0时，奖励倍增版Poly-INF仍达到匹配的极小极大率。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，即使不知道最容易的坐标，其他坐标也足以达到Pareto遗憾最优率；所提方法无额外log T因子，且不依赖于坐标数量。

Abstract: Adversarial multi-objective bandits hold the potential to help us optimize choices (arms) whose reward is a multidimensional vector chosen by an adversary and whose performance is measured by Pareto regret. We define loss as one minus reward and measure the easiness of a coordinate by the smallest cumulative loss of the arms on it, and call the coordinate easier when this quantity is smaller. Existing work suggests that in theory an easier coordinate may reduce Pareto regret. However, in practice, one may not know which coordinate is easier. On the negative side, we show that this lack of information eliminates the possibility: a smaller cumulative loss does not improve the worst-case order of Pareto regret. Precisely, let \(L_d\) be the smallest cumulative loss along coordinate $d$ over $T$ rounds. For \(K\ge4\) arms, \(T\ge6\) rounds, and at least 2 coordinates, we prove that the minimax expected Pareto regret is \(Ω(\min\{T-L_0,\sqrt{K(T-L_0)}\})\). It is monotonically decreasing in \(L_0\), even when \(L_0=\min_d L_d\) itself is known. On the positive side, this result motivates the possibility that other coordinates, not just the easy one, may suffice to attain the optimal rate of Pareto regret. When $L_0$ is known, we apply Poly-INF to a fixed coordinate and obtain an upper bound on Pareto regret that exhibits the same order and thus matches the lower bound. Without such knowledge, we develop a reward-doubling version of Poly-INF that adapts to this unknown quantity while still attaining the matching minimax rate. Another implication is that it has no extra \(\log T\) factor and is independent of the number of coordinates.

</details>


### [182] [When Does Adversarial Refinement Help? A Negative Result and Open Problem in Adapting R3GAN to Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23102)
*Yufeng He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文报告一个负面结果：即便采用训练稳定的R3GAN，对抗式细化在多变量时间序列插补上仍未提供有效增益，作者呼吁用匹配的去判别器消融来确认因果贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型与Transformer已取代GAN成为多变量时间序列插补主流方法，主要因GAN训练不稳定。R3GAN（NeurIPS 2024）通过正则化相对论损失和可证明收敛性消除了这一不稳定性，于是引出一个自然问题：稳定、现代的GAN能否让对抗式插补重新焕发生机。

Method: 将R3GAN适配到一维时序数据，采用由粗到细的细化框架与频域判别器；审计3个数据集上的14个已保存配置（含均值/零起点、非legacy线性起点、legacy异常及Weather对比），并做了重建权重扫描。作者强调这些是异质单次运行，证据属描述性而非匹配因果消融。

Result: 五个已保存的均值/零起点配置提升48.4–70.2%；八个合格的非legacy线性起点配置平均变化为-0.7%（范围-3.0%至+1.1%）；另有一个-21.9%的legacy日志异常被保留用于溯源但不计入汇总。在已保存的Weather对比中，独立R3GAN-1D比BRITS差5.8倍。

Conclusion: 作者认为"GAN优化分布目标而非逐点目标"这一常见解释并不充分，因为扩散模型同样优化分布目标却能达到SOTA插补。重建权重扫描与对抗信号惰性或有害相一致，但无法识别其因果贡献；匹配的去判别器消融是关键下一步实验。作者将一个学习到的判别器为何无法提供有效细化梯度（而学习到的扩散去噪器却能）列为开放问题，并给出对抗式细化何时值得使用的实践建议。

Abstract: Diffusion models and transformers have supplanted GANs for multivariate time series imputation, largely on grounds of GAN training instability. R3GAN (NeurIPS 2024) removes that instability via regularized relativistic losses with provable convergence, raising a natural question: do stable, modern GANs revive adversarial imputation? We adapt R3GAN to 1D temporal data with a coarse-to-fine refinement framework and a frequency-domain discriminator, and audit 14 saved configurations across 3 datasets. Because these are heterogeneous single runs, the evidence is descriptive rather than a matched causal ablation. We report a negative result. All five saved mean/zero-start configurations improve by 48.4-70.2%. Among eight eligible non-legacy linear-start configurations, the mean change is -0.7% (range -3.0% to +1.1%); a separate -21.9% legacy logging anomaly is retained for provenance but excluded from that aggregate. In a saved Weather comparison, standalone R3GAN-1D underperforms BRITS by 5.8x. Crucially, we argue the common explanation (that GANs optimize distributional rather than point-wise objectives) cannot be the whole story, since diffusion models also optimize distributional objectives yet achieve state-of-the-art imputation. Our saved reconstruction-weight sweep is consistent with the adversarial signal being inert or harmful, but cannot identify its causal contribution; a matched discriminator-removed ablation is the key next experiment. We frame the precise reason a learned discriminator fails to provide useful refinement gradients (where a learned diffusion denoiser succeeds) as an open problem, and offer practical guidance on when adversarial refinement is worthwhile.

</details>


### [183] [Whitening Inverts the Hierarchy: What the Norm of a Whitened Embedding Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23117)
*Mohammed Ahnouch,Lotfi Elaachak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 白化嵌入的平方范数并非高斯似然，而是语义非典型性的马氏度量：其半径系统性过离散，高斯尾阈值可错达数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机：修正对"白化后坐标近似标准正态，故平方范数可作无训练似然代理"的流行观察，指出该观察仅由投影中心极限定理导出，不能推出联合高斯性。

Method: 方法：跨多种编码器与三种训练目标，比较白化半径与高斯参考（含均值协方差相同的分布克隆）的过离散；证明经验范数统计与理论一致是样本内白化的代数结果；并提出用两个矩估计主导单尺度，无额外自由参数地预测不相交谱半区之间的交叉依赖。

Result: 结果：发现系统性过离散；机制为白化反转编码器谱层级，使平方范数贡献转向近退化、以噪声为主的方向，其主导变异由单一输入依赖尺度控制；该尺度预测跨谱半区依赖性；统计量与非参数密度估计一致，但高斯尾阈值可达数量级偏差。

Conclusion: 结论：平方白化范数应解释为语义非典型性的马氏度量而非对数似然，这同时解释了其实际有效性与校准失败。

Abstract: Whitening a foundation-model embedding and using its squared norm as a training-free likelihood surrogate is motivated by the observation that whitened coordinates often appear approximately standard normal. We show that this observation follows from the projection central limit theorem and therefore does not imply a Gaussian joint distribution. Across multiple encoders and three training objectives, we find systematic over-dispersion of the whitened radius relative to the Gaussian reference, including against distributional clones with identical mean and covariance. We further show that the commonly reported agreement between empirical and theoretical norm statistics is an algebraic consequence of in-sample whitening and does not constitute evidence for Gaussianity.
  We identify the mechanism behind this behavior: whitening reverses the encoder's spectral hierarchy, shifting the contribution to the squared norm toward near-degenerate directions that encode predominantly noise. In these directions, the dominant variability is governed by a single input-dependent scale. We estimate this scale from two moments and use it to predict, without additional free parameters, the cross-dependence between disjoint spectral halves. These results indicate that the squared whitened norm is better interpreted as a Mahalanobis measure of semantic atypicality than as a log-likelihood. This interpretation explains both its practical effectiveness and its calibration failures: the statistic can rank and detect atypical samples consistently with nonparametric density estimates and across encoders trained with different objectives, while Gaussian tail thresholds can be inaccurate by orders of magnitude. etc.

</details>


### [184] [Perplexity Cost Understates What Activation Quantisation Breaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23125)
*Anish Sathyanarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 困惑度作为激活量化的聚合指标，无法揭示量化对不同计算能力的差异性损伤：在困惑度仅上升1.2–1.5倍时，归纳能力仍保持0.959，而检索能力已降至0.554。该差距由误差方向（坐标对齐）而非幅度主导，旋转基可大幅恢复归纳能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活量化通常只用困惑度这类跨所有token平均的聚合指标来评价，作者质疑这种平均值能否识别量化具体损害了哪些计算，因此考察聚合信号与细粒度能力（归纳、检索）之间的关系。

Method: 在4个家族共12个模型上进行780次模型内比较，对比各臂困惑度与归纳、检索能力；用同幅度的高斯噪声对照和仅随机化量化误差符号的对照隔离幅度因素；在旋转基下进行单块干预量化，并在最高32B参数的两个额外模型及AWQ部署配置（激活4比特）上验证。

Result: 困惑度作为聚合信号较可靠：偏好更低困惑度的量化臂在98%左右的情况下保留更多归纳和检索。但困惑度仅升1.2–1.5倍时，归纳保持0.959而检索降至0.554。同幅度高斯噪声和仅翻转误差符号几乎无损，说明幅度不足以解释损伤。旋转基量化在3平均比特下单块干预将归纳从0.001恢复到0.980，检索恢复到0.694；端到端4平均比特下归纳0.968、检索0.534。

Conclusion: 困惑度目标只约束了作用于激活的变换的平均代价，本身并不能说明哪些计算得以存活；不同能力对量化误差方向的敏感度存在结构性差异。

Abstract: Activation quantisation is usually evaluated with an aggregate metric, perplexity, averaged over every token a model predicts. We ask whether that average identifies which computations a quantiser damages. Perplexity turns out to be a reliable aggregate signal: across 12 models from four families and 780 within-model comparisons, the arm perplexity prefers also retains more induction and more retrieval in all but 2.1 and 4.0 percent of cases respectively. But where perplexity has risen by only a factor of 1.2 to 1.5, induction still keeps 0.959 of its intact accuracy while retrieval has already fallen to 0.554, a gap the aggregate number does not surface. This gap has structure, not just size: a matched Gaussian-noise control of the same per-channel magnitude leaves it largely intact, and randomising only the sign of the quantisation error, every magnitude held fixed, is nearly as harmless, so magnitude alone does not explain the damage. Quantising in a rotated basis, which changes coordinate alignment without changing error magnitude, restores induction from 0.001 to 0.980 at three average bits per token in a single-block intervention, though retrieval recovers less completely at the same setting (0.694); end-to-end at four average bits, induction reaches 0.968 and retrieval 0.534. The pattern holds on two further models up to 32B parameters and, in the deployed configurations we tested, under AWQ once activations are pushed to 4 bits. A perplexity target bounds the average cost of a transformation applied to the activation; it does not, by itself, show which computations survived.

</details>


### [185] [Provably Efficient Reinforcement Learning in Continuous-Time Episodic MDPs with Poisson Decision Epochs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23127)
*Kenny Guo,Valentio Iverson,Sahan Wijetunga,William Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究事件驱动、泊松决策时刻的连续时间回合制MDP，通过时间离散化利用局部Lipschitz平滑性，将UCRL与Q-learning推广至此设定，证明模型基与无模型算法均达到Õ(T^{2/3})遗憾，并给出匹配的Ω̃(T^{2/3})下界，首次为该类问题提供最优遗憾保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多真实强化学习问题在连续时间中演化，决策发生在不规则的、事件驱动的时刻而非固定离散步；现有理论多针对离散时间，缺乏对连续时间回合制MDP的紧致遗憾保证。

Method: 研究决策时刻由齐次泊松过程驱动、奖励与转移随时间平滑变化的回合制连续时间MDP；考虑每回合固定跳跃次数与固定时间预算（泊松决策次数随机）两种设定；在时间Lipschitz连续性假设下，通过离散化利用局部平滑性，将UCRL和Q-learning推广至该设定。

Result: 对模型基（UCRL）与无模型（Q-learning）算法均证明Õ(T^{2/3})遗憾界，并建立匹配的Ω̃(T^{2/3})极小极大下界，表明该速率在对数因子内最优。

Conclusion: 首次为具有泊松决策时刻的Lipschitz平滑连续时间回合制MDP提供紧致遗憾保证，所得T^{2/3}速率为该类问题的最优速率（至对数因子）。

Abstract: Many real-world reinforcement learning (RL) problems evolve in continuous time, where decisions occur at irregular, event-driven intervals rather than at fixed discrete steps. We study episodic continuous-time Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) in which decision epochs are governed by a homogeneous Poisson process and the reward and transition dynamics vary smoothly over time. We consider both a fixed number of jumps per episode and a fixed time budget with a random number of Poisson decision epochs. Under a Lipschitz continuity assumption in time, we exploit local smoothness through discretization and extend both UCRL (Auer and Ortner 2006) and Q-learning (Jin et al. 2018) to this setting, proving $\widetilde{O}(T^{2/3})$ regret bounds for both model-based and model-free algorithms. Finally, we establish matching $\widetildeΩ(T^{2/3})$ minimax lower bounds, showing that the rate is optimal up to logarithmic factors. These results provide the first tight regret guarantees for Lipschitz-smooth continuous-time episodic MDPs with Poisson decision epochs.

</details>


### [186] [Ask for Any Appliance: A Prompt-Programmable Foundation Model for Non-Intrusive Load Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23146)
*Xudong Wang,Jiacheng Cui,Junyu Xue,Tongxin Li,Guoming Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FM4NILM——一个可通过提示编程的单一基础模型，从总电表读数估计指定电器的功率轨迹，无需为每个电器训练专用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NILM依赖电器专属模型和固定输出清单，扩展覆盖成本高。作者希望用一个共享模型，通过提示和示例灵活扩展电器覆盖范围。

Method: 采用轻量级、感知采样节奏的Transformer，在来自7个公开语料库、采样间隔1-60秒的645k序列上以掩码重建预训练；用观测掩码损失对部分标注家庭进行电器请求对齐；采用Bernoulli-lognormal解码器将活动检测与条件功率估计分离。

Result: 在REDD、UK-DALE、REFIT的留出家庭和时间段上，一个冻结的文本提示模型服务12个电器-语料请求，取得0.556事件F1、0.625 AUPRC和最低活跃窗口MAE（251.8 W）；流式分数聚合将F1提升至0.582（60秒延迟）；加入10个激活示例使微波炉AUPRC从0.132升至0.214。

Conclusion: 单一共享模型即可实现有竞争力的分解性能，支持通过提示和示例而非新增专用网络来扩展电器覆盖，具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Non-intrusive load monitoring (NILM) estimates appliance-level consumption from a whole-home meter, but appliance-specific models and fixed output inventories make coverage costly to extend. We present FM4NILM (Foundation Model for NILM), a single prompt-programmable model that estimates a requested appliance's power trajectory from aggregate measurements, a natural-language description, and optional activation exemplars. A lightweight cadence-aware transformer is pretrained by masked reconstruction on 645k sequences from seven public corpora spanning 1-60 s sampling intervals, then aligned with appliance requests using observation-masked losses for partially labeled households. A Bernoulli-lognormal decoder separates activity detection from conditional power estimation. On held-out households and time periods from REDD, UK-DALE, and REFIT, one frozen text-prompted model serves twelve appliance-corpus requests, achieving 0.556 event F1, 0.625 AUPRC, and the lowest active-window MAE (251.8 W) among seven appliance-specific baselines. Streaming score aggregation raises event F1 to 0.582 with a 60 s aggregation delay. In a separate category-held-out evaluation, adding ten activation exemplars raises microwave AUPRC from 0.132 to 0.214 without parameter updates. Input-intervention ablations probe the model's dependence on appliance requests and aggregate measurements. These results demonstrate competitive disaggregation with one shared model and support extending appliance coverage through prompts and examples rather than additional specialist networks.

</details>


### [187] [K-TRAIL: Simulator-Guided Generative Design of EM/RF Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23183)
*Piyush Saha,Evan Newell,Hanna O'Leary,Arun Natarajan,Alireza Aghasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: K-TRAIL是一个仿真器引导的生成式框架，结合扩散生成与无导数集合卡尔曼引导，用于射频/电磁电路的自动化逆向设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RF/EM电路逆向设计的难点在于：电路布局与电学响应之间关系非唯一，且全波仿真计算成本高昂。现有方法通常依赖伴随灵敏度或可微求解器，限制了实用性。

Method: K-TRAIL将基于扩散的布局生成与无导数集合卡尔曼引导相结合，利用黑盒EM仿真器的反馈在生成过程中优化候选布局，无需伴随灵敏度或可微求解器模型。框架支持两种模式：从给定S参数响应进行合成，以及直接从RF性能约束进行合成。

Result: 在多层RFIC结构上的实验表明，仿真器引导的生成能提升与目标响应的匹配度，并能发现满足电路级设计要求的、结构上不同的布局方案。

Conclusion: 该方法为生成式、验证感知的RF电路设计提供了一条实用路径，同时保留了探索多样化布局拓扑的灵活性。

Abstract: Inverse design of RF and electromagnetic (EM) circuits is challenging because the relationship between circuit layout and electrical response is non-unique, and full-wave simulation is computationally expensive. This paper presents K-TRAIL, a simulator-guided generative framework for automated EM/RF circuit synthesis. K-TRAIL combines diffusion-based layout generation with derivative-free ensemble Kalman guidance, allowing feedback from a black-box EM simulator to refine candidate layouts during generation without requiring adjoint sensitivities or differentiable solver models. The framework supports both synthesis from prescribed S-parameter responses and synthesis directly from RF performance constraints. Experiments on multi-layer RFIC structures show that simulator-guided generation improves agreement with target responses and can identify structurally distinct layouts that satisfy circuit-level design requirements. The proposed approach provides a practical path toward generative, verification-aware RF circuit design while retaining the flexibility to explore diverse layout topologies.

</details>


### [188] [Neural Residual Modeling for Scientific Data Compression under Guaranteed Error Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23185)
*Surya Majumder,Liangji Zhu,Sanjay Ranka,Anand Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种后处理流水线，将RVQ潜在空间压缩与U-Net像素空间残差校正及GAE误差保证结合，用于科学仿真数据的有损压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RVQ等潜在空间压缩架构在科学数据压缩中有效，但其残差建模完全在潜在空间进行，未处理重建的像素空间误差结构，导致重建误差未被充分利用或修正。

Method: 在RVQ压缩器后增加一个U-Net，预测并校正原始体数据与RVQ重建之间的像素空间残差；随后通过Guaranteed Autoencoder（GAE）阶段，将剩余残差投影到逐块PCA基上，以强制满足用户指定的块级误差界。

Result: 在S3D、JHTDB和E3SM数据集上，相比仅RVQ及标准残差校正基线，NRMSE和压缩比均获得一致改进，同时保持科学数据保真所需的严格误差保证。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在同一框架中结合潜在空间RVQ、基于深度空间网络的显式像素空间残差校正以及GAE误差界保证，证明像素空间残差具有空间结构，需深度空间模型而非简单统计校正。

Abstract: Lossy compression of scientific simulation data increasingly relies on learned, latent-space architectures such as Residual Vector Quantization (RVQ), which iteratively quantize a base representation and its residuals to progressively reduce reconstruction error. While effective, RVQ performs this residual modeling entirely in latent space, leaving the pixel-space error structure of the reconstruction largely unaddressed. In this work, we propose a post-processing pipeline that augments an RVQ-based compressor with a U-Net trained to predict and correct pixel-space residuals between the original volume and its RVQ reconstruction. We show that these residuals are spatially structured rather than driven by local intensity or gradient features, motivating the need for a deep spatial model rather than simple statistical correction. The U-Net-corrected reconstruction is then passed through a Guaranteed Autoencoder (GAE) stage, which projects the remaining residual onto a per-block PCA basis to enforce a user-specified block-wise error bound. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first pipeline to combine latent-space RVQ, explicit pixel-space residual correction via a deep spatial post-processing network, and GAE-based error-bound guarantees within a single framework for scientific data compression. We evaluate our approach on S3D, JHTDB and E3SM datasets, demonstrating consistent improvements in NRMSE, compression ratio] over RVQ-only and standard residual-correction baselines, while maintaining strict error guarantees required for scientific data fidelity.

</details>


### [189] [Triggers and Diagnostics for LLM-Based Interpretability Failures in Active Inference Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23215)
*Param Raval,Rohit Shenoy,Archana Vaidheeswaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文审计了自主智能体所配LLM解释器的"解释"本身，发现在观测流被污染、智能体行为出错及观测元数据被植入攻击文本三种情况下，解释器均产生流畅却错误的解释，且架构上缺乏真实性校验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM解释器被越来越多地作为运行时监督手段挂载到自主智能体上，操作员依赖生成的自然语言解释而非智能体内部状态进行判断。然而现有工作很少审计解释本身是否真实，因此作者希望系统地检验解释器在故障与攻击下的可信度。

Method: 将跟踪德国电网需求并调整发电的Active Inference（AIF）智能体与LLM解释器配对，在三种后端（GPT-4o、Claude-3-Opus、Gemini）上运行，并用三种黑盒触发方式探测：污染观测流、在智能体采取客观错误动作的时间步上测试、以及在观测元数据字段注入攻击者控制文本。评估解释是否在既定评分标准下标记问题，以及是否出现谄媚性合理化与数据外泄。

Result: 每步污染观测流600 MW使智能体后验偏移490 MW（约占电网容量的0.9%），但注入期间生成的30条解释无一按评分标准标记异常，且都流畅地叙述了被污染的信念；在智能体采取客观错误动作的时间步上，三种解释器有80–95%的概率产生谄媚性合理化（每后端n=20）；观测元数据中的攻击者文本可操纵解释器，易感程度因提供商而异，且在三种后端上数据外泄均成功。

Conclusion: 在所观察的每一种失效情形中，解释都是流畅但错误的，且解释器架构中没有任何机制在操作员据其行动前检查解释是否真实。因此，对智能体部署进行审计时必须包含对解释器的测试；作者针对每种失效提出了缓解措施但未进行评估。

Abstract: LLM explainers are increasingly attached to autonomous agents as runtime oversight, with operators reading a generated account of the agent's beliefs and actions rather than its internal state. We audit the account itself, pairing an Active Inference (AIF) agent that tracks German grid demand and adjusts generation with an LLM explainer on three backends (GPT-4o, Claude-3-Opus, Gemini), and probing the pair with three black-box triggers. Corrupting the observation stream by 600 MW per step moves the agent's posterior by 490 MW, roughly 0.9% of grid capacity. None of the 30 explanations produced during the injection flag anything under a stated rubric, and each narrates the corrupted belief fluently. On timesteps where the agent takes an objectively wrong action, all three explainers produce a sycophantic rationalization 80-95% of the time (n = 20 per backend). Attacker-controlled text in the observation metadata field steers the explainer, with susceptibility differing by provider and data exfiltration succeeding on all three. We propose mitigations for each failure but do not evaluate them. In every failure we observed, the explanation was fluent and wrong. Moreover, nothing in the explainer architecture checks whether an explanation is true before an operator acts on it. Testing the explainer therefore belongs in any audit of an agentic deployment.

</details>


### [190] [Causal Inference with Unobserved Confounding: A Mixture Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23219)
*Mansi Sood,Devavrat Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文从混合学习视角处理观察性因果推断中的未观测混杂问题，将潜在混杂因子视为导致观测数据出现混合结构的异质性来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未观测混杂是观察性数据因果推断的根本挑战，需要新的识别与估计思路。

Method: 在合适的结构与可识别性假设下，恢复混合分布与成分机制，从而估计干预分布与因果效应量；以伯努利混合变体为例，联系面板数据中的潜在因子模型与合成干预；进一步考虑具有相依结果轨迹的高维指数族混合，从反事实均值扩展到反事实分布建模。

Result: 建立了混合学习与因果推断之间的桥梁，将高维混合学习进展与可扩展的因果效应识别和估计相连接。

Conclusion: 该视角与处理未观测混杂的互补方法形成对照，同时为混合学习提出新的挑战。

Abstract: Unobserved confounding is a fundamental challenge in causal inference from observational data. This article develops a mixture-learning perspective, viewing latent confounders as sources of heterogeneity that induce mixture structure in observed data. Under suitable structural and identifiability assumptions, recovering the mixing distribution and component mechanisms enables estimation of interventional distributions and causal estimands. Using variants of Bernoulli mixtures as a running example, we contextualize mixture-learning techniques and their structural assumptions, and connect them to causal inference in panel-data settings, including latent factor models and synthetic interventions.We then consider high-dimensional exponential-family mixtures with dependent outcome trajectories, moving beyond counterfactual means to model counterfactual distributions. We situate this perspective relative to complementary approaches for unobserved confounding. Together, these ideas provide a bridge between mixture learning and causal inference, connecting recent advances in high-dimensional mixture learning to scalable identification and estimation of causal effects while raising new challenges for mixture learning.

</details>


### [191] [Proximal Residual Value Functions for Consistent Planning and Real-Time Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23242)
*Harrison Waldon,Carson Eisenach,Akhil Bagaria,Daniel Russo,Dominique Perrault-Joncas,Alisha Zachariah,Dean Foster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究两时间尺度决策系统，提出基于近端残差价值函数的端到端强化学习方法，用于学习规划层向实时优化器提供的连续价值函数，在库存放置应用中降低总路由与转运成本5.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在两时间尺度决策系统中，规划层需要周期性地向实时优化器提供连续价值函数以指导资源分配。如何学习这一函数并保证规划与执行时间尺度上决策的一致性，是核心挑战。

Method: 提出近端残差价值函数，将后决策库存的严格凸势函数与学习得到的凸残差相结合；该一般形式产生适定的优化层，支持端到端微分，同时保留用于实时执行的显式凸目标；并刻画了光滑价值函数产生跨时间尺度一致决策的充要条件。

Result: 在使用某大型电商零售商历史库存到达与需求模式的离线仿真中，学习到的近端残差价值函数相比历史生产系统代理，将总路由与转运成本降低5.0%。

Conclusion: 近端残差价值函数为两时间尺度决策系统提供了一种兼具凸优化结构与端到端可学习性的有效方法，并在库存放置应用中验证了其成本降低效果。

Abstract: We study two-timescale decision systems in which a planning layer periodically supplies a continuation-value function to a real-time optimizer that allocates arriving resources, with inventory placement as our motivating application. We propose an end-to-end reinforcement learning (RL) method for learning this function using \emph{proximal residual value functions}, which combine a strictly convex potential of post-decision inventory with a learned convex residual. This general form yields a well-posed optimization layer that supports end-to-end differentiation while preserving an explicit convex objective for real-time execution. We characterize the necessary and sufficient conditions under which a smooth value function yields decisions that are consistent across the planning and execution timescales. In an offline simulation using historical inventory arrival and demand patterns from a large e-commerce retailer, learned proximal residual value functions reduce total routing and transfer cost relative to a historical-production-system proxy by 5.0%.

</details>


### [192] [The Price of Self-Calibration: Exact Evidence Budgets and Manufactured Blind Sets in Adaptive Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23254)
*Abdou-Raouf Atarmla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文精确刻画了自校准监控器为保证任意漂移下长期误报率而付出的代价，给出三条精确定律：保证是一个与被检测故障无关的核算恒等式；证书自身的波动服从精确律，窗口报警率标准差为 O(1/L) 而非二项式的 O(1/√L)；任何容忍漂移类 D 的监控器都对 D−D 中的故障盲视。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自校准监控器能在任意漂移下在线调整阈值以保证预设的长期误报率，但这一保证的代价此前未被精确量化。作者希望计算出该保证的"价格"，并为每一条定律给出确切的适用域。

Method: 通过两个证据恒等式对在线分位数追踪器进行精确成本分析：高度为 δ 的持续阶跃和斜率为 c 的斜坡分别给出精确的超额报警质量与速率。推导窗口报警率波动的精确常数（无拟合参数）。用一个刻意的两点论证证明漂移类 D 下的盲视性，并给出精确的高斯投影界。

Result: 长时间误报率保证是与检测内容无关的核算恒等式。阶跃 δ 在一个报警内产生 δ/η 的超额报警质量，当 δ 为增益 η 的格点时路径上恰为 δ/η；斜坡 c 产生恰好 c/η 的平稳超额率，边界 c=η(1−α) 与报警率上限一致。窗口报警率标准差为 O(1/L)，闭式常数无需拟合即可验证。基于二项式标度的检测器误校准 √(ηφ(q₀)L)，正确校准使检测窗口从逆故障速度的二次变为线性。容忍漂移类 D 的监控器对 D−D 中所有故障盲视；速度有界类的盲集恰为加倍速度类；追踪器吸收由其增益固定的速度类。精确高斯投影界比 Pinsker 更紧且永不失效。

Conclusion: 自校准保证并非免费：它是与被检测对象无关的核算恒等式，其成本可通过精确证据恒等式给出。证书波动遵循 O(1/L) 而非二项式标度，常见校准因此错误。容忍某漂移类的监控器必然对相应差类盲视，且在认证机制下可能自行"制造"漂移类。这些结果为监控器的设计与校准提供了精确的适用域和界。

Abstract: Self-calibrating monitors adapt their threshold online to guarantee a prescribed long-run false-alarm rate under arbitrary drift. We compute the price of that guarantee, stating every law with its exact domain of validity. First, the guarantee is an accounting identity, insensitive to what the monitor is meant to detect. Two evidence identities make the cost exact for the online quantile tracker: a persistent step of height $δ$ yields excess alarm mass within one alarm of $δ/η$, and exactly $δ/η$ pathwise when $δ$ is a lattice multiple of the gain $η$; a ramp of slope $c$ yields a stationary excess rate of exactly $c/η$, independent of accumulated size, up to a boundary $c=η(1-α)$ coinciding with the alarm-rate cap. Second, the certificate's own fluctuation obeys an exact law: the windowed alarm rate has standard deviation of order $1/L$, not the binomial $1/\sqrt{L}$, since the windowed mass telescopes to a difference of a tight internal state; the closed-form constant is validated with no fitted parameter. Detectors calibrated on the binomial scale are miscalibrated by $\sqrt{η\varphi(q_0)L}$, and correct calibration turns detection windows from quadratic to linear in the inverse fault speed. Third, any monitor required to tolerate a drift class $\mathcal{D}$ is blind, at any horizon and for any rule, to every fault in $\mathcal{D}-\mathcal{D}$; the proof is a deliberately elementary two-point argument and the contribution is the object it identifies: for speed-bounded classes the blind set is exactly the doubled-speed class, and the tracker absorbs a speed class fixed by its own gain, so that under a certification regime declaring absorbed drift normal, the monitor manufactures $\mathcal{D}$. An exact Gaussian projection bound, sharper than Pinsker and never vacuous, quantifies power outside it.

</details>


### [193] [CTRL: Control-Based Time Series Forecasting with LLM-Guided Residual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23257)
*Minkyoung Kim,Daeun Ji,Yohan Lee,Beomsoo Kim,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CTRL框架，将语义推理与定量预测解耦：冻结骨干模型生成基础预测，专用LLM智能体作为控制器分析误差的分解成分并输出紧凑控制信号，由轻量残差解码器转换为预测修正，并支持无标签测试时自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的时间序列预测方法存在两类问题：一是把LLM降级为数值预测器，绕过了其推理优势；二是让LLM直接生成预测，在非平稳环境下导致预测不稳定。因此需要一种既能利用LLM推理能力又能保持预测稳定性的方法。

Method: CTRL将语义推理与定量预测解耦。冻结骨干模型生成基础预测；专门化的LLM智能体充当控制器，通过将骨干预测误差分解为趋势、季节和不规则成分来分析误差，使推理建立在可解释的时间结构上。每个智能体输出紧凑的控制信号，由轻量级残差解码器翻译为预测修正。此外引入无标签测试时自适应，仅从输入统计量检测分布偏移，并借助缓存仅用3-24次LLM调用重新调整控制信号。

Result: CTRL在非平稳时间动态和分布偏移下提升了鲁棒性，同时在高度平稳的时间序列上仍保持竞争力，此时自适应修正带来的额外收益有限。

Conclusion: CTRL通过解耦语义推理与定量预测，结合残差修正与高效测试时自适应，为LLM在非平稳时间序列预测中的应用提供了更鲁棒且高效的范式。

Abstract: Time series forecasting underpins critical decision-making across diverse domains. While large language models (LLMs) offer promising reasoning capabilities, existing LLM-based time series forecasting approaches either reduce them to numerical predictors that bypass their strengths, or allow direct forecast generation that destabilizes predictions in non-stationary settings. We introduce CTRL, a framework that decouples semantic reasoning from quantitative prediction. A frozen backbone generates base forecasts, while specialized LLM agents function as controllers that analyze backbone prediction errors through decomposed trend, seasonal, and irregular components, grounding reasoning in interpretable temporal structure. Each agent outputs compact control signals that a lightweight residual decoder translates into forecast corrections. CTRL incorporates label-free test-time adaptation that detects distribution shift from input statistics alone and readapts control signals with only 3-24 LLM calls via caching. CTRL is explicitly designed to improve robustness under non-stationary temporal dynamics and distribution shift, while remaining competitive on highly stationary time series where adaptive correction provides limited additional benefit.

</details>


### [194] [Why Ghost Outputs Teach: A Kernel-Based Understanding of Subliminal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23260)
*Zhe Li,Bicheng Ying,Chaosheng Dong,Haibo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从学习动力学角度对Subliminal Learning（SL）现象进行机理分析，提出"链式跨任务核"解释幽灵输出监督如何通过共享骨干表示影响任务预测，并验证了三个理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SL现象中，学生模型通过匹配看似无关的辅助输出即可获得下游任务能力，但幽灵信号究竟存在于何处已被研究，其背后的优化机制仍不清楚。作者希望弥补这一理论空白。

Method: 推导链式跨任务核（chained cross-task kernel），将幽灵输出监督与任务预测变化通过共享骨干表示显式关联；建立统一分析框架，对SL的三个核心经验谜题给出数学解释。

Result: 理论给出三点结论：(i) 共享初始化下迁移算子构成严格半正定结构，保证幽灵输出优化使学生对齐教师真实任务目标；(ii) 幽灵输出维度作为显式秩瓶颈控制任务相关特征迁移；(iii) 合成高熵输入作为宽带探针最大化跨任务核重叠，解释随机噪声优于结构化数据。实验验证全部三项预测。
