Conclusion: 该工作首次基于学习动力学为幽灵输出监督如何产生潜意识学习提供了理论解释，验证了上述三个理论预测。

Abstract: Subliminal Learning (SL) is a recently identified phenomenon in which a student model acquires downstream task capabilities by matching seemingly unrelated auxiliary outputs from a teacher, despite never observing task labels, task-specific outputs, or the original training data. While recent studies have identified where subliminal signals may reside, the optimization mechanism underlying this phenomenon remains poorly understood. In this work, we provide a mechanistic understanding of SL through the lens of learning dynamics. Specifically, we derive a chained cross-task kernel that explicitly links ghost-output supervision to changes in task predictions through shared backbone representations. Our unified analytical framework provides a rigorous mathematical explanation for three central empirical puzzles in SL: (i) under shared initialization, the transfer operator forms a strictly Positive Semi-Definite (PSD) structure, guaranteeing that ghost-output optimization aligns the student with the teacher's true task objective without explicit label exposure; (ii) the ghost-output dimensionality acts as an explicit rank bottleneck governing the transfer of task-relevant features; and (iii) synthetic, high-entropy inputs function as broadband probes that maximize cross-task kernel overlap, explaining why random noise consistently outperforms structured data for subliminal transfer. Experiments on the canonical ghost-output setting validate all three theoretical predictions, providing the first learning-dynamics-based theoretical explanation of how ghost-output supervision gives rise to subliminal learning.

</details>


### [195] [Optimal No-Regret Learning for Repeated Prophet Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23265)
*Kun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究前缀反馈下的重复先知不等式问题，提出高效算法实现 $\widetilde O(\sqrt{T})$ 期望遗憾，匹配下界并解决 Liu et al. (2025) 开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前缀反馈的重复先知不等式设定下，学习者每轮依固定顺序遇到来自 $n$ 个未知分布箱子的新值，只能观察到停止箱子之前的前缀，需要不可撤销地接受一个值。既有方法对箱子数 $n$ 存在多项式依赖，Liu et al. (2025) 留下如何消除该依赖的开放问题。

Method: 算法直接通过近最优策略进行探索，结合经验反向归纳（empirical backward induction）与箱子专属的到达奖励（box-specific reach bonuses）；并采用相对下降聚合规则（relative-drop aggregation rule），利用观测前缀的嵌套结构来保持探索，从而消除对箱子数 $n$ 的多项式依赖。

Result: 实现了 $\widetilde O(\sqrt{T})$ 期望遗憾，与下界在对数因子内匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作有效解决了前缀反馈下重复先知不等式问题中对箱子数的多项式依赖，达到了近乎最优的遗憾界，回答了 Liu et al. (2025) 提出的开放问题。

Abstract: We study repeated prophet inequalities under prefix feedback. In each of $T$ rounds, a learner encounters fresh values drawn independently from $n$ boxes with unknown $[0,1]$-supported distributions in a fixed order and must irrevocably accept one, observing only the prefix up to its stopping box. Regret is measured against the optimal stopping policy that knows the distributions. We give an efficient algorithm achieving $\widetilde O(\sqrt{T})$ expected regret, matching the lower bound up to logarithmic factors. Our algorithm explores directly through near-optimal policies, combining empirical backward induction with box-specific reach bonuses. A relative-drop aggregation rule then exploits the nesting structure of observed prefixes to preserve exploration, thereby removing the polynomial dependence on the box number $n$. This resolves an open question posed by Liu et al. (2025).

</details>


### [196] [Optimal Multi-way Decision Trees for Stratified Sampling in Online Controlled Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23308)
*Tomoka Takei,Shunnosuke Ikeda,Yuichi Takano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OMST（最优多路分层树）框架，用基于最优多路决策树的优化分层方法改进分层抽样，在不增加样本量的前提下提升A/B实验的统计功效，在真实与模拟数据上实现与现有方法相当或更优的方差缩减，并保持浅层可解释的分层树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线A/B实验的核心挑战是在不扩大实验样本量的前提下提高实验灵敏度（统计功效）。分层抽样是经典方差缩减技术，但其效果高度依赖分层结构（strata）的构造方式，而现有分层构造方法效果有限。

Method: 将分层问题建模为特征图上的路径选择问题；用精确的方差最小化二元优化公式求解所选路径，并在连续比例分配与Neyman型最优分配下优化；引入监督式最优分箱为数值特征生成与结果相关的候选切分；同时提出冗余候选路径与分配约束的约简方法，大幅缩小优化问题规模。

Result: 在真实数据集与模拟数据集上，OMST取得了与现有方法相当或更优的方差缩减效果，同时保持浅层且可解释的分层树。

Conclusion: OMST通过优化多路决策树构造分层，有效提升了分层抽样的方差缩减与实验灵敏度，且保持可解释性，为A/B实验的分层设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Online controlled experiments, or A/B tests, are widely used to estimate causal effects on digital platforms. A central challenge is to improve experimental sensitivity, or statistical power, without increasing the experimental sample size. Stratified sampling is a classical variance reduction technique; however, its effectiveness depends critically on how the strata are constructed. We thus propose an optimization-based stratification framework for stratified sampling using optimal multi-way decision trees. Our method, called Optimal Multi-way Stratification Trees (OMST), formulates stratification as a path-selection problem over a feature graph. The selected paths define interpretable stratification rules and are optimized using an exact variance-minimizing binary optimization formulation under continuous proportional allocation and a Neyman-type optimal allocation. We incorporate supervised optimal binning to generate outcome-relevant candidate splits for numerical features. Furthermore, we introduce reduction procedures for redundant candidate paths and assignment constraints, substantially reducing the optimization problem size. Experiments on both a real-world and a simulated dataset demonstrate that OMST achieves comparable or superior variance reduction to existing methods while maintaining shallow and interpretable stratification trees.

</details>


### [197] [ValueDiff: Value-Geometric KV Cache Eviction for Sink-Suppressed LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23314)
*Junyoung Park,Jungwook Choi,Mingu Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ValueDiff，一种基于 value 向量几何结构的 KV cache 淘汰方法，在 sink 较弱的现代 LLM 上优于依赖 attention sink 的现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 LLM 采用 QK-normalization、gated attention、learned attention sinks 或 logit softcapping 后，persistent attention sinks 变弱，而现有 KV cache 淘汰方法主要依赖这些 sink，因此效果下降。作者观察到 sink 变弱时，value 向量的离散度相对 key 向量更大，从而激发从 value 侧几何结构设计淘汰策略。

Method: ValueDiff 根据每个 token 的 value 向量与 cache 均值之间的 L2 偏差进行排序淘汰；该分数在关于未来 attention 的最大熵假设下等价于最小扰动淘汰。评测在固定 cache 预算下进行，prefill 阶段每个 block 边界淘汰一次，generation 阶段每步解码淘汰一次。

Result: 在 RULER 的 2k token 预算下，ValueDiff 在七个 sink-suppressed 模型上保留 dense 的 88–99%，其中 6/7 为最佳；在 LongBench 的 4k 预算下平均保留 92%，强于最强基线 83%；在 MATH-500 的 25% cache 预算下，是每个 sink-suppressed 模型上最强的非 dense 方法，在 gated-attention 模型上比先前方法高约 20 分。

Conclusion: 在三个基准上，value 几何结构是 sink-suppressed 模型中更可靠的、query-invariant 的淘汰信号。

Abstract: Modern LLMs with QK-normalization, gated attention, learned attention sinks, or logit softcapping exhibit weaker persistent attention sinks, on which existing KV cache eviction methods primarily rely. We observe that across these models, weaker sinks co-occur with greater value-vector dispersion relative to key-vector dispersion. Motivated by this value-side dispersion, we present ValueDiff, a value-geometric eviction that ranks tokens by the L2 deviation of their value vectors from the cache mean. The same score arises as the minimal-disturbance eviction under a max-entropy assumption about future attention. We evaluate under fixed cache budgets, with eviction at every block boundary during prefill and at every decoding step during generation. On RULER at a tight 2k token budget, ValueDiff retains 88--99\% of dense across seven sink-suppressed models (best on 6 out of 7). On LongBench at the 4k budget, ValueDiff averages 92\% retention across sink-suppressed models versus 83\% for the strongest prior baseline. On MATH-500, ValueDiff is the strongest non-dense method on every sink-suppressed model tested at the 25\% cache budget, outperforming prior methods by up to $\sim$20 points on gated-attention models. Across all three benchmarks, value geometry emerges as the more reliable query-invariant eviction signal for sink-suppressed models.

</details>


### [198] [CSC: Calibrated Simplicity for Conflict-Aware Social Bot Detection in the LLM Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23320)
*Yipeng Qian,Pengjie Zhao,Chaoxi Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CSC框架，通过简化原型引导图专家、校准单纯形约束融合和轻量不一致专家，解决LLM时代社交机器人检测中的模态冲突问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型使社交机器人文本语义伪装变得廉价、流畅且可扩展，仅靠文本难以检测。账号可能在语义上像人类，但在图结构、档案属性或跨模态一致性上仍可疑，形成模态冲突。现有图检测器通过增加图侧复杂度应对，但实验表明复杂度本身并非解决冲突的最可靠方式。

Method: 提出CSC（校准简洁性）框架，包含三部分：1）简化的原型引导图专家，保留有用结构偏置并去除不稳定图侧启发式；2）校准的单纯形约束融合，在后期融合前对齐异构置信空间；3）轻量不一致专家，建模跨模态分歧。

Result: 在TwiBot-22、TwiBot-20和MGStBot-large上，CSC提升校准工作点决策质量，并在外部基准上保持竞争力。分析显示校准提升置信可靠性，不一致专家主要在高冲突或近阈值区域提供局部修正，简化图侧控制带来更好的稳定性-成本权衡。语义伪装压力测试表明，将部分机器人文本替换为匹配的人类文本会显著降低独立文本专家性能，而图证据和融合证据在平衡挑战集上保持稳定。

Conclusion: 在LLM时代，冲突感知的社交机器人检测不必依赖复杂图侧设计；通过校准简洁性框架整合简化图专家、校准融合和不一致建模，可更可靠地处理模态冲突并提升决策质量。

Abstract: Social bot detection is essential for protecting online platforms from misinformation amplification, coordinated manipulation, and distorted public discourse. However, large language models have made social bots much harder to detect from text alone because semantic camouflage is now cheap, fluent, and scalable. The resulting challenge is modality conflict: an account may look human-like in semantics while remaining suspicious in graph structure, profile attributes, or cross-modal consistency. Recent graph-based detectors tackle this limitation by adding graph-side complexity, such as sparse prototype selection, adaptive gating, or architecture-specific control logic, yet our experiments suggest that complexity alone is not the most reliable way to resolve such conflict.
  We therefore propose CSC, a calibrated-simplicity framework for conflict-aware LLM-era social bot detection. The framework combines three design choices: a simplified prototype-guided graph expert that retains useful structural biases while removing unstable graph-side heuristics, calibrated simplex-constrained fusion that aligns heterogeneous confidence spaces before late fusion, and a lightweight inconsistency expert that models cross-modal disagreement. Experiments on TwiBot-22, TwiBot-20, and MGStBot-large show that \textsc{CSC} improves calibrated operating-point decision quality while remaining competitive across external benchmarks. Further analyses show that calibration improves confidence reliability, the inconsistency expert mainly provides localized corrections in high-conflict or near-threshold regions, and simplified graph-side control yields a better stability-cost trade-off. A targeted semantic-camouflage stress test further shows that replacing selected bot text with matched human text sharply degrades the standalone text expert while leaving graph and fused evidence stable on a balanced challenge set.

</details>


### [199] [Rethinking Class Imbalance for Single-Cell Foundation Models: A Systematic Benchmark Across Architectures and Long-Tail Loss Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23325)
*Zeyu Dong,Jiahui Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对三种单细胞基础模型、六个长尾损失函数、三个数据集进行162次受控训练，系统基准测试了长尾损失函数在细胞类型分类中的有效性，揭示了罕见细胞类别的两种失败模式及其嵌入几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞基础模型达到高达97.5%的细胞类型分类准确率，但总体准确率可能掩盖对稀有、疾病相关细胞群的系统性失败；而长尾损失函数被广泛认为能解决此问题，却缺乏系统验证。

Method: 在三种架构（scGPT、scBERT、Geneformer）和三个数据集（多发性硬化症、Zheng68K、人类胰腺）上，系统基准测试六种长尾损失函数（交叉熵、加权CE、类别平衡损失、焦点损失、LDAM、logit调整softmax），共进行162次受控训练（3骨干×3数据集×6损失×3随机种子）。

Result: 在全部九个（架构、数据集）设置中，普通交叉熵下总体准确率、Macro-F1与稀有类召回率之间的差距一致存在，由数据集结构而非预训练驱动。稀有类失败分为两种机制：部分类别可通过合适损失恢复；另一些虽保有线性可分性，却在所有损失和架构下被吸收进无关类别的邻域。可恢复类别的重加权效果由训练集绝对大小预测，而非其数据占比或整体不平衡比。类别平衡损失和LDAM在九种设置中最一致；logit调整以牺牲稀有类精确率换取召回率。

Conclusion: 提供了可复用的基准测试及基于机制的实际指南，用于将基础模型与不平衡生物数据相结合，强调应超越总体准确率、关注稀有类别的失败机制。

Abstract: Single-cell foundation models (scGPT, scBERT, Geneformer) achieve cell-type classification accuracy up to 97.5% in our experiments, yet this aggregate accuracy can mask systematic failure on rare, often disease-relevant cell populations that long-tail loss functions are widely assumed to address. We present a systematic benchmark of six long-tail loss functions (cross-entropy, weighted CE, class-balanced loss, focal loss, LDAM, logit-adjusted softmax) across three architectures and three datasets (Multiple Sclerosis, Zheng68K, human Pancreas), totaling 162 controlled training runs (3 backbones x 3 datasets x 6 losses x 3 seeds). The gap between overall accuracy, Macro-F1, and rare-class recall under plain cross-entropy is consistent across all nine (architecture, dataset) settings, driven by dataset structure rather than pretraining. Rare-class failure itself splits into two regimes with distinct embedding-geometry signatures, visible before any loss is chosen: some classes are recoverable by the right loss, while others retain linear separability yet are absorbed into unrelated classes' neighborhoods under every evaluated loss and architecture. Among the recoverable classes, the efficacy of reweighting is predicted by a class's absolute training-set size, rather than its share of the dataset or the dataset's overall imbalance ratio. Class-balanced loss and LDAM are the most consistent choices across all nine settings, while logit adjustment trades rare-class precision for recall rather than improving both. Our results give both a reusable benchmark and mechanism-grounded practical guidelines for combining foundation models with imbalanced biological data.

</details>


### [200] [A Patient World Model for Early Forecasting of Digital Health Campaign Outcomes: Capabilities and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23333)
*Yunlong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将数字化DTC健康营销的过程内预测建模为动态系统问题，提出一种紧凑的患者世界模型，通过学习暴露条件下的潜在状态动态与周度转化风险，向前滚动预测转化曲线，在真实数据集上实现了显著优于基线方法的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字化直达消费者（DTC）健康营销活动通常只能在事后进行效果测量，而过程内预测普遍依赖针对每个截断点和预测期限单独训练的独立分类器，这种做法效率低、泛化差。作者希望将这一任务重新构建为动态系统问题，以获得更紧凑、更统一的预测框架。

Method: 构建紧凑的患者世界模型：为每位患者维护一个潜在状态，联合学习暴露条件驱动的状态动态与周度转化风险（hazard），并向前滚动生成未来转化曲线。同时引入"密集的下一次暴露监督"作为辅助目标（由Fisher信息分析提供理论依据）。在含147,173名患者、520万风险人周的真实美国营销活动数据集上做回顾性评估，并与池化风险梯度提升模型等基线比较。

Result: 在记录的未来暴露条件下进行回顾性评估：从第4周截断起预测剩余第52周新品牌处方量的相对误差为2.9%，第8–26周截断为0.8–2.6%；最强非循环基线（池化风险梯度提升，采用相同生存滚动与信息）误差为13.6–33.1%，各期限独立分类器表现更差。移除辅助目标后处方量误差上升约2–14倍，但在更常见的专科就诊结局上无明显劣势。情景模拟显示，关闭全部未来暴露会使预测转化率由0.31升至0.89，符合观察性数据中的选择效应模式，说明暴露条件下的滚动预测难以作因果解释。

Conclusion: 患者世界模型将DTC营销预测统一为动态系统问题，预测精度远超现有基线，且密集下一暴露监督对稀疏转化事件至关重要。但情景模拟结果表明暴露条件下的滚动预测存在因果解释的局限，需谨慎对待。

Abstract: Digital direct-to-consumer (DTC) health campaigns are usually measured after the fact. In-flight forecasting commonly relies on a separate classifier for every cutoff and horizon. We treat this task as a dynamic-system problem and build a compact patient world model. The architecture maintains a latent state per patient, learns exposure-conditioned state dynamics jointly with a weekly conversion hazard, and rolls forward into future conversion curves. We evaluate it on a US campaign dataset with 147{,}173 patients and 5.2 million at-risk person-weeks. In a retrospective evaluation conditioned on recorded future exposures, the model forecasts the remaining new-to-brand prescription volume through week 52 with a relative error of 2.9\% from a week-4 cutoff and 0.8--2.6\% from cutoffs at weeks 8--26. The strongest non-recurrent baseline, a pooled-hazard gradient boosting model given the same survival rollout and information, has relative errors of 13.6--33.1\%. Per-horizon classifiers perform substantially worse. A Fisher-information analysis motivates dense next-exposure supervision when conversions are rare. Removing this auxiliary objective increases prescription-volume error by approximately $2$--$14\times$, while providing no consistent disadvantage on the more common specialist-visit outcome. We also evaluate scenario simulation. Switching all future exposure off raises predicted conversion from 0.31 to 0.89, a pattern consistent with selection effects in observational exposure data. This result highlights the limits of interpreting exposure-conditioned rollouts causally.

</details>


### [201] [What Can a Recurrent State Safely Forget?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23366)
*Linzhe Zhang,Changming Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过"预测商"(predictive quotient)刻画循环模型记忆与稳定性的内在冲突，给出精确校正的离散-连续边界，并提出可审计的有限未来框架，用高概率证书保证审计语义失真受限，同时证明探针采样复杂度O(M·Ω^{-(k+2)})的极小极大最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环模型需保留影响未来行为的信息，同时抑制隐状态误差，二者存在冲突：收缩提升稳定性，但沿"未来可区分方向"的收缩会破坏记忆。作者希望形式化这一边界，并为实际部署中的记忆保真提供可审计、可验证的保证。

Method: 1) 定义预测商：若两隐状态诱导相同条件未来则等价，等价类构成"预测纤维"；证明任何保持语义的精确校正器在该商上为恒等映射，在隐维d、预测维k的正则点处至多消除d-k个独立方向。2) 提出可审计有限未来框架：部署库W对独立审计库A(W⊆A)在声明的校正域上评估；在生成式探针访问与审计度量覆盖下，用有限随机rollout给出间隔Ω_{W|A}(δ)的高概率证书。3) 通过完整性模将保证扩展到连续未来。4) 开展安全优先的受控实验。

Result: 建立了离散-连续边界：有限预测状态存在正半径的精确校正盆地，而不可数连续的可区分未来状态无法在有限维欧氏空间中经受任意正半径扰动后被解码。探针复杂度为O(M·Ω^{-(k+2)}) (M=|A|)，并给出匹配的极小极大下界证明该指数最优。实验验证了认证间隔、缩放律与自动探针细化。

Conclusion: 保留认证间隔内的W-预测可保证有界的审计语义失真。该工作为循环模型的记忆-稳定性权衡提供了理论刻画与可审计的验证方法，其探针复杂度具最优性，并在实验中得以验证。

Abstract: Recurrent models must preserve information that changes future behavior while suppressing hidden-state error. These objectives conflict: contraction improves stability, but contraction along a future-distinguishing direction destroys memory. We formalize this boundary through the predictive quotient of a recurrent state space. Two hidden states are equivalent when they induce the same conditional future; their equivalence classes form predictive fibers. Every exact semantics-preserving corrector acts as the identity on this quotient. At a regular point with hidden dimension d and predictive dimension k, it can eliminate at most d - k independent directions. This establishes a discrete-continuous boundary: finite predictive states admit positive-radius exact correction basins, whereas an uncountable continuum of future-distinguishable states cannot be decoded after arbitrary positive-radius perturbations in finite-dimensional Euclidean space.
  To operationalize this principle, we develop an auditable finite-future framework. A compact deployment bank W is evaluated against an independent audit bank A (W subseteq A) on a declared correction domain. Under generative probe access and audit-metric coverage, finite stochastic rollouts furnish a high-probability certificate for the separation margin Omega_{W|A}(delta). Preserving learned W-predictions within this certified margin guarantees bounded audit-semantic distortion. For intrinsic audit dimension k, the required probe outcomes scale as O(M * Omega^{-(k+2)}), where M = |A|; a matching minimax lower bound proves this exponent is optimal. Extending guarantees to continuous futures is achieved via an explicit completeness modulus. Controlled experiments validate the certified margins, scaling laws, and automated probe refinement under a safety-first evaluation paradigm.

</details>


### [202] [The Evidence Ladder for Reinforcement Learning in Healthcare: From Retrospective Policies to Trusted Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23374)
*Yunfan Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文综述医疗保健强化学习（RL），提出以"证据阶梯"（从问题建模到生命周期监测）为组织框架，强调历史数据高分不等于临床获益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗RL综述多按算法或临床应用分类，难以回答"证据是否足以支持常规干预"这一关键问题；作者希望用证据成熟度的视角揭示"回顾性表现好≠改善照护"的鸿沟。

Method: 采用文献综述/框架构建方法，将医疗RL研究按六个证据层级组织：问题建模、回顾性识别、策略估计、压力测试、前瞻性评估、生命周期监测；并梳理每一层级的假设与失效模式，分析证据的可迁移性；将 restless bandits 作为特例而非组织框架，最后提出面向累积性评估的报告规范建议。

Result: 综述连接了临床治疗、患者参与和卫生系统运营三个领域，系统识别了各证据层级的假设与失效模式，明确了哪些证据可跨场景迁移、哪些不可，并给出累积评估的报告实践建议。

Conclusion: 核心结论是：医疗RL应被视为嵌入不断变化的社会技术系统中的一种干预来评估，而不只是回顾性奖励的优化器；需以证据阶梯推动从"分数高"走向"真正改善照护"的累积性证据。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) offers a natural language for healthcare decisions whose conse- quences unfold over time, yet most reported progress remains far from routine intervention. Ex- isting surveys organize the field by algorithm or clinical application. We instead review healthcare RL through an evidence ladder: problem formulation, retrospective identification, policy estima- tion, stress testing, prospective evaluation, and lifecycle monitoring. This view connects clinical treatment, patient engagement, and health-system operations while exposing a recurring gap: evi- dence that a policy scores well in a historical dataset is not evidence that it will improve care. We synthesize the assumptions and failure modes at each rung, identify what evidence can and can- not transfer across settings, and propose reporting practices for cumulative evaluation. Restless bandits are included as one special case, not as the organizing framework. The central lesson is that healthcare RL should be evaluated as an intervention embedded in a changing sociotechnical system, rather than only as an optimizer of a retrospective reward.

</details>


### [203] [Discovering Physical Representation Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23381)
*Linzhe Zhang,Changming Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"物理表示-语言发现"问题：从匿名受控实验中直接恢复隐藏本体（载体与微分序列、测量类型与取向扭转、非可逆细化语义、原对偶Maxwell图及残余等价），并给出可识别性理论与多项式时间构造程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器在发现物理定律之前，必须先发现其测量对象是什么：哪些观测存在于格点上、哪些是强度量或广延量、哪些扇区是对偶的、哪些区别只是规范。现有科学机器学习是在人类给定的语言中学习定律，但语言本身需要被发现。该工作旨在建立观测可识别性的精确理论极限，把科学ML从"用人类语言学定律"推进到"发现使定律可表达的语言"。

Method: 1) 形式化"物理表示-语言发现"问题：从匿名受控实验中恢复隐藏本体。2) 给出可识别性理论与构造性多项式时间算法，可恢复：载体与微分序列、测量类型与取向扭转、非可逆细化语义、原对偶Maxwell图、以及任何允许实验都无法打破的残余等价。3) 理论技术：将材料冗余转化为交换子（commutant），用细化把量与坐标分离，先恢复表示再选择物理。4) 对经认证的有限实验族，证明端到端两阶段测量界及在维度、精度、置信度上匹配的极小极大速率。

Result: 盲Maxwell实验在规则与无结构载体上、且观测被联合污染时，能恢复完整的原/相对对偶本体；独立的无结构RLC系统表明结论不限于Maxwell。框架可扩展至每载体数万个格点；压力审计显示在非马尔可夫记忆、非线性、非局域性和复杂本构迟滞等严酷物理机制下仍具鲁棒性。公开FDTD审计显示从不完整场数据中涌现出匿名旋度结构，并刻画了完整恢复所需的信息前提。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了从匿名实验中发现物理表示语言，并给出端到端两阶段测量界与匹配极小极大速率，建立了观测可识别性的精确理论极限；在Maxwell与RLC系统上验证了普适性，并在多种严酷物理机制下保持鲁棒，推动科学ML从"在人给语言中学定律"转向"发现使定律可表达的语言"。

Abstract: Before a machine can discover a physical law, it must discover what its measurements are: which observations live on cells, which are intensive or extensive, which sectors are dual, and which distinctions are merely gauge. We introduce physical representation-language discovery, the problem of recovering this hidden ontology directly from anonymous controlled experiments. We give an identifiability theory and constructive polynomial-time procedure that recovers a carrier and differential sequence, measurement types and orientation twist, noninvertible refinement semantics, primal-dual Maxwell diagrams, and the residual equivalences that no permitted experiment can break. The theory turns material nuisance into a commutant, uses refinement to separate quantities from coordinates, and selects physics only after its representation has been recovered. For a certified finite experiment family, we prove an end-to-end two-stage measurement bound and a matching minimax rate in dimension, accuracy, and confidence. Blind Maxwell experiments recover complete primal/relative-dual ontologies on regular and unstructured carriers under jointly corrupted observations; an independent unstructured RLC system demonstrates that the result is not specific to Maxwell. The framework scales to tens of thousands of cells per carrier, while stress audits demonstrate robustness across severe physical regimes - including non-Markovian memory, nonlinearities, nonlocality, and complex constitutive hysteresis. A public FDTD audit demonstrates the emergence of anonymous curl structure from incomplete field data, while characterizing the informational prerequisites for complete recovery. The goal is to move scientific ML from learning laws in a human-supplied language to discovering the language in which laws become expressible, establishing exact theoretical limits on observational identifiability.

</details>


### [204] [Blind Thermodynamic Ontology Discovery from Anonymous Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23387)
*Linzhe Zhang,Changming Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从"匿名"受控实验中自动发现隐藏的热力学本体（外延/内蕴变量、接触配对、稳定势与储库拟阵），提出可辨识性理论与多项式时间构造算法，并证明残余观测等价类形式，在模型与真实流体数据上验证其鲁棒性与迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在学习热力学状态方程前，必须先弄清测量通道的物理含义：哪些随系统规模标度、哪些是内蕴共轭量、扇区如何通过接触配对、何种势决定稳定性。当传感器只能给出外延态与内蕴响应的未知线性混合时，被动观测无法把物理量与坐标假象分开，因此需要一个从匿名受控实验出发发现该"热力学本体"的理论框架。

Method: 提出可操作的可辨识性理论，并给出多项式时间构造算法：通过复制对比（replication contrasts）提取外延与内蕴标度扇区；借助热接触与互易性恢复其对偶余切配对；用离散循环凹性验证全局容许的凹势；确定储库系综的不变拟阵。同时证明残余观测等价性定理。

Result: 证明残余观测等价严格形如 (x, λ) ~ (A x, a A^{-T} λ + β)，给出任何允许实验都无法突破的观测极限。在 van der Waals 流体与 Curie-Weiss 磁体的盲评测中，算法在病态混合条件下仍能稳健恢复，正确解析匿名的 Maxwell 共存线并排除非平衡延续。对 NIST WebBook 六个真实流体的外部验证表明，本体发现可跨真实物质迁移且无坐标泄漏。

Conclusion: 热力学本体可以仅从匿名受控实验中被操作性地、可辨识地发现，且其残余模糊性恰由 (A, a, β) 变换刻画；该框架在合成模型与真实流体上均有效，说明方法具有跨物质的可迁移性与实际可用性。

Abstract: Before a machine learning model can learn a thermodynamic equation of state, it must discover what its measurements represent: which channels scale with system size, which are intensive conjugates, how sectors pair through contact, and which potential governs stability. When sensors expose only an unknown linear mixture of extensive states and intensive responses, passive observations cannot disentangle physical quantities from coordinate artifacts. We formulate the problem of discovering this hidden thermodynamic ontology directly from anonymous controlled experiments. We present an operational identifiability theory and a constructive polynomial-time algorithm that extracts extensive and intensive scaling sectors from replication contrasts, recovers their dual cotangent pairing from thermal contact and reciprocity, verifies a globally admissible concave potential via discrete cyclic concavity, and determines an invariant matroid of reservoir ensembles. We prove that the residual observational equivalence is strictly (x, lambda) ~ (A x, a A^{-T} lambda + beta), establishing the sharp observational limit that no permitted experiment can break. Blind evaluations on van der Waals fluids and Curie-Weiss magnets confirm robust recovery under ill-conditioned mixing, correctly resolving anonymous Maxwell tie-lines while rejecting non-equilibrium continuations. External validation across six real fluids from the NIST WebBook demonstrates that operational ontology discovery transfers across real physical substances without coordinate leakage.

</details>


### [205] [Tool-Augmented On-Policy Distillation for LLM Domain Adaptation in Sequence-Based Omics Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23435)
*Jie Ying,Zhefan Wang,Zihong Chen,Zhengqing Li,Jinzhe Li,Gang Li,Jian Liu,Fang Hu,Tao Luo,Zhonghang Yuan,Wanli Ouyang,Stan Z. Li,Fan Yang,Nanqing Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OmicsBench与TA-OPD，前者是首个面向多组学序列的推理评测基准，后者是一种对齐序列预测与证据推理的后训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组学序列蕴含复杂生物模式，但现有评测仅依赖分类与回归指标，无法判断模型是否真正掌握底层生物学证据，可能掩盖捷径学习问题。

Method: 构建OmicsBench：含1,160个专家验证问题，覆盖DNA调控、RNA加工、蛋白功能三类共六个任务，要求可追溯证据链，并使用领域专家制定的实例级评分标准进行评估；在此基础上提出工具增强的在线策略蒸馏（TA-OPD），将序列预测与基于证据的生物学推理对齐，并在五个0.8B–27B参数的Qwen3.5模型上验证。

Result: 评测17个LLM发现：科学专用LLM在序列分类准确率上优于通用LLM，但无法提供有效证据支撑其预测；TA-OPD在五个模型规模上均持续增强生物学证据接地，并在多数任务上提升预测性能。

Conclusion: 更强的序列推理并非仅由模型容量增加带来，而可通过证据感知训练改善；OmicsBench与TA-OPD共同为诊断多组学LLM的推理失败提供了框架，并指向预测更有生物学依据的模型路径。

Abstract: Multi-omics sequences contain complex biological patterns, yet deciphering their mechanisms for automated scientific discovery remains challenging. As large language models (LLMs) interpret these sequences, evaluating both predictions and scientific reasoning is critical. However, existing benchmarks for multi-omics sequence tasks rely on classification and regression metrics, neglecting whether models grasp the underlying biological evidence. We introduce OmicsBench, the first reasoning benchmark for multi-omics sequences, comprising 1,160 expert-validated questions across six tasks spanning DNA regulation, RNA processing, and protein function. OmicsBench requires traceable evidence chains, evaluated using instance-specific rubrics developed with domain experts. Evaluating 17 LLMs reveals an inverse relationship: while scientific LLMs outperform general-purpose LLMs in sequence classification accuracy, they fail to provide valid evidence to support their predictions. One plausible interpretation is shortcut learning: specialized models may rely on statistical patterns rather than the biological mechanisms needed for scientific discovery. Motivated by this finding, we introduce tool-augmented on-policy distillation (TA-OPD), a post-training method to align sequence prediction with evidence-grounded biological reasoning. Across five Qwen3.5 models spanning 0.8B to 27B parameters, TA-OPD consistently strengthens biological evidence grounding while improving predictive performance on most tasks. These gains persist across model scales, indicating that stronger sequence reasoning does not arise solely from increased model capacity, but can be improved through evidence-aware training. Together, OmicsBench and TA-OPD provide a framework for diagnosing reasoning failures in multi-omics LLMs and a path toward models whose predictions are better grounded in biologically meaningful evidence.

</details>


### [206] [RLVR$^{2}$: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rubric-based Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23457)
*Hao Li,Zhengkun Zhang,Gangqiang Hu,Zhen Zhang,Yude Gao,Dai Dai,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RLVR²，用可验证的排序范式替代线性加权聚合，解决多维度评分标准下奖励标量化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR正从数学、代码等有明确正确性信号的任务扩展到多维度rubric描述的质量要求。由于策略优化每次rollout只消耗一个标量，rubric流程必须把多个criterion分数映射为标量奖励。这一聚合常被视为简单的分数缩放，但实际上隐含决定了训练中各质量维度之间的权衡方式。主流做法（各criterion归一化后线性组合）假设不同criterion的基数分差可比、且一个维度的收益可补偿另一维度的失败；当criterion语义异质时这两个假设都不可靠。

Method: 提出Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rubric-based Ranking（RLVR²），一种面向rubric-based RLVR的可验证排序范式。对每个criterion，将rubric分数转换为组内序数结果，从比较矩阵中恢复潜在效用，再将各效用合并为单一训练信号。由于只保留组内排序、丢弃原始分数尺度，避免了异质rubric量纲的校准问题。还支持保持目标的属性调整：与观测排序相关但非训练目标的辅助属性可进入估计，而无需扩展rubric或直接对其奖励。

Result: 在三种模型规模和16个基准上，RLVR²一致优于代表性rubric-based基线，在每个规模下大多数基准取得最佳整体表现。分析表明它能在保持质量目标的同时，控制与推理效率和回复格式相关的系统性效应。

Conclusion: 将rubric分数聚合视为权衡决定而非简单缩放，用组内序数比较与潜在效用恢复，可在不校准异质量纲的前提下得到可验证的训练信号，并支持辅助属性的隐式纳入，在多规模多基准上验证有效。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is expanding from tasks with well-defined correctness signals, such as mathematics and code, toward multifaceted quality requirements specified by multi-dimensional rubrics. Since policy optimization consumes one scalar per rollout, rubric-based pipelines must map multiple criterion scores into a scalar reward. This aggregation is often treated as score scaling, but it implicitly determines how quality dimensions trade off during training. The prevailing practice, normalizing each criterion and taking a linear combination, assumes that cardinal score differences are comparable across criteria and that gains on one criterion compensate for failures on another; both assumptions are unreliable when criteria are semantically heterogeneous. We propose Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rubric-based Ranking (RLVR$^2$), a verifiable ranking paradigm for rubric-based RLVR. For each criterion, RLVR$^2$ converts rubric scores into criterion-specific within-group ordinal outcomes, recovers a latent utility from the resulting comparison matrix, and merges these utilities into one training signal. By retaining only within-group ordering and discarding raw score magnitudes, RLVR$^2$ avoids calibrating heterogeneous rubric scales. It further supports objective-preserving attribute adjustment: auxiliary attributes that correlate with observed rankings but are not training objectives can enter the estimation without expanding the rubric or rewarding them directly. Across three model scales and 16 benchmarks, RLVR$^2$ consistently outperforms representative rubric-based baselines, achieving the best overall performance on most benchmarks at every scale. Analysis shows it controls systematic effects tied to reasoning efficiency and response formatting while preserving the quality objective.

</details>


### [207] [TRACE: Tractable Routing Autoencoder for Clinical ECG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23460)
*Shunbo Jia,Runze Ma,Haonan Lyu,Haijin Zhang,Qiang Yang,Caizhi Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE通过预先由领域知识指定32维临床潜空间并分区路由，实现可审计的ECG诊断，性能超越无约束分类器和千万级预训练基础模型，参数量仅约八分之一，且不损失macro-F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最精准的ECG深度学习模型（千万级预训练基础模型）缺乏决策路径可审计性：临床医生无法将诊断追溯到某一生理通路，也无法对通路进行干预。因此需要一个既可解释又可干预的自动化ECG诊断框架。

Method: 提出TRACE（Tractable Routing Autoencoder for Clinical ECG）。其32维临床潜空间由领域知识预先指定而非优化发现，划分为灌注、结构、传导三个子空间，每个子空间按设计路由至独立诊断头，并用正交性惩罚正则化分区；解码器支持潜变量扰动以重建ECG。在PTB-XL与Georgia上评估，并用线性探针冻结特征与ECG基础模型比较；在无结构类的九标签CPSC2018上仅重设路由表迁移；进行联合探针、擦除和扰动分析验证路由契约。

Result: 在PTB-XL和Georgia上超过无约束分类器并领先于千万级预训练ECG基础模型（线性探针），参数量约八分之一；CPSC2018上仅改路由表即可迁移。扰动去极化和复极化通路可调节重建波形。移除指定分区及正交惩罚在PTB-XL损失1.70 AUC和11.30 macro-F1，Georgia损失2.76 AUC和16.92 macro-F1。容量匹配的置换控制在0.34 AUC内接近本体路由，macro-F1统计持平（p=0.619）。

Conclusion: 本体路由的TRACE在不牺牲macro-F1的前提下提供了决策路径可审计性，并具备跨队列迁移能力和通路级可干预性。

Abstract: Deep learning has advanced automated electrocardiogram (ECG) diagnosis, but the field's most accurate models, foundation models pretrained on millions of recordings, are not decision-pathway auditable: a clinician cannot trace a diagnosis to a physiological pathway or intervene on one. We propose TRACE, a Tractable Routing Autoencoder for Clinical ECG, whose 32-dimensional clinical latent space is specified in advance from domain knowledge rather than discovered by optimization. TRACE partitions this space into perfusion, structure, and conduction subspaces, routes each to its own diagnostic head by design, regularizes the partition with an orthogonality penalty, and reconstructs the ECG through a decoder that permits latent perturbation. On PTB-XL and Georgia, TRACE exceeds unconstrained classifiers and stays ahead of an ECG foundation model pretrained on ten million recordings, evaluated by linear probe on frozen features, at roughly an eighth of the parameter count. On the nine-label CPSC2018 cohort, which carries no structural class, the framework transfers with only the routing table re-specified to a perfusion/rhythm/conduction partition. Joint probe, erasure, and perturbation analyses verify the routing contract, and perturbing the depolarization and repolarization pathways modulates the reconstructed waveform. Removing the specified partition and its orthogonality penalty costs 1.70 AUC and 11.30 macro-F1 points on PTB-XL, and 2.76 AUC and 16.92 macro-F1 points on Georgia. A capacity-matched permutation control places arbitrary assignments within 0.34 AUC points of the ontology routing and leaves macro-F1 statistically level (p=0.619): the ontology supplies decision-pathway auditability at no macro-F1 cost.

</details>


### [208] [ITSY: Causal Discovery From Irregular Time-Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23516)
*Wenbo Xu,Yue He,Yunhai Wang,Yueguo Chen,Kun Kuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ITSY：首个针对不规则时间序列的连续优化因果发现方法，在缺失数据下联合补全与学习同期和滞后因果图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列结构因果模型虽能恢复同期与滞后效应，但大多要求完整观测窗口，样本缺失时会产生模型误设。

Method: 在线性模型下提出ITSY，重构造结构方程，用最近可用历史进行预测而非可能缺失的当前切片，并联合补全缺失值、同时学习两个图；采用加权重构目标纠正重构造引入的噪声变换。

Result: 在变化缺失率、规模、图密度和噪声的合成场景及真实基准上，ITSY相对代表性SCM基线持续提升图恢复效果。

Conclusion: 为不规则线性一阶动力学提供了针对性解决方案，并明确了向非线性或高阶推广所需的假设。

Abstract: Structural causal models for time series recover contemporaneous and lagged effects, but most methods require complete observation windows and become misspecified when samples are missing. We introduce ITSY, the first continuous-optimization method for causal discovery from irregular time series under a linear model. ITSY reformulates the structural equation so that prediction uses the nearest available history rather than the possibly missing current slice, and jointly imputes missing values while learning both graphs. A weighted reconstruction objective corrects the noise transformation induced by this reformulation. Across synthetic regimes varying missingness, scale, graph density, and noise, and on a real world benchmark, ITSY consistently improves graph recovery over representative SCM-based baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed method. The results establish a focused solution for irregular linear first-order dynamics and clarify the assumptions required for nonlinear or higher-order extensions.

</details>


### [209] [Feature Suppression and Differential Privacy for Residential Traffic Classification: A Two-Home Federated Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23521)
*Márton Pál Lipcsey-Magyar,Adrian Pekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在模拟双客户端联邦学习下比较特征抑制(FS)与DP-SGD,发现FS-mild在合并宏F1与最差组F1上更优,且不构成正式隐私保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住宅流量分类有助于服务管理,但跨家庭学习需应对流量异构与隐私约束;隐私感知训练可能对各类别施加不均匀代价,需研究此权衡。

Method: 基于162万条网关采集的预处理流、六类别的模拟双客户端联邦学习;比较全特征基线、特征抑制(FS-mild,从16个输入中剔除4个时序特征)与固定记录级隐私设置下的DP-SGD,采用按规模比例聚合,在分层与时间划分、两种模型容量、五个随机种子上评估,并用基于损失与影子模型的成员推断探针检验。

Result: FS-mild在所有五个种子、两种容量及两种划分下,合并宏F1与最差组F1均高于DP-SGD;测试的DP-SGD配置对少数类别损失显著,尤其在较小家庭中;FS-mild相对全特征基线并非一致改善;分层划分模型上成员推断探针接近随机水平,但各探针排序不一致。

Conclusion: FS可作为一种输入最小化的基线,但不能替代正式隐私保证;近乎随机的成员推断结果不能证明隐私等价。

Abstract: Residential traffic classification supports service management, but learning across homes must account for heterogeneous traffic and privacy constraints. Privacy-aware training may impose uneven costs across traffic categories. We study this tradeoff in simulated two-client federated learning using 1.62 million preprocessed gateway-collected flows across six categories. We compare a full-feature baseline, feature suppression (FS), and differentially private stochastic gradient descent (DP-SGD) under one fixed record-level privacy setting. FS-mild excludes four timing features from 16 model inputs; it provides no formal privacy guarantee. With size-proportional aggregation, FS-mild achieves higher combined macro-F1 and worst-group F1 (the minimum per-class F1 across homes) than DP-SGD in all five seeds at both model capacities under stratified and temporal splits. The tested DP-SGD configuration incurs pronounced minority-category losses, especially in the smaller home, but FS-mild does not uniformly improve on the full-feature baseline. On stratified-split models, loss-based and shadow-model membership probes show near-chance aggregate discrimination without a consistent ranking across probes; this does not establish equivalent privacy. These findings support FS as an input-minimization baseline, not a substitute for formal privacy.

</details>


### [210] [Predicting Out-of-Distribution Generalization of Neural Operators via Observable Spectral Error Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23529)
*Hang-Cheng Dong,Pengcheng Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出结构保持框架，将神经算子参数化为谱滤波器，实现对分布外泛化的可预测与可审计评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子求解PDE虽强大，但在分布偏移下的可靠性成为部署障碍；现有OOD泛化方法多为经验性黑箱，仅报告聚合误差，无法解释误差成因或预测其增长时机。

Method: 将学习到的解算子参数化为作用在椭圆算子特征值上的谱滤波器h_θ(λ)，用Chebyshev多项式展开实现并以弱形式目标训练；该参数化可将能量范数误差精确分解为模型相关的谱近似项与输入诱导的分布相关谱加权项，由此导出三个诊断量：保守带内上确界ε_sup、全局RMS代理ε_rms、样本相关的有效度量ε_eff(f)，均无需真解即可计算。

Result: 通过四组受控实验表明，ε_eff(f)‖f‖在分布内、带内谱偏移、带外尾部及复合偏移情形下均能一致预测能量误差，而全局度量会产生系统性误导。

Conclusion: 该框架将神经算子的OOD评估从黑箱基准测试转向算子结构诊断，为可审计的科学机器学习提供实用路径。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as powerful surrogates for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), yet their reliability under distribution shift remains a critical barrier to deployment. Existing approaches to out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization in operator learning are largely empirical and black-box: they report aggregate error metrics without explaining why errors arise or when they will grow. We propose a structure-preserving framework that makes OOD generalization predictable and auditable. Our key idea is to parameterize the learned solution operator as a spectral filter $h_θ(λ)$ acting on the eigenvalues of the underlying elliptic operator, implemented via Chebyshev polynomial expansions and trained with a weak-form objective. This parameterization admits an exact decomposition of the energy-norm error into two observable components: a model-dependent spectral approximation term and a distribution-dependent spectral weighting term induced by the input. From this decomposition we derive three diagnostics: a conservative in-band supremum $\vareps_{\mathrm{sup}}$, a global RMS proxy $\vareps_{\mathrm{rms}}$, and a sample-dependent effective metric $\vareps_{\mathrm{eff}}(f)$. These diagnostics can be computed without access to ground-truth solutions. Through four controlled experiments, we show that $\vareps_{\mathrm{eff}}(f)\|f\|$ consistently predicts energy error under in-distribution, in-band spectral shift, out-of-band tail, and compound shifts, whereas global metrics can be systematically misleading. Our framework shifts OOD assessment of neural operators from black-box benchmarking to operator-structure diagnostics, providing a practical route to auditable scientific machine learning.

</details>


### [211] [Decoupled Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23535)
*Zhengkang Guan,Fei Wu,Kun Kuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出解耦因果发现（DCD），一种不依赖条件独立性检验、结构评分或函数假设的新视角，通过加权函数解耦非目标变量直接识别马尔可夫边界（MB），并利用MB内结构不对称性迭代构建CPDAG，理论上证明了可识别性、可靠性与完备性，在噪声环境下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测数据进行因果发现是科学研究中的基础且具挑战性的任务。现有方法主要依赖条件独立性检验、结构评分或限制性函数假设，存在局限性。作者希望提出一种不依赖上述方法的新范式。

Method: 提出解耦因果发现（DCD）：通过加权函数解耦非目标变量，使得在解耦分布下只有MB内的变量与目标保持依赖关系，从而直接识别马尔可夫边界；在此基础上，利用MB内部的结构不对称性迭代构建完全部分有向无环图（CPDAG）。

Result: 理论上建立了DCD的可识别性、可靠性与完备性；实证评估表明DCD性能强劲，尤其在具有挑战性的噪声环境中表现突出。

Conclusion: DCD提供了一种基于解耦的新因果发现视角，无需条件独立性检验、结构评分或函数假设，能够直接识别MB并构建CPDAG，兼具理论保证与在噪声场景下的优异实证表现。

Abstract: Causal discovery from observational data is a fundamental yet challenging task in scientific research. While existing approaches are primarily based on conditional independence tests, structure scores, or restrictive functional assumptions, we propose Decoupled Causal Discovery (DCD), a novel decoupling-based perspective that does not rely on these methodologies. DCD directly identifies the Markov boundary (MB) by decoupling non-target variables via weighting functions, such that only variables within the MB preserve dependence with the target under the decoupled distribution. Building on this, DCD iteratively constructs the Completed Partially Directed Acyclic Graph (CPDAG) by exploiting structural asymmetries within the MBs. We establish the theoretical identifiability, soundness, and completeness of DCD. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that DCD achieves strong performance, particularly excelling in challenging noise regimes.

</details>


### [212] [Preserving Geometric Integrity in Graph Prompting via Measure-Constrained Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23547)
*Xiangyu Wang,Shuo Wang,Ruiyi Fang,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MINT（Measure-INtegrity Transport），将图提示学习中的节点到提示分配建模为全局耦合的最优传输问题，通过熵正则化和指定的提示侧边际分布控制图级别的提示利用率，并给出精确的方差分解与冻结编码器前向映射的条件稳定性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图提示学习以轻量提示参数适配冻结的图神经网络。但随着路由变得节点自适应，独立优化的局部决策会共同把分配质量集中到有限共享提示库中的一小部分提示上，即使单个节点—提示匹配在局部仍合理。这种全局分配失衡需要一种全局耦合的分配视角来显式控制提示利用率。

Method: 将节点到提示的适配形式化为全局耦合的分配问题，采用熵正则化最优传输框架：传输代价偏好局部几何相容性，同时用预先规定的提示侧边际显式控制图范围的提示利用率。此外推导了精确的方差分解，将提示侧几何方差拆分为保留的提示更新变异与节点内重心离散度，并给出冻结编码器前向映射的条件稳定性界。

Result: 在标准引文网络及额外异质图上，MINT 在少样本适配中保持有竞争力。受控实验与端到端实验进一步区分了路由与拓扑的作用：固定边际路由控制图范围的提示利用率，并在引文网络上产生可测量的端到端效果；拓扑增强则提供互补的、依赖图的机制来应对结构失配。

Conclusion: MINT 通过全局最优传输与固定提示边际有效缓解了自适应路由导致的提示利用率集中问题，理论上刻画了方差来源与稳定性，实验上验证了少样本适配的竞争力，并厘清了路由与拓扑在其中的不同角色。

Abstract: Graph prompt learning enables parameter-efficient adaptation of frozen Graph Neural Networks to downstream tasks through lightweight prompt parameters. As routing becomes increasingly node-adaptive, however, independently optimized local decisions can collectively concentrate assignment mass on a small subset of a finite shared prompt bank, even when individual node--prompt matches remain locally meaningful. We propose MINT (Measure-INtegrity Transport), an entropically regularized optimal transport framework that formulates node-to-prompt adaptation as a globally coupled allocation problem. The transport cost favors local geometric compatibility, while a prescribed prompt-side marginal explicitly controls graph-wide prompt utilization. We further derive an exact variance decomposition that separates prompt-side geometric variance into retained prompt-update variation and within-node barycentric dispersion, together with a conditional stability bound for the frozen-encoder forward map. Across standard citation networks and additional heterophilic graphs, MINT remains competitive in few-shot adaptation. Controlled and end-to-end experiments further distinguish the roles of routing and topology: fixed-marginal routing controls graph-wide prompt utilization and has measurable end-to-end effects on citation networks, while topology augmentation provides a complementary, graph-dependent mechanism for addressing structural mismatch. Code is available at https://github.com/Ga1axy0051/MINT.

</details>


### [213] [Physics-residual machine learning predicts oxygen-evolution catalyst activity beyond the training range from sparse polarization measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23549)
*Yong-Woon Kim,Jihyeok Lee,Sungtae Park,Sooseok Choi,Yung-Cheol Byun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理残差机器学习方法，通过Tafel方程外推并结合学习残差与适用域评分，实现催化剂活性外推预测，显著降低外推误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合库筛选氧析出催化剂时，需要决定哪些候选物接受剩余测量，而决定性的活性位于候选物测量电位窗口之外、常高于拟合时记录的所有活性，因此需要对外推活性进行可靠预测。

Method: 物理残差机器学习：Tafel方程外推候选物自身测得的电流与斜率，学习到的残差按特征空间距离衰减以修正幅度，并用适用域评分在测量前识别超出训练范围的预测。

Result: 在独立制备的322候选库中（282个超出训练最大值），每个候选两次测量下平均绝对误差为0.203 mA cm$^{-2}$，而所选数据驱动机器学习模型为1.330；训练范围内的误差相当，35个已标注催化剂即可拟合；在两个独立数据集中，该构造使过电位误差降低29%至52%。

Conclusion: 该方法可缩短每次测量时间，同时仍能对最活跃组分进行排序，为催化剂筛选提供高效途径。

Abstract: Screening oxygen-evolution catalysts on combinatorial libraries requires deciding which candidates receive the remaining measurements. The deciding activity lies beyond each candidate's measured potential window and often above every activity recorded during fitting. We predict it by physics-residual machine learning: the Tafel equation extrapolates the candidate's own measured current and slope, a learned residual attenuated with feature-space distance corrects the magnitude, and an applicability-domain score identifies predictions above the training range before measurement. In a separately fabricated 322-candidate library, 282 above the training maximum, two measurements per candidate gave a mean absolute error of 0.203 mA cm$^{-2}$ against 1.330 for the selected data-driven machine-learning model. Errors inside the training range remained comparable, and 35 labelled catalysts were enough to fit it. In two independent datasets the same construction lowered the overpotential error by 29 to 52%. Campaigns can therefore shorten each measurement and still rank the most active compositions.

</details>


### [214] [Global Ranks Survive, Selected Heads Shift: BOS-Sink Topology under 4-bit Weight-Only Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23585)
*Kuanlin Chen,Chen-Wei Kuo,Cheng-En Ou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"注意力汇（sink）感知部署"捷径在4-bit NF4权重量化下的安全性：全局排名虽高度保持，但离散top-k头集合、层局部策略与跨域校准在量化后需重新验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sink-aware部署常假设量化前识别出的首token注意力头映射可在量化后直接复用到边缘端。作者旨在检验这一捷径在4-bit NF4权重-only PTQ下何时安全，即量化前后sink拓扑是否真正一致。

Method: 提出Sink Topology Consistency（STC）指标，将全局排名保持、top-k集合重叠和逐层sink质量偏移分离，并区分逐输入敏感性与校准映射迁移。在Qwen2.5-0.5B、Qwen2.5-1.5B、Llama-3.2-1B上评估4-bit NF4 PTQ、C4到LongBench的域迁移、匹配域重校准（n=8至32）及Jetson Orin NX上的设备端测量。

Result: 4096 token下全局bf16到4-bit排名保持很高（ρ_s≥0.980），但top-k Jaccard仅0.619-0.793（成员保持率76.5-88.5%）。全局统计掩盖局部失败：Qwen末端层偏移达模型均值6.2-7.9倍，Llama-3.2-1B则近乎均匀低漂移。跨域重叠退化比域内精度比较更严重（Qwen两模型），Llama无此现象。匹配域4-bit重校准在Qwen上n=8、Llama上n=32达分裂半稳定性平台90%；Qwen仅更新部分层无法达到全图稳定性标准。Jetson Orin NX上16样本工作负载仅需数秒。

Conclusion: 全局排名通常可迁移，但离散头集合、层局部策略和跨域校准应在量化后重新验证，不能直接复用量化前的sink映射。

Abstract: Sink-aware deployment may identify important first-token attention heads before a model is quantized, then reuse that map at the edge. We test when this shortcut is safe for 4-bit NF4 weight-only post-training quantization (PTQ). Our Sink Topology Consistency (STC) metrics separate global rank preservation, top-$k$ set overlap, and layerwise sink-mass shift, and distinguish per-input sensitivity from calibration-map transfer. Across Qwen2.5-0.5B, Qwen2.5-1.5B, and Llama-3.2-1B, global bf16-to-4-bit ranks remain high at 4,096 tokens ($ρ_s \geq 0.980$), yet top-$k$ Jaccard overlap is only 0.619-0.793, corresponding to 76.5-88.5% membership retention. The global statistic also masks local failures: terminal Qwen layers shift by 6.2-7.9x their model means, whereas Llama-3.2-1B shows low, nearly uniform drift. Under a C4-to-LongBench shift, cross-domain overlap degrades more than the within-domain precision comparison for both Qwen models, but not for Llama-3.2-1B. Matched-domain 4-bit recalibration reaches 90% of a split-half stability plateau at the smallest tested $n=8$ for both Qwen models and $n=32$ for Llama-3.2-1B, though not as a sharp threshold; for the two Qwen models, updating only selected layers does not reach the full-map stability criterion. On Jetson Orin NX, the 16-sample workload takes seconds for the two models with valid on-device sink measurements. The practical message is precise: global rankings often transfer, but discrete head sets, layer-local policies, and cross-domain calibration should be revalidated after quantization.

</details>


### [215] [Cost-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Action Masking and Projection for Battery Energy Storage Dispatch under Suppressed-Spread Market Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23590)
*Kuanlin Chen,Chen-Wei Kuo,Cheng-En Ou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于PPO的电池储能系统(BESS)调度控制器，将物理动作掩码与应急投影同因果预测驱动的经济咨询模块分离，并与代理成本MPC进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电池储能调度需在保证运行可行性的同时应对价差收窄导致的循环收益下降，因此需要将可行性约束与经济决策解耦并加以评估。

Method: 采用PPO控制器，物理动作预选掩码与应急投影独立于因果、基于预测的经济咨询；所有依赖预测的方法共享同一24步因果预测与电网侧结算。设置五种PPO随机种子，在T1、T2窗口及不相交的周块上对比日度、周度、混合季节性与持续性预测，并开展M0-M6消融实验以分离各层次作用。

Result: 五种种子下，开启咨询的PPO净收益为30.59与18.04 USD(每336小时T1与T2窗口)，低于代理成本MPC的36.77与22.94 USD。咨询使T2收益由16.45升至18.04 USD并降低吞吐量，但在T1无实质影响。PPO在日度、周度与混合季节预测下表现稳定，在持续性预测下减弱，且始终低于代理成本MPC。配对诊断定位变化集中于5-10 USD/MWh区间，SoC相关效应不一。消融显示去除掩码会向投影发送数千次不可行请求，同时去除两层物理层则暴露爬坡违规。

Conclusion: 研究将经济筛选与可行性执行相分离，但未声称形式化安全性、全生命周期最优老化控制或RL的全面优越性。PPO与代理成本MPC仍存在收益差距。

Abstract: Battery energy storage system (BESS) dispatch must preserve operational feasibility while declining price spreads reduce the margin available to pay for cycling. We study a proximal policy optimization (PPO) controller whose pre-selection physical action mask and emergency projection are separated from a causal, forecast-informed economic advisory. All forecast-dependent methods receive the same causal 24-step forecast and grid-side settlement. Across five PPO seeds, advice-on net profit is 30.59 and 18.04 USD per 336-hour T1 and T2 window, versus 36.77 and 22.94 USD for proxy-cost MPC; PPO remains below this reference in both periods. Advice raises T2 profit from 16.45 to 18.04 USD while reducing throughput, but is immaterial in T1. On disjoint weekly blocks, PPO is stable under daily, weekly, and blended seasonal forecasts, weakens under persistence, and remains below proxy-cost MPC. Paired diagnostics localize changes to the observed 5-10 USD/MWh regime with mixed SoC-dependent effects. An M0-M6 ablation shows that mask removal sends thousands of infeasible requests to projection, while removing both physical layers exposes ramp violations. The evidence separates economic screening from feasibility enforcement without claiming formal safety, lifecycle-optimal aging, or RL dominance.

</details>


### [216] [Bilinear Optimization Divergence: Diagnosing Factor-Constrained LoRA Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23594)
*YongShun Wang,JianLin Su,Yong Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过双线性优化发散（BOD）诊断框架，揭示LoRA因子正交性本身不足以说明组合更新保护了什么，并据此提出SFOR与WRP方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA因子中的正交性并不能单独确定组合更新究竟保护了什么，其效果取决于任务起始状态、参数化方式以及优化器实际位移。作者希望从机制上澄清"该施加何种约束、如何施加、如何解读其实证价值"这一架构依赖问题。

Method: 提出双线性优化发散（BOD），一种以锚点为参照、针对选定历史特征的有效更新响应诊断方法。有限步分析区分两种情形：共享适配器中保护路由位移会留下学习锚点残差；全新零输出块中可行路由状态可同时保护组合更新并保持当前因子可训练。由此导出SFOR（共享适配器）与当前块硬保护（累积O-LoRA），并用权重残差投影（WRP）在优化器步后强制所需位移。

Result: 受控双任务轨迹验证了预测的残差路径，共享族归一化历史响应从19.12%降至0.005%，累积族从7.72%降至0.002%。Qwen3-8B四任务实验中，SFOR将BWT从-2.47提升至-0.86且AA几乎不变；O-LoRA硬保护将三阶平均AA从80.27%提升至81.30%，FM从2.20降至0.43。组件控制还表明更严格的可行性未必改善最终任务性能。

Conclusion: 正交性只有结合起始状态、参数化与优化器位移才能界定其保护对象；不同架构需施加不同约束，且约束强度与最终性能并非单调关系。

Abstract: Orthogonality in a LoRA factor does not by itself specify what the composed update protects: the answer depends on the task-start state, the parameterization, and the realized optimizer displacement. We formalize this question through Bilinear Optimization Divergence (BOD), an anchor-relative diagnostic of effective-update response on selected historical features. The finite-step analysis distinguishes two cases. In a shared adapter, protecting the routing displacement leaves a learned-anchor residual through the changing companion factor. In a fresh zero-output block, a feasible routing state can protect the composed update while both current factors remain trainable. These conditions yield Semi-Frozen Orthogonal Routing (SFOR) for shared adapters and current-block hard protection for cumulative O-LoRA; Weight Residual Projection (WRP) enforces the required displacement after the optimizer step. Controlled two-task traces verify the predicted residual paths, reducing normalized historical response from 19.12% to 0.005% in the shared family and from 7.72% to 0.002% in the cumulative family. Four-task experiments on Qwen3-8B characterize the resulting trade-offs: SFOR improves backward transfer (BWT) from -2.47 to -0.86 with nearly unchanged average accuracy (AA), while O-LoRA hard protection improves three-order mean AA from 80.27% to 81.30% and forgetting measure (FM) from 2.20 to 0.43. Component controls also show that stricter feasibility need not improve final task performance. Together, the analysis and evidence provide an architecture-conditioned account of which constraint to enforce, how to enforce it, and how to interpret its empirical value.

</details>


### [217] [ETH-TraceBench: A Large-Scale Event-Stream Benchmark for Ethereum DeFi under Temporal, Protocol, and Contract Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23659)
*Kemal Kirtac,Carsten Maple*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ETH-TraceBench，一个用于评估以太坊 DeFi 表示学习在时间、协议、池/基础设施和符号偏移下鲁棒性的基准，强调困难迁移与受控输入条件而非单一聚合分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以太坊 DeFi 提供公开、带时间戳的交易级事件流，但公开符号容易诱导机器学习捷径（如依赖 emitter/topic 身份），导致聚合测试分数虚高而缺乏真实的时序建模与迁移能力。因此需要一个系统性评估不同偏移条件（时间、协议、池、符号）的基准。

Method: 构建 ETH-TraceBench：原始事件覆盖 2021-01 至 2025-12，含 13.5 亿笔交易日志、50.1 亿原始日志行。模型评估使用固定 911,267 条监督样本，训练 2021-2024、验证选择 2025H1、测试 2025H2。设置四类偏移：时间、协议新颖性、严格未见池、联合遮蔽 emitter 与 topic 身份；并对比标准 Transformer 与确定性打乱（shuffle）的日志事件序列。还进行自然流行度审计与 400 笔交易确定性审计。

Result: 聚合时间测试中 TraceStats-GB 达 0.953 macro-F1、TopicEmitterHashMLP 达 0.959；协议新颖性下分别降至 0.794、0.743、0.766，而严格未见池分数为 0.927、0.897、0.935；Uniswap v4 与 Ekubo v1 明显更难。联合遮蔽 emitter 与 topic 后 DEX macro-F1 降至 0.916、清算任务降至 0.774。标准 Transformer 相对确定性打乱无一致优势。审计估计 2025H2 DEX 约占总日志以太坊交易 22.5%，400 笔审计与标签源及独立重查日志计数完全一致。

Conclusion: ETH-TraceBench 表明高聚合分数可能来自符号捷径而非时序建模能力；应以困难迁移与受控输入条件为核心评估目标，而非单一聚合指标。

Abstract: Ethereum decentralized finance (DeFi) provides a public, time-stamped record of transaction-level event streams, but the same public symbols can create strong machine-learning shortcuts. We introduce ETH-TraceBench, a benchmark for evaluating Ethereum DeFi representations under temporal, protocol, pool/infrastructure, and symbolic shift. The raw event universe covers January 2021-December 2025 and contains 1.35 billion transactions with logs and 5.01 billion raw log rows. Model evaluation uses a fixed 911,267-instance supervised sample, training on 2021-2024, selecting models on 2025H1, and testing on 2025H2. Simple models perform strongly on the aggregate temporal test: TraceStats-GB reaches 0.953 macro-F1 and TopicEmitterHashMLP 0.959 on the canonical DEX test set. Performance drops sharply under protocol novelty, with macro-F1 of 0.794, 0.743, and 0.766 for TraceStats-GB, TopicEmitterTrace-SGD, and TopicEmitterHashMLP, while strict unseen-pool scores remain 0.927, 0.897, and 0.935. Uniswap v4 and Ekubo v1, both absent from supervised training, are materially harder than the full test. Jointly masking emitter and topic identity reduces DEX macro-F1 to 0.916 and liquidation macro-F1 to 0.774 for TopicEmitterTrace-SGD. A standard Transformer over log-index-ordered events provides no consistent advantage over a deterministic shuffle of the same events, indicating that high aggregate scores can arise without sophisticated chronological modeling. A natural-prevalence audit estimates 2025H2 DEX prevalence among logged Ethereum transactions at about 22.5%, and a deterministic 400-transaction audit finds complete agreement with task label sources and independently re-queried raw-log counts. ETH-TraceBench therefore treats difficult transfer and controlled-input conditions, rather than a single aggregate score, as the main evaluation target.

</details>


### [218] [One Patch, Three Roles: What Is Actually Coupled in Autoregressive Time-Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23686)
*Ziang Li,Yue Huang,Guoxu Zhou,Na Han,Jie Wen,Lunke Fei,Xiaozhao Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出ATD（自回归轨迹蒸馏）将输入表示、状态转移与递归执行解耦为独立设计轴，实现可选ATD-1/2/4/8执行并保持质量，同时提出无需额外参数的Spectrum Tangent校正方法提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于patch的自回归时间序列预测通常将输入表示、学习到的状态转移和递归执行绑定在同一个patch长度上，作者希望探究这些角色能否被分别调整。

Method: 通过原子编码研究分析模型宽度与原子分组的敏感性；提出ATD，将冻结父模型的递归轨迹进行蒸馏，支持ATD-1/2/4/8多种执行模式；进一步发现沿训练选取的周期性历史方向存在可校正残差投影，提出Spectrum Tangent，在不增加神经参数或Transformer调用的情况下进行校正。

Result: ATD-8在四数据集配对比较中达到5.54倍端到端加速且质量稳定；ATD在全部21次种子运行中提升轨迹保真度，但在匹配干净未来监督下仅15次提升预测精度；Spectrum Tangent在horizon 720、七个数据集和两种输出宽度下将MSE和MAE分别降低2.54%和2.33%，同时比递归推理快3.24倍。

Conclusion: 轨迹可压缩性、保真度-精度不匹配以及校正现象在三个公开AR父模型上重复出现，表明表示、转移与执行可作为自回归设计的独立轴。代码已开源。

Abstract: Patch-based autoregressive time-series forecasting often ties input representation, learned transitions, and recursive execution to one patch length. We ask which of these roles can be adjusted separately. A supporting atomic-encoding study finds greater sensitivity to model width than to atom grouping on the evaluated grid. Our main finding is that a frozen parent's recursive trajectory is easier to fit than the observed future with lightweight parallel exits. Autoregressive Trajectory Distillation (ATD) turns this into selectable ATD-1/2/4/8 execution, with ATD-1 exactly recovering the parent. On a paired four-data-set comparison, ATD-8 reaches $5.54\times$ end-to-end speedup with stable quality across widths. Fewer calls do not automatically remove the parent's existing forecast error: ATD improves trajectory fidelity in all 21 seed runs but forecast accuracy in only 15 against matched clean-future supervision. We further find a correctable residual projection along a train-selected periodic history direction. Spectrum Tangent applies this correction without adding neural parameters or Transformer calls. At horizon 720, it reduces mean squared error (MSE) and mean absolute error (MAE) by 2.54% and 2.33% over seven data sets and two output widths, while remaining $3.24\times$ faster than recursive inference. Level and shape projections sometimes disagree. Trajectory compressibility, the fidelity-accuracy mismatch, and the correction recur across three public AR parents. Together these results separate representation, transition, and execution as AR design axes. Code is available at https://github.com/RowanFFF/ATD-Spectrum-Tangent.

</details>


### [219] [A multi-temporal dataset for mapping burned areas in the Brazilian Cerrado using time series of remote sensing imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23687)
*Alisson Cleiton de Oliveira,Thales Sehn Körting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于CBERS-4A、CBERS-4和AMAZONIA-1卫星WFI传感器多时相表格数据集，用于绘制巴西Chapada dos Veadeiros国家公园2020–2022年火烧区，采用随机森林分类并评估多种验证方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为火烧区制图提供多时相、多光谱及多种光谱指数的卫星数据集，并验证WFI传感器在无星间光谱定标条件下的可用性。

Method: 利用卫星影像蓝、绿、红、近红外波段及BAI、EVI、GEMI、NDVI、NDWI指数，按规则网格组织数据集；使用随机森林分类器，基于完全烧毁、部分烧毁、未烧毁样本，测试二分类与三分类两种方案，并用七种验证方案评估精度、精确率、召回率和IoU。

Result: 当TB、PB、NB作为单独类别，并将TB重分类为BA、PB与NB合并为非烧毁时IoU更高。与MCD64A1相比，BA类别漏分误差2020年22%、2021年28%、2022年59%，错分误差分别为46%、43%、46%。

Conclusion: 研究表明WFI传感器在无需星间光谱定标的情况下可用于火烧区制图，并建议进一步探索其他机器学习算法以评估该数据集的潜力与局限。

Abstract: This paper introduces a multi-temporal tabular dataset derived from satellite images to map burned areas in the Chapada dos Veadeiros National Park, in Goiás, Brazil, covering the years 2020 to 2022. The dataset contains blue, green, red, and near-infrared bands, as well as the BAI, EVI, GEMI, NDVI, and NDWI spectral indices from the WFI sensor on the CBERS-4A, CBERS-4, and AMAZONIA-1 satellites, organized into a regular grid. We applied the Random Forest classifier to develop and validate models based on samples labeled as totally burned, partially burned, and non-burned. Two classification approaches were tested: one combining burned and non-burned areas into binary classes and another distinguishing between totally burned (TB), partially burned (PB), and non-burned (NB) classes. Seven validation approaches assessed different post-classification combinations, focusing on accuracy, precision, recall, and intersection over union (IoU) metrics. Results showed higher IoU when TB, PB, and NB were used as individual classes and TB was reclassified as burned area (BA) while PB and NB were grouped as non-burned. Comparing the annual results of this approach to the MCD64A1 product, the errors of omission for the BA class were 22% in 2020, 28% in 2021 and 59% in 2022, while the errors of commission were 46%, 43% and 46%, respectively. The study highlights the utility of the WFI sensor for burned area mapping without inter-satellite spectral calibration and suggests further exploration with other machine learning algorithms to evaluate the dataset potential and limitations.

</details>


### [220] [Tail-Weight Control and Localized Generalization in Nearly Low-Rank Adversarial Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23688)
*Kunyu Wang,Dehan Wang,Wenjun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高斯模型下带范数约束的线性分类在欧氏对抗扰动下的表现，模型包含低维信息子空间和独立噪声尾部。针对有界斜坡损失，证明主空间见证者风险低于一半时，所有近最优预测器必须具有小尾部权重；通过路径特定密度界获得无需正尾部方差的常数。在各向同性主协方差下建立唯一总体极小值及联合局部增长，边界归一化去除中心间隔中的公共攻击惩罚，给出由主维度和总尾部能量控制的局部有限样本保证。实验验证超额风险和尾部使用随样本量下降，且固定总能量下尾部维度增长时行为几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高维线性分类在对抗扰动下的泛化与鲁棒性问题，特别是当数据分布在低维信息子空间加独立噪声尾部时，理解对抗训练中的样本复杂度、尾部依赖与维度效应，填补现有理论中关于尾部方差条件与有限样本保证的空白。

Method: 在高斯模型下分析带范数约束的线性分类；采用有界斜坡损失，利用主空间见证者和路径特定密度界；在各向同性主协方差假设下推导唯一总体极小值与联合局部增长；通过边界归一化处理中心间隔以消除公共攻击惩罚；使用全局化增长放宽入口条件；结合模型感知比较保留局部保证；实验设置二十组配对重复以验证理论。

Result: 有界斜坡损失下，主空间见证者风险低于一半可迫使所有近最优预测器具有小尾部权重；路径特定密度界给出无需正尾部方差的常数；各向同性主协方差下存在唯一总体极小值和联合局部增长；边界归一化后获得由主维度和总尾部能量控制的局部有限样本保证；全局化增长弱化常数并去除入口条件；实验显示超额风险和尾部使用随样本量递减，固定总能量下尾部维度增长时行为几乎不变；纯噪声控制和优化器诊断进一步明确结论的适用范围与局限。

Conclusion: 该工作为低维信息子空间加噪声尾部的高斯模型下对抗线性分类提供了系统的理论刻画与有限样本保证，揭示了主维度与尾部能量对鲁棒泛化的核心作用，并通过实验支持理论预测，同时指出结论的适用边界。

Abstract: We study norm-constrained linear classification under Eu clidean adversarial perturbations in a Gaussian model with a low-dimen sional informative subspace and an independent noise tail. For bounded ramp loss, we prove that a principal-space witness with risk below one half forces every near-optimal predictor to have small tail weight. A path-specific density bound yields constants without requiring positive tail variance. Under isotropic principal covariance, we establish a unique population minimizer and joint local growth. Boundary normalization then removes the common attack penalty from centered margins, giving localized finite-sample guarantees governed by principal dimension and total tail energy. Globalized growth removes the entrance condition at weaker constants; a model-aware comparison retains local guarantees. Experiments with twenty paired repetitions show decreasing excess risk and tail use with sample size, and nearly unchanged behavior when tail dimension grows at fixed total energy. Pure-noise controls and optimizer diagnostics clarify the scope and limitations of these conclusions.

</details>


### [221] [GenVoid: Uncertainty-Aware Learning of Subsurface Material Defects with an Experimentally Validated Physics-Informed Generative Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23761)
*Trishit Mondal,Prajwal Bharadwaj,Nikhil Karanjgaokar,Ameya D. Jagtap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GenVoid物理信息生成模型框架，仅凭表面位移测量即可识别二维/三维固体内部空洞，并输出概率化重建结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内部空洞普遍存在但其几何不可见，只能通过可测表面量间接推断，现有方法难以应对复杂几何、多种材料行为及测量噪声与不确定性。

Method: 将力学控制方程嵌入生成式推断框架，支持线弹性、超弹性与塑性材料及复杂二/三维结构；显式建模位移测量中的噪声与不确定性；并引入"可观测性"度量，刻画边界测量对内部局部刚度扰动的敏感性。

Result: 在高保真合成数据集及原位力学实验测得的位移场上验证了方法有效性，能概率性重建隐藏空洞几何，并揭示缺陷位置、传感器配置与重建精度之间的关系。

Conclusion: 建立了物理信息、不确定性感知的隐藏缺陷无损表征方法，并为固体力学反问题的测量策略设计提供定量依据。

Abstract: Internal voids are ubiquitous defects in manufactured structures, yet their characterization remains challenging because their geometry is hidden and can only be inferred indirectly from accessible measurements. Here we introduce \textit{GenVoid}, a physics-informed generative model-based framework for identifying internal voids in complex two- and three-dimensional solids from surface displacement measurements alone. By incorporating the governing mechanics into a generative inference framework, \textit{GenVoid} enables void identification across linear elastic, hyperelastic and plastic material behaviours and accommodates complex two- and three-dimensional structural geometries. Importantly, the framework explicitly accounts for uncertainty and noise in displacement measurements, producing probabilistic reconstructions of internal void geometry rather than a single deterministic estimate. We demonstrate the approach using high-fidelity synthetic datasets and experimentally measured displacement fields obtained from in-situ mechanical experiments, establishing its ability to infer hidden voids from realistic displacement measurements. To quantify the fundamental limits of such inference, we further introduce an observability measure that characterizes the sensitivity of boundary measurements to localized stiffness perturbations within the interior under an ensemble of applied loads. This framework provides a direct connection between defect location, sensor configuration and reconstruction fidelity, enabling systematic assessment of how the number and spatial distribution of boundary measurements govern void-identification accuracy. To this end, these results establish a physics-informed and uncertainty-aware approach for non-invasive characterization of hidden defects and provide a quantitative basis for designing measurement strategies for inverse problems in solid mechanics.

</details>


### [222] [Statistical Convergence of Transformer Encoder-Accelerated Robust Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23775)
*Suman Banerjee,Hiroyasu Tsukamoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统计严格的鲁棒强化学习收敛框架：用基于Transformer的动作价值函数预测进行热启动，结合R-污染模型与共形预测，给出同时覆盖所有迭代的收敛边界，并形成预认证停止规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模状态-动作空间中求最优动作价值函数计算代价极高；现有收敛保证往往依赖对真实转移核的充分知识，且边界较松，难以实用。

Method: 采用R-污染模型刻画状态转移核的不确定性；用自然语言提示编码任务规范，由Transformer预测动作价值函数进行热启动；以收缩Bellman残差构造轨迹级非一致性分数，通过共形预测得到共形分位数边界，并据此设计预认证停止规则。

Result: 在不同规模和污染程度的扰动迷宫环境上的数值实验表明，Transformer热启动可明显降低初始误差并加速收敛；所提共形边界比现有保证更紧地跟踪真实误差轨迹。

Conclusion: 该框架在几乎不需要真实转移核知识的情况下，提供了可认证的收敛保证与停止准则，兼具理论严谨性与实践有效性。

Abstract: Obtaining the optimal action-value function in Markov decision processes is computationally intensive in large state--action spaces. In this study, we present statistically rigorous convergence results for a robust reinforcement learning algorithm warm-started by a transformer-based action-value function prediction, where natural language prompts encode task specifications. Our framework adopts the R-contamination model to characterize uncertainty in the state transition kernel, and employs conformal prediction to certify convergence via trajectory-level nonconformity scores constructed from the contracting Bellman residual. The resulting conformal quantile bounds the gap between the running and optimal action-value functions simultaneously over all iterations, thereby yielding a pre-certified stopping rule that requires little knowledge of the true transition kernel. Numerical case studies on perturbed maze environments of varying size and contamination level confirm that the transformer-based warm start measurably reduces the initial error and accelerates convergence, while the proposed conformal bounds track the true error trajectory more tightly than existing guarantees.

</details>


### [223] [Falling Trees: A Model Class for Interpretable Risk Prioritization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23780)
*Varun Babbar,Zachery Boner,Margo Seltzer,Cynthia Rudin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 falling trees（坠落树），在保持风险单调递减约束的同时允许树状分支，并给出 GRAVITree 动态规划算法学习其 Rashomon 集，在临床与公开数据集上匹配或优于 FRLs 等可解释基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策常需优先处理高风险案例（如临床优先诊疗高危患者）。现有 falling rule lists（FRLs）虽可解释且风险单调递减，但其单一决策路径结构导致模型类别高度受限，表达力不足。作者希望在保留单调风险约束的前提下提升模型表达能力。

Method: 提出 falling trees 模型族，强制与 FRLs 相同的单调风险约束，但允许树状分支结构。设计 GRAVITree 算法，基于带界限的动态规划（dynamic-programming-with-bounds）学习满足深度与分支约束的 falling trees 的 Rashomon 集。该形式化可在 rule lists 与完整决策树之间插值，从而按用户需求调节模型表达力。

Result: 在一个新的临床数据集以及多个公开分类基准上，falling trees 匹配或优于 FRLs 及其他可解释基线，且对高风险样本常产生更稀疏的决策。

Conclusion: falling trees 在高风险场景下实现了可解释性、表达力与风险优先级之间的实用平衡，为可解释风险优先决策提供了新框架。

Abstract: Many real-world decisions require prioritizing high-risk cases, such as clinicians prioritizing high-risk patients before lower-risk ones. Falling rule lists (FRLs), which are ordered if--then rules with monotonically decreasing risks, provide an interpretable framework for such tasks; however, their single-path structure yields a highly restricted model class. We introduce falling trees, a new family of interpretable models that enforces the same monotonic risk constraint while permitting tree-structured branching. We present GRAVITree, a novel dynamic-programming-with-bounds algorithm for learning the Rashomon set of falling trees under depth and branching constraints. Our formulation can interpolate between rule lists and full decision trees, enabling user-desired model expressivity. In a new clinical dataset and in many public classification benchmarks, falling trees match or outperform FRLs and other interpretable baselines, often producing more sparse decisions for high-risk instances. Our results show that falling trees strike a practical balance between interpretability, expressiveness, and risk prioritization for high-stakes settings.

</details>


### [224] [Belted Engression: Sufficient Dimension Reduction for Generative Distributional Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23789)
*Wenxi Tan,Bing Li,Lingzhou Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Belted Engression，一个统一且参数高效的生成式分布回归框架，将充分降维（SDR）嵌入生成架构，形成端到端的“先压缩后生成”范式，理论上证明其最优解等价于保分布的生成分解，并获得更紧的有限样本收敛率与更小参数规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代条件生成模型在学习复杂协变量依赖时面临挑战；尽管充分降维（SDR）提供了压缩此类依赖的原则性方法，但传统SDR框架并非为条件生成而设计，存在理论与方法上的缺口。

Method: 提出Belted Engression框架，在生成架构中嵌入结构化瓶颈，基于充分表示学习实现端到端的“压缩—生成”流程；理论上建立标准SDR条件与保分布生成因子分解的等价性，并刻画能量分数损失的局部Bernstein型控制以推导有限样本收敛率。

Result: 理论上证明总体Belted Engression目标的全局最优可实现保分布生成分解，得到比现有结果更紧的有限样本收敛率，并证明该带式架构相对无结构基线具有严格更小且渐近消失的参数数量；大量模拟与真实应用表明其在更少可训练参数下实现更优的分布预测与SDR恢复。

Conclusion: Belted Engression为条件生成中的复杂协变量依赖学习提供了统一、参数高效且有理论保证的方案，兼顾分布预测性能与充分降维恢复能力。

Abstract: Modern conditional generative models face significant challenges when learning complex covariate dependencies. While sufficient dimension reduction (SDR) provides a principled approach to compress these dependencies, traditional SDR frameworks were not formulated for conditional generation. To bridge this gap, we propose Belted Engression, a unified and architecturally parameter-efficient framework for generative distributional regression. Our approach establishes an end-to-end compress-then-generate paradigm driven by sufficient representation learning, embedding a structural bottleneck into the generative architecture. Theoretically, we prove that the standard SDR condition is equivalent to a law-preserving generative factorization, which is achieved at the global optimum of the population Belted Engression objective. Furthermore, by uncovering a localized Bernstein-type control for the energy-score loss, we establish finite-sample convergence rates that are sharper than those of existing results. We also prove that this belted architecture is strictly smaller, operating with an asymptotically vanishing parameter count relative to the unstructured baseline. Extensive simulations and real-world applications demonstrate that Belted Engression achieves superior distributional prediction and SDR recovery with fewer trainable parameters.

</details>


### [225] [Iterative Atom Refinement: A Monotonicity Principle for Dictionary Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23812)
*Alexander Christie,Miguel Moscoso,Alexei Novikov,George Papanicolaou,Chrysoula Tsogka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代原子精炼（IAR）算法，用于从稀疏观测中恢复字典原子，并给出严格的收敛理论，证明小初值优势会被放大，三步内即可识别生成原子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字典学习旨在从观测 y_i = A x_i（稀疏系数）中恢复未知字典 A，其中恢复单个字典原子是核心难点。作者希望提出一个简单算法并给出其严格收敛保证，弥补理论分析的不足。

Method: 提出 Iterative Atom Refinement（IAR）算法：从随机方向出发，反复选择与当前迭代方向相关性最强的观测，并对所选数据取平均以更新方向。理论分析使用高维概率估计和关于原子选择概率的新单调性原理。

Result: 理论证明：一个原子的微小初始优势会被逐步放大，直到该原子被隔离出来；在模型假设下，IAR 仅需三步精炼即可识别生成原子。数值实验支持该理论，并表明所得动力学准确刻画了字典精炼中的实际行为。

Conclusion: IAR 是一种简单且具有严格收敛保证的字典原子恢复方法，其动力学行为与实际字典精炼现象一致。

Abstract: Dictionary learning seeks to recover an unknown dictionary $A$ from observations ${\bf y}_i = A{\bf x}_i$ with sparse coefficient vectors ${\bf x}_i$. We introduce the \emph{Iterative Atom Refinement} (IAR) algorithm, a simple procedure for recovering individual dictionary atoms. Starting from a random direction, IAR repeatedly selects the observations most strongly correlated with the current iterate and updates the direction by averaging the selected data. Our main contribution is a rigorous convergence theory of IAR. Using high-dimensional probabilistic estimates and a novel monotonicity principle for atom-selection probabilities, we show that a small initial advantage of one atom is amplified until that atom is isolated. Under our model assumptions, IAR identifies a generating atom after only three refinement steps. Numerical experiments support the theory and show that the resulting dynamics accurately capture the behavior observed in dictionary refinement.

</details>


### [226] [Real-time Generalizable Heart Valve Mechanics for Clinical Disease Assessment via a Physics-Conditioned Neural Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23826)
*Shawn Koohy,Wensi Wu,Matthew A Jolley,Paris Perdikaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理条件神经算子（PCNO），一种基于Transformer的代理模型，可快速预测二尖瓣和三尖瓣的瓣叶位移、应变与应力场，实现高达15,260倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二尖瓣反流是全球最常见的心脏瓣膜疾病，影响超过2%的全球人口，75岁以上成年人中至少达10%，并造成约15%的瓣膜性心脏病相关死亡。然而只有少数重症患者接受矫正手术。快速评估瓣膜力学有望实现更早、更精准的干预，但传统有限元仿真对于临床时间线和参数扫描而言过慢。

Method: 提出物理条件神经算子（PCNO），一种基于Transformer的代理模型，以收缩期血压和组织属性为条件，预测二尖瓣与三尖瓣几何结构上的瓣叶位移、应变和应力场。训练数据涵盖功能性、反流性和病理性瓣膜，包括牵拉、P2脱垂和瓣环扩张。

Result: 相比细网格有限元仿真，PCNO实现高达15,260倍的加速，且精度相当；能够识别病理类别，并在分布外外推条件下将诊断指标误差控制在3.5%以内。

Conclusion: PCNO作为快速、准确的瓣膜力学代理模型，有望支持更早期、更精准的临床干预，并替代传统有限元仿真以适配临床时间线与参数扫描需求。

Abstract: Mitral regurgitation is the most common heart valve disorder worldwide, affecting over 2% of the global population, rising to at least 10% in adults over 75, and causing approximately 15% of valvular heart disease-related deaths. Yet only a minority of patients with severe disease undergo corrective surgery. Rapid assessment of valve mechanics could enable earlier, more precise intervention, but traditional finite element simulations remain too slow for clinical timelines and parameter sweeps. We introduce the Physics-Conditioned Neural Operator (PCNO), a transformer-based surrogate that predicts leaflet displacement, strain, and stress fields across mitral and tricuspid geometries, conditioned on systolic blood pressure and tissue properties. Trained on functional, regurgitated, and pathological valves, including tethering, P2 prolapse, and annular dilation, PCNO achieves up to a 15,260x speedup over fine mesh finite element simulations with comparable accuracy, identifies pathology class, and resolves diagnostic metrics within 3.5% error under out-of-distribution extrapolation.

</details>


### [227] [Actionable Insights from Observational Data: The Case of Advanced Classes in K-12 Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23836)
*Nabit Bajwa,Seth B. Hunter,Sanmay Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文利用美国某公立学区引入可选开放招生的初中高级课程这一政策变化所产生的观测数据，在存在自选择偏误的情况下分析选修高级课程的效果，发现高级英语课程总体有温和正向效应，但获益不均且部分高获益学生反而较少选修。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-12教育中"选修更具挑战性课程"的效果是根本性难题，因为学生（或其家长）自主决定是否选课，导致因果推断困难；本研究旨在应对这一识别挑战。

Method: 借助美国某公立学区的新颖数据集，其中记录学生选课决定、既往学业史、人口学特征及后续结果，并涵盖全区推行可选开放招生高级初中课程的政策变化；针对"影响选课的因素同时预测学业结果"这一核心识别难题，采用审慎的数据分析方法以缓解混杂。

Result: 选修高级英语课程对学生成绩有净正向但温和的效应；收益分布不均：部分预测获益较大的学生（往年"中等成绩者"）反而更少选修；黑人学生及社会经济地位较低群体的选课倾向也显著更低。

Conclusion: 预测获益与实际选课之间的差距表明，严谨的数据分析可从大规模观测数据中提取可行动洞见，识别出那些本有条件受益却未选择高级课程的学生。

Abstract: A fundamentally challenging question in K-12 education is about the effects of taking more advanced or challenging classes. It is particularly complex because students (and/or their parents) choose whether to enroll in these classes, making causal analysis challenging. In this paper, we begin to tackle this question by taking advantage of a novel dataset from a public school system in the US. This dataset records students' course enrollment decisions, prior academic histories, demographics, and subsequent outcomes around the time of a district-wide change that introduced optional open-enrollment advanced middle-school courses in subject areas. This is a rich observational dataset, but enrollment in advanced classes is driven by student characteristics and choices rather than random assignment. This creates a core identification challenge: the same factors that influence enrollment in advanced courses are also predictive of academic outcomes. As a result, simple comparisons between enrolled and non-enrolled students are confounded, and naive estimates may reflect underlying differences in student ability, motivation, or support rather than the impact of coursework itself. Our analysis shows that enrolling in advanced English courses has a net positive but modest effect on student achievement outcomes. However, these benefits are unevenly distributed: some students with relatively large predicted gains ("middle achievers" in prior years) are less likely to enroll than others. Some other groups (e.g. Black students and those with lower socio-economic status) also demonstrate significantly lower propensity to enroll. This gap between predicted benefit and observed enrollment illustrates how careful data analysis can extract actionable insights from large observational datasets, including identifying students who appear well-positioned to benefit but do not select into advanced options.

</details>


### [228] [From Regional to Global: Transfer Learning for Atmospheric Transport Emulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23838)
*Jeff Clark,Elena Fillola,Nawid Keshtmand,Raul Santos-Rodriguez,Matthew Rigby*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估大气传输模拟器（近似LPDM足迹）在四个全球区域（南美、东亚、南亚、北非）间的空间可迁移性，通过区域专用模型、多区域模型及留一区域实验，刻画区域差异并探讨跨区域泛化与迁移学习，以助力高效的全球温室气体排放估算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
