Motivation: 温室气体排放估算需结合大气浓度观测与化学传输模型，传统LPDM等物理模拟器运行昂贵且难以适配现代高分辨率卫星数据。作者此前已开发出针对南美、比UK Met Office LPDM快约1000倍的大气传输模拟器，但大气传输具有区域异质性，直接扩展到全球模拟并不容易，因此需要研究模型的空间可迁移性。

Method: 在四个世界区域（南美、东亚、南亚、北非）上评估模型的空间可迁移能力，采用区域专用模型与多区域模型，并进行留一区域（leave-one-region-out）实验；同时结合输入变量分布与输出足迹分布来刻画区域差异。

Result: 通过区域特异与多区域模型以及留一区域实验，对比了各区域模型的表现，并结合输入变量与输出足迹的分布特征揭示了区域间的差异。（摘要未给出具体量化结果。）

Conclusion: 该工作有助于建立对跨区域泛化与迁移学习的直觉认识，可提升各区域性能，进而支撑高效、低成本的全球排放估算。

Abstract: Greenhouse gas emissions estimates can be derived using inverse methods by combining atmospheric concentration observations with chemical transport models. The latter traditionally use physics-driven simulators such as Lagrangian Particle Dispersion Models (LPDMs), which are expensive to run and do not scale well to modern satellites' high resolution data. Previously we developed a performant atmospheric transport emulator that approximates LPDM outputs ("footprints") over South America ~1,000X faster than the UK Met Office's LPDM. Expanding towards global emulation is not straightforward, as atmospheric transport is regionally heterogeneous. This paper evaluates spatial transferability capabilities of models across four world regions: South America, East Asia, South Asia, North Africa using both region-specific and multi-region models, and leave-one-region-out experiments. Regional differences are characterised in the context of input variable and output footprint distributions. This work builds intuition in cross-region generalisation and transfer learning, aiding regional performance towards efficient global emissions estimates.

</details>


### [229] [PROSE: A Theory of Optimal Stopping with Perishable Evidence for Peer Selection in Intermittently Connected Decentralised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23845)
*Christos Anagnostopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为去中心化联邦学习中的对等节点选择问题建立了一套基于最优停止理论的分析框架，并提出轻量级本地策略PROSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习没有聚合服务器，协作依赖短暂的对等节点可用性。在移动或间歇连接系统中，评估对等节点会消耗接触时间，甚至导致交换机会消失；学习器获得的关于节点的证据具有"易逝性"——既因链接过期而衰减，也因节点模型漂移与测量老化而失效。因此需要一套针对该场景的对等节点选择理论。

Method: 将接收方在单次接触内的决策形式化为带昂贵信息获取与未来到达外部选项的有限时域马尔可夫最优停止问题，证明其存在由保留价值刻画的Snell包络最优策略。在此基础上证明四组结果：(i) 阶段一致、漂移感知的集中不等式与高概率正确的maximin认证规则及有限样本识别界；(ii) 移动感知的信息价值停止规则及比较静态，表明链路风险越高，继续探测的价值越低、停止区域越大；(iii) 标记泊松接触到达下的等待价值闭式解及搜索论保留价值比较静态；(iv) 近视最优性定理，说明在足够波动的单调移动机制下，单步置信安全规则可作为最优策略的可靠替代且不会过早停止。

Result: 理论全部为解析性成果，未进行实验验证。核心结果包括最优停止策略的Snell包络结构、漂移感知认证与识别界、移动感知停止规则的比较静态、泊松到达下的等待价值闭式解，以及近视最优性定理；并给出静态接触与无漂移极限下退化为经典序贯决策问题的条件。

Conclusion: 论文将理论实例化为PROSE——一种轻量、完全本地的策略，并刻画了经典序贯决策问题得以恢复的静态接触与无漂移极限。整体为纯分析性工作。

Abstract: Decentralised federated learning removes the aggregation server but makes collaboration dependent on transient peer availability. In mobile and intermittently connected systems, evaluating a promising peer consumes contact time and may cause the exchange opportunity itself to vanish, so that the evidence a learner gathers about a peer is perishable: it decays because links expire and because peer models drift while old measurements age. This paper develops a self-contained theory of optimal stopping for the resulting peer-selection problem. We formalise a receiver's within-contact decision as a finite-horizon Markov optimal-stopping problem with costly information acquisition and a future-arrival outside option, and prove that it admits an optimal policy characterised by a reservation value (Snell-envelope structure). Around this formulation we prove: (i) stage-uniform, drift-aware concentration and a maximin certification rule that is correct with high probability together with a finite-sample identification bound; (ii) a mobility-aware value of-information stopping rule and comparative statics showing that higher link hazard lowers the value of continued probing and enlarges the stopping region; (iii) a closed-form value of waiting under marked-Poisson contact arrivals, together with a search-theoretic reservation value whose comparative statics we characterise; and (iv) a myopic-optimality theorem establishing that, in sufficiently volatile (monotone) mobility regimes, the one-step confidence-safe rule is a sound surrogate for the optimal policy and never stops prematurely. We instantiate the theory as PROSE (Perishable-evidence Reservation-value Optimal Stopping for Exchange), a lightweight, fully local policy, and delineate the static contact and drift-free limits in which classical sequential decision problems are recovered. The development is entirely analytical.

</details>


### [230] [VISTA: An Attention-Based Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Architecture for Space Situational Awareness Sensor Tasking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23875)
*Miguel Leiva-Vélez,Adalberto Claudio Quiros,Nicolas Gaston Rozado,Hodei Urrutxua,Víctor Rodríguez-Fernández*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VISTA是一种可扩展的深度强化学习架构，用于变规模空间目标编目维护的传感器任务分配，在从单传感器到大规模异构协同感知等场景中均优于经典方法与固定维度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间目标数量快速增长，使空间态势感知传感器任务分配日益复杂。现有深度强化学习方法虽在简化场景中有效，但其固定维度的状态与动作表示难以扩展到大规模动态编目和分布式感知网络。

Method: VISTA结合了物理与任务信息引导的top-K检索、以实体为中心的注意力机制、循环记忆以及基于指针的动作解码，使每个智能体的观测与动作空间不依赖编目规模。

Result: 在30个轨道目标下，VISTA比固定维度循环基线快31.2%完成编目恢复；大规模场景中，五小时不确定度较最强经典方法降低97.5%，较循环学习器降低99.3%；零样本测试至20,000个目标显示感知容量、编目规模与恢复时域近似线性关系；策略还表现出传感器模态自适应以及对目标数量和初始不确定度变化的泛化能力。

Conclusion: VISTA为跨大规模、分布式异构天基与地基传感器网络的自适应空间态势感知任务分配提供了可扩展框架。

Abstract: The rapid growth of resident space objects is increasing the complexity of space situational awareness sensor tasking, challenging classical optimization methods as they allocate finite, heterogeneous, and distributed sensing resources across ever-larger catalogues. Existing deep reinforcement learning approaches show promise in reduced settings, but fixed-dimensional state and action representations limit their ability to scale to large, dynamic catalogues and distributed sensing networks. We introduce VISTA (Variable-Entity Intelligent Sensor Tasking Architecture), a scalable deep reinforcement learning architecture for persistent uncertainty-driven catalogue maintenance across variable object populations and sensor configurations. VISTA combines physics- and mission-informed top-K retrieval with entity-centric attention, recurrent memory, and pointer-based action decoding, thereby keeping each agent's observation and action spaces independent of catalogue size. We evaluate VISTA across different scenarios, from fixed-size single-sensor benchmarks to large-scale space-based tasking and heterogeneous cooperative sensing. With 30 orbiting targets, VISTA recovers the catalogue 31.2% faster than the fixed-dimensional recurrent baseline. In the large-scale regime, VISTA reduces five-hour uncertainty by 97.5% relative to the strongest classical reference and by 99.3% relative to the recurrent learner. Zero-shot tests up to 20,000 objects reveal near-linear relations between sensing capacity, catalogue size, and recovery horizon. Learned policies also exhibit sensor modality adaptation and generalization to population and initial-uncertainty shifts. Together, these results demonstrate that VISTA provides a scalable framework for adaptive space situational awareness sensor tasking across large, distributed networks of heterogeneous ground- and space-based sensors.

</details>


### [231] [GLR-MM: Graph-Based Global-Local Reconstruction for Robust Multimodal Chest X-ray and EHR Representation Learning under Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23876)
*Surbhi Sharma,Nikhil Manali,Devesh Maheshwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GLR-MM，一个基于图的全局-局部重建框架，用于ICU早期死亡预测，在严重模态缺失时表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床多模态模型常需在胸部X光和电子健康记录输入不完整时进行预测。现有方法或对齐已观测表征、或建模缺失、或跨模态重建，但未能联合利用患者内部与临床相似患者间的证据。

Method: 将五种CXR-EHR模态映射到共享空间，通过互补的局部跨模态分支和全局图注意力分支重建缺失嵌入，自适应融合两者估计，并优化类别平衡的预测、重建与对比目标。

Result: 在9,620个MIMIC来源ICU住院记录上，以10%、30%、50%随机模态缺失率评估（共享确定性掩码）。轻度与中度缺失下MUSE更优；50%缺失时GLR-MM的AUROC和AUPRC分别高出0.0088和0.0249。

Conclusion: 图引导的重建在输入严重不完整时最为有用。

Abstract: Clinical multimodal models must often predict before all chest X-ray (CXR) and electronic health record (EHR) inputs are available. Existing approaches align observed representations, model missingness, or reconstruct across modalities, but do not jointly exploit within-patient and clinically similar inter-patient evidence. We propose GLR-MM, a Graph-Based Global-Local Reconstruction framework for early ICU mortality prediction. It maps five CXR-EHR modalities to a shared space, reconstructs missing embeddings through complementary local cross-modal and global graph-attention branches, adaptively fuses their estimates, and optimizes class-balanced prediction, reconstruction, and contrastive objectives. On 9,620 MIMIC-derived ICU stays, we evaluate 10%, 30%, and 50% random modality missingness with shared deterministic masks. MUSE performs better under mild and moderate missingness, whereas GLR-MM achieves higher AUROC and AUPRC at 50% by 0.0088 and 0.0249, respectively. These results indicate that graph-guided reconstruction is most useful when inputs are severely incomplete.

</details>


### [232] [Collaborative Streaming Anomaly Detection with Interactive Explanations and Ensemble Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23883)
*Diogo Risca,Afonso Lourenço,Ricardo Martins,Goreti Marreiros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个协作式流式异常检测系统，将人类分析师纳入决策回路，结合异构检测器、归一化加权共识与代理模型，实现可解释且可人工干预的异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速数据流中的异常检测需要兼顾检测性能与可解释性，同时让人类分析师能够有效介入决策过程，以应对部署环境中的不确定性并提升结果的可信度与可操作性。

Method: 系统整合多种异构检测器，通过基于归一化的加权共识聚合其输出，并辅以伪影感知规则在部署条件下稳定异常评分；同时构建代理模型近似集成共识，暴露与异常行为相关的可读传感器条件，使分析师能够审查异常事件段、调整共识行为并优化用于异常预测的代理规则，形成人工调整后的集成。

Result: 在一个包含26万个事件、3个异常事件段的工业数据流上进行评估，展示了稳健的检测性能以及可操作的人机交互效果。

Conclusion: 将人类分析师显式纳入流式异常检测回路，结合异构检测器共识与可解释代理模型，能在工业高速数据流场景中实现稳健检测与有效的人机协作。

Abstract: We present a collaborative streaming anomaly detection system for high-speed data streams that explicitly integrates human analysts into the decision loop. The system combines heterogeneous detectors and aggregates their outputs through a normalization-based weighted consensus, complemented by artifact-aware rules to stabilize anomaly scoring under deployment. To improve interpretability, it derives surrogate models that approximate the ensemble consensus and expose human-readable sensor conditions associated with anomalous behavior. Analysts can actively intervene by reviewing anomaly episodes, adjusting consensus behavior, and refining surrogate rules used for anomaly prediction, producing a human-adjusted ensemble. We evaluate the approach on an industrial stream with 260\,000 events and 3 anomalous episodes, showing robust detection and actionable human-AI interaction.

</details>


### [233] [Circuit-Diff: Factual Edit-based Intervention Method for Localizing Knowledge in Attribution Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23892)
*Edward G. Friedman,Xiangchen Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Circuit-Diff，一种通过低秩事实编辑干预模型、并观察 CLT 归因图中特征角色变化来定位与编辑知识相关节点的方法，从而简化电路发现流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性将特征视为网络的基本单元、将电路视为加权子图，但单个神经元具有多义性。跨层转码器（CLT）通过生成归因图来近似电路，然而图中节点是无标签特征，读取图需要先剪枝再人工推断每个存活节点的含义，耗时费力。为让 CLT 更易用于电路发现，作者希望自动识别与特定知识相关的特征节点。

Method: Circuit-Diff 对模型本身施加低秩事实编辑干预，并将归因图中角色随该编辑而改变的特征视为与所编辑知识相关。作者形式化该方法，测量冻结 CLT 在事实编辑后的可靠性，并通过在多达 24 个 CounterFact 编辑上进行节点修补来因果检验所选节点，还给出案例研究，并发布基于 circuit-tracer 包的开源实现及两个附加工具：多提示聚合与基于规则的超节点标注。

Result: 在所考察的编辑上，被标记的节点不仅是对象 token 的检测器；根据 CLT 发布的特征仪表盘读取，它们还包含围绕新旧对象的历史、地理与关联特征。作者通过修补最多 24 个 CounterFact 编辑对所选出节点进行了因果测试，并给出了案例研究。

Conclusion: Circuit-Diff 通过事实编辑干预自动把归因图节点与所编辑知识关联起来，使 CLT 更易用于电路发现，且所选节点具有超越对象 token 检测的语义丰富性，并获得因果支持。作者开源实现并附带多提示聚合与规则化超节点标注工具，便于后续使用。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability defines features as the fundamental units of a neural network and circuits as the weighted subgraphs that carry out its computation. Because individual neurons are polysemantic, Cross-Layer Transcoders (CLTs) were introduced as a way to approximate a model's circuits by generating an attribution graph. The nodes of that graph, however, are unlabeled features: reading a graph means pruning it and then working out by hand what each surviving node means. To make CLTs easier to use for circuit discovery, we introduce Circuit-Diff, which intervenes on the model itself with a low-rank factual edit and takes the features whose role in the attribution graph changes under that edit as related to the edited knowledge. On the edits we examine, the flagged nodes are not only detectors of the object token: read off the CLT's released feature dashboards, they include features for the history, geography and associations surrounding the old and new objects. We formalize the method, measure how reliable a frozen CLT remains after a factual edit, test the selected nodes causally by patching them on up to 24 CounterFact edits, give a case study, and release an open-source implementation built on the circuit-tracer package, together with two further tools (multi-prompt aggregation and rule-based supernode labeling).

</details>


### [234] [GDN Tree-Scan: Served Tree Verification for Recurrent-Hybrid Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23900)
*Zhiyuan Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GDN Tree-Scan：为Gated-DeltaNet混合模型在vLLM中实现树状推测解码验证器，通过分支局部循环扫描与仅接受链状态发布，在B=1 SWE/Codex场景下提升词元吞吐27.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力型Transformer的树状推测解码验证只需祖先掩码，但循环混合模型（如Gated-DeltaNet）的候选行还必须携带其根到节点路径上原生顺序解码本应产生的循环状态，否则即使注意力掩码正确，验证器也可能基于不可能出现的循环历史进行条件计算。

Method: 提出GDN Tree-Scan，集成于vLLM的Gated-DeltaNet混合模型服务端验证器，结合FlashAttention-2树偏置注意力、分支局部GDN扫描/重放、设备端多草稿提交以及仅接受链的状态发布。

Result: 在公开Qwen3.6-27B-FP8检查点上，B=1的SWE/Codex解码门控（温度0.6）中，六节点根分支树使每次事件提交词元增加17.2%，验证前向时间接近原生；达到23.88词元加权解码词元/秒，对比原生五步MTP（E5）的18.80，提升27.0%。每请求等延迟视角为+4.0%，端到端任务墙钟时间仍以预填充为主。

Conclusion: 实证等价性证据仅限于循环预言机概率重打分（p-rescore）在观测到的原生翻转下限内闭合，并非完整的分布距离证明。

Abstract: Tree speculative decoding verifies multiple candidate continuations in one target forward pass. For attention-only transformers, the verifier mainly needs an ancestry mask. Recurrent-hybrid language models break this assumption: a candidate row must also carry the recurrent state that native sequential decode would have produced along its root-to-node path. Otherwise, a verifier can use a correct attention mask while still conditioning on an impossible recurrent history.
  We present GDN Tree-Scan, a served verifier for Gated-DeltaNet hybrid language models integrated into vLLM. The system combines FlashAttention-2 tree-bias attention, branch-local GDN scan/replay, device-side multidraft commitment, and accepted-chain-only state publication. On the public Qwen3.6-27B-FP8 checkpoint, in a clean batch-one (B=1) SWE/Codex decode gate at temperature 0.6, a six-node root-branch tree increases committed tokens/event by 17.2% at near-native verify-forward time and reaches 23.88 token-weighted decode tokens/s versus 18.80 for native five-step MTP (E5), a 27.0% token-weighted decode-throughput gain. The per-request-equal latency view is +4.0%, and end-to-end task wall time remains prefill-heavy. Empirical equivalence evidence is scoped to recurrent-oracle probability-rescore (p-rescore) closure within the observed native flip floor, not a full distribution-distance proof.

</details>


### [235] [Multivariate quantile regression via Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23906)
*Andrew Polar,Michael Poluektov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种预测随机系统中向量值目标条件联合分布的新算法，采用KAN集成作为函数逼近器，并引入新的联合分布差异度量与拟合优度检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机系统的随机性为内在随机性，而非观测误差或加性噪声所致；多元分位数回归虽也建模条件联合分布但任务较简单，需要更强的方法来处理条件联合分布的预测问题。

Method: 采用Kolmogorov–Arnold网络（KAN）集成作为灵活函数逼近器进行识别；方法理论上不限于KAN，但KAN特别适合该构造。包含训练流程，并引入新的联合分布差异度量及基于该度量的拟合优度检验。

Result: 所提拟合优度检验最初用于验证和校准识别技术，在此以ad hoc方式使用；该检验更广泛适用，虽可制表供更广泛使用，但本工作未进行制表。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于KAN集成、用于预测向量值目标条件联合分布的新算法，并配套新的差异度量与拟合优度检验；检验具通用性，但制表留待后续工作。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel algorithm for predicting conditional joint distributions of vector-valued targets in stochastic systems whose randomness is intrinsic rather than arising from observation errors or additive noise. Multivariate quantile regression also involves modeling conditional joint distributions but represents a less challenging task. It predicts the probability that vector-valued targets fall within predefined regions, identifies regions corresponding to predefined probability levels, or performs both tasks simultaneously. The proposed identification technique employs ensembles of Kolmogorov--Arnold networks (KANs) as flexible function approximators. Although the suggested technique is not theoretically restricted to KANs, KANs are particularly well suited to the proposed construction and are therefore used throughout this study. In addition to the training procedure, this work introduces a new discrepancy measure for joint distributions and a goodness-of-fit (GoF) test based on it. This GoF test was initially developed to validate and calibrate the proposed identification technique and is used here in an ad hoc manner. Although the test could be tabulated for broader use, such a tabulation is not pursued in this work. The test is also applicable more generally.

</details>


### [236] [A discrete generative model of neuronal spiking activity on microelectrode arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23907)
*Md Sayed Tanveer,Mohammed A. Mostajo-Radji,Ge Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于共享时空motif词汇表的离散生成模型，实现阵列级稀疏脉冲活动的生成建模，无需特定实验参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经活动生成模型通常假设固定的已排序神经元集合，但高密度微电极阵列产生的是极其稀疏、跨阵列的二进制脉冲体数据，观测到的电极子集在不同实验中会变化，因此需要新的建模方法。

Method: 构造离散生成模型，用共享的时空motif词汇表表示活动。采用残差向量量化自编码器学习motif词汇表，并用因子化掩码Transformer预测活动出现位置及每个活跃位置对应的motif。

Result: 在涵盖人脑类器官和急性离体人海马组织的31项实验上评估。motif被广泛复用，实验身份仅解释motif使用熵的9%，跨组织类型motif重叠度与组织内部相当。在独立评估表示质量时，体素级重建平均精度为匹配flat tokenizer的5.2倍；在掩码补全和自由生成任务中，站点级平均精度为匹配生成基线的1.4–2.6倍，并在全部四类生成指标上均优于基线。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了面向阵列级脉冲活动的紧凑、可复用表示，无需学习实验特定参数，为跨多种神经制备的生成建模提供了可扩展基础。

Abstract: Generative models of neural activity could help characterize tissue dynamics, compare experimental conditions, and simulate population activity for applications ranging from disease and drug-response studies to closed-loop experimentation. Existing approaches, however, typically assume a fixed set of sorted neurons, whereas high-density microelectrode arrays produce extremely sparse, array-wide binary spike volumes in which the observed subset of electrodes varies across assays. We introduce a discrete generative model that represents this activity using a shared vocabulary of spatiotemporal motifs. A residual vector-quantized autoencoder learns the motif vocabulary, while a factorized masked transformer predicts where activity occurs and which motif appears at each active location. We evaluate the model on 31 assays spanning human brain organoids and acute \emph{ex vivo} human hippocampal tissue. The learned motifs are broadly reused: assay identity explains only $9%$ of the entropy in motif use, and motif overlap across tissue types is comparable to overlap within them. When representation quality is evaluated independently of the generative prior, our approach achieves $5.2\times$ the voxel-level reconstruction average precision of a matched flat tokenizer. For masked completion and free generation, the full model achieves $1.4$--$2.6\times$ the site-level average precision of the matched generative baseline and outperforms it across all four families of generation metrics. These results establish a compact, reusable representation for array-wide spiking activity without learned assay-specific parameters, providing a scalable foundation for generative modeling across diverse neural preparations.

</details>


### [237] [Matched-Input Estimates Differ in Sign Across Architectures: Auditing EEG Foundation Models on Motor Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23924)
*Kevin Zhou,Sparsh Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文审计了LaBraM和CBraMod两个EEG基础模型在运动想象任务上的迁移表现，发现在严格验证锁定协议下，四分类BCI IV-2a上所有监督基线均优于基础模型；且输入管线（宽带vs.窄带）是重要混淆因素，不同架构的匹配输入精度差异符号相反，说明单一对照器无法给出架构不变的性能差距分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练EEG基础模型被广泛提议作为脑机接口的通用编码器，但近期基准测试对其表征何时能迁移到下游任务存在分歧。作者希望厘清这种分歧的来源，特别是评估协议和输入管线差异带来的混淆。

Method: 采用验证锁定协议（validation-locked protocol）审计LaBraM和CBraMod：预处理、架构、优化、冻结深度、检查点、温度和模型选择均仅用训练会话数据确定。在四分类BCI Competition IV-2a上与监督基线对比，并将三个监督架构在基础模型所使用的宽带数组上重新训练以实现匹配输入比较；同时在两分类BNCI2014-004上检验结论的可复现性，并用验证集拟合的温度缩放检验校准。

Result: 四分类BCI IV-2a上，所有监督对照器均优于所有基础模型配置（包括验证选择的微调）。匹配输入后，宽带输入使ATCNet精度提升0.078，却使EEG Conformer精度下降0.088，符号相反；在n=9时三个匹配输入项经多重比较校正后均不显著。四分类差距在BNCI2014-004上未能复现。温度缩放可将基础模型校准误差降至监督范围，尽管其四分类精度仍显著更低。

Conclusion: 单一对照器可能无法提供架构不变的“预训练vs监督”性能差距分解；基础模型的不足并非普遍存在，而是依赖于数据集类别数与输入管线。评估EEG基础模型时需严格控制输入管线并谨慎解读单一架构对照结果。

Abstract: Pretrained EEG foundation models are increasingly proposed as general-purpose encoders for brain-computer interfaces, yet recent benchmarks disagree about when their representations transfer to downstream tasks. We audit LaBraM and CBraMod on motor imagery under a validation-locked protocol in which preprocessing, architecture, optimization, freeze depth, checkpoint, temperature, and method selection are determined using training-session data only. On four-class BCI Competition IV-2a, every supervised comparator evaluated here outperforms every foundation-model configuration, including validation-selected fine-tuning. We then examine a key confound: foundation models and task-specific decoders are normally evaluated using different input pipelines. Retraining three supervised architectures on the broadband arrays consumed by the foundation models produces matched-input accuracy differences of opposite sign across architectures: broadband input improves ATCNet by 0.078 accuracy while reducing EEG Conformer accuracy by 0.088. None of the three individual matched-input terms is significant after multiple-comparison correction at n = 9, so we treat the sign variation descriptively rather than as a formal architecture-by-pipeline interaction. These observed sign differences suggest that a single comparator may not provide an architecture-invariant decomposition of a pretrained-versus-supervised performance gap. The four-class deficit also does not reproduce uniformly across motor-imagery datasets: on two-class BNCI2014-004 we cannot detect the same separation between fine-tuned CBraMod and the supervised comparators. Finally, validation-fitted temperature scaling returns foundation-model calibration error to the supervised range despite substantially lower four-class accuracy.

</details>


### [238] [The Neural Forcing for Three-Dimensional Incompressible Navier-Stokes finite time blowup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23934)
*Beibei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个两部分神经网络框架，用于研究三维不可压缩Navier–Stokes方程的强迫流问题：第一部分构建外力生成与优化系统，第二部分提供从计算到连续统的认证与有限时间奇点判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维不可压缩Navier–Stokes方程的光滑解的有限时间爆破是数学与流体力学中的核心难题。作者希望通过神经网络搜索外部强迫项，寻找可能诱发Riccati型增长与有限时间光滑性丧失的强迫轨迹，并用严格的连续统分析加以验证。

Method: 第一部分：物理信息神经模型生成结构化外力轨迹，通过可微PDE展开或PPO-Clip对候选强迫进行优化，冻结选定的强迫并用独立固定强迫回放检验。第二部分：将神经候选发现与连续统分析分离，推导互易涡量积分判据以导出Riccati型增长和有限时间光滑延拓失效，发展经过验证的计算到连续统转移策略，并在非退化神经输出分布下建立条件性正概率闭合论证。

Result: 框架在连续统层面完成证明；得到互易涡量积分判据可推出Riccati型增长和有限时间光滑性丧失；建立了计算到连续统的经过验证的转移策略；在非退化神经输出律下给出条件性正概率闭合结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将神经网络驱动的候选强迫搜索与严格的连续统数学认证相结合，为研究三维Navier–Stokes强迫流的有限时间奇点提供了新的两阶段方法，且证明在连续统层面完整。

Abstract: We present a two-part neural framework for forced three-dimensional incompressible Navier--Stokes flow. Part~I develops the computational forcing system. A physics-informed neural model generates structured external-force trajectories, candidates are optimized through differentiable PDE rollouts or PPO-Clip, and selected forcings are frozen and checked by independent fixed-force replay. Part~II provides the mathematical certification layer. It separates neural candidate discovery from continuum analysis, derives integrated reciprocal-vorticity criteria that imply Riccati-type growth and finite-time loss of smooth continuation, develops a validated computational-to-continuum transfer strategy, and establishes a conditional positive-probability closure for a nondegenerate neural output law. The proof is complete at the continuum level.

</details>


### [239] [MGRD: Compact morphology-gated residual diffusion for variance-aware cross-domain neurite forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23990)
*Tsung Yeh Hsieh,Cosmin Anitescu,Chunghwan Kim,Victoria A. Webster-Wood,Yongjie Jessica Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MGRD：紧凑随机替代模型，联合预测神经突形态，提升精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期延时成像监测神经突形态资源消耗大、难以规模化，现有数字孪生模型（如gSTA）仅给出单一确定性预测，无法刻画多种可能未来的变异性。

Method: 提出形态门控残差扩散（MGRD），一种紧凑的随机替代模型，基于10帧观测联合预测20帧未来神经突形态，并以最新观测导出的形态特征作为条件；重复采样提供病例级方差分数用于排序预测难度。

Result: 在相场轨迹上，轨迹级平均MAE较匹配对照降低9.7%，更新参数少4.46倍；在人类iPSC神经元显微图像上四项指标均优于gSTA，MAE降39.6%、骨架F1升45.3%；无需小鼠域再训练即可泛化至小鼠皮层神经球数据；保留约60%低方差样本使MAE降17.6%（iPSC）和16.8%（仿真）。

Conclusion: MGRD参数量仅为gSTA的1.01%，训练更新时间不足其十分之一，缓存形态特征时DDIM轨迹生成快7.9%，是跨仿真与显微数据的紧凑随机神经突形态预测与病例优先级排序模型。

Abstract: Tracking neurite morphology over time helps characterize structural changes during neuronal development and deterioration, but long-term time-lapse imaging is resource-intensive and difficult to scale. Forecasting future morphology could reduce this burden. Existing neurite digital-twin models such as gated spatiotemporal attention (gSTA) produce a single deterministic forecast without representing variability among plausible futures. We introduce Morphology-Gated Residual Diffusion (MGRD), a compact stochastic surrogate that jointly forecasts twenty future neurite-morphology frames from ten observed frames while conditioning on morphology features derived from the latest observation. On controlled phase-field trajectories, MGRD reduces trajectory-wise mean MAE by 9.7% relative to a matched control while updating 4.46 times fewer parameters. On human iPSC-derived neuron microscopy, MGRD improves all four reported metrics over gSTA, including a 39.6% reduction in trajectory-wise mean MAE and a 45.3% increase in skeleton F1. Without mouse-domain retraining or fine-tuning, MGRD also improves MAE and skeleton F1 on mouse cortical-neurosphere microscopy across 10-40-min sampling intervals and forecast horizons beyond 13 hours. Repeated sampling provides a case-level variance score for ranking forecast difficulty. Retaining approximately 60% of the lowest-variance cases reduces mean MAE by 17.6% on iPSC microscopy and 16.8% on simulation data. MGRD uses 1.01% of gSTA's parameters, requires less than one tenth of its training-update time, and generates a 50-step DDIM trajectory 7.9% faster when morphology features are cached. These results establish MGRD as a compact stochastic surrogate for neurite-morphology forecasting and case prioritization across simulation and microscopy datasets.

</details>


### [240] [Simpler Methods Work Better for L1 Penalized Logistic Models and Large Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23995)
*Edward Raff,James Holt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评测了文献中多种声称"最先进"的L1正则线性模型求解器，发现在大规模工业任务中，旧方法反而远优于新方法，而一个被学术界因理论不收敛而否定的简单基线（LBFGS配次梯度）经微调后表现极佳且更易生产部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L1正则线性模型在超高维（d>1,000,000）工业任务中仍是主流，但许多L1求解器通用性差、速度慢、并行化效果不佳，难以在MLOps流水线中训练大规模工业语料。作者希望澄清哪些求解器真正适用于生产，并指出学术研究易产生过度自信结论的问题。

Method: 对文献中若干被宣称"最先进"的L1求解器进行实验测试与对比，同时提出一个简单基线：在次梯度上使用LBFGS并做少量微调。

Result: 实验表明旧方法在通用性上目前远优于新提出的方法；被理论判定不收敛的LBFGS次梯度基线在实践中表现极具竞争力，且更易支持与扩展。

Conclusion: 作者向学术界提出若干建议，以避免产生过度自信的结果、阻碍成果向生产转化；并认为简单但实用的方法在工业生产环境下应获得更多重视。

Abstract: Linear models with an $L_1$-norm penalty remain state-of-the-art for high-dimensional ($d > 1,000,000$) tasks, offering a straightforward method for solving real-world industry problems. Despite their widespread use in industry and utility, many $L_1$ solvers are not effective for general use, are prohibitively slow, and are ineffective in parallelization. This makes them difficult to train in an MLOps pipeline on large industry-scale corpora. In this work, we test several proposed ``state-of-the-art'' solutions from the literature and find that older methods are currently far superior for general use. We also identify several recommendations for academics to perform research that avoids erroneously overconfident results, which can prevent the transition to production use. Equally surprising, we find that a new and simple baseline, using LBFGS on a sub-gradient, is highly effective with minor tweaks, despite being dismissed in the literature for theoretical non-convergence. In practice, we find it is an easier-to-support and easier-to-scale method for production use.

</details>


### [241] [Misaligned Clinical Risk Classification and Cost Asymmetry in Open-Weight Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23999)
*Star S. D. Liu,Xiyu Ding,Robert B. Barrett,Alberto Santamaria-Pang,Nic Dobbins,Harold P. Lehmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究四款开源大模型（Qwen-2.5-7B/32B、Llama-3.1-8B/70B）如何在内部表征临床成本权衡，发现模型可线性恢复患者风险与成本方向，但无法可靠地将其整合为符合成本取向的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在临床决策中如何将患者风险与临床成本权衡相结合尚不清楚。既有评估多关注预测性能，而忽视成本/代价结构与措辞敏感性问题，因此需要探究LLM内部表征与行为输出是否一致地反映成本取向与幅度。

Method: 使用公开糖尿病数据集，设定11种假阴性（FN）与假阳性（FP）成本比，并在三种措辞表述下测试四个开源权重模型；通过线性探针等方法考察内部表征，并对比其行为输出，检验成本方向、幅度以及镜像对称编码假设。

Result: 患者风险可线性恢复，性能与传统分类器相当（AUC约0.83），成本方向在每个模型中都可识别；但成本方向的表征变化仅在两个较大模型中与输出变化同步，成本幅度响应多为方向不敏感。12种模型-措辞组合中仅2种同时呈现对FN与FP成本上升的相反响应及成本正确的排序。在单侧成本上拟合的方向迁移到另一侧时并未反转，与镜像对称编码预期不符。

Conclusion: LLM能够编码风险与成本信息，但不能可靠地将其整合为成本正确的决策。临床评估除预测性能外，还应纳入权衡测试、措辞敏感性与默认操作点。

Abstract: How large language models (LLMs) integrate patient risk with clinical cost tradeoffs remains poorly understood. We investigated how four open-weight LLMs (Qwen-2.5-7B/32B and Llama-3.1-8B/70B) internally represent cost tradeoffs, how these representations relate to clinical predictions, and whether decisions shift as predicted by the specified cost direction and magnitude. Using a public diabetes dataset, we varied 11 false-negative (FN) to false-positive (FP) cost ratios across three phrasings and examined representations and behavioral outputs. Patient risk was linearly recoverable on par with conventional classifiers (AUC $\approx 0.83$), and cost direction was recoverable in every model. However, representational shifts in cost direction tracked output changes only in the two larger models, and responses to cost magnitude were predominantly direction-agnostic. Only 2 of 12 model-phrasings showed both opposing responses to increasing FN versus FP costs and cost-correct ordering. Representationally, a direction fitted on one cost side did not invert when transferred to the other, as expected under mirror-symmetric encoding. These findings suggest that LLMs encode risk and cost information but do not reliably integrate them into cost-correct decisions. Clinical evaluations should therefore include tradeoff tests, phrasing sensitivity, and default operating points alongside predictive performance.

</details>


### [242] [ShapeLex: Decoupling Local Shape Symbolization and Global Scale Modeling for Text-Controlled Time Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24003)
*Subo Wei,Jianqi Gao,Mingyan Fan,Shaorong Xie,Xinzhi Wang,Yongpeng Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ShapeLex，将文本控制的时间序列生成解耦为局部形状的离散符号化与全局属性的连续建模，在12个公开数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有范式在单一连续隐空间中同时进行语义理解与序列建模，缺乏显式的局部语义锚点，也未能区分全局连续属性与局部离散形状，导致关键局部结构被平滑、遗漏或错位。

Method: ShapeLex分为三部分：从训练数据中归纳可复用的离散形状单元词表（如上升、尖峰、骤降），构建可解释的符号空间；用自回归生成器按文本描述选择形状、调整位置与持续时间等属性，并按时间顺序组合为形状骨架；最后用混合密度尺度头对整体水平与波动率建模采样，恢复真实的全局尺度。

Result: 在12个公开数据集、真实用户撰写文本以及下游预测任务上，ShapeLex生成的序列比现有方法更贴合真实数据分布；同时可从学习到的词表自动合成配对监督，避免随数据规模增长的标注成本，提升可扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过离散形状符号与连续全局属性的解耦建模，ShapeLex在生成质量和可扩展性上均取得优势，并提供了可解释的符号空间。

Abstract: Text-controlled time series generation aims to synthesize sequences that follow natural-language descriptions while remaining faithful to real data distributions. Existing paradigms often couple semantic understanding and sequence modeling in a single continuous latent space, lacking explicit local semantic anchors and separation between global continuous attributes and local discrete shapes. As a result, key local structures may be smoothed, missed, or misplaced. We propose Shape Lexicon (ShapeLex), which decouples text-to-sequence generation into discrete symbolization of local shapes and continuous modeling of global attributes. ShapeLex first induces a reusable vocabulary of discrete shape units, such as rises, spikes, and sharp drops, from training data, forming an interpretable symbolic space. An autoregressive generator then selects shapes according to the textual description, adjusts attributes such as position and duration, and composes them in temporal order into a shape skeleton. Finally, a mixture-density scale head models and samples the overall level and volatility to restore realistic global scale. Experiments on twelve public datasets, real user-written text, and downstream forecasting tasks show that ShapeLex generates series that better match real data distributions than existing methods. In addition, paired supervision is automatically synthesized from the learned vocabulary, avoiding annotation costs that grow with dataset size and improving scalability.

</details>


### [243] [Graph-to-Grid (G2G): Continuous-Coordinate Feature Painting for Soccer Pass Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24040)
*Kaan Günay,Orhun Gun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评测了稠密传球面（dense pass surface）系统中"坐标涂绘"（painting）这一接口选择：将球员特征按实测坐标双线性散布到网格上，而非把状态读作逐格计数的栅格。在2022世界杯53,628次传球上，涂绘使选择似然比纯栅格输入提升约四分之一 nat，且该增益可通过十三项预设研究分解定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有绘制传球面的网络把状态读成逐单元计数的栅格，丢失了球员在单元内部的精确位置；LiDAR检测、BEV感知与图天气模型等系统也各自采用将实体特征映射到网格的接口，但缺乏对这些接口本身的定量评估。本文旨在测量输入接口（栅格化 vs. 坐标涂绘）对传球面预测的实际影响。

Method: 在双线性插值形式下，把每名球员的特征按其实测坐标双线性散布到网格上，使传球面损失端到端训练每球员编码器。以2022世界杯53,628次传球为数据，进行八折交叉验证、每条对照臂五种随机种子调参，并在另一数据提供方的7场德甲及德乙比赛上重训验证；通过十三项预设研究分解增益来源，并与其他变体（偏移通道、更细栅格、注意力涂绘器、无栅格解码器）及SoccerMap、标准U-Net对比。

Result: 涂绘相对同一核心的纯栅格输入，使选择似然提升约四分之一 nat，且在每个比赛、每折交叉验证与集成到七场德甲比赛后均成立。用九个原始球员特征涂绘而不加编码器即贡献约四分之三的增益；学习编码器与消息传递带来较小但显著的增量。涂绘也能提升原版SoccerMap与标准U-Net，而偏移通道、更细栅格、注意力涂绘器与无栅格解码器无此效果。跨提供方冻结时似然优势消失，注入跟踪误差会压缩该优势。

Conclusion: 输入接口（坐标涂绘 vs. 逐格栅格）对传球面预测有可测量的实质影响，且主要通过保留球员在单元内的精确位置实现；但该结果仅适用于观测端点预测，并非对假设传球的校准评估。

Abstract: Dense pass surfaces give, for every pitch cell, whether a pass played there would arrive, whether the carrier would choose it, and what the possession would then be worth. The networks that draw them read the state as a raster of per-cell counts, losing where inside a cell each player stands. LiDAR detectors, bird's-eye-view perception and graph weather models move entity features onto a grid, binning each entity to a cell or learning the transfer. We evaluate the interpolated form: each player's features are scattered bilinearly onto the grid at the player's measured coordinates, so the surface loss trains the per-player encoder end to end. Those systems adopt an interface; this paper measures one. On 53,628 passes from the 2022 World Cup, painting improves selection likelihood over the same core fed rasters alone by about a quarter of a nat: in every match of an eight-fold cross-validation, with every arm tuned over five seeds, and after retraining on seven Bundesliga and 2. Bundesliga matches from another provider. Thirteen pre-specified studies locate the gain: painting the nine raw player features with no encoder carries three quarters of it, and the learned encoder and message passing add a smaller, resolved increment. Painting also helps the original SoccerMap and a canonical U-Net, whereas offset channels, a finer raster, an attention painter and a raster-free decoder do not. Frozen across the provider boundary the likelihood advantage is lost; injected tracking error compresses it. These results concern observed-endpoint prediction, not calibrated evaluation of hypothetical passes.

</details>


### [244] [Q-DEQ: Discrete Solving and Quantization for Deep Equilibrium Models in Time Series Forecasting under Edge Deployment Coding Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24042)
*Ruotong Yang,Hongdong Zhu,Qi Gao,Yin Ma,Hai Wei,Kai Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Q-DEQ，将深度均衡模型（DEQ）前向求解中的局部更新建模为离散优化问题，用QUBO结合模拟退火或相干伊辛机求解，并配合W8A8量化，在时间序列预测中以更少参数实现与显式多层基线相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘部署需要参数量小、低位表示的预测模型。DEQ通过重复使用共享层获得隐式深度，降低参数开销，但其常用的Anderson求解器在连续实数域中搜索更新系数，未充分利用离散/低位部署的优势。

Method: Q-DEQ将DEQ前向求解中的局部更新表述为离散优化问题：由当前状态和迭代历史构造候选方向，用局部二次残差模型评估其组合；对方向系数进行二进制编码，得到可由模拟退火（SA）或相干伊辛机（CIM）求解的QUBO问题。定点求解后进行再前向传递，对共享层的权重和激活施加W8A8伪量化。

Result: 在iTransformer骨干和五个多变量时间序列预测数据集上评估。相对显式多层基线的MSE差异范围为-1.16%至+2.90%，在两个数据集上MSE更低。DEQ参数共享使参数量减少1.80×–3.82×；结合W8A8后静态权重存储减少4.3×–12.8×。使用CPU端SA与Kaiwu CIM物理后端求解的局部QUBO问题产生高度一致的下游预测。

Conclusion: 局部离散求解可作为DEQ时间序列预测的可行组成部分，并提供了通过不同组合优化后端执行定点更新的路径。

Abstract: Edge deployment motivates forecasting models with compact parameter storage and low-bit representations. Deep equilibrium models (DEQs) obtain implicit depth by repeatedly applying a shared layer, reducing the parameter cost of explicit layer stacking. Their usual Anderson solver, however, searches for update coefficients in the continuous real domain. We propose Q-DEQ, which formulates local updates in DEQ forward solving as discrete optimization problems. Candidate directions are constructed from the current state and iteration history, and a local quadratic residual model is used to evaluate their combinations. Binary encoding of the direction coefficients yields a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problem that can be solved by simulated annealing (SA) or a coherent Ising machine (CIM). After fixed-point solving, a re-forward pass applies W8A8 fake quantization to the shared layer's weights and activations. We evaluate Q-DEQ with an iTransformer backbone on five multivariate time series forecasting datasets. Relative MSE differences from the explicit multi-layer baseline range from $-1.16\%$ to $+2.90\%$, with lower MSE on two datasets. DEQ parameter sharing reduces parameter counts by factors of $1.80\times$--$3.82\times$; combined with W8A8, static weight storage is reduced by factors of $4.3\times$--$12.8\times$. Local QUBO problems solved using CPU-based SA and the Kaiwu CIM physical backend produce closely matching downstream forecasts. These results establish local discrete solving as a viable component of DEQ time series forecasting and provide a route for executing fixed-point updates through different combinatorial optimization backends.

</details>


### [245] [FlashBoB: I/O-Efficient Exact Backward-over-Backward for Softmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24089)
*Anthony Givans,Michael Crawshaw,Mingrui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FlashBoB，一种用于 softmax 注意力中 backward-over-backward（BoB）的精确、I/O 高效算法，避免所有 N×N 中间张量，将精确二阶注意力扩展到超长序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softmax 注意力是长上下文工作负载的主要瓶颈。FlashAttention 虽使前向和首次反向 I/O 高效，但不支持 BoB（对反向过程再做反向），而 BoB 对二阶优化、测试时训练、基于梯度的记忆和元学习等应用至关重要。现有 BoB 实现要么物化大型中间张量，要么在长序列下耗尽 GPU 显存。

Method: 关键洞察是 softmax 双反向中的分层仿射结构：两个逐行标量通过仿射变换决定所有输出。据此设计两趟调度方案，将计算保持在片上 tile 内，SRAM 使用有界，并最小化 HBM 流量，避免所有 N×N 中间张量。

Result: FlashBoB 达到 Θ(N²d²/M) 的 HBM 流量（d 为头维度，M 为内存大小），在标准 FlashAttention 式分数重计算模型下，匹配精确前向注意力的大缓存下界。在单张 A100 80GB 上，将精确注意力 BoB 扩展到 N=262K，而此前 PyTorch 精确基线在 N=16K 即失败，且比 FlashBack 快至多 6.3 倍。

Conclusion: FlashBoB 使精确二阶注意力在长上下文序列长度下变得实用，而此前实现无法高效运行。

Abstract: Transformer models built on the attention mechanism have become a central building block in modern deep learning, yet softmax attention remains a major bottleneck for long-context workloads. While FlashAttention makes the forward and first backward passes I/O-efficient, it does not support backward-over-backward (BoB), which enables exact differentiation through the backward pass for applications such as second-order optimization, test-time training, gradient-based memory, and meta-learning. Existing BoB implementations either materialize large intermediate tensors or exhaust GPU memory at long sequence lengths. We present FlashBoB, an exact, I/O-efficient algorithm for BoB in softmax attention that keeps computation within on-chip tiles and avoids all $N \times N$ intermediate tensors, where $N$ is the sequence length. The key insight is a hierarchical affine structure in the softmax double backward: two row-wise scalars determine all outputs through affine transformations. This yields a two-pass schedule with bounded on-chip static random-access memory (SRAM) usage and minimal off-chip high-bandwidth memory (HBM) traffic. FlashBoB achieves $Θ(N^2 d^2/M)$ HBM traffic ($d$ is the head dimension and $M$ is the memory size) and, within the standard FlashAttention-style score-recomputation model, matches the inherited large-cache lower bound for exact forward attention. Empirically, it scales exact attention BoB to $N=262\text{K}$ on a single A100 80GB GPU, where prior PyTorch exact baselines fail by $N=16\text{K}$, and is up to $6.3\times$ faster than FlashBack. These results make exact second-order attention practical at long-context sequence lengths where prior implementations cannot run efficiently.

</details>


### [246] [Reinforcement Learning under State and Outcome Uncertainty: A Foundational Distributional Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24103)
*Larry Preuett,Qiuyi Zhang,Muhammad Aurangzeb Ahmad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将分布式强化学习（DistRL）从全可观测域扩展到部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），提出新的分布式贝尔曼算子并证明其在 supremum p-Wasserstein 度量下的收敛性，同时用 psi-vectors 给出回报分布的有限表示，进而提出 DPBVI 算法，为部分可观测下的风险敏感控制奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实规划任务中，智能体需同时应对环境状态的不确定性以及所选策略结果的可变性。现有 DistRL 仅适用于全可观测域，无法建模部分可观测情形下的回报分布，而这对需要管理罕见高影响事件的风险敏感算法至关重要。

Method: 1) 将 DistRL 扩展到 POMDP，使智能体可为每个条件计划学习回报分布；2) 引入适用于部分可观测性的新分布式贝尔曼算子，并在 supremum p-Wasserstein 度量下证明其收敛性；3) 提出 psi-vectors 作为回报分布的有限表示，推广了 POMDP 求解器中的经典 alpha-vectors；4) 基于此提出 Distributional Point-Based Value Iteration（DPBVI），将 psi-vectors 集成到标准 point-based backup 过程中。

Result: 证明了新分布式贝尔曼算子在 supremum p-Wasserstein 度量下的收敛性；给出了基于 psi-vectors 的回报分布有限表示，并成功实现 DPBVI，将 DistRL 与 POMDP 规划相衔接；同时提供了源代码以促进相关研究。

Conclusion: DPBVI 通过跟踪回报分布，为部分可观测域中需谨慎管理罕见高影响事件的风险敏感控制奠定了基础，并推动了鲁棒决策在部分可观测条件下的进一步研究。

Abstract: In many real-world planning tasks, agents must tackle uncertainty about the environment's state and variability in the outcomes of any chosen policy. We address both forms of uncertainty as a first step toward safer algorithms in partially observable settings. Specifically, we extend Distributional Reinforcement Learning (DistRL)-which models the entire return distribution for fully observable domains-to Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs), allowing an agent to learn the distribution of returns for each conditional plan. Concretely, we introduce new distributional Bellman operators for partial observability and prove their convergence under the supremum p-Wasserstein metric. We also propose a finite representation of these return distributions via psi-vectors, generalizing the classical alpha-vectors in POMDP solvers. Building on this, we develop Distributional Point-Based Value Iteration (DPBVI), which integrates psi-vectors into a standard point-based backup procedure-bridging DistRL and POMDP planning. By tracking return distributions, DPBVI lays the foundation for future risk-sensitive control in domains where rare, high-impact events must be carefully managed. We provide source code to foster further research in robust decision-making under partial observability.

</details>


### [247] [SPeaR: Test-Time Adaptation with Steering Primitives for Realigning Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24111)
*Muhammad Sudipto Siam Dip,Ali Etemad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPeaR方法，通过冻结预训练网络并调控其中间表征来实现测试时自适应（TTA），仅需插入轻量可学习模块即可在无源数据、无监督预热的条件下直接从测试流优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA方法通过更新参数适配预训练模型，这限制了可适配的对象范围以及网络内可发生适配的位置。作者希望突破参数更新的局限，转而调控中间表征。

Method: SPeaR在阶段边界插入轻量可学习模块，直接从测试流优化。每个模块采用门控目标函数（仅在适配有益时降低不确定性）、多样性正则化防止坍塌，以及多深度锚点稳定适配。研究发现调控早期表征最有效，且同一模块可在CNN与Transformer架构间迁移。

Result: 在CIFAR-10-C、CIFAR-100-C和ImageNet-C上，SPeaR持续达到或超越那些适配参数多出数个数量级的方法，在广泛批次大小下保持稳健，并在持续适配中保持源域性能。

Conclusion: 通过冻结网络并调控中间表征，SPeaR为TTA提供了一种高效、可迁移且稳定的新范式，证明了调控表征比更新参数更具优势。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) addresses distribution shift using only unlabeled test data. Existing methods typically adapt pretrained models by updating their parameters, limiting both what is adapted and where adaptation can occur within the network. We instead keep the pretrained network frozen and steer its intermediate representations. We introduce SPeaR (Steering Primitive for Realigning Representations), which inserts lightweight learnable modules at stage boundaries and optimizes them directly from the test stream, requiring neither source data nor supervised warm-up. Each primitive is optimized using a gated objective that reduces uncertainty only when adaptation is beneficial, along with a diversity regularizer to prevent collapse, and a multi-depth anchor to stabilize adaptation. We show that steering early representations is the most effective strategy, and that the same primitive transfers across convolutional and Transformer architectures. Across CIFAR-10-C, CIFAR-100-C, and ImageNet-C, SPeaR consistently matches or outperforms methods that adapt orders of magnitude more parameters, remains robust across a wide range of batch sizes, and preserves source-domain performance during continual adaptation.

</details>


### [248] [PAC-Bayesian Meta-Learning for Few-Shot Identification of Linear Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24117)
*Chenfeng Huang,George Michailidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PBML-LTI，一个面向少样本LTI系统辨识的PAC-Bayes元学习框架，通过学习可迁移先验并做贝叶斯自适应，在时间相关的轨迹数据下提供准确估计与不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短、含噪、高维轨迹下的LTI系统辨识困难；传统方法独立处理各系统，无法利用相关系统间的共享结构；且LTI轨迹违反i.i.d.假设，使多数PAC-Bayes元学习分析失效。

Method: 每个任务对应一个未知LTI系统；元学习器用训练轨迹学习转移矩阵的数据依赖先验；对新系统在少量数据下做贝叶斯自适应得到任务后验。针对时间依赖，采用鞅PAC-Bayes分析处理依赖轨迹损失，导出support-query预测风险界，并据此提出fit-KL元训练目标。

Result: 得到适用于依赖轨迹损失的PAC-Bayes界，明确经验拟合、后验复杂度与先验质量在少样本自适应中的作用；并导出转移矩阵恢复与多步轨迹预测的推论，建立不确定性感知元辨识的有限样本保证。

Conclusion: PBML-LTI在保留任务异质性的同时实现可迁移先验学习，为依赖动力数据下的少样本系统辨识提供了带不确定性量化的理论保障。

Abstract: Identifying linear time-invariant (LTI) dynamical systems is challenging when trajectories are short, noisy, or high-dimensional. Traditional system identification typically treats each system independently and cannot exploit shared structure across related systems. We propose PBML-LTI, a PAC-Bayesian meta-learning framework for few-shot LTI system identification that learns a transferable prior over task-specific dynamics while preserving task heterogeneity. Each task corresponds to an unknown LTI system, and the meta-learner uses training trajectories to learn a data-dependent prior over transition matrices. For a new system with limited data, PBML-LTI performs Bayesian adaptation under this prior to obtain a task-specific posterior, providing accurate estimates and principled uncertainty quantification.
  A key challenge is temporal dependence, since LTI trajectories violate the i.i.d. assumptions underlying most PAC-Bayes meta-learning analyses. We address this with a martingale PAC-Bayes analysis for dependent trajectory losses and derive a support-query predictive-risk bound that motivates a fit-KL meta-training objective. The bound clarifies the roles of empirical fit, posterior complexity, and prior quality in few-shot adaptation under sequential dependence. We further derive corollaries for transition-matrix recovery and multi-step trajectory prediction, connecting uncertainty-aware meta-identification with finite-sample guarantees for dependent dynamical data.

</details>


### [249] [CLOOPD: Closing the Learner Loop in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24141)
*Keye Zheng,Hanyu Li,Zhan Cheng,Yuan Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出闭环框架CLOOPD，将教师信号获取与学生侧实现分离，通过多次actor更新充分复用已评分批次，在更少教师token和GPU工时下提升蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在on-policy蒸馏中，每批数据需学生生成轨迹、教师评分，代价高昂，但现有方法通常只做一次actor更新就丢弃该信号，导致教师评分token利用率低、性价比不高。作者希望将“信号获取”与“学生实现”解耦，在固定token预算下更充分地利用教师信号。

Method: CLOOPD在KL包络内选取自适应α路点，冻结已评分批次及其优势值，每次actor更新后重新前向学生、度量实现程度，并在独立的token预算下分配actor工作量。框架包含确定性的两遍、三遍策略，按token定价的CLOOPD-TPMR，以及预算匹配的对照设置。

Result: 在8-H20节点上六组300步运行中，各CLOOPD策略在相近教师token规模下均优于单遍TOP-D基线：宏观准确率由15.41提升至CLOOPD-Fixed2的17.78和CLOOPD-Fixed3的19.36。第100步时CLOOPD-Fixed3达15.35，接近TOP-D第300步水平，同时教师评分token减少67.2%、GPU工时减少28.0%。早前8-A100消融显示自适应α消除了观察到的信任包络违规，第三次迭代带来额外提升空间。

Conclusion: CLOOPD将蒸馏重新定义为“如何为学生从教师评分token中学习设置预算”的框架，在相同教师成本下显著提升性能，为on-policy蒸馏的信号复用与算力分配提供了有效范式。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) pays twice for each fresh batch: the student generates trajectories and a stronger teacher scores them. Existing methods improve which trajectories are scored and how the teacher signal is constructed, but usually consume it with one actor update. We introduce CLOOPD, a closed-loop framework separating teacher-signal acquisition from student-side realization. CLOOPD selects an adaptive $α$ waypoint inside a KL envelope, freezes the scored batch and its advantages, re-forwards the student after each actor pass, measures realization, and allocates actor work under a separate token budget. The framework includes deterministic two- and three-pass policies, token-priced CLOOPD-TPMR, and a budget-matched control. Across six 300-step runs on an 8-H20 node, every CLOOPD policy improves the one-pass TOP-D anchor at comparable teacher-token scale: macro accuracy rises from 15.41 to 17.78 with CLOOPD-Fixed2 and 19.36 with CLOOPD-Fixed3. At step 100, CLOOPD-Fixed3 reaches 15.35, nearly matching TOP-D at step 300 while using 67.2% fewer teacher-scored tokens and 28.0% fewer GPU-hours. Earlier 8-A100 ablations show adaptive $α$ eliminates observed trust-envelope violations; a third pass adds headroom. These results position CLOOPD as a framework for budgeting how fully students learn from teacher-scored tokens.

</details>


### [250] [Luck Is Not Skill: When Do Paired Rollouts Help Group-Relative RL of LLM Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24144)
*Nazmus Sakib*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究配对 rollout（组内共享事件键噪声调度）在组相对强化学习中的方差效应，发现配对可降低 reward-contrast 方差但不必然降低梯度方差，实验显示工具故障下提升最终测试成功率但未达预注册学习曲线标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组相对强化学习通过在组内比较同一 prompt 的多个 rollout 来估计优势，但独立的环境噪声会干扰这些比较。作者希望探究通过"配对 rollout"（组内共享事件键噪声调度、同时保持各 rollout 边际分布不变）能否减少比较方差并改善学习。

Method: 理论分析配对对 reward-contrast 方差与梯度方差的影响，针对单边 grader 噪声推导出方差降低的精确条件并给出反例；开展对照实验，在工具故障和 grader 翻转两种噪声下训练 2B 工具使用智能体，每种设计 3 个随机种子，协议经注册且披露了先前完成的试点；另在来自两条故障训练轨迹的 8 个不同 checkpoint 上做梯度探针测量。

Result: 工具故障下配对使最终含噪测试成功率平均提升 +5.1 个百分点，三个种子差异均为正，但未达注册的学习曲线标准。grader 翻转下同样未达标准，验证 AUC 差异为 +0.003（95% 区间 [-0.029, +0.033]）。梯度探针显示两种噪声下 mean-centered 协方差迹均更低：grader 翻转降低 21–30%，工具故障降低 40–63%。

Conclusion: 有限样本测量支持方差机制的存在，但不足以确立普遍的学习速度收益。研究区分了三件常被混淆的事：改善奖励比较、降低估计量方差、改善学习效果——配对能改善奖励比较并降低方差，却不必然带来更快的学习。

Abstract: Group-relative reinforcement learning compares rollouts of the same prompt, but independent environment noise can obscure these comparisons. We study paired rollouts, which share an event-keyed noise schedule within each group while preserving each rollout's marginal distribution. Pairing removes the between-schedule component of reward-contrast variance, but need not reduce gradient variance. For one-sided grader noise, we derive an exact condition for reduction and give a counterexample in which reward contrasts improve while gradient variance increases. A controlled study trains a 2B tool-use agent under tool faults and grader flips, with three seeds per design. The protocol was registered with a disclosed, previously completed pilot. Under tool faults, pairing improves final noisy-test success by +5.1 percentage points on average, with all three seed differences positive, but misses the registered learning-curve criterion. The criterion is also missed under grader flips: the validation-AUC difference is +0.003 (95% interval [-0.029, +0.033]). A gradient probe on eight distinct checkpoints from two fault-trained trajectories finds lower mean-centered covariance traces under both noise types: 21 to 30% for grader flips and 40 to 63% for tool faults. These finite-sample measurements support the variance mechanism without establishing a general learning-speed benefit. The results distinguish improving reward comparisons, reducing estimator variance, and improving learning.

</details>


### [251] [Mind or Message? Auditing Theory of Mind in Multi-Agent Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24146)
*Cong Li,Cheng Chen,Thomas Fung,Alex Rossi,Yi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 语言模型智能体在多议题谈判中社交流畅但经济低效：96.2%达成协议、0协议失败，却仅0.7%落在帕累托前沿、错失20.5%共同价值、76.6%漏掉完全利益一致议题；探针显示其依赖自我投射而非对伙伴心理的建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体被广泛用于模拟社会互动，其对话记录看起来像是彼此理解。本文要追问：这种"理解"表象究竟建立在对伙伴心理的建模上，还是仅建立在对伙伴言辞的表面记录上。作者构建一个两个问题都有精确答案的社会模拟来区分这两种可能。

Method: 构建40个多议题谈判，其隐藏偏好权重与完整帕累托前沿均由构造已知；两个模型家族在160个二元组中谈判，所有对话记录在测量前冻结；随后用2880次反事实探针在保持证据字节完全一致的前提下，每次只变动一个因素：读者自身利益、伙伴语气、身份标签、递归顺序。

Result: 智能体社交流畅但经济低效：96.2%的二元组达成协议且0协议失败，但仅0.7%的交易落在帕累托前沿，未获取20.5%的可得联合价值，76.6%的交易漏掉双方利益完全一致的那个议题；在前沿和该一致议题上，从双方都会接受的方案集中随机抽取的表现与之相当。探针定位了失败来源：仅调换读者自身的收益表（伙伴言辞与提议不变）就使推断出的首要优先项变动15.0个百分点，属自我中心投射而非推断；语气改写使其变动5.3个百分点，身份标签为0.0。最具说明性的是，智能体预测伙伴对自己的看法有72.5%的命中率，而该伙伴的看法本身正确率仅51.2%——智能体对对话的追踪远好于对对话背后心智的追踪。

Conclusion: 语言模型智能体在谈判中表现出的"相互理解"主要是对话表层记录的产物，而非对伙伴心理模型的推断：其优先级判断受自身利益驱动的自我中心投射影响最大，对伙伴信念的把握也远超伙伴信念本身的准确性。这表明社会模拟中的流畅表象不能等同于真正的心理建模。

Abstract: Language model agents are increasingly used to simulate social interaction, and the resulting transcripts read as though the agents understand one another. We ask whether that appearance rests on a model of the partner's mind or on the surface record of what the partner said. We build a social simulation in which both questions have exact answers: 40 multi-issue negotiations whose hidden preference weights and whose full Pareto frontier are known by construction. Two model families negotiate across 160 dyads, every transcript is frozen before any measurement, and 2880 counterfactual probes then hold the evidence byte identical while moving one factor at a time: the reader's own stake, the partner's tone, an identity label, and the order of recursion. The agents are socially fluent and economically poor. They reach agreement in 96.2% of dyads with 0 protocol failures, yet only 0.7% of deals land on the Pareto frontier, they leave 20.5% of the available joint value unclaimed, and they miss the one issue on which their interests are perfectly aligned in 76.6% of deals; on the frontier and on that aligned issue, a package drawn at random from the set both sides would accept does as well. The probes locate the failure. Swapping only the reader's own payoff sheet, while the partner's words and offers stay identical, moves the inferred top priority by 15.0 percentage points, which is egocentric projection rather than inference, while a tone rewrite moves it by 5.3 percentage points and an identity label by 0.0. Most tellingly, an agent predicts what its partner believes about it 72.5% of the time while that partner's belief is itself correct only 51.2% of the time: the agents track the conversation far better than they track the mind behind it.

</details>


### [252] [Acceptance-Aware Draft Model Training for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24150)
*Tianhua Xia,Mugilan Ganesan,Yifei Feng,Haiyu Wang,Maximilian Egger,Sai Qian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向接受长度的训练损失（EAL与WTV），直接优化投机解码中的期望接受长度，在多种模型与解码设置下均优于KL式训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码的加速效果主要由接受长度决定，但现有草稿模型训练方法多以交叉熵或KL散度为代理目标，只鼓励分布匹配，并未直接优化接受长度，且贪心解码与采样解码的接受机制不同。

Method: 提出直接优化投机窗口内期望接受token数的损失：针对贪心验证推导期望接受长度（EAL）损失；针对采样解码提出窗口全变差（WTV）损失，优化温度缩放后草稿与目标分布的 overlap 并考虑序列接受依赖；两者可进一步结合以模拟接受长度为奖励的组相对强化学习（GRPO）阶段。

Result: 在不同目标模型与草稿模型、任务及解码设置下，所提损失持续提升接受长度，优于基于KL的训练；WTV在采样解码下增益尤为显著，EAL更契合贪心验证。

Conclusion: 直接优化接受目标、并针对解码模式设计损失，比传统分布匹配目标更有效。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by using a lightweight draft model to generate multiple candidate tokens that are verified by the target model in a single forward pass. Its speedup is largely determined by the acceptance length, yet existing draft-model training methods mainly optimize cross-entropy or Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence as proxies. These objectives encourage distribution matching but do not directly optimize acceptance length, and the acceptance mechanism also differs between greedy and sampling-based decoding.
  In this work, we propose acceptance-length-aware training losses that directly optimize the expected number of accepted tokens within a speculative window. For greedy verification, we derive an expected accepted length (EAL) loss that explicitly maximizes expected acceptance length. For sampling-based decoding, we introduce a window total variation (WTV) loss that optimizes the overlap between temperature-scaled draft and target distributions while accounting for sequential acceptance dependencies. Both objectives can be further combined with a group-relative reinforcement learning stage (GRPO) using simulated acceptance length as the reward.
  Experiments across different target and draft models, tasks, and decoding settings show that our losses consistently improve acceptance length over KL-based training. WTV provides particularly strong gains under sampling-based decoding, while EAL better matches greedy verification. These results show that directly optimizing the acceptance objective, with losses tailored to the decoding mode, is more effective than conventional distribution-matching objectives.

</details>


### [253] [H-Spec: Parallel Speculative Decoding Without a Drafter-Side KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24197)
*Weifan Jiang,Krishna Teja Chitty-Venkata,Megan Flynn,Reed Meyerson,Zhenting Qi,Tianyu Wu,Eldar Kurtic,Minlan Yu,Alexandre Marques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 H-Spec：一种混合 Mamba-attention 并行草稿模型，通过"目标 KV 直接复用 + 仅末位隐藏态注入"的混合上下文注入方式，避免独立草稿侧 KV 缓存，在三种目标模型上显著提升接受长度与推理加速，并在并发服务下降低 KV 占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码可无损加速大语言模型推理，近期块扩散草稿模型通过并行预测多个 token 降低草稿延迟。但现有块草稿器将每个输入位置的目标隐藏态投影到独立的草稿侧 KV 缓存，带来随并发增长的每请求内存与 KV 写入开销；若改为原位直接复用目标 KV，虽可消除该缓存，却无法在整个块内维持草稿质量。

Method: 提出混合目标上下文注入方法：在直接复用目标 KV 的基础上，仅在最后一个输入位置补充目标隐藏态，从而无需独立草稿侧 KV 缓存。基于该设计构建 H-Spec——一种混合 Mamba-attention 并行草稿器，用互补模块消费两类目标上下文：Mamba 模块以投影后的末位 token 目标隐藏态初始化，attention 模块原位复用目标 KV。尽管 Mamba 为循环式公式，其并行扫描特性使 H-Spec 仍保持块并行草稿。

Result: 在三种目标模型与多种任务上，H-Spec 相较最佳基线平均接受长度提升 5.0–13.3%，batch-size-1 的 token 间延迟加速提升 5.3–12.6%。在并发服务场景下，H-Spec 在各评估并发级别均取得更高吞吐，同时保持比基线更低的 KV 缓存利用率。

Conclusion: 通过混合目标上下文注入与 Mamba-attention 互补结构，H-Spec 在不引入独立草稿侧 KV 缓存的前提下兼顾了草稿质量与效率，验证了该设计在单请求延迟与并发吞吐两方面的有效性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding losslessly accelerates large language model inference by having a lightweight draft model predict future tokens for verification by the target model. Recent block diffusion drafters further reduce drafting latency by predicting multiple tokens in parallel. However, existing block drafters project target hidden states at every input position into a separate drafter-side KV cache, incurring per-request memory and KV-write overhead that grow with concurrency; directly reusing target KVs in place removes this cache but fails to sustain draft quality throughout the block. We propose a hybrid target-context injection method that complements direct target KV reuse with target hidden states only at the last input position, requiring no separate drafter-side KV cache. Building on this design, we propose H-Spec, a hybrid Mamba-attention parallel drafter that consumes the two target-context sources through complementary modules. Mamba modules are initialized with projected last-token target hidden states, while attention modules reuse target KVs in place. Despite its recurrent formulation, Mamba's parallel scan allows H-Spec to preserve block-parallel drafting. Across three target models and diverse tasks, H-Spec improves over the best baseline by 5.0--13.3% in mean accepted length and 5.3--12.6% in batch-size-1 inter-token latency speedup. Under concurrent serving, H-Spec consistently achieves higher throughput while maintaining lower KV cache utilization than baselines across evaluated concurrency levels.

</details>


### [254] [Opinion Leader Dynamics: How Sparse Attention Shapes Token Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24202)
*Jingkun Liu,Yue Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将token建模为单位球上的粒子，提出"意见领袖动力学"框架，理论刻画稀疏注意力下token分组内部收敛而组间保持分离的机制，并在多个前沿LLM上验证其预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏注意力降低了全局自注意力的二次成本并保持较强性能，但其受限交互如何塑造token表示的演化在理论上仍缺乏探索。作者希望从动力学角度理解稀疏注意力支持组内对齐、保持组间分离的机制。

Method: 将token视为单位球上的粒子，提出意见领袖动力学框架，包含两种机制：显式模型中固定代表诱导势场，将token吸引至不同局部极大值；隐式模型中不连通的交互组演化至各自共识方向。两者均被表述为反向Wasserstein梯度流，并在适当条件下建立指数收敛。同时在前沿稀疏注意力LLM上进行实证验证，并用有限粒子模拟说明理论收敛行为。

Result: 在四个基准上，Kimi-K3、MiniMax-M3和DeepSeek-V4-Flash在投影token表示上比稠密注意力模型GLM-4.7-Flash表现出更清晰的簇分离和更高的聚类分数。有限粒子模拟展示了理论预测的收敛行为。

Conclusion: 结果将受限token交互与不同的组级吸引子联系起来，提供了稀疏注意力如何在组内支持对齐、同时保持组间分离的动力学解释；观测支持预测的多组结构在前沿训练LLM中的相关性。

Abstract: Sparse attention reduces the quadratic cost of global self-attention while retaining strong empirical performance, but how its restricted interactions shape the evolution of token representations remains theoretically underexplored. Modeling tokens as particles on the unit sphere, we introduce opinion leader dynamics, a framework that identifies two mechanisms through which token groups converge internally while maintaining distinct limiting directions. In the explicit model, fixed representatives induce a potential that attracts tokens toward distinct local maxima. In the implicit model, disconnected interaction groups evolve toward separate consensus directions. We formulate both models as reverse Wasserstein gradient flows and establish exponential convergence under suitable conditions. We further connect these theoretical predictions to token evolution in frontier sparse-attention LLMs that motivate our framework. Across four benchmarks, Kimi-K3, MiniMax-M3, and DeepSeek-V4-Flash consistently exhibit clearer cluster separation and higher clustering scores than the dense-attention model GLM-4.7-Flash in projected token representations. These observations support the relevance of the predicted multiple-group structure to trained frontier LLMs, while finite-particle simulations illustrate the theoretical convergence behavior. Together, our results connect restricted token interactions to distinct group-level attractors, providing a dynamical account of how sparse attention can support alignment within groups while preserving separation between them.

</details>


### [255] [Displacement Geometry Captures Platonic Shared Reality Across Models and Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24209)
*Chenming Shang,Yujin Tang,Jun Jie Ou Yang,Ruize Xu,Adam Breuer,Nikhil Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 模型间共享的不是样本的绝对位置，而是样本间的位移方向；这一几何结构源于语义对齐与能力私有的分解，并催生了"影投射"能力迁移方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柏拉图表示假说（PRH）认为独立训练的模型会收敛到关于现实的共享统计模型，但近期研究只发现模型间存在微弱的逐点相似性。本文旨在解释这一矛盾：澄清模型之间究竟共享了什么、不共享什么，并利用该结构实现高效的能力迁移。

Method: 在单一正交对齐（仅旋转与反射）下，检验 44 个独立训练的视觉与语言编码器间位移向量与绝对位置的保持情况；将表示分解为线性对齐的共享语义成分与不可对齐的私有能力成分；从概念层面刻画几何结构（父概念与子变化向量正交、跨模型概念位移平行）；并通过微调与关系蒸馏实验对理论进行可证伪的预测与验证。据此提出"影投射"（Shadow Casting），实现 SHADOWCLIP。

Result: 位移向量在跨模态、跨能力不对称配对的 44 个编码器间被显著保持，而绝对位置不被保持，二者共同来自同一分解。实验证实：微调保持逐点相似性但破坏位移，关系蒸馏则相反。SHADOWCLIP 以数量级更少的计算量超越强微调基线。

Conclusion: 由于语义可线性对齐而能力不可，能力可通过源模型的单次缓存前向传播导入其他模型。缓存可与开放权重一同发布，使一个模型的能力可被下载并导入任意数量的其他模型而无需微调。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) claims that independently trained models converge on a shared statistical model of reality, yet recent work finds only weak pointwise similarity between models. In this paper, we show that what models share is not the location of samples in representation space, but the directions (displacement vectors) between them. Under a single orthogonal alignment--rotation and reflection only--these displacement vectors are substantially preserved across 44 independently trained vision and language encoders spanning modalities and asymmetric capability pairs, consistent with the PRH evidence. The samples' absolute positions are not, consistent with recent counter-evidence. Both arise from a single decomposition: representations split into a shared semantic component that is linearly aligned across models, and a private capability component that is not. We trace this geometry to concept-level structure: within a model, parent concepts are orthogonal to their child variation vectors; across models, concept displacements are parallel. Our theory falsifiably predicts (and experiments confirm) that fine-tuning preserves pointwise similarity but collapses displacement, and that relational distillation does the opposite.
  A major implication is that, because semantics align linearly but capabilities do not, capabilities can be imported from one model to another using a single cached forward pass through the source. We call this Shadow Casting. As a proof of concept, our SHADOWCLIP instantiation outperforms strong fine-tuned baselines at orders of magnitude less compute. A cache can be released alongside open model weights, letting one model's capabilities be downloaded and imported into any number of other models without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [256] [Adaptive Forgetting for Nonstationary Optimization: Towards Robust EEG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24233)
*Hongyu Zhu,Lin Chen,Jing Chen,Yuting Zhou,Mingsheng Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出张量级自适应优化器AFOR，将固定的二阶矩衰减系数改为由局部梯度状态在线估计的动态系数，在三个EEG跨被试基准上均取得最佳平均性能，平均测试准确率较Adam提升3.00%、2.07%和4.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG解码中跨被试泛化仍是核心难题。现有解码器通常使用Adam/AdamW并以固定的二阶矩衰减系数训练，但跨被试学习面临低信噪比、被试间差异大和梯度非平稳等问题。固定系数隐含假设各层与各时刻的梯度统计是同质的，限制了模型对跨被试EEG信号的适应性并损害泛化能力。

Method: 提出AFOR，一种张量级自适应优化器，将固定的二阶矩衰减系数转化为由局部梯度状态在线估计的动态系数。AFOR包含两个组件：残差对齐信号评分器（RASS）和自适应遗忘控制器（AFC）。RASS将局部梯度残差与方向一致性汇总为信号质量得分；AFC通过自参照归一化将该得分映射为逐步骤的有界衰减系数，并用累积乘积初始化校正以在时变衰减下保持一致。

Result: 在覆盖三个代表性领域的三个EEG基准上，采用严格的跨被试协议，AFOR在对比优化器中取得最佳平均性能，平均测试准确率相较Adam分别提升3.00%、2.07%和4.38%。

Conclusion: 通过将二阶矩衰减系数动态化并在线估计，AFOR缓解了固定衰减系数在跨被试EEG学习中的局限，有效提升了跨被试泛化能力，为EEG解码优化器设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) provides non-invasive monitoring of brain activity and is widely used in emotion recognition, motor imagery and sleep staging. Although within-subject decoding has achieved considerable progress, cross-subject generalization remains a central challenge in practical applications. EEG decoders are typically trained with Adam/AdamW under a fixed second-moment decay coefficient, even though cross-subject learning involves low signal-to-noise ratios, subject variability, and gradient nonstationarity. A fixed coefficient implicitly assumes that gradient statistics are homogeneous across layers and time, which can limit model's adaptability to cross-subject EEG signals and degrade generalization. To address these issues, we propose AFOR, a tensor-wise adaptive optimizer that converts the fixed second-moment decay coefficient into a dynamic coefficient estimated online from local gradient state. AFOR combines a Residual-Alignment Signal Scorer (RASS) and an Adaptive Forgetting Controller (AFC). RASS summarizes local gradient residuals and directional agreement into a signal-quality score, and AFC maps this score through self-referential normalization to a bounded per-step decay coefficient, with cumulative-product initialization correction maintaining consistency under time-varying decay.
  Under a strict cross-subject protocol on three EEG benchmarks that cover three representative fields, AFOR achieves the best average performance among the compared optimizers, improving the mean test accuracy over Adam by 3.00%, 2.07%, and 4.38%, respectively.

</details>


### [257] [Hessian Rank Constraint for Learning Structure of Nonlinear Latent Variable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24241)
*Zijian Li,Ruichu Cai,Feng Xie,Xinshuai Dong,Haoyue Dai,Yuewen Sun,Yujia Zheng,Guangyi Chen,Yingyao Hu,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出交叉Hessian秩约束(HRC)，作为非线性潜变量因果发现的秩工具，可识别潜变量维度并恢复因果结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测数据中发现潜变量及其因果关系是基础且困难的问题。现有方法多依赖线性关系或可逆混合函数等限制性假设，难以应对一般非线性混合情形，因此需要更通用的非线性潜变量因果发现方法。

Method: 提出交叉Hessian秩约束(HRC)。证明非线性情形下观测数据对数密度的交叉Hessian会产生秩性质，揭示潜变量信息，并在线性高斯情形退化为Tetrad约束。当两组观测变量被低维潜变量d-分离时，在条件对数密度导数的温和仿射导数假设下，该交叉Hessian的秩等于潜变量维度；该假设在低噪声或中等非线性下自然满足。并将HRC实例化到纯单因子测量场景。

Result: 理论上，交叉Hessian的秩等于被d-分离的潜变量维度，且在线性高斯下退化为Tetrad约束。在纯单因子测量场景下可定位潜变量并恢复其因果结构至Markov等价类。合成与真实数据集实验支持理论结论。

Conclusion: HRC为一般非线性混合下的潜变量因果发现提供了基于秩的原语工具，能够在较温和假设下识别潜变量维度与因果结构，并得到实验验证。

Abstract: Uncovering latent variables and their causal relations from observed data is a fundamental yet challenging problem. Existing methods often rely on restrictive assumptions, such as linear relations or invertible mixing functions. To better address this problem under general nonlinear mixing procedures, we propose a condition called the cross-Hessian Rank Constraint (HRC), which serves as a primitive rank-based tool for nonlinear latent causal discovery. In particular, we show that a rank-based property arises from the cross-Hessian of the observed-data log-density in the nonlinear case, revealing information about the latent variables, and reduces to the Tetrad constraints in the linear Gaussian case. More specifically, when two groups of observed variables are d-separated by a set of lower-dimensional latent variables, the rank of this cross-Hessian is equal to the dimension of the latent variables, under a mild affine derivative assumption on the conditional log-density derivatives. This assumption can be naturally satisfied when the noise level is low or the relevant nonlinearity is moderate. As a downstream application, we instantiate HRC in the pure one-factor measurement setting for locating latent variables and recovering their causal structure up to Markov equivalence. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets support the theoretical claims.

</details>


### [258] [Reinforcement Learning Inspired Black-box Adversarial Attacks for Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24249)
*Florian Krone,Elena Hoemann,Sven Hallerbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RIBA，一种基于强化学习的黑盒对抗攻击方法，可在少量查询下生成对抗扰动，性能优于现有攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络（卷积或Transformer）广泛用于计算机视觉，但易受微小扰动影响而改变预测。多数攻击采用白盒威胁模型，需完全访问目标模型，实践中不现实，因此需要更现实的黑盒攻击方法。

Method: 利用强化学习概念，在不可微的目标模型上优化扰动，借助强化学习算法的查询高效性设计黑盒对抗攻击（RIBA）。

Result: 在Cifar10和ImageNet上对比不同模型的最先进攻击：对ResNet-18生成对抗图像所需中位查询数减少25.4%；对ImageNet的Vit-B/16模型减少22.5%。并且在对抗训练模型上可匹配白盒攻击性能。

Conclusion: RIBA在黑盒威胁模型下以更少查询实现有效攻击，甚至可在对抗训练模型上达到白盒攻击水平，验证了强化学习用于黑盒对抗攻击的潜力。

Abstract: Neural networks, both convolution or transformer based, are essential for modern computer vision systems. However, they are vulnerable to small perturbations, almost imperceptible to humans, which significantly alter the model's prediction. These adversarial attacks are often considered to be a significant threat to the implementation of neural networks in safety-critical applications. Most attacks utilize the white-box threat model and therefore require full access to the target model, making them unrealistic to use in practice. We propose a novel approach under the more realistic black-box threat model that utilizes concepts from reinforcement learning to optimize perturbations with a non-differentiable target model. Reinforcement learning algorithms have already been optimized to be query efficient, making them an ideal starting point when designing black-box adversarial attacks. We show the success of our reinforcement learning inspired black-box adversarial attack (RIBA) in generating adversarial perturbations using only a small number of queries to the target model, by comparing it to state of the art attacks on different models on the Cifar10 and ImageNet data sets. RIBA takes $25.4\%$ fewer median queries to generate attacked images against a ResNet-18 on Cifar10 and $22.5\%$ fewer median queries to fool a Vit-B/16 model on ImageNet. Additionally, we demonstrate that RIBA can match the performance of white-box attacks on an adversarially trained model.

</details>


### [259] [Explainable Predictive Condition-based Maintenance of Naval-Propulsion Systems using Fuzzy Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24250)
*Dionisis Kalogeropoulos,Georgia Sovatzidi,Panagiotis G. Kalozoumis,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模糊决策树与深度残差神经网络的框架，用于船舶的可解释预测性维护，实验准确率达99.24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航运业对全球经济影响重大，需通过有效维护技术保障运营可用性与安全。预测性维护虽优于传统维护方式（可预测部件损伤、减少停机、延长设备寿命、提升航行安全），但现有方法无法向用户解释其结果，用户难以理解可能发生的故障。

Method: 提出一种结合模糊决策树与深度残差神经网络的新型框架，针对海军舰艇执行可解释的预测性维护；该框架能基于所用数据集生成模糊局部规则，并利用因果关系对结果作出用户可理解的解释。

Result: 在公开数据集上的实验验证了框架的有效性，准确率达到99.24%。

Conclusion: 该框架可为预测性维护结果提供可理解的因果解释，从而增强用户信任，并在实验中展现出高准确率。

Abstract: The shipping industry has a significant impact on the global economy, emphasizing the need for operational availability and safety through the use of effective maintenance techniques. During the last decades, predictive maintenance (PdM) has emerged as a promising solution compared to the existing conventional maintenance systems. This is because it offers several advantageous functions, such as damage predictions for vessel components, reduced downtime, improved and extended life of machinery, as well as higher safety during voyages. However, existing methodologies developed for performing PdM do not provide explanations of their results to users, so that they can understand the failures that may occur. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a novel framework based on a fuzzy decision tree and a deep residual neural network, aiming to perform explainable PdM on naval vessels. The proposed framework is able to generate fuzzy local rules based on the dataset used, and can provide explanations of its outcomes, using cause-and-effect relationships, in a way that are understandable to users, thereby gaining their trust. Experiments using a publicly available dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework, as it achieves an accuracy of 99.24%.

</details>


### [260] [MemCalib: Benchmarking and Optimizing Memory Use in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24259)
*Ruike Cao,Fanyu Zhao,Fugen Yao,Liang Dong,Jian Xu,Guanjun Jiang,Yifei Zhao,Han Zhang,Li Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MemCalib基准评估LLM对上下文中记忆的"适度使用"能力，发现前沿模型普遍存在过度或不足使用记忆的问题，并提出MemCalib-RL算法通过有序双向反事实信用分配加以改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体记忆的有效性取决于底层LLM能否对上下文中的每条记忆赋予恰当的影响力，然而这一能力此前被严重忽视，且缺乏相应评估工具与优化方法。

Method: 构建MemCalib基准，基于真实记忆系统场景评估记忆使用情况；分析常见后训练算法（如GRPO、在线自蒸馏）的表现；提出MemCalib-RL——一种有序双向反事实信用分配算法，将过度使用与不足使用的信号分离，并通过精确原子消融将信用定位到响应token。

Result: MemCalib测试表明前沿开源与闭源模型均难以恰当使用记忆，常出现过度或不足使用；常见后训练算法存在明显的方向性偏斜（一侧改善而另一侧恶化）。MemCalib-RL在Qwen3-8B、Ministral-3-8B-Instruct、Qwen3.5-35B-A3B等模型上取得最佳整体表现，更好地平衡过度与不足使用，且增益可泛化到外部基准。

Conclusion: MemCalib-RL能有效提升模型对记忆的适度使用能力并具备泛化性，消融实验支持其设计选择与鲁棒性，并揭示了其训练动态。

Abstract: The effectiveness of agent memory ultimately depends on whether the underlying LLM gives each memory in context an appropriate degree of influence over its response. Yet this capability has remained largely overlooked. To assess this capability, we introduce MemCalib, a benchmark grounded in realistic memory-system scenarios for evaluating memory use and advancing optimization algorithms. Results on the MemCalib test set reveal that frontier open- and closed-source models struggle to use memory appropriately. They frequently over-use or under-use memory rather than matching each proposition's actual use to its target level, leading to biased, low-quality responses. Experiments with common post-training algorithms, including group relative policy optimization and on-policy self-distillation, further reveal a clear directional skew: trained models improve in one direction while deteriorating in the other. We therefore propose MemCalib-RL, an ordered bidirectional counterfactual credit-assignment algorithm that separates over- and under-use signals and localizes their credit to response tokens through exact atom ablation. Results across model families and scales (Qwen3-8B, Ministral-3-8B-Instruct, and Qwen3.5-35B-A3B) show that MemCalib-RL achieves the best overall performance while better balancing over-use and under-use, with gains generalizing beyond MemCalib in external benchmark evaluation. Further experiments support its design choices and robustness and provide insight into its training dynamics.

</details>


### [261] [High-Dimensional Online Change Point Detection with Adaptive Thresholding and Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24278)
*Sven Jacob,Bardh Prenkaj,Weijia Shao,Gjergji Kasneci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于切片Wasserstein距离的变点检测框架，兼顾可扩展性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变点检测方法在高维场景下计算困难，且对检测到的变化缺乏解释，限制了实际应用。

Method: 利用切片Wasserstein距离将多元序列转为1维分数；分析随机切片的分布特性（可用Gamma分布近似）以校准阈值；提出自适应在线变点检测算法；并构建针对标注变点的对比解释框架。

Result: 相比流行的在线与离线基线，误报平均至少降低48%，同时保持相当或更优的检测性能，并能生成可解释的变点标注。

Conclusion: 该框架有效提升变点检测的可扩展性与可解释性，适合高风险场景部署。

Abstract: Change point detection (CPD) identifies abrupt and significant changes in sequential data, with applications in human activity recognition, financial markets, cybersecurity, manufacturing, and autonomous systems. Traditional CPD methods often face computational challenges in high-dimensional settings and typically provide limited explanations for detected changes, which can restrict their practical usability. This paper introduces a CPD framework that improves scalability and interpretability by leveraging the Sliced Wasserstein (SW) distance. Our contributions are fourfold: (1) we transform multivariate sequential data into one-dimensional scores using the SW distance, making the resulting representation compatible with existing CPD methods; (2) we analyze the distributional behavior of random slices of the SW distance and show that, under suitable assumptions, they can be approximated by a Gamma distribution, providing a principled basis for threshold calibration; (3) we propose a self-adapting online CPD algorithm that combines this SW-based score with an adaptive quantile-based threshold; (4) we introduce a model-specific framework for generating contrastive explanations for annotated change points. Empirically, our method reduces false positives by at least $48\%$ on average compared with popular online and offline CPD baselines, while maintaining competitive or superior detection performance. Code is available at https://github.com/jsve96/SWCPD_Code. At the same time, it produces interpretable change-point annotations, making it practical for deployment in high-stakes applications.

</details>


### [262] [TTSE: A Two-Track Online Self-Evolution Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24289)
*Ruimin Pei,Yongkang Wu,Shangyi Zheng,Yaqing Zhang,Deyang Li,Jianjun Tao,Xinyu Zhang,Xiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双轨在线自进化框架TTSE，将演化知识分离为环境事实(FACT)与任务条件化实现流程(TIP)，并从决策论角度分解超额风险，在多个基准上验证了双轨进化的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在持续交互环境中应用时，驱动自身能力进化是实现长期自主性的核心问题。当前环境知识多被视为外部固定输入而非智能体持续进化的一部分；强化学习方法往往只适应固定任务分布或单一环境，难以支持持续自主进化。

Method: 提出TTSE双轨在线自进化框架，将演化知识分为FACT（通过交互证据持续验证可靠性的环境事实）与TIP（任务条件化实现流程）。从决策论角度将智能体超额风险分解为环境表征遗憾与条件执行遗憾，刻画环境条件化策略严格优于无条件化策略的条件，并以FACT识别误差与跨条件不匹配代价界定下游风险。通过基于检索的注入方式集成到通用智能体框架。

Result: 在GDPevo上的消融实验验证了双轨进化的优势；在ALFWorld与ScienceWorld上展现出更优的任务适应能力；与Bayesian-Agent算法结合后，单轨消融验证了双轨优势，在SOPBench五大领域的聚合得分在三次独立重复中大幅提升；在真实端到端任务基准PinchBench上，通过检索式注入集成到通用智能体框架，在三次独立运行中稳定优于基线。
